*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Pearl结构因果模型（SCM）的两大限制——必须预设因果方向且禁止反馈循环——提出扩展：通过因果零点和激活算子处理对称物理/经济约束（如理想气体定律），并利用因果微分方程（CDEs）将表观瞬时循环归因于时间压缩，从而在时间展开的无环因果过程中统一处理因果零点和反馈，并给出扩展do-演算、可识别性条件、反事实语义及开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的SCM框架要求所有关系预设因果方向且禁止反馈循环，但这无法处理两类现象：一是对称约束（如理想气体定律）无内在因果方向，方向仅由干预产生；二是反馈循环被完全排除。本文旨在扩展SCM以容纳这两种情况。

Method: 1) 针对对称约束，提出扩展因果模型，引入激活算子（activation operator）和因果零点（causal zeros），并施加局部可解性和图可接纳性条件。2) 针对反馈循环，将表观瞬时循环视为时间压缩的伪影，使用因果微分方程（CDEs）建模，其中瞬态区是时间展开的无环因果过程，因果零点作为吸引平衡流形的定义函数出现，周期和混沌吸引子则通过吸引子相对干预处理。3) 给出扩展do-演算、可识别性条件和反事实语义。

Result: 成功形式化了对称物理/经济约束在因果框架中的表达，将反馈循环归约为时间展开的无环因果过程，并为两类扩展提供了完整的干预语义（扩展do-演算）、可识别性条件和反事实推理框架。

Conclusion: Pearl的SCM可通过引入激活算子和因果微分方程得到实质性扩展，从而容纳对称约束和反馈循环。该扩展保留了do-演算的核心精神，但需要重新思考因果方向的本体论地位——方向不再是关系的固有属性，而是干预的产物。

Abstract: Pearl's structural causal model (SCM) framework, built on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) and the do-calculus, is the dominant formal language for causal reasoning. Yet it carries two structural restrictions: every relationship must be pre-specified as a directed causal edge, and feedback cycles are forbidden. This paper examines two classes of phenomena that strain these restrictions. First, symmetric physical and economic constraints, the ideal gas law being the canonical case, carry no intrinsic causal direction. Direction emerges only under intervention, and which variable is solved for must be specified as part of the intervention. We formalize such constraints as causal zeros within an Extended Causal Model by adding an activation operator, subject to local solvability and graph-admissibility conditions. Second, for the class of finite-propagation state-space systems considered here, we treat apparent instantaneous cycles as artifacts of suppressed time and ground both causal zeros and feedback in Causal Differential Equations (CDEs). In these, the transient regime is a time-unrolled acyclic causal process, and causal zeros arise as the defining functions of attracting equilibrium manifolds; periodic and chaotic attractors define further regimes of the same dynamics, treated through attractor-relative intervention. We give the extended do-calculus, identifiability conditions, counterfactual semantics, and open problems.

</details>

*Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多阶段约束优化框架(MCOF)，通过熵约束VAE、均匀变换和约束优先滤波器方法，在低维隐空间中进行约束优化，在合成问题和ZINC250k药物设计任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE在约束优化中存在三个挑战：隐空间有效采样、识别影响目标和约束的活跃决策变量、在不破坏训练稳定性的前提下施加约束。

Method: MCOF包含三个阶段：(1) 熵约束VAE(EC-VAE)结合特征选择器，将目标和约束信息嵌入指定子集隐变量；(2) 均匀变换(UT)模块，通过逐维概率积分变换将非规则聚合后验替换为有界盒上的均匀分布；(3) 约束优先滤波器方法(CPFM)，通过交替的违反减少和目标减少步骤求解替代问题，无需乘子估计。未选择的隐变量坐标被重采样以生成多样化解码。

Result: 在合成问题上消融每个阶段并恢复了解析最优解；在ZINC250k药物设计任务上，生成的分子满足施加的约束且相对于训练集完全是新颖的。

Conclusion: MCOF有效解决了VAE约束优化的三个挑战，能够在低维子空间中高效优化并在真实药物设计任务中生成满足约束的新颖分子。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) transform high-dimensional, often noisy data into a compact latent representation, making downstream optimization more tractable. Three challenges persist in VAE-based constrained optimization: (i) sampling effectively within the latent space, (ii) identifying the active decision variables that actually influence the objective and constraints, and (iii) enforcing constraints without destabilizing training. We propose a Multi-stage Constrained Optimization Framework (MCOF). First, an entropy-constrained VAE (EC-VAE) coupled with a feature selector embeds objective and constraint information into a designated subset of latent variables, so that optimization proceeds over a low-dimensional subspace while the remaining coordinates supply solution diversity. Second, a Uniform Transformation (UT) module applies a per-dimension probability integral transform, replacing the irregular aggregate posterior with a uniform distribution over a bounded box and mitigating posterior collapse and Gaussian mixture bias. Third, a constraint-priority filter method (CPFM) solves the resulting surrogate problem by alternating violation-reduction and objective-reduction steps under a filter acceptance test, returning solutions that are feasible for the learned surrogate to a specified tolerance without requiring multiplier estimation. Finally, unselected latent coordinates are resampled to generate diverse decodings of a single optimized solution. We validate MCOF on a synthetic problem, where we ablate each stage and recover the analytic optimum, and on a ZINC250k drug design task, where the generated molecules satisfy the imposed constraints and are entirely novel relative to the training set.

</details>


### [37] [Hidden Boundary Motion in Transformer Optimization: Function-Space Orthogonalization of Affine Weight and Bias Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22927)
*Zhang Gongyue,Sheng Yixuan,Liu donghan,Wang Zhiyong,Ren Weihong,Liu honghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现当仿射层输入均值非零时，权重更新中包含与偏置更新功能不可区分的"边界运动"项ΔWμ，其量级远超显式偏置更新。基于此设计SBO-AdamW优化器将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，但存在偏置坐标漂移问题，需进一步研究稳定的中心化仿射参数化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化中权重和偏置被视为独立参数张量，但当仿射层输入均值非零时，权重更新包含一个与样本无关的位移项ΔWμ，其在功能上与偏置更新不可区分。现有优化方法忽略了这一隐藏贡献，可能导致优化效率低下。

Method: 1) 将每个更新分解为随样本变化的"形状"分量和共享的"边界"分量；2) 在四层Transformer上量化边界运动的量级；3) 设计SBO-AdamW诊断优化器，将g_W-g_bμ^T和g_b用独立Adam状态优化，并补偿权重诱导的边界位移。

Result: 边界运动的中位数范数占原始权重梯度范数的0.664；||ΔWμ||/||Δb||中位数为134.7；||ΔWμ||/||Δb+ΔWμ||中位数为0.994。SBO-AdamW将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，验证选定的测试准确率从78.73%提升至82.73%，最佳验证检查点从3000步提前至800步。

Conclusion: 隐藏边界运动是重要的优化机制，但SBO-AdamW存在严重偏置坐标漂移和边界能量大幅降低的问题，未能建立最终通用的优化器，需要稳定的中心化仿射参数化作为下一步。

Abstract: Weights and biases are normally optimized as separate parameter tensors, yet they do not represent separate functions when the input to an affine layer has nonzero mean. For an affine map $z=Wx+b$ with input mean $μ$, a weight update contains a sample-independent displacement $ΔWμ$ that is functionally indistinguishable from a bias update. We call this hidden contribution \emph{boundary motion} and decompose each update into a centered, sample-varying \emph{shape} component and a shared \emph{boundary} component. On a four-layer Transformer trained from scratch on IMDb, the bias-like term $g_bμ^\top$ has a median norm equal to 0.664 of the raw weight-gradient norm across affine layers and training checkpoints. More strikingly, the median ratio $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb}$ is 134.7, while $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb+ΔWμ}$ is 0.994. Thus, under AdamW, the observed boundary motion is almost entirely realized through the weight matrix rather than the explicit bias.
  We implement a diagnostic optimizer, Shape--Boundary Orthogonal AdamW (SBO-AdamW), that optimizes $g_W-g_bμ^\top$ and $g_b$ with independent Adam states and compensates the weight-induced boundary displacement. In a single-seed experiment, SBO-AdamW raises validation accuracy from 81.68\% to 85.81\% and validation-selected test accuracy from 78.73\% to 82.73\%, with the best validation checkpoint occurring at step 800 instead of step 3000. However, the moving-batch-center compensation produces severe bias-coordinate drift and strongly reduces boundary energy. The present evidence therefore supports hidden boundary motion as an important optimization mechanism, but it does not yet establish a final general-purpose optimizer. A stable centered-affine parameterization is identified as the required next step.

</details>

*Jake Schwaderer,Alexander Bastien,Omid Khormali,Alejandro Navarrete,Mia Pesavento,Angelika Elderbrook*

TL;DR: 本文利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）和图论方法，基于2000-2025年ATP单打比赛数据预测网球比赛结果。第一种方法通过下星过滤提取拓扑特征，结合随机森林模型达到66.2%准确率（AUC=0.719），其中排名贡献36.3%、中心性25.5%、TDA特征24.0%；仅用拓扑特征仍可达到63.56%准确率。第二种方法使用改进的Katz相似度指数，准确率为62.48%。这是首次将下星过滤应用于网球预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑数据分析（TDA）在体育预测领域的应用潜力，验证网络拓扑特征能否捕捉球员竞争结构中的有意义信息，并与传统排名特征互补以提升预测性能。

Method: 两种方法：（1）对球员竞争网络进行下星过滤，通过持续同调提取拓扑特征，使用四种汇总方法（VAB、HNAV、HWNAV、OW-HNPV）结合修正带深度分析，并采用ego图近似和三角形消除等优化处理约6.6万场比赛；随机森林模型结合拓扑、图论和排名特征。（2）改进的Katz相似度指数，融入时间边权重。

Result: 随机森林模型准确率66.2%（AUC=0.719），特征重要性：排名36.3%、中心性25.5%、TDA特征24.0%；仅用拓扑特征准确率63.56%。第二种方法准确率62.48%。

Conclusion: TDA能够通过网络拓扑单独实现高于随机水平的预测，同时在与传统特征结合时提供额外价值。这是下星过滤在网球预测中的首次应用，并对四种拓扑汇总方法进行了系统比较。

Abstract: We present two approaches for predicting tennis match outcomes using topological data analysis and graph theory on ATP singles matches from 2000-2025. The first method applies lower-star filtration to player competitive networks, extracting topological features through persistent homology using four summary methods (VAB, HNAV, HWNAV, OW-HNPV) combined with Modified Band Depth analysis. Algorithmic optimizations including ego graph approximations and triangle elimination enable analysis of about 66k matches. Our Random Forest model achieves 66.2% accuracy (AUC = 0.719) using topological, graph-theoretic, and ranking features. Feature importance analysis reveals that rankings contribute 36.3%, centralities 25.5%, and TDA features 24.0%, with topological features providing complementary signal. When rankings are unavailable, the topology-only model maintains 63.56% accuracy, demonstrating that network-derived features alone capture meaningful competitive structure. The second method uses a modified Katz similarity index with temporal edge weighting, achieving 62.48% accuracy on held-out test data. This work represents the first application of lower-star filtration to tennis prediction, provides systematic comparison of four topological summary methods in sports analytics, and demonstrates that TDA can achieve above-chance prediction using network topology alone while providing additional value when combined with traditional features.

</details>


### [39] [Distribution-Specific Curvature Control with Finite-Sample Guarantees for Open-Weight Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22929)
*Domenic Rosati,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Xijie Zeng,Hassan Chowdhry,Subhabrata Majumdar,Hassan Sajjad,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HarmAlign的方法，通过在估计的对比激活子空间上进行保函数谱变形，在防止有害微调的同时保留良性适应性，并提供了理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如谱变形）虽然能提供曲率证书来防止有害微调，但全局膨胀曲率会同时阻碍良性适应。需要一种能够在局部阻止有害微调而保持良性任务可训练性的方法。

Method: HarmAlign在估计的对比激活子空间上进行保函数谱变形，推导了子空间能量和局部有害分布曲率下界的有限样本界，利用稳定-进步二分法将认证曲率转化为条件收敛速率控制。

Result: 在固定架构、有限预算的一阶威胁模型下，HarmAlign在危险知识再学习和有害辅助微调两个设置中，阻止了直接微调和三种数据/目标自适应攻击，同时受保护的良性任务保持可训练性，且效果跨优化器变体、检查点和分布外有害微调持续存在。

Conclusion: HarmAlign通过局部化的谱变形实现了安全防护与良性适应的平衡，并扩展到意外的安全性退化和新兴错位等重要场景。

Abstract: A short fine-tuning run can undo the safety guards of an open-weight model---retraining a refusal-trained assistant to aid weapons development or produce hate speech. Preventing such harmful fine-tuning while retaining benign adaptability remains difficult: the only prior method with an explicit curvature certificate, spectral deformation, inflates curvature globally and thereby obstructs benign adaptation along with harmful adaptation. We propose HarmAlign, which applies function-preserving spectral deformation along a estimated contrastive activation subspace. We derive finite-sample bounds for the estimated subspace energy and the resulting local harmful-distribution curvature lower bound. A stability--progress dichotomy for constant-step gradient descent turns the certified curvature into conditional convergence-rate control. Empirically, within a fixed-architecture, finite-budget first-order threat model, HarmAlign blocks direct fine-tuning and three data- or objective-adaptive attacks across a hazardous-knowledge relearning setting and a harmful-assistance fine-tuning setting, while the protected benign tasks remain trainable. The block persists across the tested first-order optimizer variants over every attack checkpoint, and under out-of-distribution harmful fine-tuning, and it extends to important cases in our threat model: accidental safety degradation and emergent misalignment.

</details>


### [40] [Breaking the Total Variance Barrier: Sharp Sample Complexity for Linear Heteroscedastic Bandits with Fixed Action Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23679)
*Heyang Zhao,Tianyuan Jin,Weixin Wang,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Pan Xu,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究异方差噪声下的随机线性赌博机问题，提出方差自适应算法VAEE，通过主动探索策略实现了几乎调和平均依赖的简单遗憾界，首次突破了$\sqrt{Λ}$障碍，并给出了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异方差噪声线性赌博机的工作使用累积方差$Λ$刻画统计复杂度，但即使在一半轮次噪声接近零时$Λ$仍保持相同量级，表明$Λ$依赖并非最优，本文旨在打破$\sqrt{Λ}$障碍。

Method: 对于大动作集，提出VAEE算法，在未被淘汰的候选动作集中主动探索最大化信息增益的动作；对于有限动作集，提出基于方差感知的G-optimal设计探索策略。

Result: VAEE算法实现了几乎调和平均依赖的简单遗憾界；对于有限动作集，实现了对维度$d$更尖锐依赖的简单遗憾界；并给出了几乎匹配的下界，表明调和平均依赖率是不可避免的。

Conclusion: 这是首个打破异方差噪声随机线性赌博机$\sqrt{Λ}$障碍的工作，证明了调和平均依赖率的最优性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed increasing interests in tackling heteroscedastic noise in bandits and reinforcement learning. In these works, the cumulative variance of the noise $Λ= \sum_{t=1}^T σ_t^2$, where $σ_t^2$ is the variance of the noise at round $t$, is used to characterize the statistical complexity of the problem, yielding \emph{simple regret} bounds of order $\tilde{\cal{O}}(d \sqrt{Λ/ T^2})$ for $d$-dimensional linear bandits with heteroscedastic noise. However, with a closer look, $Λ$ remains the same order even if the noise is close to zero at half of the rounds, which indicates that the $Λ$-dependence is not optimal. In this paper, we revisit the stochastic linear bandit problem with heteroscedastic noise, where the action set is prefixed throughout the learning process. We propose a novel variance-adaptive algorithm \texttt{VAEE} (Variance-Aware Exploration with Elimination) for large action set, which actively explores actions that maximizes the information gain among a candidate set of actions that are not eliminated. With the active-exploration strategy, we show that \texttt{VAEE} achieves a \emph{simple regret} with a nearly \emph{harmonic-mean} dependent rate. For finitely many actions, we propose a variance-aware variant of G-optimal design based exploration, which achieves a simple regret with sharper dependence on $d$. We also establish a nearly matching lower bound for the fixed action set setting indicating that \emph{harmonic-mean} dependent rate is unavoidable. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work that breaks the $\sqrtΛ$ barrier for stochastic linear bandits with heteroscedastic noise.

</details>


### [41] [Spectral-Aware Analytic Class-Incremental Learning for Long-Tailed Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22931)
*Quyen Tran,Hai Nguyen,Quan Dao,Zhuowei Li,Nam Le,Trung Le,Dimitris Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对解析式持续学习（ACL）在长尾分布下的类别增量学习（CIL）中性能退化问题，本文提出几何-谱整流（GSR）框架。GSR作为一种各向异性谱滤波器，通过构造数据相关的谱扰动矩阵选择性膨胀尾部类的坍缩特征值，解决了标准岭回归对各向同性正则化无法有效处理尾部类谱坍缩的局限。理论证明GSR可改善Gram矩阵的稳定秩，实验表明其在长尾CIL场景中实现了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于递归最小二乘（RLS）的ACL方法在长尾分布的类别增量学习场景中表现显著下降，其根本原因是类别不平衡加剧了自相关（Gram）矩阵的病态条件，导致尾部类出现严重的谱坍缩现象——其子空间在数值上与噪声无异。标准岭回归（L2正则化）采用各向同性均匀惩罚，无法在不过度收缩头部类的情况下稳定尾部类，因此亟需一种能区分头部和尾部类特征谱特性的正则化方法。

Method: 提出几何-谱整流（GSR）框架，核心思想是将长尾学习视为谱正则化问题。GSR构建一个结构化的、数据依赖的谱扰动矩阵Δ，该矩阵能够选择性地膨胀Gram矩阵中尾部类坍缩的特征方向（即小特征值对应的方向），而对头部类的大特征值影响较小。这本质上是一种各向异性谱滤波器，与标准岭回归的各向同性正则化形成对比。

Result: 理论分析证明GSR能够保证Gram矩阵的稳定秩得到改善，确保数值稳定性。大量实验表明GSR在长尾类别增量学习中建立了新的最优性能（state-of-the-art），在计算效率与鲁棒泛化之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: GSR通过各向异性谱正则化有效解决了长尾CIL中尾部类谱坍缩问题，为解析式持续学习在非平衡分布下的应用提供了理论扎实且实践高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Analytic Continual Learning (ACL) offers a computationally efficient alternative to gradient-based approaches. Recent ACL methods are based on Recursive Least Squares (RLS) and have achieved the state-of-the-art results compared to other alternatives. However, they falter significantly in Class-Incremental Learning scenarios characterized by Long-Tailed distributions. While the ill-conditioning of the autocorrelation (Gram) matrix is a known limitation of RLS, we demonstrate that class imbalance exacerbates this issue into a distinct spectral pathology: "tail" classes suffer from severe spectral collapse, rendering their subspaces numerically indistinguishable from noise. Standard Ridge Regression ($L_2$) fails to address this effectively as it applies isotropic regularization - a uniform penalty that is insufficient to stabilize the tail without over-shrinking the head. To address this, we propose Geometry-Spectral Rectification (GSR), a theoretically grounded framework that treats long-tailed learning as a spectral regularization problem. Unlike standard isotropic regularization (Ridge) which uniformly penalizes all eigenvalues, GSR acts as an anisotropic spectral filter, selectively inflating the collapsed eigenvalues of tail classes. We construct a structured, data-dependent spectral perturbation matrix $Δ$ that selectively inflates collapsed tail eigen-directions of the Gram matrix. Theoretical analysis proves that GSR guarantees an improved stable rank for the Gram matrix, ensuring numerical stability. Extensive experiments show that GSR establishes a new state-of-the-art for analytic CIL, offering a superior trade-off between computational efficiency and robust generalization in long-tailed settings.

</details>

*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了LoRA微调中入侵维度（Intruder Dimensions）出现的每层临界更新强度阈值s*，通过矩形尖峰变形变换（rectangular spiked-deformation transform）和精确的久期方程表征，无需拟合参数即可预测入侵维度的出现位置。实验覆盖18个适配器、9840层扫描，阈值定位误差在2倍以内的层占82%，区分入侵维度有无的AUC均值0.89，并解释了LoRA与全量微调的不对称性。基于阈值导出的尖峰预算规则无需验证扫描即可减少最多62%的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调会产生入侵维度（intruder dimensions），即与预训练奇异向量几乎正交的新主导奇异向量，导致灾难性遗忘。此前缺乏理论能够逐层、基于实测谱预测入侵维度何时出现。

Method: （1）推导每层临界更新强度s* = θ̄/(γσ₁(BA))，通过矩形尖峰变形变换从W的实测谱计算；（2）利用精确久期方程表征更新后的谱，无拟合参数；（3）在4个密集Transformer族、状态空间模型、混合专家模型、编码器-解码器模型（共18个适配器、9840层扫描）上验证；（4）提出基于阈值的尖峰预算规则（spike-budget rule），仅需一次SVD。

Result: 阈值定位在2倍误差因子内的层占82%；区分入侵维度有无的AUC为0.89；两种预设边缘评估组合达98%，外部适配器确认达0.997；全量微调的更新远低于每层阈值；基于阈值的尖峰预算规则在最脆弱模型上减少62%遗忘且不损失任务性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次提供了预测LoRA入侵维度出现的逐层理论，解释了LoRA与全量微调的不对称性（全量微调的更新分散且低于每层阈值），并提供了无需验证扫描的遗忘缓解方案。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning can create intruder dimensions: new leading singular vectors of the updated weight matrix $W+BA$ that are nearly orthogonal to all pretrained singular vectors and that drive catastrophic forgetting. Since their discovery, no theory has predicted, layer by layer on measured spectra, when they appear. We derive a per-layer critical update strength $s^\ast=\barθ/(γσ_1(BA))$, computed from the measured spectrum of $W$ alone through the rectangular spiked-deformation transform, together with an exact secular-equation characterization of the updated spectrum, with no fitted parameters. In a pre-specified study spanning four dense Transformer families, a state-space model, a mixture-of-experts model, and an encoder-decoder (18 adapters, 9{,}840 layer scans), the law localizes the empirical threshold within a factor of two on $82\%$ of layers, separates intruder-bearing from intruder-free layers at deployment with a mean AUC of $0.89$, holds unchanged on six third-party adapters, and predicts where WikiText-2 perplexity begins to degrade; a combination of the two pre-specified edge evaluations reaches $98\%$ and is confirmed out-of-bag on the external adapters ($0.997$). Full fine-tuning disperses its update far below the threshold of every layer, which resolves the asymmetry between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Norm-matched interventions confirm that threshold-crossing layers, rather than update magnitude, carry the forgetting, and a spike-budget rule derived from the thresholds, requiring one SVD and no validation sweeps, reduces forgetting by $62\%$ on the most fragile model at no task cost.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究具有最低曝光约束的随机Bandit问题，提出基于块状时间变化底线的BDQ-UCB算法，通过舍入方法实现确定性满足曝光约束，并推导出由非强制性预算R（而非时间T）参数化的后悔上界O(√(KR log(KT)))，以及针对重叠组约束的Beck-Fiala零空间舍入方法。实验验证了方法的可行性及与调优Lagrangian基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统、内容策展和受监管分配中，每个提供者/臂/组必须在每个周期内获得保证的最低曝光量，而不仅仅是累积曝光量。现有方法通常依赖罚函数调参，缺乏确定性可行性保证和精确的遗憾分析。

Result: BDQ-UCB的高概率遗憾为O(√(KR log(KT))); MOSS变体达到O(√(KR)); 匹配下界给出极小化最优率Θ(√(KR)); 重叠组下Beck-Fiala舍入的违反度低于臂度数t; 非重叠系统下闭合解达到Θ̃(√(KT)); 路径可行计划采样规则达到条件性Õ(√(KT))保证。

Conclusion: 基于舍入的块状底线方法能精确满足曝光约束且无需惩罚调参，遗憾由非强制性预算R而非时间T决定，为Bandit问题中带确定性曝光约束的场景提供了理论和实践上有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Minimum-exposure constraints arise in recommendation, content curation, and regulated allocation when each provider, arm, or group must receive guaranteed exposure inside a period rather than only in aggregate. We study stochastic bandits with exact exposure floors and show that the right object is a rounding problem: a fractional fair schedule is realized as integral pulls, and the exposure error is exactly a discrepancy vector. The main contribution is a blockwise model with time-varying floors. BDQ-UCB satisfies every block floor deterministically and has fair regret governed by the nonmandatory budget $R$, not the horizon $T$, with high-probability regret $O(\sqrt{KR\log(KT)})$. A MOSS residual variant attains $O(\sqrt{KR})$, and a matching lower bound gives the minimax rate $Θ(\sqrt{KR})$, even with positive mandatory exposure; a kl-UCB$^{++}$ residual rule adds instance-dependent optimality. The formulation becomes essential for overlapping group floors: per-arm rounding can violate a group constraint by $Ω(s)$ in the group size, whereas Beck--Fiala null-space rounding meets every group floor within the block budget with violation below the arm degree $t$, and composes with UCB at the same $R$-parametrized regret. For learned group plans, we close disjoint systems at $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{KT})$, give a dual-ledger decomposition explaining why naive index rules fail under overlap, and prove a plan-sampling rule that is pathwise feasible under an initial cover-slack condition and attains a conditional $\widetilde O(\sqrt{KT})$ guarantee, leaving the condition-free overlap rate open. Experiments on synthetic floors, MovieLens-100k genre exposure, and deployment stress tests show exact feasibility without penalty tuning and regret competitive with tuned Lagrangian baselines.

</details>

*Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于熵约束（EC）和权重过滤方法的VAE训练框架，在保持潜变量解耦性的同时提升编码容量，通过约束单个潜变量的熵来平衡KL正则化带来的容量-解耦权衡，并利用软约束的松弛性在后续训练中剪枝低熵维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE的潜空间需要在高编码容量（每个潜变量能携带足够信息）和低维解耦组织之间取得平衡，但现有方法中增强KL正则化提升解耦性会剪枝掉潜变量，削弱KL正则化提升容量又会损害解耦性，两者难以兼得。

Method: 1) 将VAE训练建模为软约束优化问题，对每个潜变量施加基于熵的约束（EC），证明潜变量的熵是其与数据生成因子互信息的上界；2) 提出权重过滤方法，利用软约束的松弛性在后续训练中自动剪枝低熵（信息量少）的潜变量维度。

Result: 在dSprites上，EC使潜变量激活得分提升43-62%，FactorVAE得分达到0.891（对比β-VAE变体的0.847），重构误差降低38%；在MNIST上，权重过滤将下游分类器使用的潜维度从10降至2，准确率仍保持90%以上，收敛速度比无EC的相同流程快37%。

Abstract: The usefulness of a variational autoencoder (VAE) depends on two properties of its latent space that are hard to obtain together: high encoding capacity in the individual latent variables, and a low-dimensional, disentangled organization of those variables. Weakening the Kullback-Leibler regularization raises capacity but degrades disentanglement, while strengthening it prunes latent variables away entirely. We formulate VAE training as a soft-constrained optimization problem that addresses both. First, we impose an entropy-based constraint (EC) on individual latent variables, showing that the entropy of a latent code upper-bounds the mutual information it carries about the generative factors of the data. Second, we propose a weight-filter method that exploits the slack of the soft constraint to prune low-entropy dimensions during downstream training. On dSprites, the EC raises the aggregate latent-variable activation score by 43-62% over a vanilla VAE, attains the highest FactorVAE score among the \b{eta} \b{eta}-VAE variants (0.891 vs 0.847), and lowers reconstruction error by up to 38%. On MNIST, the weight filter reduces the latent dimensionality supplied to a downstream classifier from ten to two while holding accuracy above 90%, converging in 37% fewer epochs than the same procedure without the EC. We also find that low-entropy discrete factors tend to merge into a single latent variable, whereas high-entropy continuous factors are distributed across several.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VML虽可解释但仅提交单一假设，缺乏不确定性度量，且不同优化运行间假设差异大，需要引入贝叶斯框架量化不确定性。

Method: 将VML转化为贝叶斯推理：维护自然语言假设的粒子种群，通过Metropolis-Hastings（VPP-MH）或序贯蒙特卡洛（VPP-SMC）更新粒子，以贝叶斯模型平均进行预测；将LLM视为黑盒，无需logits或梯度。

Result: 在所有基准任务上优于单次VML运行，在大部分任务上匹配或超过最优独立VML集成的oracle，消除了VML偶尔产生的灾难性单次运行失败。

Abstract: Verbalized Machine Learning (VML) parameterizes a model as a natural-language prompt that an LLM evaluates as f(x; theta). The framework is interpretable, but it commits to a single hypothesis with no measure of uncertainty, and that hypothesis varies substantially across optimization runs on the same data. We propose the Verbalized Particle Posterior (VPP), which treats verbalized learning as a Bayesian inference problem: maintain a population of natural-language hypotheses as particles, update them with Metropolis-Hastings (VPP-MH) or Sequential Monte Carlo (VPP-SMC), and predict by Bayesian model averaging. Both algorithms treat the LLM as a black box, requiring no access to logits or gradients. A distinctive consequence follows. In classical Bayesian learning, model selection sits outside the posterior; in VPP both model structure and parameters share a single language space, and the posterior ranges over both. We evaluate VPP on regression, classification, and rule-discovery benchmarks. It improves over a single VML run on every benchmark and matches or exceeds an oracle-best ensemble of independent VML runs on most, while eliminating the catastrophic single-run failures that VML occasionally produces. Because each particle is a human-readable hypothesis, the posterior is itself something a reader can inspect, seeing in plain text which explanations the data supported and which it ruled out.

</details>

*Freddy Yu,Jashanjeet Kaur Dhaliwal,Subhadeep Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了物理信息神经网络（PINN）在预测农业N₂O通量排放中的应用，基于DayCent过程模型的机理方程构建物理残差，在四个美国农业站点数据集上训练MLP基PINN。结果表明PINN始终优于未校准的Cycles模拟（R²=0.01），物理约束虽降低分布内精度但提升了分布外鲁棒性，跨站点泛化仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N₂O是21世纪主要臭氧消耗物质和第三大人为温室气体，70%以上来自农业。现有预测方法包括过程模型（如DayCent、Cycles）和经典AI模型，但PINN在该领域的应用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于DayCent过程模型的机理方程推导出可溯源文献的物理残差，构建并训练MLP基PINN。使用多站点农业数据集（四个美国地理分布不同的农业站点），物理损失加权超参数λ进行了不同取值实验。

Result: PINN在所有λ取值下始终优于未校准的Cycles模拟（R²=0.01）；MLP基线在10个随机种子下平均R²=0.411。低λ时物理约束导致轻微性能下降，高λ时显著下降；留一站点验证中物理约束持续提升性能并降低变异性。跨站点泛化仍困难，所有种子和λ值在保留的地理上独立站点上R²为负。

Conclusion: 物理约束在牺牲分布内精度的同时提升了分布外鲁棒性，将模型锚定至生物地球化学上合理的行为。PINN在农业N₂O通量预测中有潜力，但跨站点泛化仍是未解决的挑战。

Abstract: Nitrous oxide (N$_2$O) is the dominant ozone-depleting substance emitted in the 21st century, and the third largest contributor to anthropogenic greenhouse gases due to its high potency and long atmospheric lifetime, with more than 70% of N$_2$O emissions occurring as a result of agricultural processes. Current approaches to predicting N$_2$O flux emissions include process-based models such as DayCent and Cycles, as well as classical AI models, but the application of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) to predicting N$_2$O flux emissions is largely underexplored. Our paper draws upon the mechanistic equations that underlie the DayCent family of process-based models to construct a rigorously derived, literature-traceable physics residual. We then build and train an MLP-based PINN on a multi-site agricultural dataset spanning four geographically distinct US agricultural sites. Across all tested values of the physics loss weighting hyperparameter $λ$, our PINN consistently and substantially outperformed uncalibrated Cycles simulation (R$^2=0.01$), with our MLP baseline achieving mean R$^2=0.411$ across ten random seeds. Physics constraints consistently degrade model performance in holdout validation, with marginal degradation at low $λ$ and significant degradation at high $λ$, but consistently improve model performance and reduce performance variability in leave-one-site-out validation. This suggests that physics constraints sacrifice in-distribution accuracy for out-of-distribution robustness, anchoring the model toward biogeochemically plausible behavior on unfamiliar soil conditions --- though cross-site generalization remains challenging, with negative R$^2$ across all seeds and $λ$ values on our geographically distinct held-out site.

</details>

*Evan Wang,Simon Chess,Sophie Szeto,Theodore Meek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种失败触发的级联学习策略，在Lean~4的\grind{}策略已经失败后才介入学习到的启发式方法，从而在不破坏原有证明能力的前提下提升性能。应用于代价感知的\ematch{}过滤和前瞻步骤，分别提升了约5%的速度和解决了5个之前超时的定理。同时报告了静态预测case split不比随机更好的负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \grind{}策略依赖手工调优的启发式方法来决定实例化和case split的位置，这些启发式方法适合用学习来改进。但由于\grind{}的搜索是非单调的，一个学习的启发式方法帮助了一个证明却可能破坏另一个，始终开启的替换方案通常净收益接近零。

Method: 提出失败触发的级联机制：仅在原始\grind{}已经失败后才调用学习到的干预，通过构造保证不会丢失\grind{}原本能证明的定理。将该机制应用于两个内部决策：1）代价感知的\ematch{}过滤；2）前瞻步骤。

Result: 代价感知的\ematch{}过滤解决了略多的问题且运行速度快约5%；前瞻步骤证明了5个之前超时的定理。同时报告了负面结果：跨四个基于特征模型，静态预测正确的case split不比随机更好。

Conclusion: 在定理证明策略中，学习最有效的方式是作为决定何时以及如何花费有限搜索的机制，并以可靠的符号化回退作为后备。

Abstract: Lean~4's \grind{} tactic combines congruence closure, \ematch{}ing, and case-splitting into a single automated solver, and like any such solver, it relies on hand-tuned heuristics to decide what to instantiate and where to case-split. These heuristics are tempting targets for learning, but there is a catch: because \grind{}'s search is non-monotone, a learned heuristic that helps one proof can break another, and an always-on replacement usually nets out near zero. We avoid this by invoking a learned intervention only after stock \grind{} has already failed: a failure-triggered cascade that, by construction, cannot lose a proof \grind{} already had. We apply it to two of \grind{}'s internal decisions. A cost-aware \ematch{} filter solves slightly more problems and runs about 5\% faster. A lookahead step, proves five theorems it otherwise times out on. We also report the negative result that motivated the design: across four feature-based models, statically predicting the correct case split is no better than random, because whether a split explodes is a runtime property that the features do not capture. Our results suggest that learning within theorem-proving tactics is most effective as a mechanism for deciding when and how to spend bounded search, backed by a reliable symbolic fallback.

</details>


### [48] [Bayesian Complete-Pooling in Cross-Subject Classification for Motor Imagery Electroencephalogram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22980)
*Ethan Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模对比研究（20个数据集），发现贝叶斯完全合并模型在跨被试运动想象EEG分类中，虽在统计上显著改善了可靠性并增加了预测不确定性，但实践收益有限，且能耗约为频率派方法的13倍。作者认为部分合并（partial-pooling）是更具前景的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑机接口长期追求免校准操作，但现有分类器仅关注判别性能（如AUROC），忽略概率预测是否经过良好校准。鉴于EEG信号的非平稳性以及分布偏移下点估计分类器可能过度自信，有必要系统评估贝叶斯方法在校准可靠性方面的实际价值。

Method: 横跨20个数据集的左右手运动想象EEG分类实验，对比6对频率派与贝叶斯完全合并流水线（共享相同特征工程，贝叶斯模型通过MCMC后验采样拟合）。主要指标为Brier分数（分解为可靠性与分辨率），辅以AUROC（判别）和Shannon熵（锐度），采用随机效应元分析（REML，Knapp-Hartung校正）及留一影响分析。

Result: 贝叶斯完全合并模型在可靠性上产生统计显著但实践不显著的提升，且预测不确定性增加（锐度降低）；Brier分数、分辨率和判别性能无显著差异。各指标研究间异质性低，但可靠性结果对留一剔除敏感。贝叶斯流水线能耗约为频率派的13倍，但绝对能耗相对于常见家用电器仍较低。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯完全合并模型对跨被试运动想象分类的实践收益有限，跨被试和跨 session 的部分合并（partial-pooling）是未来更有前景的方向。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) have long sought calibration-free operation, but classifiers are typically benchmarked by discrimination alone, blind to whether predicted probabilities are well calibrated - a meaningful gap given nonstationary electroencephalogram (EEG) signals and the risk of overconfident point-estimate classifiers under distribution shift. We conducted a large-scale study contrasting Bayesian complete-pooling models against frequentist baselines for cross-subject, left-hand versus right-hand motor imagery EEG classification across 20 datasets. Six frequentist pipelines were each paired with an analogous Bayesian pipeline sharing identical feature engineering, fit via Markov chain Monte Carlo posterior sampling. Our primary metric was the Brier score, decomposed into reliability and resolution, alongside AUROC for discrimination and Shannon entropy for sharpness. Each metric was analyzed via random-effects meta-analysis (REML, Knapp-Hartung adjustment), verified by leave-one-out influence analysis. Bayesian complete-pooling produced statistically but not practically significant improvements in reliability and increases in predictive uncertainty (lower sharpness); Brier score, resolution, and discrimination showed no significant differences. Between-study heterogeneity was low across all metrics, though the reliability result was sensitive to leave-one-out removal. We additionally profiled computational cost, finding that Bayesian pipelines consumed roughly thirteen times more energy than their frequentist counterparts, a cost that remains modest relative to common household appliances. These results suggest that Bayesian complete-pooling alone offers limited practical benefit for cross-subject motor imagery classification, and that partial-pooling across subjects and sessions is a more promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [49] [Label-free Industrial Fault Detection via Adversarial Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A System for Run-to-Failure Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22987)
*Dhiraj Neupane,Mohamed Reda Bouadjenek,Richard Dazeley,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对抗逆强化学习(AIRL)框架用于机械故障检测，通过从状态转移中恢复内在"健康"奖励，无需故障标签，在三个轴承退化数据集上优于传统监督、上下文老虎机和重构基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MFD方法依赖监督学习，但实际场景中故障标签稀缺；当前"基于RL"的方法退化为静态上下文老虎机，忽略了状态转移和时间折现因子，本质上是标准监督分类；重构方法依赖静态误差阈值，无法捕捉退化过程的时间动态性。

Method: 将机械故障检测建模为离线逆强化学习问题，使用对抗逆强化学习(AIRL)框架，直接从观测的状态转移中恢复表征设备"健康"状态的内在奖励函数，无需人工设计奖励或故障标签。

Result: 在HUMS2023、IMS、XJTU-SY三个全寿命轴承退化数据集上，AIRL是唯一在所有数据集上都实现非饱和的后检测一致性的方法；CB基线无法检测渐进退化，重构模型崩溃为全异常状态。

Conclusion: 逆强化学习范式为无监督机械故障检测提供了有效框架，通过恢复内在健康奖励函数，能够捕捉退化过程的时间动态特性，克服了传统方法对标签依赖和动态建模不足的问题。

Abstract: Machinery fault detection (MFD) remains heavily reliant on supervised learning, which struggles with the scarcity of fault labels in real-world settings. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a framework to model the sequential nature of degradation, current ``RL-based'' MFD methods reduce the problem to a static contextual bandit (CB) formulation: by ignoring state transitions and discarding the temporal discount factor, they collapse to standard supervised classification. We propose an adversarial inverse reinforcement learning (AIRL) framework that treats MFD as an offline IRL problem. Unlike reconstruction-based approaches that rely on static error margins, or CBs that ignore dynamics, our method recovers an intrinsic "health" reward directly from observational state transitions, requiring neither manual reward engineering nor fault labels. On three run-to-failure benchmarks (HUMS2023, IMS, XJTU-SY), AIRL is the only method achieving non-saturated post-detection consistency across all datasets, while CB baselines fail to detect gradual degradation and reconstruction models collapse into always-anomalous states. Code and data: https://github.com/dhirajneupane/AIRL-MFD-DN.

</details>


### [50] [Recycling computational processes of dynamic programming for combinatorial optimization problems: a reservoir computing approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23009)
*Sora Todaka,Akihiro Yamamoto,Nozomi Akashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的方法，利用动态规划的计算结果作为特征进行线性回归，实现多个组合优化问题之间的计算资源共享，在旅行商和子集和问题上验证了该方法能提高近似精度并减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算重用局限于单一问题内部，跨问题共享计算过程虽有潜力但手工设计算法困难，因此希望利用机器学习自动发现跨任务的复用算法。

Method: 基于储层计算（reservoir computing）思想，将动态规划求解组合优化问题过程中记录的计算结果作为特征，用于线性回归以辅助其他组合优化问题的求解。

Result: 在旅行商问题和子集和问题上，复用动态规划过程相比使用通用特征提高了近似精度，且相比独立求解减少了计算时间。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的计算范式，多个计算过程能够高效共享和回收中间结果与状态，区别于传统的计算设计方式。

Abstract: Reusing previously computed results is a long-standing principle for reducing computational cost, but such reuse has largely been confined to a single problem's computation. Sharing computational processes across multiple simultaneously solved problems remains possible in principle, yet designing algorithms that exploit nontrivial cross-task relationships is difficult to do manually. Here, we use machine learning to discover such algorithms automatically. Specifically, based on reservoir computing, we propose a method that uses computation results recorded by dynamic programming for combinatorial optimization problems as features for linear regression, leveraging them to assist other combinatorial optimization computations. We validate the approach on the traveling salesman and subset sum problems. Multiplexing the dynamic programming process improves approximation accuracy over generic features and reduces computation time compared with independent solutions. These results suggest a new form of computation, distinct from conventional computational design, in which multiple processes efficiently share and recycle intermediate results and states.

</details>


### [51] [Mini-batch Noise Lowers Sharpness via Dominant-Subspace Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23012)
*Junho So,Dongwook Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出SGD训练中梯度与Hessian矩阵top-k特征向量主导子空间强对齐，但该子空间对损失降低无直接贡献；相反，它通过小批量噪声产生锐度校正项，主导了SGD的锐度动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观察发现SGD梯度与Hessian主导子空间对齐，但该子空间内的更新对损失降低无实质帮助，这存在矛盾。作者旨在重新解释该子空间的实际作用。

Method: 理论分析主导子空间中梯度波动在方向上的平均效应，推导出由小批量噪声诱导的锐度校正项；实验上将该校正项加入GD，观察GD的锐度演化是否更接近SGD。

Result: 将推导的校正项加入GD后，GD的锐度演化与SGD的锐度演化更接近。

Conclusion: Hessian主导子空间的主要作用不是降低损失，而是通过小批量噪声诱导的锐度校正项解释SGD的锐度动态。

Abstract: During SGD training, the gradients often align strongly with the dominant subspace spanned by the top-$k$ eigenvectors of the Hessian of the loss. While this seems to naturally imply that loss reduction mainly occurs within this space, prior work has shown that updates within this dominant subspace make no meaningful progress in reducing the loss. In this work, we argue that the dominant subspace is better understood not as the main space for loss reduction, but as a key subspace for explaining the sharpness dynamics of mini-batch SGD. To explain the role of the dominant subspace in reducing top-$k$ sharpness, we show how the averaged gradient over fluctuations in the dominant directions produces a sharpness correction term, and derive a sharpness correction term induced by mini-batch noise in the dominant directions. Experimental results show that adding the derived correction term to GD brings the sharpness evolution of GD closer to that of SGD.

</details>


### [52] [All in One: Generative Modeling as Mean-Field Game Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23026)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MFGLab库，将12种连续时间生成模型统一为均值场博弈的变分问题特例；同时提出DI-Flow（可微熵函数）和基于学习的MFG求解器，填补了交互项和求解器在生成建模中的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间生成模型（如CNF、Score-based Models、Schrödinger Bridges等）虽可统一在MFG框架下，但两个维度未探索：交互项$\mathcal{I}$在多数模型中为零；丰富的MFG求解器从未应用于生成建模。

Method: 1) 构建MFGLab开源库，以代价元组为核心API，四组合成代价函数即可指定12种模型，自动共享训练循环、log-Jacobian和逆向ODE采样器。2) 提出DI-Flow，使用可微熵函数鼓励模式覆盖。3) 引入基于学习的MFG求解器，在随机动力学行上优于神经训练。

Result: 在2个2-D基准实验上，统一API相对于手写实现是无损的；基于学习的MFG求解器在随机动力学行上显著优于神经训练。

Conclusion: MFG框架可有效统一生成模型，交互项和MFG求解器是值得探索的新方向，MFGLab为后续研究提供了可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Mean-field games (MFGs) offer a unifying lens on continuous-time generative modeling: a cost tuple recovering twelve prominent models---Continuous Normalizing Flows, OT-Flow, Score-based Models, Schrödinger Bridges, and more---as special cases of one variational problem. Yet two dimensions of this space remain entirely unexplored: the interaction term $\mathcal{I}$ is set to zero in many existing models, and the rich family of MFG solvers has never been applied to generative modeling. We address both gaps with MFGLab an open-source PyTorch library whose primary API is the cost tuple: all twelve models are specified by four composable cost functions, and the training loop, log-Jacobian, and reverse-ODE sampler are shared automatically. We additionally propose DI-Flow, a novel cost design that uses a differentiable entropy functional to encourage mode coverage, and provide learning-based MFG solvers that substantially outperform neural training on stochastic-dynamics rows. Experiments on two 2-D benchmarks confirm that the unified API is lossless relative to hand-coded implementations.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-Agent Privacy Game in Federated Learning: A Unified Mean-Field View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23029)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将联邦学习隐私保护建模为平均场隐私博弈的框架，每个客户端策略性地选择隐私预算，通过平均场统计量与其他客户端交互，在无限客户端规模下达到可解纳什均衡，实现个性化隐私保护与指数衰减隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护联邦学习方法存在两个问题：(1) 注入校准噪声的方法限制了组合保证；(2) 多智能体博弈方法在客户端数量增多时纳什均衡变得不可解。本文旨在弥合这两条研究路线，提出一个兼顾可扩展性与隐私保证的框架。

Method: 将隐私保护联邦学习建模为平均场隐私博弈：每个客户端策略性地选择隐私预算，仅通过单个平均场统计量与群体交互。利用平均场极限获得任意数量客户端的可解均衡，并通过对数Sobolev收缩获得指数衰减的隐私保证。该框架将熵隐私基线作为同质特例，多智能体隐私博弈作为有限种群特例。

Result: 在二次回归、逻辑回归和MNIST数据集上的实验表明，该框架达到了与熵隐私基线相当的隐私-效用权衡，同时能够提供同质基线无法表达的个性化隐私保证。

Conclusion: 平均场隐私博弈框架成功将可扩展性与个性化隐私保证结合起来，为大规模联邦学习中的差异化隐私预算选择提供了理论支持与实用方法。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without centralising their data, yet privacy remains a persistent concern because the shared model updates can leak information about local datasets. Existing privacy-preserving methods either inject calibrated noise into client updates, limiting their composition guarantees, or formulate client privacy choices as a multi-agent game whose Nash equilibrium becomes intractable as the number of clients grows. We bridge these two lines of work by formulating privacy-preserving federated learning as a mean-field privacy game: each client strategically chooses its own privacy budget while interacting with the population only through a single mean-field statistic. The mean-field limit yields a tractable equilibrium for arbitrarily many clients, accommodates heterogeneous client preferences, and inherits an exponentially decaying privacy guarantee through a log-Sobolev contraction. The framework recovers the entropic privacy baseline as the homogeneous special case and the multi-agent privacy game as the finite-population case. Experiments on quadratic regression, logistic regression, and MNIST demonstrate that the proposed framework attains the privacy-utility trade-off of the entropic baseline while delivering a personalized privacy guarantee that the homogeneous baseline cannot express.

</details>


### [54] [Online Policy Evaluation for MDPs with Dynamic UBSR Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23030)
*Weikai Wang,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对风险感知强化学习中策略评估的效率问题，提出了基于线性函数近似的在线学习算法UBSR-TD及其变体，用于处理动态效用型短缺风险度量，证明了其几乎必然收敛性，并通过数值实验和易腐库存管理案例验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险感知策略评估方法要么局限于特定类型的风险度量，要么依赖模拟器访问，无法在完全在线场景中应用。因此需要开发计算高效且适用于在线学习的策略评估算法。

Method: 提出UBSR-TD算法，将损失函数引入时序差分误差中，使现有风险中性MDP的策略评估算法可直接适配到动态UBSR设定；在此基础上开发了多种加速收敛的变体。

Result: 建立了UBSR-TD算法几乎必然收敛的条件；数值实验验证了理论发现；在易腐库存管理问题中展示了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将风险度量嵌入损失函数形式，风险中性的策略评估算法可自然地推广到动态UBSR设置中，为风险感知强化学习提供了高效实用的在线解决方案。

Abstract: Developing efficient function-approximation methods for policy evaluation is a fundamental challenge in risk-aware reinforcement learning. Existing approaches either focus on restrictive classes of risk measures or rely on access to a simulator, limiting their applicability in fully online settings. In this work, we propose computationally efficient online learning algorithms for policy evaluation in Markov decision processes (MDPs) with dynamic utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR) measures under linear function approximation. Specifically, we introduce the UBSR-TD algorithm, establish conditions under which it converges almost surely, and develop several variants designed to accelerate convergence. Our formulation shows that existing policy evaluation algorithms for risk-neutral MDPs can be readily adapted to dynamic UBSR settings by incorporating a loss function into the temporal-difference error. Numerical experiments support our theoretical findings, and an application to a perishable inventory management problem with shelf-life uncertainty demonstrates the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [55] [MixQuant: Adaptive Mixed-Precision Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23047)
*Ashitabh Misra,Madhav Agrawal,Arham Jain,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MixQuant是一种技术无关的自适应混合精度量化框架，通过边缘化每层在上游随机量化配置下的失真来获得预算无关的分数，从而在单次离线校准后服务于任意内存预算，在Llama等模型上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度量化方法针对单一固定内存预算求解分配方案，但实际部署中预算会变化且在校准时未知。自适应量化虽能服务任意预算，但现有方法评估层敏感性时未考虑其与其他层量化水平的依赖关系。

Method: MixQuant是一个技术无关的自适应框架，可包裹任意基础量化器。其核心方法包括：(1) 通过边缘化每层在上游随机量化配置下的失真，获得预算无关的敏感性分数；(2) 在分配器自身产生的方案上校准量化器参数；(3) 惩罚将层维持在最低位宽的分配方案。部署时通过单次贪心扫描服务任意预算。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-2-7B和Mistral-7B上，使用AWQ和GPTQ基础量化器，MixQuant在所有设置下均优于自适应基线和混合精度基线，在最紧预算下平均准确率提升高达8个点，困惑度从12.43降至10.70，同时以可忽略的部署成本匹配ILP求解器效果。

Conclusion: MixQuant通过建模层间量化依赖关系，实现了无需重新校准即可服务任意预算的高效自适应混合精度量化，显著提升了量化模型在多种部署场景下的性能。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization improves the accuracy of post-training quantization by allocating higher bitwidths to sensitive layers, but existing methods solve the allocation for a single fixed memory budget. In practice the budget varies across deployments and is unknown at calibration time. Adaptive quantization addresses this with one offline calibration that serves any budget, yet current methods score layer sensitivity in a manner that does not consider its dependency on quantization levels of other layers. We show that a layer's sensitivity depends strongly on the bitwidths of its upstream layers and that this dependence shifts the resulting preferred bit allocation. We propose MixQuant, a technique-agnostic adaptive framework that wraps any base quantizer. MixQuant marginalizes each layer's distortion over random quantized upstream configurations to obtain budget-agnostic scores, calibrates the quantizer's parameters on plans the allocator itself produces, and penalizes allocations that leave layers at the lowest bitwidths. A single greedy pass then serves any budget at deployment. Across Llama-3.2-3B, Llama-2-7B, and Mistral-7B under AWQ and GPTQ, MixQuant outperforms adaptive and mixed-precision baselines in every setting, improving average accuracy by up to 8 points and reducing perplexity from 12.43 to 10.70 at the tightest budget, while matching an ILP solver at negligible deployment cost.

</details>


### [56] [The Entropic Bound for Transformers: Why Static Rank Fails and Attention-Native Rank Recovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23050)
*Byeong Hoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer任务定义了注意力原生容量度量——熵界（Entropic Bound），证明在线性注意力代理下，token混合算子的本征秩r*是所需最小容量的紧下界，且梯度下降可恢复该秩。通过分析从线性到软注意力的迁移失败原因，引入QK核的本征秩作为注意力原生容量度量，恢复了熵界的全部结构（不足性、可达性、可恢复性），并刻画了数据可预测性的精确边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律描述了损失随模型、数据和计算增长而下降的规律，但无法回答一个更基础的问题：对于固定任务，解决它所需的最小模型容量是多少？本文旨在从Transformer注意力机制的角度，建立任务固有容量的理论框架。

Method: 首先在线性注意力代理模型中，理论上证明token混合算子本征秩r*是紧下界；然后通过可控插值实验定位从线性到真实注意力迁移失败的原因（输入条件化的混合算子），引入QK核本征秩作为注意力原生容量定义；最后使用能量有效秩作为对softmax失真鲁棒的估计量，恢复熵界结构。

Result: 证明r*是紧下界且可被梯度下降恢复；定位出迁移失败的原因是注意力混合算子的输入条件化特性而非softmax或秩约束；引入注意力原生本征秩后恢复熵界结构；发现线性QK注意力下r*可精确恢复，软注意力仅部分恢复。

Conclusion: 熵界从事后描述符转变为注意力原生容量度量，具有精确刻画的可预测性边界，为理解Transformer任务所需最小容量提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how loss decreases as models, data, and compute grow, but they do not answer a prior question: for a fixed task, what is the minimum model capacity required to solve it? We study this through the Entropic Bound, a spectral notion of task-intrinsic capacity for Transformers. We first prove that, in a linear attention surrogate, the intrinsic rank $r^*$ of the token-mixing operator is a tight lower bound: any rank-deficient model incurs unavoidable excess risk, and the bound is achievable at $r^*$. We further show that gradient descent recovers this rank under standard low-rank implicit-bias assumptions, confirm all three properties empirically, and show $r^*$ is recoverable from data before training. We then ask whether this transfers to real attention. A naive transfer fails, and a controlled interpolation ladder localizes the cause precisely: it is not softmax and not a rank constraint, but the input-conditioned nature of attention's mixing operator, which a static weight kernel cannot summarize. Motivated by this, we introduce an attention-native intrinsic rank -- the minimum query-key kernel rank realizing the task within the attention class -- and show that under this definition the full Entropic Bound structure (deficiency, achievability, recovery) is restored for both linear and softmax attention, with the energy effective rank as the estimator robust to softmax distortion. Finally, we map the boundary of data-only predictability: $r^*$ is exactly recoverable for linear QK attention, even without the value map at scale, while softmax attention admits only partial pre-training recovery due to nonlinear inversion and kernel-value identifiability effects. Our results reframe the Entropic Bound from a post-hoc descriptor into an attention-native capacity measure with a precisely characterized predictability frontier.

</details>


### [57] [Through the Bottleneck: How Multi-head Latent Attention Separates Content from Position in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23054)
*Dhruvil S,Fenil Sojitra,Ravirajsinh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对DeepSeek-V2提出的多头潜注意力(MLA)进行机制可解释性研究，训练了一个1.14亿参数的Transformer，通过SVD、注意力头分类、线性探测和破坏归因分析发现：(1) cKV瓶颈学习纯内容表示，保留实体身份(98%)而丢弃位置信息；(2) 归纳头共位于单层(Layer 12)，而非标准MHA中的分布式形成；(3) 单个"语义枢纽"层(Layer 15)同时具有最高SVD有效秩和最强破坏归因分数；(4) 瓶颈全局过度配置，平均仅使用46%容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA已在DeepSeek-V2等大规模生产模型中被采用，实现了81%的KV缓存压缩，但尚无先前工作研究该瓶颈保留或丢弃了什么信息，以及它如何重塑Transformer内部电路结构。

Method: 训练1.14亿参数Transformer（在网页/代码/数学混合数据上预训练，在TinyStories上微调），运用SVD分解、注意力头分类法、线性探测以及破坏归因分析四种方法进行机制可解释性研究。

Result: (1) cKV瓶颈学习纯内容表示，保留实体身份(98%)，丢弃位置信息；(2) 归纳头共位于单层(Layer 12)；(3) Layer 15同时具有最高SVD有效秩和最强破坏归因分数；(4) 瓶颈平均仅使用46%容量。

Conclusion: MLA不仅仅是被动压缩注意力，而是重塑了模型组织内容、位置和电路结构的方式。本文是这一方向的初步数据点，限制条件详见第5节。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA), introduced in DeepSeek-V2, compresses key-value pairs through a shared low-rank bottleneck (cKV), achieving 81% KV-cache reduction during inference. Despite its adoption in massive production models, no prior work has studied what information this bottleneck preserves or discards, nor how it reshapes internal transformer circuits. We present the first comprehensive mechanistic interpretability study of MLA, training a 114M-parameter transformer (pretrained on a web/code/math mixture, fine-tuned on TinyStories) and analyzing its representations through SVD, attention head taxonomy, linear probing, and a disruption-attribution analysis. Our key findings are: (1) the cKV bottleneck learns a pure content representation, preserving entity identity (98% retention) while discarding positional information, validating MLA's separation of content from position via RoPE; (2) induction heads co-locate at a single layer (Layer 12), unlike their distributed formation in standard MHA; (3) a single "semantic hub" layer (Layer 15) simultaneously exhibits the highest SVD effective rank and strongest disruption-attribution score; and (4) the bottleneck is globally over-provisioned, using only 46% of its capacity on average. These findings suggest MLA does not merely compress attention passively, but reshapes how the model organizes content, position, and circuit structure. We view this as an initial data point and detail scope limitations in Section 5.

</details>


### [58] [Self-Boosting Vision-Language Models with Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23125)
*Shuai Wang,Daoan Zhang,Zhe Tang,Hao Cheng,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NOPD（噪声学生同策略自蒸馏），一种无需外部模型或真实答案即可提升视觉语言模型性能的自蒸馏方法，通过干净输入与噪声输入之间的预测差异产生自监督信号，在五项视觉推理任务上匹配甚至超越RL和外部蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM的后训练方法依赖人工标注数据、外部模型蒸馏、RLHF或可验证答案，缺乏无需外部监督的自我提升能力。

Method: NOPD（噪声学生同策略自蒸馏）：模型在噪声输入下学习，同时以自身在干净输入下的预测作为token级监督信号，利用干净与噪声输入间的预测差异构建自监督。

Result: 在五项视觉推理任务上有效；使用Geometry3K的2.1K样本训练后，Qwen2.5-VL-7B在验证集提升20点，MathVista提升7.4点；在三个模型、12个基准上均有提升。

Conclusion: NOPD是一种通用的VLM增强方法，无需外部监督即可自提升，性能可匹配甚至超越依赖外部模型或RL的方法。

Abstract: Post-training enables vision-language models (VLMs) to understand human instructions and perform various downstream tasks. Current post-training methods usually rely on human-annotated data, distillation from external models, reinforcement learning with human feedback, or verifiable answers. This limits their ability to improve without external supervision. To tackle this, we propose NOPD (Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation), a simple yet effective self-distillation approach that improves VLMs without any external models or ground-truth answers. Our key insight is that prediction discrepancies between clean and corrupted inputs naturally induce a self-supervision signal. In NOPD, the model learns from corrupted inputs while using its own predictions under clean inputs as token-level supervision. We show the effectiveness of NOPD on five visual reasoning tasks; it can match and even outperform reinforcement learning approaches or distillation from external models. Notably, when trained with 2.1K samples from Geometry3K, NOPD improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 20 points on its validation set. It also shows generalization on out-of-distribution test sets and achieves 7.4 point gains on MathVista. Furthermore, we demonstrate that NOPD is a general approach to enhance VLMs, achieving improvements across three models on 12 benchmarks.

</details>


### [59] [Diffusion-Guided Search via Exponential Tilting (DiffTilt): An Application to Falsification of Safety-Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23134)
*Tanmay Khandait,Preetom Biswas,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Giulia Pedrielli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffTilt框架，通过指数倾斜扩散模型诱导的联合分布来放大故障概率，解决自主系统中因乘性稀有性导致的安全关键故障发现困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反例搜索方法依赖条件采样策略，对环境和系统执行轨迹的联合分布进行因子分解，导致乘性稀有性效应——故障诱导输入和故障诱导轨迹的同时稀缺使穷举搜索成本过高。

Method: DiffTilt框架：对扩散模型诱导的联合分布进行指数倾斜，利用扩散引导采样实现联合空间中的重要性采样，通过引导分数实现概率质量向故障相关行为的KL最优重分配。

Result: 在ARCH-COMP基准和提出的牵引车-拖车基准上，DiffTilt在反例搜索性能上达到或超越现有最优方法，当规范定义不限于STL公式时优势更显著。

Conclusion: 联合生成模型可作为可重用的场景先验，无需忠实表示被测系统；昂贵的系统仿真仅用于学习评分函数，实现了仿真资源的选择性和自适应使用。

Abstract: Discovering rare safety-critical failures in autonomous and cyber-physical systems is a fundamental challenge in verification and validation. Existing falsification approaches rely on conditional sampling strategies that factor the joint distribution over environments and system executions, and therefore suffer from multiplicative rarity effects: the simultaneous scarcity of failure-inducing inputs and failure-inducing traces makes exhaustive search prohibitively expensive.
  This paper develops DiffTilt, a distributional framework that exponentially tilts a diffusion model-induced joint distribution over environments and executions. We show that diffusion-guided sampling admits an exact interpretation as importance sampling in the joint space, where guidance scores induce a KL-optimal reallocation of probability mass towards failure-relevant behaviors. We further show that tilting provably amplifies failure probability and strictly outperforms conditional sampling, which is limited by multiplicative rarity. In this framework, the joint generative model serves as a reusable prior over scenarios and need not faithfully represent the system under test. Expensive system simulations are instead limited to learning a scoring function that characterizes scenario quality, enabling their selective and adaptive use.
  We study DiffTilt on ARCH-COMP benchmarks, and we propose an additional tractor-trailer benchmark showing the behavior of several approaches when scenario generation is guided by a well-defined specification rather than a reward. The proposed method achieves competitive or improved falsification performance compared to state-of-the-art approaches, with larger gains when specification definition is not limited to STL formulas.

</details>


### [60] [Perturbative-NeuSA: A Structured Spectral Framework for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24345)
*Xianli Zhu,Jia Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Perturbative-NeuSA，将神经谱求解器重构为残差形式：仅学习未解析的高频扰动，而低频背景由固定谱算子+背景依赖修正+PDE缺陷项组成的结构化求解器处理。在Burgers、Klein-Gordon和波动方程上，结构化求解器即超越NeuSA基线；神经闭包仅在背景分辨率不足时有效，在分辨率适中时中性，在分辨率充足时反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经谱PDE求解器即使廉价近似模型已能捕捉大部分轨迹，仍学习整个未解析向量场，导致计算浪费且难以区分物理结构与神经闭包的各自贡献。

Method: 将目标解分解为低保真背景与高分辨率扰动，从精确扰动方程出发，组合固定谱算子、背景依赖修正、背景缺陷项和可选神经闭包，使物理结构与神经闭包的角色可分别度量。

Result: 在2D Burgers上，结构化修正将训练和泛化误差分别降低24倍和44倍；Klein-Gordon扫参显示神经闭包在低分辨率改进3.6倍、中分辨率中性、高分辨率降级；波动方程上闭包额外降低18%界面局部残差；有用闭包区间依赖初始条件谱且可在外推中消失。

Conclusion: Perturbative-NeuSA将神经闭包重塑为条件性、可诊断的修正，其效果由背景保真度、残差组织方式与闭包模型兼容性共同决定。

Abstract: Neural spectral PDE solvers often learn an entire unresolved vector field even when an inexpensive approximate model can already capture most of the trajectory. Here we introduce Perturbative-NeuSA, a residual formulation that decomposes the target solution into a low-fidelity background and a high-resolution perturbation, so that only the unresolved dynamics is learned. Starting from the exact perturbation equation, the method combines a fixed spectral operator, a background-dependent correction, the background defect in the target PDE, and an optional neural closure. This construction makes the roles of physical structure and neural closure separately measurable. Across 2D Burgers, Klein-Gordon, and heterogeneous 2D wave equations, the deterministic structured solver outperforms the trained NeuSA baseline while requiring no neural-network training. The largest gains occur on Burgers, where the deterministic correction reduces training and extrapolation errors by factors of 24 and 44, respectively. In addition, a Klein-Gordon sweep over seven background resolutions shows that the effect of the closure is conditional: it improves a poor background by 3.6 times, becomes neutral at intermediate resolutions, and degrades a well-resolved background. For the wave equation, however, the closure provides an additional 18% reduction when the remaining residual is interface-localized. Multi-initial-condition diagnostics further show that the useful closure regime depends on the initial-condition spectrum and can disappear in extrapolation when structured correction already captures the dominant Burgers dynamics. Perturbative-NeuSA therefore reframes neural closure as a conditional, diagnosable correction governed by background fidelity, residual organization, and compatibility with the closure model.

</details>


### [61] [Foundation Models and Fine-Tuning: Toward a New Generation of Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23146)
*Morad Laglil,Bertrand Pracca,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向零样本时间序列预测的基础模型（Foundation Models），涵盖其架构、预训练策略与优化方法，并通过微调实验证明后预训练微调能持续提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大语言模型在自然语言处理中突破性进展的启发，探索基础模型作为零样本时间序列预测的新范式，以消除数据集特定模型设计与人工调参的需求，提供统一的预测解决方案。

Method: 1) 综述了基础模型的主要架构、预训练策略和优化方法；2) 对选定的基础模型进行后预训练微调（post-pre-training fine-tuning），以增强其在特定数据集上的表现。

Result: 微调步骤在多个数据集上一致地提升了相比零样本基线的预测精度，支持了点预测与概率预测任务。

Conclusion: 基于大规模预训练的时间序列基础模型在零样本场景下表现优异，且通过微调可进一步适配特定数据集，是一种通用且高效的预测范式。

Abstract: Inspired by recent breakthroughs in large language models for natural language processing, foundation models have emerged as a promising paradigm for zero-shot time series forecasting, enabling accurate predictions on datasets never seen during pre-training. Ranging from tens to hundreds of millions of parameters, these models are pre-trained on vast and diverse collections of time series, learning generalizable representations that support both point and probabilistic forecasting. This approach alleviates the need for dataset-specific model design and manual tuning, offering a unified solution across forecasting problems. In this work, we review the main architectures, pre-training strategies, and optimization methods underpinning these models. We further investigate post-pre-training fine-tuning of selected foundation models to enhance their performance on specific datasets. Our empirical results demonstrate that this step consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the zero-shot baseline.

</details>


### [62] [XGRVFL-MV: Residual-Coupled Graph-Embedded Multi-View Random Vector Functional Link Network with FleXi Guardian Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23149)
*Yogesh Kumar,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差耦合和图嵌入的多视图RVFL模型（XGRVFL-MV），通过引入FleXi Guardian损失函数、图嵌入和残差耦合项，解决了多视图分类中视图特定几何结构保持、大预测残差抑制和视图间关系建模等挑战，在多个基准数据集上取得了竞争力的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机向量函数链接（RVFL）网络的多视图方法虽然利用多视图互补信息，但在保持视图特定的几何结构、限制大预测残差的影响以及建模多视图间关系等方面仍存在不足，因此需要一种更优的多视图分类模型。

Method: 提出XGRVFL-MV模型：为每个视图构建RVFL表示，采用局部Fisher判别分析加权方案构造内在图和惩罚图以嵌入图结构，引入有界非对称的FleXi Guardian（XG）损失进行残差学习，通过残差耦合项促进视图特定预测残差间的一致性，并采用基于Nesterov加速梯度下降的无逆一阶优化过程求解。

Result: 在UCI、KEEL、AwA和Corel5k基准数据集上的实验结果表明，结合统计分析和超参数敏感性分析，XGRVFL-MV在分类性能上优于基线方法，具有竞争力。

Conclusion: XGRVFL-MV通过图嵌入、XG损失和残差耦合机制有效提升了多视图分类性能，是一种有效的多视图RVFL扩展方法。

Abstract: Random Vector Functional Link (RVFL) networks provide an efficient randomized learning framework for classification. Existing multi-view RVFL methods utilize complementary information from multiple views. However, preserving view-specific geometric structure, limiting the influence of large prediction residuals, and modeling relationships between multiple views remain challenging. This paper proposes a Residual-Coupled Graph-Embedded Multi-View RVFL model with fleXi guardian loss (XGRVFL-MV) for multi-view classification. The proposed model constructs RVFL representation for each view, incorporates graph embedding with intrinsic and penalty graphs constructed using the Local Fisher Discriminant Analysis weighting scheme. It also uses the bounded and asymmetric FleXi Guardian (XG) loss for residual learning. A residual-coupling term is introduced to encourage consistency among view-specific prediction residuals while preserving view-specific representations. The resulting optimization problem is solved using an inversion-free first-order optimization procedure based on Nesterov accelerated gradient descent. We evaluate the proposed model on UCI, KEEL, AwA, and Corel5k benchmark datasets. Experimental results, together with statistical analyses and hyperparameter sensitivity analyses, show that XGRVFL-MV achieves competitive classification performance compared with the baseline methods across the evaluated benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [63] [Low-Rank Dependence Decomposition via Accelerated Symmetric Non-negative Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24518)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai,Jake Goldberg,Roman Yokunda Enzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需存储完整n×n矩阵的大规模对称非负矩阵分解（SymNMF）方法，在单GPU上可处理n≈10^5规模，多节点扩展到n=10^6。通过评估7类算法家族（30+配置），发现AdaGrad族方法在大规模下表现最佳，其中Block-SVRG AdaptGrow在尾部依赖矩阵上胜出，而全批量AdaGrad在相关矩阵上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称非负矩阵分解（SymNMF）能够从依赖矩阵中恢复潜在组结构，但其目标函数需要存储和操作稠密的n×n矩阵，导致内存开销为二次方复杂度，限制了此前工作只能处理中等规模的问题。本文旨在突破这一规模瓶颈，实现大规模应用。

Method: 1) 通过迹恒等式（trace-identity）重构目标函数，消除所有n×n中间矩阵的存储需求；2) 在绝对Pearson相关矩阵和极值理论尾部成对依赖矩阵上，系统评估7类算法家族（30+配置）；3) 采用两阶段协议（先小规模筛选收敛性，再大规模测试效率）；4) 提出三种新方法：分段AdaGrad、行随机SVRG、块SVRG AdaptGrow。

Result: 单GPU可达n≈10^5，多节点分布式扩展到n=10^6。11种方法在中规模收敛，6种在n=10^5时高效（5种AdaGrad族+ADMM），5种AdaGrad族方法在n=10^6时仍收敛。在尾部依赖矩阵（平坦、病态谱）上Block-SVRG AdaptGrow最优，在相关矩阵（优势低秩谱）上全批量AdaGrad最优。球面K-means作为硬标签基线在存在角度聚类结构时更便宜，但矩阵坍缩为单一公共因子时退化。

Conclusion: 大规模SymNMF可行，AdaGrad族方法在极端大规模下最具竞争力；不同谱结构需要不同优化策略，块SVRG适合长运行、病态谱场景，全批量AdaGrad适合短运行、低秩谱场景；球面K-means在特定条件下可作为替代但存在退化风险。

Abstract: Symmetric non-negative matrix factorization (SymNMF) recovers latent group structure from a dependence matrix, but its dense, quadratic-memory objective has confined prior work to moderate sizes. We present a large-scale GPU study of seven algorithm families (over 30 configurations) on absolute Pearson correlation and tail pairwise dependence matrices from Extreme Value Theory, two proxies for empirical risk-factor estimation on large portfolios. A trace-identity reformulation eliminates all $n \times n$ intermediates, so a single GPU reaches $n \approx 10^5$ and multi-node distribution scales to $n = 10^6$ and beyond. Under a two-phase protocol, eleven methods converge at moderate scale; six remain efficient enough at $n = 10^5$ (five AdaGrad-family plus ADMM), and five AdaGrad-family methods still converge at $n = 10^6$: AdaGrad, RMSprop, and three we introduce (Piecewise AdaGrad, Row-Stochastic SVRG, Block-SVRG AdaptGrow). At $n = 10^6$ the fastest solver tracks the matrix spectrum: Block-SVRG AdaptGrow wins on the flat, ill-conditioned tail-dependence spectrum, where its lower per-iteration cost decides a long factorization, and full-batch AdaGrad wins on the dominant-low-rank correlation spectrum, where the run is short. We also benchmark spherical K-means as a hard-label baseline: cheaper when angular cluster structure is present, yet provably degenerate once the matrix collapses toward a single common factor, where the soft factorization remains necessary.

</details>


### [64] [In-Context Learning as Implicit Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23153)
*Masahiro Kaneko,Timothy Baldwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于分数的上下文学习（score-conditioned ICL）与策略梯度优化之间的结构对应关系。通过构造性证明展示自注意力机制可以在特定权重配置下实现类似REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合，并推导了有界注意力更新引起分布偏移的精确上界，建立了与KL约束策略优化的信任区域类比。实验验证了LLMs能有效利用分数信息将输出分布向高评分样本偏移，且注意力权重与样本分数强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有经验发现LLMs可以通过将生成样本及其评估分数作为上下文示例来迭代改进输出，但这一现象背后的理论基础尚不清晰。本文旨在建立score-conditioned ICL的理论基础，揭示其与强化学习中策略梯度优化之间的内在联系。

Method: 1) 构造性证明：展示自注意力机制在特定权重矩阵配置下可实现类似REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合；2) 理论分析：在简化隐状态模型下推导有界注意力更新引起的分布偏移上界，建立与KL约束策略优化的类比；3) 实验验证：在多个LLM上验证理论预测，包括分数信息的利用效率和注意力权重与分数的相关性。

Result: 1) 建立了ICL与策略梯度优化的结构对应关系；2) 推导了分布偏移的精确上界；3) 实验证实LLMs能有效利用分数信息将输出分布向高评分样本偏移；4) 发现注意力权重与样本分数之间存在强相关性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上揭示了score-conditioned ICL与策略梯度优化之间的对应关系，为理解LLMs如何通过上下文示例中的分数信息进行迭代改进提供了理论基础。实验验证了理论预测，表明自注意力机制能够实现类似策略梯度的优化行为。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can iteratively improve their outputs by incorporating generated samples and their corresponding evaluation scores as in-context examples. Despite these empirical findings, the theoretical foundations underlying this phenomenon remain poorly understood. In this paper, we show that score-conditioned In-Context Learning (ICL) admits a structural correspondence to policy gradient optimization. We first provide a constructive proof that self-attention mechanisms can implement reward-weighted aggregation analogous to the REINFORCE algorithm under specific weight matrix configurations, and discuss the relationship between this construction and the behavior of pretrained transformers. The correspondence is directional in hidden-state space and holds exactly only under the stated simplifying conditions; we quantify its strength empirically. Within our simplified hidden-state model, we furthermore derive an exact upper bound on the distribution shift induced by a bounded attention update, yielding a trust-region-like analogy to KL-constrained policy optimization. We validate our theory through extensive experiments across multiple LLMs, demonstrating that LLMs effectively utilize score information to shift output distributions toward high-scoring exemplars, and that attention weights exhibit a strong correlation with example scores.

</details>


### [65] [Global Convergence of DGM and PINN Algorithms for Solving Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24726)
*Justin Sirignano,Konstantinos Spiliopoulos,Samuel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于一类半线性PDE，使用梯度下降最小化PDE残差的神经网络（如DGM和PINNs）会收敛到PDE解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DGM和PINNs广泛应用于科学机器学习求解PDE，但由于PDE残差目标函数的非凸性，训练所得神经网络可能只收敛到局部极小值而非PDE解。这一数学基础问题长期存在，亟需从理论上证明这类算法能够收敛到PDE解。

Method: 针对一类半线性PDE（解及其一阶导数均为非线性），从理论上证明了使用梯度下降法最小化PDE残差目标函数训练的神经网络将收敛到PDE真实解。

Result: 成功证明了对于一类半线性PDE，基于梯度下降训练神经网络的DGM/PINNs方法能够收敛到PDE解。

Conclusion: 该工作为DGM和PINNs提供了坚实的数学理论基础，回答了这些算法能否收敛到PDE解这一长期存在的核心问题。

Abstract: The Deep Galerkin Method (DGM) and Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have become widely-used methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) in the rapidly growing field of scientific machine learning. In these methods, a neural network is trained to approximate the PDE solution by using (stochastic) gradient descent to minimize the PDE residual of the neural network. Due to the non-convexity of the PDE residual objective function, the trained neural network may, in principle, only converge to a local minimizer of the objective function (which would not be a solution of the PDE). Therefore, there is a longstanding question regarding the mathematical foundations of these algorithms, and it is highly valuable to establish that the trained neural network will converge to the PDE solution. For a class of semi-linear PDEs (nonlinear in the solution and its first derivative), we prove that neural networks trained with gradient descent to minimize the PDE residual objective function will converge to the PDE solution.

</details>


### [66] [Wrong Design Intent Is Worse Than None: A Derangement-Control Diagnosis of Header Conditioning in CAD Program Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23191)
*Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过可执行几何断言作为独立指标，系统性地验证了代码LLM在参数化CAD生成中是否真正读取了设计意图头部（header），发现错误语义的header会显著降低生成质量，且该效应需要通过学习的header→程序映射实现，而非边际分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过轻量级设计意图头部（design-intent header）引导代码LLM生成参数化CAD模型，但缺乏独立于条件本身的指标以及因果控制来验证模型是否真的读取了头部内容，而非仅依赖统计关联或机械分布。

Method: 对Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B进行LoRA微调，在CadQuery风格的草图-拉伸程序前附加五特征设计意图头部（CADCON），通过生成实体的B-rep几何断言进行重打分作为独立指标（与头部定义的正则提取器无代码共享）。实验设置包括3个种子、预注册的{0%,40%}前缀×{正确,错误,遮盖}头部矩阵，以及头部洗牌（derangement）对照。

Result: (i) 40%前缀条件下，错误头部使模型性能降至无头部基线以下（0.43→0.30/0.21 text/token），但仅限于模型本就能无条件渲染的设计意图（多边形、薄几何）；圆形和高意图在此检查点处于生成地板水平，对比无效。(ii) 头部洗牌对照（保留边际分布但破坏内容相关性）仍能生成但不受错误头部影响，而标准模型受影响（3/3种子显著，p≤4.2×10⁻³），证明伤害需通过学习到的header→程序映射。(iii) 独立指标暴露了正确头部的实际收益远低于正则指标（token: +0.21正则→+0.02几何），量化了指标循环。(iv) 0%前缀时无条件基线完全无法生成有效CAD，错误头部伤害具有情境特异性。

Conclusion: 错误的设计意图头部并非噪声，而是主动误导代码LLM生成；独立于条件机制的几何验证指标对评估模型是否真正遵循设计意图至关重要，且该效应仅在模型已有一定生成能力时显现。

Abstract: Fine-tuned code LLMs can be conditioned on a lightweight design-intent header to steer parametric CAD generation, but whether the model actually reads the header's content has not been tested under a metric independent of the conditioning itself, nor with a causal control. We study CADCON, a five-feature design-intent header prepended to CadQuery-style sketch-extrude programs during LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B, re-scored by executable geometric assertions on the produced B-rep solid, sharing no code with the header-defining regex extractor. Across three seeds and a pre-registered {0%, 40%}-prefix $\times$ {correct, wrong, masked}-header matrix we find: (i) in conditional completion (40% prefix), a semantically wrong header degrades adherence below the no-header baseline (0.43 $\to$ 0.30/0.21 text/token) on design intents the model can render unconditioned -- polygonal and thin geometries; circle and tall intents sit at a baseline generation floor for this checkpoint ($\approx$0 in both compared arms) and are uninformative for this contrast; (ii) a derangement control -- retrained with shuffled ground-truth headers, identical header marginal but destroyed content correlation -- remains competent yet is immune to wrong headers, while the standard model is not (text headers; interaction significant on 3/3 seeds, p $\leq$ $4.2\times10^{-3}$): the harm requires the learned header$\to$program mapping, excluding the marginal/mechanical distribution-shift confound; (iii) the independent metric deflates the apparent benefit of a correct header (token: +0.21 regex $\to$ +0.02 geometry), quantifying metric circularity; (iv) the harm is regime-specific -- at 0% prefix the unconditioned baseline cannot generate valid CAD at all. Wrong intent is not noise: it actively misdirects generation.

</details>


### [67] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对小规模工业系统中数据稀缺导致图模型易学到虚假相关性的问题，提出DPR-GM框架，利用大语言模型从系统文档提取传感器间的物理耦合关系作为先验图结构，并结合正常数据的皮尔逊相关系数进行调控，最终在SKAB基准上超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的方法在小规模物理系统中，因标签异常和正常数据均稀缺，容易捕获虚假相关性并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: 1) 利用LLM从系统文档提取有向物理耦合，编码为二值域邻接矩阵作为结构门控；2) 用正常数据中的皮尔逊相关系数对该门控进行调制；3) 基于变异系数计算传感器级可靠性权重以调整异常分数；所有图组件在训练前固定，不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计和深度学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: 在数据稀缺的CPS场景中，领域结构化图先验可作为完全学习拓扑的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

</details>


### [68] [Variance-Preserving Orthogonal Selection (VPOS): Greedy Feature Selection via Orthogonal Deflation in PCA Loading Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23198)
*Baran Koseoglu,Berrin Yanikoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPOS是一种在加权PCA加载空间中进行无监督特征选择的贪心框架，通过零空间紧缩投影去除已选特征的方差方向，每次选择降低加载矩阵秩1，在8个基准数据集上均取得最低重构MSE，且比基于图的方法快10-140倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督特征选择方法（如图方法）计算成本高，而PCA类方法缺乏对冗余特征的有效去相关机制。VPOS旨在通过正交选择策略在保持低计算复杂度的同时提升特征选择质量。

Method: 1) 在加权PCA加载空间操作；2) 每次选择后通过零空间紧缩投影（null-space deflation）去除该特征方差方向；3) 保证后续选择覆盖协方差结构的正交部分；4) 每一步证明降低加载矩阵秩1；5) 贪心目标与单调子模最大化相关；6) 超参数d通过敏感性扫描中最小化重构MSE的规则选择。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，VPOS在所有数据集上取得最低重构MSE，运行速度比基于图的方法快10-140倍。与不使用紧缩的PCA相比（相同d），紧缩机制使MSE降低10-73%。

Conclusion: VPOS通过正交紧缩机制有效消除特征间的冗余方差，在保持高效计算的同时显著提升重构性能，证实了方差方向投影去除策略在无监督特征选择中的核心作用。

Abstract: We propose Variance-Preserving Orthogonal Selection (VPOS), a greedy framework for unsupervised feature selection that operates in the weighted PCA loading space. After each selection, VPOS projects out the chosen feature's variance direction via null-space deflation, forcing subsequent selections to cover orthogonal parts of the covariance structure. Each step provably reduces the loading matrix rank by one, and the greedy objective connects to monotone submodular maximization. The single hyperparameter $d$ is selected via a reproducible rule: the value minimising reconstruction MSE in a sensitivity sweep. On eight benchmarks, VPOS achieves the lowest reconstruction MSE on all eight while running 10-140x faster than graph-based methods at scale. Comparing against PCA (no deflation) at matched $d$ confirms deflation as the primary driver, reducing MSE by 10-73%.

</details>


### [69] [ParasGB: A Graph Benchmark Suite for Parasitic Estimation on AMS Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23225)
*Jiajun Zou,Jiawei Liu,Ao Liu,Junnong Tian,Yibin Zhang,Chengjie Liu,Yuxi Wang,Shan Shen,Wenhua Gu,Jun Yang,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParasGB是首个面向预布局阶段寄生参数预测的开源基准测试套件，提供从流片验证设计中提取的大规模、异构RC网络，并配套统一评估协议和标准化训练流水线，用于可重复的电路图学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进制程下互连寄生效应主导AMS电路性能，导致昂贵布局迭代，而早期寄生估计对寄生感知设计探索至关重要。然而，基于GNN的寄生建模缺乏公开、高保真的RC基准来支持可重复评估。

Method: 构建ParasGB基准套件，包含从商用EDA工具提取的大规模异构RC网络，覆盖节点级对地电容、边级电阻和耦合电容；提供统一评估协议和标准化训练流水线，并暴露标签不平衡、长尾分布和结构异质性等挑战。

Result: 提供了首个开源基准，支持多种GNN架构的标准化评估，并揭示了寄生预测任务中标签不平衡、长尾分布和结构异质性等关键挑战。

Conclusion: ParasGB为早期寄生预测建立了物理可解释的标准基准，为电路图学习和寄生感知模型开发提供了开放的可重复研究平台。

Abstract: As chip manufacturing processes advance to deep submicron nodes, parasitic interconnect effects increasingly dominate the performance of analog and mixed-signal (AMS) circuits and often lead to costly layout iterations. This makes early-stage estimation of parasitic capacitance and resistance important for parasitic-aware design exploration before full physical implementation. However, progress on GNN-based parasitic modeling has been hindered by the lack of public, high-fidelity RC benchmarks that support reproducible evaluation. To address this gap, we introduce ParasGB, the first open-source benchmark suite for pre-layout parasitic parameter prediction on circuit graphs. ParasGB provides large-scale, heterogeneous RC networks extracted with commercial EDA tools from tape-out-proven designs, together with a unified evaluation protocol covering node-level ground capacitance, edge-level resistance, and edge-level coupling capacitance. Within this framework, we benchmark diverse GNN architectures using a standardized training pipeline and expose challenges such as extreme label imbalance, long-tailed parasitic distributions, and strong structural heterogeneity. By establishing a physically grounded and standardized benchmark for early-stage parasitic prediction, ParasGB provides an open platform for reproducible research on circuit graph learning and parasitic-aware model development. All datasets, preprocessing scripts, and configurations are publicly available in our code repository https://github.com/ShenShan123/ParasGB.git.

</details>


### [70] [From Score Learning to Discretized Sampling: An End-to-End Generalization Analysis of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23226)
*Jinshu Huang,Yiming Jiang,Chunlin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于分数的扩散模型，建立了一个统一的理论分析框架，将有限样本学习、ResNet型网络参数化与数值离散化联合纳入考量，给出了端到端的总变差距离估计，并将生成误差分解为截断误差、反向离散化误差、泛化误差和训练优化差距四个可解释分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论分析通常依赖于先知评分函数或预设误差阈值，缺乏对有限样本学习、网络参数化与数值离散化三者如何共同决定生成质量的完整理论理解。

Method: 建立了从实际有限样本离散时间学习问题到理想连续时间总体目标的收敛性与泛化性统一框架。基于评分函数学习问题的泛化结果，分析由学习到的评分函数驱动的采样过程，并给出生成终态分布的端到端总变差距离估计。

Result: 将整体生成误差显式分解为四个可解释分量：前向过程的截断误差、反向时间的离散化误差、包含有限数据和前向离散化的泛化误差、以及训练优化差距。

Conclusion: 定量刻画了训练样本量、时间离散化网格和优化精度如何共同控制扩散模型生成样本的最终保真度。

Abstract: Despite the empirical success of score-based diffusion models, a complete theoretical understanding of how finite-sample learning, network parameterization, and numerical discretization jointly dictate generative quality remains underdeveloped. Existing sampling analyses often evaluate the generative performance conditional on an oracle score or a pre-specified error threshold.
