Method: 提出物理引导的生成式AI框架，包含三个组件：(1)属性感知变分自编码器，学习紧凑且物理信息丰富的潜空间；(2)条件潜扩散模型，以目标孔隙率和方向渗透率为条件生成多孔结构；(3)独立训练的、可微的结构-属性代理模型，在去噪和解码过程中提供属性级别的反馈以优化生成样本。

Result: 在程序生成结构和真实微CT多孔介质数据集上，与代表性的属性感知变分自编码器和潜扩散基线相比，该方法在目标属性匹配、方向渗透率控制和属性相关性方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一条可扩展的复杂多孔几何可控逆向设计路径，为工程和先进材料发现中基于模拟的生成式AI工具奠定了基础。

Abstract: Inverse design of three-dimensional porous media is central to applications in filtration, catalysis, energy storage, fuel cells, thermal management, and biomedical scaffolds, but remains challenging because many distinct pore geometries can share similar porosity or permeability while small structural changes can strongly affect transport behaviour. This paper proposes a physics-guided generative AI framework for property-targeted porous media design, combining a property-aware variational autoencoder, a conditional latent diffusion model, and an independently trained differentiable structure-to-property surrogate. The framework learns a compact, physically informative latent design space, generates porous structures conditioned on target porosity and directional permeability, and refines generated samples using property-level feedback during denoising and decoding. Experiments on procedurally generated structures and real micro-CT porous-media datasets show improved target-property matching, directional permeability control, and property correlation compared with representative property-aware variational-autoencoder and latent-diffusion baselines. The results demonstrate a scalable route towards controllable inverse design of complex porous geometries and establish a foundation for simulation-informed generative AI tools in engineering and advanced materials discovery.

</details>


### [145] [MEGA-CL: A Molecular Foundation Model for Generalizable ADMET Prediction through Graph External Attention and Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24314)
*Tinghui Jin,Kedu Jin,Ying Li,Guanghui Ren,Jingzhi Xue,Shiyu Zhou,Xiaoli Dai,Li-bin Wei,Xijing Chen,Di Zhao,Jinfeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEGA-CL是一个基于图神经网络的基础框架，通过自监督对比学习、多头外部注意力机制和增强的消息传递架构，在13个基准数据集和21个ADMET下游任务上优于现有模型，并在清除率、分布容积等回归任务以及CYP450抑制预测中展现出临床相关的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小分子ADMET性质的准确预测在药物发现中仍然是一个重大挑战，现有方法在建模局部化学子结构和全局图间关系方面存在不足，且深度图网络容易出现过平滑问题。

Method: MEGA-CL融合了自监督对比学习、多头外部注意力机制和增强的消息传递架构，能够同时建模局部化学子结构和全局图间关系，同时缓解深度图网络中的过平滑问题。

Result: 在13个基准数据集和21个ADMET下游任务上持续优于现有基线模型；在清除率(CL)和稳态分布容积(VDss)等回归任务上表现稳健；超过75%的预测落在3倍误差范围内；在新近FDA批准药物的18个新化合物外部评估中，超过50%的人肝微粒体清除率(HLMC)预测在2倍误差内；在3个临床前候选药物的前瞻性评估中，HLMC预测值在实验测量值的2.5倍以内，73.3%的CYP450抑制终点(11/15)正确分类。

Conclusion: MEGA-CL作为一个通用框架，有望加速计算机辅助ADMET评估和早期候选药物优化。

Abstract: Predicting the absorption, distribution, metabolism, excretion and toxicity (ADMET) properties of small molecules remains a major challenge in drug discovery. Here, we present MEGA-CL, a foundation graph neural network framework for universal molecular ADMET prediction. MEGA-CL integrates self-supervised contrastive learning with a multi-head external attention mechanism and an enhanced message-passing architecture, enabling simultaneous modeling of local chemical substructures and global inter-graph relationships while mitigating over-smoothing effects commonly observed in deep graph networks. Across 13 benchmark datasets and 21 downstream ADMET tasks, MEGA-CL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baseline models. In particular, the framework demonstrates robust performance on challenging regression tasks, including clearance (CL) and steady-state volume of distribution (VDss), while maintaining strong generalization ability in independent external validation. Clinically relevant predictive accuracy was achieved, with more than 75% of predictions falling within a 3-fold error range. In an external evaluation on 18 novel compounds derived from recently approved FDA drugs, over 50% of human liver microsome clearance (HLMC) predictions were within a 2-fold error range. To further assess its practical applicability, MEGA-CL was prospectively evaluated on three preclinical drug candidates using in vitro hepatic microsomal metabolism assays and CYP450 inhibition assays guided by model predictions. The predicted HLMC values for all candidates were within 2.5-fold of the experimentally measured values, and 73.3% of CYP450 inhibition endpoints (11/15) were correctly classified. These results demonstrate the potential of MEGA-CL as a generalizable framework for accelerating in silico ADMET evaluation and early-stage drug candidate optimization.

</details>


### [146] [DynaCalKV: Key-Value Cache Compression via Head Grouping and Adaptive Rank Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24331)
*Tan T. Nguyen,Quan V. Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的低秩KV缓存压缩框架，针对Key和Value缓存分别处理：Key缓存基于CKA相似度动态分组注意力头并自适应分配秩预算，Value缓存采用ReCalKV方法通过离线校准改进重构质量。实验表明该方法在减少参数的同时保持精度，对MHA模型尤其有效，对GQA模型需更保守应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理阶段的长上下文窗口导致KV缓存成为显著瓶颈，现有低秩压缩方法很少区分Key和Value缓存的不同作用，且通常使用固定的注意力头分组，未能充分利用注意力头之间的结构相似性。

Method: 1) Key缓存：基于CKA相似度动态分组注意力头，在参数预算下自适应分配秩预算；2) Value缓存：采用ReCalKV方法，通过离线校准改进低秩分解的重构质量。

Result: 在三个指令微调LLM上的实验表明，该方法减少了Key缓存参数数量，同时保持了有竞争力的准确率。对MHA模型效果显著，对GQA模型需更保守应用。

Conclusion: 提出的低秩KV缓存压缩框架通过区分Key和Value缓存并动态分组注意力头，有效缓解了长上下文场景下的KV缓存瓶颈，在不同注意力架构上表现出差异化的适用性。

Abstract: As the inference phase of Large Language Models (LLMs) requires handling long context windows, the Key-Value (KV) cache initially appears to address this challenge but eventually becomes a significant bottleneck as the context window continues to grow. Low-rank compression has recently been studied as an effective approach to reduce KV cache memory while maintaining model performance. However, only a few existing methods treat the Key and Value caches differently, despite their distinct roles. Moreover, these methods typically employ fixed attention-head grouping, which may not fully exploit the structural similarity among attention heads. In this paper, we propose an improved low-rank KV cache compression framework. For the Key cache, we dynamically group attention heads based on Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) similarity and allocate the rank budget adaptively under a parameter budget. For the Value cache, we adopt the same approach as ReCalKV, refining the low-rank decomposition through offline calibration to improve reconstruction quality. Experimental results on three instruction-tuned LLMs show that our method reduces the number of Key cache parameters while maintaining competitive accuracy. We further observe that the proposed strategy is particularly effective for Multi-Head Attention (MHA) models, whereas it should be applied more conservatively to Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) models, especially in long-context settings.

</details>


### [147] [Unsupervised Graph Representation Learning with Complementary View Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24338)
*Zengyi Wo,Shiyu Zhang,Qiyao Peng,Tianpeng Li,Xuan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlignGAE 通过双编码器架构和互补视图对齐策略，在无监督图表示学习中保留全频谱信息，有效解决了传统自编码器在同质图假设下对异质图表现不佳的问题，在12个基准数据集上，异质图节点分类任务最高提升18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAE的无监督图表示学习方法依赖消息传递机制，隐含同质性假设，导致在异质图上性能下降，丢失了识别异质模式所需的关键高频分量。

Method: 提出AlignGAE，包含：(1) 双编码器架构分别处理结构和属性信息；(2) 节点位置编码近似邻域身份分布(NID)；(3) 边和节点属性的双重建任务；(4) 基于理论的NID对齐策略保证视图间语义一致性。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上，AlignGAE在异质图节点分类任务中比SOTA方法提升最高18.7%，在同质图上保持竞争力。光谱分析证明对齐损失收敛时达到最优表示特性。

Conclusion: 建立了频率感知图表示学习的新范式，通过保留全频谱信息解决了异质图上的表示学习难题。

Abstract: Unsupervised graph representation learning aims to derive meaningful node embeddings by capturing both structural and attribute information without relying on labeled data. Existing methods, such as GAEs, have demonstrated effectiveness but typically rely on message-passing mechanisms that assume homophily, leading to performance degradation on heterophilous graphs, where connected nodes exhibit dissimilar features. This homophily bias results in the loss of critical high-frequency components that are essential for identifying heterophilous patterns. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{AlignGAE}, a novel extension of \textit{MaskGAE} that preserves the full frequency spectrum through complementary view alignment. Our framework introduces a dual-encoder architecture that separately processes structural and attribute information, incorporates node positional encoding to approximate Neighborhood Identity Distribution (NID), and employs dual reconstruction tasks for both edges and node attributes. We further propose theoretically grounded NID alignment strategies that ensure semantic consistency across views while preserving their distinct characteristics. Through comprehensive spectral analysis, we demonstrate that \textsc{AlignGAE} achieves optimal representation properties when the alignment loss converges. Extensive experiments across 12 benchmark datasets validate our approach, showing that \textsc{AlignGAE} outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 18.7\% on heterophilous graphs in node classification, while maintaining competitive performance on homophilous graphs. Our results establish a new paradigm for frequency-aware graph representation learning.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Aggregate Risk: Role-Stratified Conformal Risk Control for LLM Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24343)
*Md Ashikur Rahman,Md Arifur Rahman,Niamul Hassan Samin,Khandaker Rifah Tasnia,Sifat Rahman Ahona,Juena Ahmed Noshin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出角色分层每场域共形风险控制方法，为语言模型智能体的结构化工具调用中不同语义角色（如收件人、账号、命令等）分别设置风险阈值和预算，相比传统对整个动作进行统计风险控制的方法，能够更精确地保证高风险字段的有限样本风险控制。实验在AgentDojo和InjecAgent上使用六种语言模型验证，证明了该方法在模型迁移、攻击迁移、检测器噪声、分布漂移等场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对整个工具调用动作进行统计风险控制，忽略了不同参数角色（如收件人、账号、命令 vs 邮件正文）承载的风险差异，导致高风险稀有字段的风险被低风险常见字段掩盖，无法提供角色级别的精确风险保证。

Method: 提出角色分层每场域共形风险控制：1) 对每个语义角色分别校准风险阈值和预算，为频繁出现的角色提供直接有限样本保证；2) 对于稀有角色，采用池化认证（pooled certification）处理；3) 可作为任何检测器的校准层，无需修改底层模型。

Result: 在AgentDojo和InjecAgent上使用六种语言模型的实验中，该方法实现了最一致的角色级预算合规性，在模型迁移、攻击迁移、检测器噪声、渐进漂移、未见工具套件和自适应攻击下均优于聚合式方法。理论分析表明，聚合式认证需要有效预算αp_r才能保证角色级风险α，而角色分层认证直接对充分采样的角色提供有限样本保证。

Conclusion: 结构化工具调用应当在语义角色层面而非整个动作层面进行认证，角色分层共形风险控制提供了形式化的每角色有限样本保证，并在分布漂移下具有经验合规性。

Abstract: Language-model agents act through structured tool calls whose arguments carry different risks. Untrusted content may safely influence an email body but should not determine a recipient, account, command, or credential. Existing statistical methods typically control risk over the entire action, allowing failures in rare, high-risk fields to be obscured by benign arguments. We introduce role-stratified per-field conformal risk control, a calibration layer that wraps any per-field detector and sets separate thresholds and risk budgets for semantic argument roles. For a role with prevalence $p_r$, aggregate-only certification must use an effective budget of $αp_r$ to guarantee role-specific risk $α$, whereas role-stratified calibration certifies each sufficiently sampled role directly with a finite-sample guarantee; rarer roles are handled by pooled certification. Across AgentDojo and InjecAgent with six language models, the empirical utility gap tracks this predicted price of coarseness, and our method achieves the most consistent role-specific budget compliance under model and attack transfer, detector noise, gradual drift, unseen tool suites, and adaptive attacks. It provides formal per-role guarantees under exchangeability or after recalibration, and empirical compliance under frozen distribution shift. These results suggest that structured tool calls should be certified at the semantic-role level, not the whole action.

</details>


### [149] [MobiWave: Dispatch-Oriented Graph Wavelets and Drift-Guided Selective Optimization for Autonomous Fleet Rebalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24365)
*Xiao Han,Pinbo Wang,Yuanshao Zhu,Guojiang Shen,Xiangjie Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MobiWave框架，通过多尺度图小波模块和漂移引导的逐层选择性优化（DGLS），解决自动驾驶车队调度中的多尺度交通模式混淆和策略漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车队调度中，重叠的区域和局部交通模式会隐藏有用道路，且策略漂移使训练好的策略不可靠。现有空间聚合方法混淆了这些模式，全参数更新成本高且会破坏稳定知识。

Method: 提出MobiWave框架，包含两个核心模块：1）面向调度的多尺度图小波模块，分离图频率模式并按各尺度对需求预测和可行调度的价值加权；2）漂移引导的逐层选择性优化（DGLS），通过调度加权谱漂移测量、资源预算内选择受影响层、漂移感知的快-慢更新分离短期冲击与持续性变化，并通过候选验证拒绝未改善调度奖励或恶化约束的更新。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟环境上的实验表明，MobiWave相比当前最先进方法具有有效性。

Conclusion: MobiWave通过多尺度图小波表示和选择性层更新，有效解决了自动驾驶车队调度中的多尺度模式混淆和策略漂移问题，在真实数据和模拟环境中均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Autonomous fleets enable mobility platforms to coordinate idle vehicles directly, making fleet-wide rebalancing possible. However, two obstacles limit reliable deployment: overlapping regional and local traffic patterns can hide roads that remain useful for dispatch, and mobility drift can make a trained policy unreliable. Existing spatial aggregation mixes these patterns, while updating all parameters from limited recent data is costly and can damage stable knowledge. We propose \name, a framework that connects a dispatch-oriented multi-scale graph wavelet module with Drift-Guided Layer-Selective Optimization (DGLS). The first module addresses the representation challenge by separating graph-frequency patterns and weighting each scale according to its value for demand prediction and feasible rebalancing. DGLS addresses the adaptation challenge by measuring Dispatch-weighted Spectral Drift, selecting affected layers within a resource budget, and separating short shocks from persistent changes through a drift-aware fast--slow update. Candidate validation further rejects updates that fail to improve held-out dispatch reward without worsening monitored service or safety constraints. Experiments on both real-world datasets and simluated environments demonstrate the effectiveness of \name\ in comparing with state-of-the-art methods. The source code and datasets are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MobiWave-40F8/.

</details>


### [150] [MXAttention: Data-Free Optimal Scaling and Pre-Normalization Quantization for MXFP4 Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24377)
*Jianlin Yu,Jing Lin,Linghui Kong,Aiyue Chen,Weiyi Sun,Chenyu Zeng,Wangli Lan,Jinxi Li,Zhuo Zheng,Ziyang Yue,Danning Ke,Fei Yi,Tianchi Hu,Yuan Ding,Yiwu Yao,Junsong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MXAttention是一种针对MXFP4注意力机制的数据无关后训练量化框架，通过通用最优缩放（UOS）和预归一化量化（PNQ）解决了MXFP4量化中的数值问题，在Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo上实现了接近FP16的生成质量，VBench指标退化小于0.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力二次成本是扩散视频生成模型的主要瓶颈，MXFP4注意力虽能提升推理效率但直接量化会因两个数值问题（2的幂次缩放的裁剪-下溢权衡和softmax循环中的行归一化误差）导致生成质量下降。

Method: 提出MXAttention框架，包含两个组件：1) 通用最优缩放（UOS），利用2的幂次微缩的周期结构导出分布无关的最优缩放边界Qmax=7.25，无需校准或搜索；2) 预归一化量化（PNQ），在行求和之前量化未归一化的softmax指数，通过构造保持归一化。

Result: 在Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，MXAttention弥合了至少95%的OCP MXFP4与FP16之间的VBench成像质量差距，显著提升了帧级相似度，在所有报告的VBench指标上退化小于0.01，同时在与NVFP4基线对比中表现具有竞争力，且融合到注意力pipeline中开销可忽略。

Conclusion: MXAttention是一种高效的数据无关后训练量化方案，有效解决了MXFP4注意力量化中的数值问题，在保持FP16级生成质量的同时实现了高效推理，且已在MindIE-SD中开源。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of attention is a major bottleneck in diffusion-based video generation models. MXFP4 attention provides a promising path toward efficient inference, but direct MXFP4 quantization often degrades generation quality due to two numerical issues: the clipping-underflow trade-off from power-of-two scaling and the row-wise normalization error introduced in the softmax loop. We propose MXAttention, a data-free post-training quantization framework for MXFP4 attention. MXAttention introduces two components: Universal Optimal Scaling (UOS), which exploits the periodic structure of power-of-two microscaling to derive a distribution-independent optimal scaling boundary Qmax=7.25 without calibration or search, and Pre-Normalization Quantization (PNQ), which quantizes unnormalized softmax exponentials before row-wise summation to preserve normalization by construction. Experiments on Wan2.2 and HunyuanVideo show that MXAttention closes at least 95% of the VBench Imaging Quality gap between OCP MXFP4 and FP16, substantially improves frame-level similarity, and preserves FP16-level generation quality with less than 0.01 absolute degradation on all reported VBench metrics. MXAttention also achieves performance competitive with strong NVFP4-based baselines with negligible overhead when fused into the attention pipeline. The implementation is publicly available in MindIE-SD.

</details>


### [151] [K-Survival Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24405)
*Abdallah Alabdallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出K-SurvMeans，一种将K-Means扩展至生存数据聚类的新方法，通过显式利用生存结果优化聚类中心以最大化簇间生存差异，并采用粒子群算法处理非可微优化问题，同时在降维后的潜在空间中运行以提升灵活性和效率。实验表明该方法在生存分布分离度上优于现有深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存聚类方法未能直接在聚类过程中利用生存结果来优化簇间生存差异，缺乏一个能够显式最大化生存分布分离度的聚类框架。

Method: 1) 提出K-SurvMeans，将生存结果纳入K-Means的目标函数，通过最大化簇间生存差异来优化聚类中心；2) 由于目标函数非可微，采用粒子群优化算法进行求解；3) 为缓解维度灾难并提升灵活性，在通过降维得到的低维潜在空间中执行聚类。

Result: 在多个公开生存基准数据集上，K-SurvMeans生成的聚类在生存分布分离度上 consistently 优于现有的基于深度学习的生存聚类方法。

Conclusion: K-SurvMeans通过显式优化生存差异并结合降维策略，能够有效提升生存数据聚类的质量，是一种有前景的生存聚类新范式。

Abstract: In this work, we propose K-SurvMeans, a novel extension of K-Means for clustering survival data. The method explicitly uses the survival outcome in the clustering process to optimize cluster centers, thereby maximizing pairwise survival differences between clusters. The objective function encourages the clusters to be well-separated from the survival perspective. Since the resulting optimization problem is non-differentiable, we employ the Particle Swarm algorithm for the Optimization process.
  To further improve flexibility and mitigate the curse of dimensionality, we extend the framework to operate in a learned low-dimensional latent space obtained via a dimensionality reduction. This allows the method to capture better-separated clusters and enhance optimization efficiency by reducing the search space.
  Experiments on multiple publicly available benchmark survival datasets demonstrate that K-SurvMeans consistently yields clusters with improved separation in survival distributions compared to existing deep learning-based survival clustering methods.

</details>


### [152] [Context Is King: How In-Context Specification Shapes the Geometry of Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24425)
*Elad David,Max Fomin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中的几何结构（如星期、月份在流形上的排列）主要由上下文决定而非固定的先验世界模型。即使是无意义的标记，模型也能按指令构建循环或树状拓扑结构，且这种结构在能力较强的模型中会主导预训练先验。因果激活修补实验证实该几何结构被模型实际用于推理。大规模模型能更清晰地使用该几何结构，而小规模模型则较弱或相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战大语言模型中概念几何结构由固定先验世界模型决定的传统观点，探究上下文（in-context specification）对模型内部几何拓扑结构的控制能力及其与预训练先验的竞争关系。

Method: 通过对比实验，测试模型对标记（如星期、月份、无意义标记）在上下文指令下构建不同拓扑结构（循环、树状）的能力；使用表征相似性分析（RSA）量化与上下文指定结构及先验的匹配度；采用激活修补（activation patching）进行因果检验，验证几何结构是否真正被模型使用。

Result: 上下文指定的几何结构在能力较强的模型中主导预训练先验（表征相似度0.6-0.9 vs 接近零）；激活修补显示该结构被因果使用；这种能力在Gemma（至31B）和Qwen（至27B）的大规模模型中清晰涌现，小规模模型中则较弱或缺失。

Conclusion: 大语言模型所使用的几何结构主要由上下文指定而非固定先验，但该结构能否被清晰使用受模型规模制约——机制在大模型中存在，在小模型中可能完全缺失。

Abstract: Large language models place structured concepts on geometrically faithful manifolds: weekdays lie on a circle, months on another, usually taken to be a fixed world-model the network stores and looks up. We show that context is king: the structure a model actually uses is set by the in-context specification. A declarative rule fixes not only which relations the geometry encodes but its topology type: the same tokens form a cycle or a branching tree on command, built even on arbitrary, meaning-free tokens with no prior to inherit, which a relabeled stored shape cannot do. When the specification conflicts with a strong pretrained prior, the context-set geometry dominates it in capable models, read from the same activations (representational similarity 0.6--0.9 to the imposed structure versus near-zero to the prior), across the priors we test and both families we study (Gemma, Qwen). Activation patching shows the map is causally used, not a probe correlate: swapping one entity's activation for another's makes the model answer with the other entity's successor under the imposed order. A rough map forms readily, present even in small and base models; what scale gates is using it cleanly: clean dominance and the causal crossover emerge only in the larger models (up to Gemma-31B and Qwen-27B) and weaken or reverse below, so a mechanism present in a large model can be absent in a smaller one of the same family. Whether the model builds this geometry anew or reconfigures a stored one we leave open; operationally, the geometry it uses is the one the context specifies.

</details>


### [153] [DraftExpert: Expansion-Aware Self-Speculative Decoding for End-Device MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24434)
*Dengke Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DraftExpert提出了一种面向专家卸载场景的扩展感知型自推测解码框架，通过为每层训练一个轻量级驻留草稿专家，结合置信度-扩展截断和目标专家预取技术，在CPU-GPU和Flash-NPU卸载场景下平均提升解码吞吐量1.45倍，草稿接受率达84-87%，预取命中率达86-88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型MoE模型在端侧部署时，路由专家权重超出加速器内存，需要按需从CPU或Flash加载，导致自推测解码面临新的瓶颈：增大草稿专家集提升准确率但引发额外专家加载，小规模草稿接受率低，且多token验证激活的专家并集远多于单步目标。

Method: 为每层训练一个轻量级驻留草稿专家（通过自蒸馏残差、logit/token和路由器对齐信号）；推理时采用固定占用共享+top-1+草稿专家组合的草稿器，配合置信度-扩展截断和目标专家预取，最终token由目标模型精确验证。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Moonlight-16B-A3B上，CPU-GPU和Flash-NPU卸载场景下，解码吞吐量平均提升1.45倍，草稿接受率84-87%，预取命中率86-88%。

Conclusion: DraftExpert通过扩展感知的草稿设计有效解决了专家卸载场景下自推测解码的加载-准确率权衡问题，兼顾了高草稿接受率和低加载开销。

Abstract: Large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models are attractive for end-device deployment because only a small subset of experts is active per token, but their routed expert weights often exceed accelerator memory. We target latency-critical single-user settings where routed experts are staged on demand from CPU memory to a GPU or from Flash to a mobile NPU. In this setting, self-speculative decoding faces a new bottleneck: increasing the draft expert set improves accuracy but triggers extra expert loading, while cheap small-footprint drafts have low acceptance; moreover, verifying a multi-token block activates the union of target experts and is no longer close to one target step. We propose DraftExpert, an expansion-aware self-speculative decoding framework for expert-offloaded MoE inference. DraftExpert trains one lightweight accelerator-resident draft expert per layer by self-distilling residual, logit/token, and router-agreement signals from the frozen target MoE. At inference time, it uses a fixed-footprint shared+top-1+draft-expert drafter together with confidence--expansion truncation and target-expert prefetching, while final tokens are still exactly verified by the target model. On DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Moonlight-16B-A3B across CPU-GPU and Flash-NPU offload, DraftExpert improves decode throughput by 1.45x on average, raises draft acceptance to 84~87%, and achieves 86~88% prefetch hit rates.

</details>


### [154] [What do Reward Models Memorize?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24484)
*Ivo Verhoeven,Pushkar Mishra,Ekaterina Shutova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究判别式奖励模型在人类偏好数据上的反事实记忆，发现模型存在三种偏差：对简单偏好对的记忆过度分配、记忆数据集特定捷径、以及对未见偏好对过度泛化简单启发式特征（如长度、顺从性）。这表明当前RM尚不具备判断上下文依赖的响应质量的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究判别式奖励模型从人类偏好数据中训练时究竟记住了什么，揭示其潜在的记忆偏差问题。

Method: 在两个人类偏好数据集上测量反事实记忆（counterfactual memorization），分析奖励模型对训练样本的记忆模式。

Result: 发现三种记忆偏差：1) 过度记忆简单、高边际的偏好对；2) 记忆数据集特有的捷径特征（如模型身份、用户采样策略）；3) 对未见偏好对过度泛化简单启发式关联（如响应长度、顺从性）。

Conclusion: 当前基于人类偏好数据的判别式奖励模型训练存在系统性偏差，尚无法在依赖上下文的场景中准确判断响应质量。

Abstract: This paper studies what discriminatively trained reward models (RMs) memorize by measuring counterfactual memorization on two human preference datasets. We show that RMs 1) misallocate memorization to easy, high margin preference pairs, 2) memorize dataset-specific shortcuts (e.g., model identity, user sampling strategy), and 3) overgeneralize simple heuristic correlates of human preference (e.g., length, compliance) when confronted with unseen preference pairs. Overall, our findings indicate that discriminative training of RMs from human preference data results in biased RMs not yet capable of judging response quality in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [155] [UNIFUSION: Adapting Autoregressive Language Models into Discrete Diffusion under a Unified Reverse-Rate Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24507)
*Xiaoyi Jiang,Jingyuan Li,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用的条件损失框架，将SEDD、MDLM/GIDD、M2S和Neural CTMC统一为广义KL散度目标，并建立从干净token预测到多种参数化的转换接口。基于此，提出\ours{}方法，通过连续预训练将预训练GPT2检查点直接适配到均匀噪声扩散模型。在124M和355M参数规模上，\ours{}在生成困惑度与均匀熵的权衡上持续优于现有方法，并在WinoGrande、SIQA和BBH基准上取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要将预训练自回归语言模型适配到掩码扩散，而直接在均匀噪声扩散上适配AR检查点更具挑战性，因为现有扩散语言模型使用不同的目标和预测参数化。作者希望建立统一框架并实现从AR检查点到均匀噪声扩散的直接适配。

Method: 1) 将SEDD、MDLM/GIDD、M2S和Neural CTMC的条件损失统一为广义KL散度目标；2) 推导从干净token预测到concrete-score、posterior-mean和exit-rate/jump参数化的转换，形成共享的x₀接口以支持掩码核与均匀核之间的切换；3) 提出\ours{}连续预训练方法，直接将预训练GPT2检查点适配到均匀噪声扩散。

Result: 在124M和355M参数规模上，随着采样步数从16增加到256，\ours{}持续改善GenPPL与均匀熵的权衡。256步时，\ours{}-S和\ours{}-M分别达到97.783/5.2626和71.516/5.6669的GenPPL/熵对。在两个规模上，\ours{}在WinoGrande、SIQA和BBH基准上的准确率均高于其他对比扩散模型。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架和x₀接口的设计，\ours{}成功将预训练AR检查点适配到均匀噪声扩散，在生成质量和语义理解基准上均取得优异表现，证明了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Existing methods mainly adapt pretrained autoregressive (AR) language models to masked diffusion, whereas we directly adapt them to uniform-noise diffusion, where every token remains editable during sampling. However, adapting AR checkpoints across corruption kernels remains challenging because existing DLMs use different objectives and prediction parameterizations. We establish connections among SEDD, MDLM/GIDD, M2S, and Neural CTMC by expressing their conditional losses as a single generalized Kullback--Leibler objective over model reverse rates. We further derive conversions from clean-token predictions to concrete-score, posterior-mean, and exit-rate/jump parameterizations, yielding a shared \(x_0\) interface that supports switching between mask and uniform kernels. Building on these connections, we propose \ours{}, a simple continual pre-training approach for directly adapting pretrained GPT2 checkpoints to uniform-noise diffusion. Through systematic evaluation of 124M- and 355M-parameter models, we show that \ours{} steadily improves the trade-off between generative perplexity (GenPPL) and unigram entropy as the sampling budget increases from 16 to 256 steps. At 256 steps, \ours{}-S and \ours{}-M achieve GenPPL/entropy pairs of \(97.783/5.2626\) and \(71.516/5.6669\), respectively; no evaluated model at the same scale simultaneously outperforms \ours{} on both metrics. At both scales, \ours{} also achieves the highest WinoGrande, SIQA, and BBH accuracy among the compared diffusion models.

</details>


### [156] [Physics Transformer: Tailoring Transformer for General PDE Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24513)
*Guoze Sun,Rui Zhang,Jiankai Tang,Mengtao Yan,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Physics Transformer，一种基于函数投影的Transformer架构，将物理场视为连续函数，通过自适应局部基函数投影获得紧凑物理表征，结合分解注意力机制实现高效全局交互，在二维PDE到三维工业CFD模拟中取得最优预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构处理PDE时面临核心挑战：物理场是无限维连续函数的有限采样，不同于离散的语言token或固定分辨率图像块。因此需要一个能尊重物理场函数本质、从任意离散化中构建物理表达力token的标记化方法。

Method: 1) 将物理场视为连续函数，离散化划分为保持局部性的空间块；2) 每个块内动态学习一组自适应局部基函数，将采样场投影到基上获得紧凑物理token；3) token捕获多样潜在物理状态并保留细粒度空间结构；4) 通过空间与物理状态的分解注意力实现高效全局交互；5) 投影表示支持任意查询位置的高效解码。

Result: 在二维PDE动力学到工业级三维CFD模拟的多个基准测试中，Physics Transformer精准捕捉细粒度物理结构，取得最先进的预测性能。

Conclusion: 函数投影作为设计PDE求解Transformer架构的实用且有效的基石。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have attracted increasing attention for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), owing to their flexibility in handling irregular discretizations and their ability to capture long-range physical dependencies. However, unlike discrete language tokens or fixed-resolution image patches, observed physical fields are finite samples of underlying infinite-dimensional functions. Consequently, effectively applying Transformers to PDEs requires a tokenizer that respects the functional nature of physical fields and constructs physically expressive tokens from arbitrary discretizations.To this end, we propose \methodname{Physics Transformer}, a function-projection-based Transformer architecture for physical field prediction. Physics Transformer treats a physical field as a continuous function and partitions its discretization into locality-preserving spatial patches. Within each patch, it dynamically learns a set of adaptive local basis functions and projects the sampled field onto these bases to obtain compact physics tokens. The resulting tokens capture diverse latent physical states while preserving fine-scale spatial structures, enabling efficient global interaction through factorized attention across space and physical states. The projected representation further supports efficient decoding at arbitrary query locations. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks, ranging from two-dimensional PDE dynamics to industrial-scale three-dimensional CFD simulations, demonstrate that Physics Transformer accurately captures fine-grained physical structures and achieves state-of-the-art predictive performance. These results establish function projection as a practical and effective foundation for designing Transformer architectures for PDE solving.

</details>


### [157] [Stress-Testing EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Decoding: Dataset Identity and Targeted Negative Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24519)
*Marzieh Zare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种预训练EEG基础模型在临床任务中的表现，发现其性能强烈依赖于评估单位、数据集偏移、比较器强度和针对性控制措施。冻结REVE模型在韩国痴呆症任务上AUROC仅0.568（不及经典特征的0.769），数据集身份可被完美解码（AUROC 1.000），而随机初始化编码器在某些任务上甚至优于预训练模型（0.659 vs 0.570）。唯一明确的积极结果是CHB-MIT癫痫发作检测（AUROC 0.793，比随机初始化高9.2个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预训练EEG基础模型被越来越多地用于临床解码，但其在不同人群间的迁移能力和对阴性对照的鲁棒性尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏系统性的跨任务、跨数据集基准测试，特别是缺乏对随机初始化、特征置换等严格对照实验的评估。

Method: 对六种EEG基础模型（LaBraM、EEGMamba、CBraMod、REVE、BENDR、BIOT）在四个数据集的五项临床任务上进行基准测试，采用冻结线性探针和留一受试者、受试者分组或明确记录的记录级别划分。对REVE的发现进行随机初始化、随机特征、标签置换、乱序标签微调和投影敏感性等对照测试。

Result: 1) 韩国痴呆症任务：冻结REVE AUROC 0.568，经典特征0.769；2) 数据集身份可轻易从冻结嵌入中解码（AUROC 1.000）；3) 随机初始化编码器在韩国痴呆症任务上优于预训练REVE（0.659 vs 0.570）；4) 阿尔茨海默病任务中经典特征优于REVE；5) CHB-MIT癫痫检测中REVE AUROC 0.793，比随机初始化高9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的结论高度依赖于评估单位、数据集偏移、比较器强度和针对性控制措施。现有模型在多项临床任务中未能超越经典特征或随机基线，其实际临床效用需要更严格的基准测试和对照实验验证。

Abstract: Pretrained EEG foundation models are increasingly proposed for clinical decoding, but their transfer across populations and robustness to negative controls remain unclear. We benchmark six models (LaBraM, EEGMamba, CBraMod, REVE, BENDR, and BIOT) on five clinical tasks across four datasets using frozen linear probes with leave-one-subject-out, subject-grouped, or explicitly identified recording-level splits. Selected REVE findings are tested against random initialisation, random features, label permutation, scrambled-label fine-tuning, and projection sensitivity. On Korean dementia (CAUEEG, three-way), frozen REVE reaches 0.568 AUROC versus 0.769 for classical features; the ordering persists on a patient-disjoint held-out split (0.565 versus 0.768). Dataset identity is readily decoded from frozen embeddings (AUROC 1.000 at PCA-50; 0.9998 after band restriction and per-epoch z-scoring), whereas the same PCA-50 pipeline decodes Korean diagnosis at 0.528. A randomly initialised encoder also outperforms pretrained REVE on this task (0.659 versus 0.570). On Alzheimer's disease, Gaussian random projection and PCA of the same pretrained embeddings perform similarly, and classical features nominally exceed REVE at the subject level. The clearest controlled positive is cross-subject ictal detection on CHB-MIT (n=23), where REVE achieves 0.793 AUROC, 9.2 percentage points above a randomly initialised encoder. These results show that EEG foundation-model conclusions depend strongly on evaluation unit, dataset shift, comparator strength, and targeted controls.

</details>


### [158] [FlowCTS: On-policy Continuous Trajectory Supervision of Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24522)
*Kaiyang Ye,Yuan Ge,Junxiang Zhang,Bei Li,Ziming Zhu,Haishu Zhao,Xiaoqian Liu,Chenglong Wang,Jingbo Zhu,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowCTS方法，通过连续轨迹监督匹配学生与参考流模型的轨迹，解决了流模型上策略蒸馏中稀疏奖励和暴露偏差问题，在图像生成任务上显著优于KL-based OPD和混合奖励RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管策略蒸馏(OPD)在解决大语言模型后训练的稀疏奖励和暴露偏差问题上有效，但其在流模型上的扩展尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Flow Continuous Trajectory Supervision (FlowCTS)，通过从同一学生访问状态初始化后续学生和参考轨迹，利用轨迹与速度场之间的积分关系推导出时间加权速度匹配上界，并离散化为以监督步数为参数的实用目标，采用多参考设置实现单状态策略蒸馏。

Result: FlowCTS-OPD在图像生成任务上将GenEval从0.90提升至0.93，OCR从0.90提升至0.92，PickScore从22.75提升至23.06，且在各项指标上均优于混合奖励RL基线。进一步分析揭示了KL-based OPD中辅助SDE转移核导致的时序监督不匹配问题。

Conclusion: FlowCTS在策略蒸馏和标准SFT设置下均优于基线方法，增加监督步数在更丰富的轨迹信息与更大的优化难度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: While on-policy distillation (OPD) effectively addresses sparse rewards and exposure bias in large language model post-training, its extension to flow models remains underexplored. To this end, we propose Flow Continuous Trajectory Supervision (FlowCTS), which matches subsequent student and reference trajectories initialized from the same student-visited state. Using the integral relation between trajectories and velocity fields, we derive a temporally weighted velocity-matching upper bound and discretize it into practical objectives parameterized by the number of supervision steps. Under a multi-reference setup, single-state FlowCTS-OPD outperforms vanilla KL-based OPD with faster convergence. FlowCTS-OPD improves GenEval from 0.90 to 0.93, OCR from 0.90 to 0.92, and PickScore from 22.75 to 23.06, while outperforming a mixed-reward RL baseline across all target metrics. Further analysis reveals a clear temporal supervision mismatch in vanilla KL-based OPD arising from its auxiliary SDE transition kernels. Beyond on-policy setting,FlowCTS also consistently outperforms vanilla SFT , particularly on OCR, while increasing supervision steps exhibit a trade-off between richer trajectory information and greater optimization difficulty.

</details>


### [159] [From Machine Learning to Large-Scale EO Products: Best Practices for Making Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24532)
*Ghjulia Sialelli,Robin Young,Yuchang Jiang,Cesar Aybar,Linus Scheibenreif,Damien Robert,Clemens Mosig,Adam J. Stewart,Jan D. Wegner,Aleksis Pirinen,Olof Mogren,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了从地球观测卫星数据到可操作地图产品的全流程最佳实践，涵盖数据基础设施、预处理、机器学习数据集构建与模型训练、不确定性量化、地图生产与分发以及验证六大主题，强调了各环节间的紧密耦合关系及常见陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习和大规模计算基础设施的进步，基于地球观测数据生成大尺度地理空间地图的产能迅速增长，但成熟的行业最佳实践尚未建立，早期设计决策可能悄无声息地向最终产品传播错误。生产技术可靠且科学可信的产品仍具挑战性，各环节决策高度耦合。

Method: 本文以端到端的视角，围绕EO数据基础设施、数据选择与预处理、ML数据集构建与模型训练、不确定性量化、地图生产与分发、验证六个相互关联的主题，提供贯穿全流程的推荐实践。这是完整指南的浓缩版本。

Result: 本文梳理了各阶段的关键挑战与推荐实践，包括：预处理决策影响训练信号、数据集设计决定模型可学习内容及性能评估可靠性、全球尺度推理带来工程挑战与伪影问题，以及不确定性量化和独立地图验证需要专门的方法论关注。

Conclusion: 生产科学可信的地球观测地图产品需要各环节的精心设计与协调，每个阶段的决策都相互影响。本文提供了端到端的实践指导，完整指南可在指定网址获取。

Abstract: Recent years have seen a rapid expansion in the production of large-scale geospatial maps derived from Earth observation (EO) data, driven largely by advances in machine learning (ML) and large computing infrastructure. Although the barrier to generating such maps has dropped substantially, established best practices have yet to emerge, and design decisions made early in the pipeline can quietly propagate errors into the final product. Producing a technically sound and scientifically credible product remains challenging. Choices made at every stage are tightly coupled: preprocessing decisions shape the training signal, dataset design governs what the model can learn and how reliably its performance can be assessed, and global-scale inference introduces engineering challenges in compute and data access at scale, as well as artifact mitigation. Furthermore, uncertainty quantification and independent map validation each require dedicated methodological attention that is often underestimated. This paper presents a concise, end-to-end account of the recommended practices spanning the pipeline from satellite data to an operational map product. We organize the discussion around six interconnected themes: the EO data infrastructure landscape, data selection and preprocessing, ML dataset construction and model training, uncertainty quantification, map production and distribution, and validation. This paper is a condensed version of a longer guide that provides greater depth across all stages, accessible online at ghjuliasialelli.github.io/MLEO-Maps/.

</details>


### [160] [The K-SCAN Clustering Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24537)
*Filip Kosiorowski,Grzegorz Sroka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-SCAN是一种混合聚类算法，通过Mini-Batch K-Means向量量化与密度分析相结合，在保持线性计算复杂度的同时实现高噪声鲁棒性和非线性流形识别，速度比BIRCH快3倍以上，但对过平滑和密度异质性敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统密度聚类算法O(N²)时间复杂度与划分聚类算法（如K-Means）强制球形簇及对异常值敏感之间的权衡问题，在大数据时代实现可扩展且鲁棒的聚类。

Method: 两阶段混合方法：先使用随机Mini-Batch K-Means进行向量量化，提取一组带权重的微簇；随后对微簇进行基于密度的结构分析。

Result: 在百万级数据集上验证了线性时间复杂度；相比BIRCH实现3倍以上加速；在噪声高达55%时仍保持ARI > 0.99；能精确识别非线性流形。

Conclusion: K-SCAN有效平衡了可扩展性与聚类质量，但存在对过平滑和局部密度高度异质性敏感的限制，在复杂视觉空间中可能丢失最精细的拓扑细节。

Abstract: In the Big Data era, the scalability of clustering algorithms constitutes a key challenge. Traditional density-based methods (e.g., DBSCAN) offer robustness to noise and the ability to detect non-linear clusters, yet their quadratic time complexity $O(N^2)$ drastically limits their applicability. Conversely, partitional algorithms (e.g., K-Means), with their linear complexity $O(N)$, impose sphericity on the resulting groups and fail in the presence of outliers. This paper presents K-SCAN -- a novel hybrid algorithm that optimizes this trade-off. The method integrates preliminary vector quantization (stochastic Mini-Batch K-Means) to extract a reduced set of weighted micro-clusters, followed by a subsequent density-based structural analysis. Empirical evaluation on datasets of up to $10^6$ samples confirms the linear computational complexity of the proposed solution. K-SCAN achieves more than a 3-fold speed-up over the hierarchical BIRCH algorithm, avoiding the costly management of tree-based structures. The method precisely identifies non-linear manifolds while maintaining structural stability (Adjusted Rand Index > 0.99), even with noise levels reaching 55\% of the data volume. The main limitation of the proposed algorithm, which could not be fully eliminated in the present study, remains its susceptibility to over-smoothing and its difficulty in separating clusters with highly heterogeneous local density. In complex visual spaces, this can lead to the loss of the finest topological details.

</details>


### [161] [EchoBridge: Long-Tail-Aware ECG-Echocardiography Text Alignment for Echocardiography-Derived Cardiac Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24553)
*Xiaocheng Fang,Jieyi Cai,Guangkun Nie,Haoyu Wang,Jiarui Jin,Yujie Xiao,Bo Liu,Chenyang He,Qinghao Zhao,Gaofeng Cheng,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoBridge通过互补共享-私有投影（CSPP）和自适应原型边界校准（APBC）实现心电图表示与超声心动图发现的对齐，在多个评估协议下显著提升了分类性能，尤其对低患病率瓣膜病变有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准化的超声心动图结论可为学习心电图表示提供监督信号，但全局ECG-文本对齐会纠缠模态特定因素，且长尾分布导致低患病率疾病的监督信号稀疏。

Method: 提出EchoBridge方法，包含CSPP（将各模态映射为共享投影和辅助私有投影，通过模态内正交性减少方向冗余，双向对齐归一化共享投影）和APBC（用类别原型组织共享超球面，引入训练频率自适应的角度边界和球形Riesz排斥）。

Result: 在EchoNext-Mini及两个独立数据集上，EchoBridge在无分类器的AUROC、AUPRC和F1上分别提升7.88、5.61和4.54个点，在所有域内和跨中心评估中均取得最高点估计，对多数疾病（包括低患病率瓣膜发现）均有提升。

Conclusion: EchoBridge通过解耦模态共享与私有信息并自适应校准分类边界，有效改善了低患病率条件下的心电图-超声心动图对齐性能。

Abstract: Standardized echocardiography conclusions provide meaningful supervision for learning ECG representations of echocardiography-derived cardiac findings. Global ECG--text alignment may entangle modality-specific factors, while long-tailed finding distributions provide sparse positive supervision for low-prevalence conditions. We propose EchoBridge with Complementary Shared--Private Projection (CSPP) and Adaptive Prototype Boundary Calibration (APBC). CSPP maps each modality into shared and auxiliary private projections, reduces directional redundancy via within-modality orthogonality, and bidirectionally aligns normalized shared projections. APBC organizes the shared hypersphere with class-specific prototypes, training-frequency-adaptive angular margins, and spherical Riesz repulsion. We evaluate EchoBridge on EchoNext-Mini and independent PKUPH and SHTMU cohorts under four protocols: prompt-based inference without downstream classifier training, in-domain frozen linear probing, target-domain cross-center frozen linear probing, and source-only cross-center transfer, supplemented by finding-specific analyses. EchoBridge improves classifier-free AUROC, AUPRC, and F1 over the strongest baselines by 7.88, 5.61, and 4.54 points, respectively, and achieves the highest point estimates across all in-domain and target-domain probing budgets and both source-only transfer cohorts. Finding-specific analyses show gains for most conditions, including several low-prevalence valvular findings.

</details>


### [162] [LOCKS: Page-Local Compact Key Summaries for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24555)
*Junsung Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOCKS是一种为每个KV缓存页分配独立谱摘要（约缓存大小的1/10）的方法，通过仅基于摘要选择top页面进行注意力计算，在不读取完整候选键值的情况下，以2%的token参与度匹配全KV质量，实现2倍解码延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型长上下文推理时，KV缓存成为瓶颈，每个解码步骤需读取全部缓存。现有方法（如低秩近似）忽视页面特有方向信息，导致精度损失。

Method: 每个KV页面维护自己的谱摘要（约缓存大小的1/10），利用该摘要重建页面内logits，通过log-sum-exp估计每个页面的注意力质量，仅选择top页面进行注意力计算。选择过程本身不读取任何候选键或值。

Result: 在LongBench-v1上接近全缓存性能；在RULER检索密集任务中追踪全键读取的oracle；在AIME26、MATH-500长程推理任务上优势最大（基线选择器失效）；2048 token预算下匹配100K+上下文的全KV质量，仅关注约2%的token；1M token时解码延迟减半（2.0倍加速）。

Conclusion: LOCKS可作为vLLM的即插即用插件，在CUDA图模式下运行批处理解码，有效突破长上下文KV缓存瓶颈。

Abstract: Serving large language models at long context is bottlenecked by the key-value (KV) cache, which is read in full at every decode step. Attention keys are locally low-rank though globally high-rank: shared low-rank bases discard page-specific directions that a page's own compact basis retains. LOCKS gives every page its own spectral summary (resident, about a tenth the cache's size), reconstructs within-page logits, estimates each page's attention mass by log-sum-exp, and attends only the top pages; selection itself reads no candidate keys or values. Selecting on this summary alone stays within about a point of the full cache on long-document QA (LongBench-v1), tracks the read-every-key oracle on retrieval-dense RULER down to the smallest budgets, and shows its largest margins on long-form reasoning (AIME26, MATH-500), where baseline selectors collapse. At its shipped $2048$-token budget LOCKS matches FullKV aggregate quality at $100$K$+$ context while attending about $2\%$ of the tokens, and halves per-token decode latency ($2.0\times$ at $1$M tokens) against dense attention. LOCKS ships as a drop-in plugin for unmodified vLLM, with batched decode running in full CUDA graphs.

</details>


### [163] [BettiSplit: Topology-Guided Privacy-Aware Split Learning Against Feature Inversion and Gradient Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24556)
*Akarsh K. Nair,Muhammad Arifur Rahman,David Brown,Mufti Mahmud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于持续贝蒂复杂度的拓扑引导框架，用于隐私感知的拆分学习。通过分析各层激活的拓扑特征，发现隐私风险在层间高度非均匀分布，并存在尖锐的转变区域。基于此提出BettiSafe策略，无需显式攻击即可识别隐私敏感层，相比深度启发式方法将特征重构难度提升2-5倍；同时Betti正则化在不降低模型效用的前提下，将反演难度提升近5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拆分学习中不恰当的层分割点会导致中间表示泄露严重隐私，现有方法未能有效捕捉各层隐私风险的非均匀分布和突变区域。

Method: 通过持续贝蒂复杂度分析各层激活的拓扑特征，提出BettiSafe拓扑引导分割选择策略和Betti正则化方法，无需显式攻击即可识别隐私敏感层。

Result: BettiSafe将特征反演抵抗能力提升2-5倍；Betti正则化将反演难度提升近5倍，且不降低分类准确率。

Conclusion: 拓扑复杂度可作为拆分学习中隐私保护的有效结构描述符，实现安全、自适应的表示感知型拆分学习。

Abstract: Split learning enables collaborative model training by partitioning neural networks across clients and servers. However, improper split placement can lead to severe privacy leakage through intermediate representations. In this work, we propose a topology-guided framework for privacy-aware split learning based on the persistent Betti complexity of smashed activations. Through comprehensive layer-wise analysis, we show that privacy risk in split learning is highly non-uniform across layers and exhibits sharp transition regions that are not captured by architectural depth alone. In particular, feature inversion fidelity increases from negligible reconstruction to as high as 0.98 SSIM at deeper, privacy-critical split points. We further demonstrate that Betti complexity consistently identifies representation regimes associated with elevated feature-space privacy leakage across architectures and datasets. Leveraging this observation, we introduce BettiSafe, a topology-guided split selection strategy that identifies privacy-sensitive layers without requiring explicit attack execution. BettiSafe improves resistance to feature inversion by 2 to 5 times compared to depth-based heuristics while preserving classification accuracy. In addition, Betti-based regularisation increases inversion difficulty by nearly 5 x without degrading model utility, enabling a favourable privacy utility tradeoff. Overall, our results highlight topological complexity as a promising structural descriptor for secure, adaptive, and representation-aware split learning in real-world collaborative systems

</details>


### [164] [Bit-Accurate FPGA Evaluation of Learned Feature Gating in a Fixed-Point Fourier-Feature Automatic Modulation Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24568)
*Gawthaman Senthilvelan,Luthira Abeykoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在FPGA上实现学习型特征重加权（门控机制）对自动调制分类（AMC）的性能与资源开销之间的权衡。实验表明，门控机制虽然增加了硬件资源消耗（平均增加1318个ALM、1557个寄存器、4个DSP块和3140个处理周期），但并未提升分类精度（门控模型准确率反而略低于非门控模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型特征重加权在软件层面能改善AMC性能，但将其部署到FPGA上会引入额外的算术运算和延迟。该工作旨在量化这种性能与硬件开销之间的权衡。

Method: 使用24个稀疏DFT能量特征、8个相位/统计特征及32-128-11多层感知机构建紧凑型定点分类器；第二架构在分类器前插入一个32元素、8位、输入相关的门控模块。采用后训练量化和量化感知训练两种方法，以两个匹配的随机种子训练门控与非门控模型，共生成8个检查点，各自独立编译到Intel Cyclone V FPGA上，在352,000次物理板级分类上进行评估。

Result: 在所有四组匹配的门控对比中，非门控模型均实现了更高的测试准确率，门控与非门控在PTQ和QAT下的平均准确率差异分别为-0.784和-0.616个百分点。QAT的效果在两个训练种子之间方向发生改变。硬件上，门控平均增加1318个ALM、1557个寄存器、4个DSP块和3140个处理周期。所有352,000次板级预测与独立整数参考完全一致，一个种子的3,760个捕获中间值也完全匹配。

Conclusion: 对于该特征表示和实现方案，学习型门控在FPGA上增加了硬件成本，但并未提升分类精度。

Abstract: Learned feature reweighting can improve automatic modulation classification (AMC) in software, but the same operation introduces additional arithmetic and latency when implemented on an FPGA. This work measures that trade-off in a compact fixed-point classifier using 24 sparse DFT-energy features, 8 phase/statistical features, and a 32-to-128-to-11 multilayer perceptron. A second architecture inserts a learned 32-element, 8-bit, input-dependent gate before the classifier. Gated and ungated models are trained using post-training quantization (PTQ) and quantization-aware training (QAT) with two matched training seeds. The resulting eight checkpoints are compiled independently for an Intel Cyclone V FPGA and evaluated over 352,000 physical-board classifications. Ungated models achieve higher test accuracy in all four matched gate comparisons, with mean gated-minus-ungated differences of -0.784 percentage points under PTQ and -0.616 percentage points under QAT. The effect of QAT changes direction between the two training seeds. In hardware, the gate adds an average of 1,318 adaptive logic modules (ALMs), 1,557 registers, 4 DSP blocks, and 3,140 processing cycles. All 352,000 board predictions agree exactly with an independent integer reference, and 3,760 captured intermediate values from one training seed also match. For this feature representation and implementation, learned gating increases FPGA cost without improving classification accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [Evaluating Fuzz Testing for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24577)
*Zhibin Kang,Hanmo You,Dong Wang,Haiming Zheng,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对强化学习（RL）智能体的模糊测试方法进行了全面的实证研究，从有效性、多样性、效率及实用价值四个维度系统评估了五种最先进的模糊测试方法，揭示了不同方法的优劣，并提出了结合互补策略与多层次多样性分析的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量RL模糊测试方法被提出，但现有研究在评估设置、基准方法和评价指标上存在显著差异，导致难以得出关于这些方法相对有效性和实用性的可靠结论。为填补这一空白，亟需进行统一的、多维度对比的实证研究。

Method: 在三个复杂度递增的环境（MountainCar、BipedalWalker、CARLA）中，将五种最先进的RL模糊测试方法与随机测试进行对比，从有效性、多样性、效率、实用价值四个维度进行系统评估，并进一步分析检测到的崩溃对智能体鲁棒性提升和安全监控的下游价值。

Result: 吞吐量优先的方法（如MDPFuzz）在崩溃发现的有效性和效率上表现最优；而明确鼓励探索的方法（如SeqDivFuzz）在发现多样化的崩溃行为方面更为突出。模糊测试生成的崩溃数据能有效提升智能体鲁棒性，并支持跨方法泛化的准确安全监控。

Conclusion: 建议结合互补的模糊测试策略，并采用多层次多样性分析，以实现更全面和实用的RL系统测试。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains such as robotics, autonomous driving, and drone control, where unexpected behaviors may lead to severe real-world consequences. Fuzz testing has recently emerged as a promising method for exploring the vast state spaces of RL agents and exposing crashes. Although numerous RL fuzzing methods have been proposed, existing studies often differ in evaluation settings, baselines, and metrics, making it difficult to draw reliable conclusions about their relative effectiveness and practical usefulness. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive empirical study that systematically evaluates RL fuzzing methods from four complementary perspectives: effectiveness, diversity, efficiency, and practical utility. We benchmark five state-of-the-art methods alongside random testing under unified configurations across three environments of increasing complexity (MountainCar, BipedalWalker, and CARLA), and further assess the downstream usefulness of detected crashes for agent robustness improvement and safety monitoring. Our results reveal several key insights. For instance,throughput-oriented methods like MDPFuzz demonstrate superior effectiveness and efficiency in crash discovery, while methods explicitly designed to encourage exploration like SeqDivFuzz excel at uncovering diverse crash behaviors. We also show that fuzzing-generated crashes can meaningfully improve agent robustness and enable accurate safety monitoring with strong cross-method generalization. Beyond these empirical findings, we distill actionable guidance for both researchers and practitioners, highlighting the benefits of combining complementary fuzzing strategies and adopting multi-level diversity analysis to achieve more comprehensive and practical RL testing.

</details>


### [166] [PYPM-GGD: Pitman-Yor Process Mixture with Generalized Gaussian Density using ADAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24583)
*Kart-Leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于常数步长随机梯度上升的非共轭变分后验大规模贝叶斯非参数学习算法，无需闭式期望表达式，仅要求变分后可微，并引入自适应步长提升收敛性能，在MIT67和SUN397等大数据集上兼容ResNet特征，在聚类任务上达到或超越当前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机变分推断（SVI）依赖共轭变分后验假设，无法处理非共轭后验的大规模学习问题；现有基于蒙特卡洛的方法计算复杂度高且多用于逻辑回归等简单模型，难以扩展到贝叶斯非参数（BNP）任务。

Method: 基于常数步长随机梯度上升（constant stepsize stochastic gradient ascent），无需变分后验期望的闭式表达式，仅要求变分后可微；借鉴SVI和Adam的思想，引入自适应步长机制以提升随机环境下的收敛性。

Result: 该方法兼容ResNet特征，在大类数数据集MIT67和SUN397上有效；与深度聚类算法等近期工作相比，在聚类指标上达到或超越了最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种不依赖共轭假设、可微即可的大规模BNP学习方法，通过自适应步长改善收敛，实验证明其在复杂数据集和聚类任务上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Large scale Bayesian nonparametrics (BNP) learner such as Stochastic Variational Inference (SVI) can handle datasets with large class number and large training size at fractional cost. Like its predecessor, SVI rely on the assumption of conjugate variational posterior to approximate the true posterior. A more challenging problem is to consider large scale learning on non-conjugate posterior. Recent works in this direction are mostly associated with using Monte Carlo methods for approximating the learner. However, these works are usually demonstrated on non-BNP related task and less complex models such as logistic regression, due to higher computational complexity. In order to overcome the issue faced by SVI, we develop a novel approach based on the recently proposed constant stepsize stochastic gradient ascent to allow large scale learning on non-conjugate posterior. Unlike SVI, our new learner does not require closed- form expression for the variational posterior expectatations. Our only requirement is that the variational posterior is differentiable. In order to ensure convergence in stochastic settings, SVI rely on decaying step-sizes to slow its learning. Inspired by SVI and Adam, we propose the novel use of adaptive stepsizes in our method to significantly improve its learning. We show that our proposed methods is compatible with ResNet features when applied to large class number datasets such as MIT67 and SUN397. Finally, we compare our proposed learner with several recent works such as deep clustering algorithms and showed we were able to produce on-par or outperform the state-of-the-art methods in terms of clustering measures.

</details>


### [167] [Attribution and Uncertainty Behavior of Learned Residual Gyro Correction for Gyro-Stellar Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24608)
*Mariela De Lucas Álvarez,Melvin Laux,Arthur de Freitas Precht,Maurice Martin,Edoardo Caroselli,Frank Kirchner,Alexander Fabisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于深度学习的陀螺仪偏置校正框架中的不确定性分解与可解释性。1D-CNN网络预测残差角速率修正，输出均值与异方差偶然不确定性，通过集成模型估计认知不确定性。梯度归因方法揭示修正与不确定性的驱动证据。结果表明偶然不确定性随扰动强度增加但分布重叠，认知不确定性对分布偏移更敏感，能更好区分标称与扰动工况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于学习的姿态估计系统中，不确定性量化与可解释性对于飞行安全关键应用至关重要。现有工作较少同时分析偶然不确定性与认知不确定性的协作行为，以及结构化扰动对它们的影响，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用1D-CNN从多传感器输入（陀螺仪和星敏感器）预测残差角速率修正，同时输出异方差偶然不确定性；通过独立训练模型的集成估计认知不确定性。利用梯度归因方法对修正和不确定性输出进行归因分析，并在旋转轴和工况间聚合归因模式。

Result: 偶然不确定性随扰动强度增加而增大，但分布存在重叠且标定不一致；认知不确定性在分布偏移发生时信号更清晰，模型分歧增大。偶然与认知不确定性表现出良好的协作关系，认知不确定性更擅长区分标称与扰动工况。

Conclusion: 偶然不确定性和认知不确定性能够有效协作，认知不确定性更适合作为下游监控和故障检测的信号。研究为混合学习型状态估计组件的行为提供了深入洞察，并推动了不确定性在安全关键系统中的实用化。

Abstract: This work investigates uncertainty decomposition and explainability in a deep learning-based framework for gyroscope bias correction. A 1-D Convolutional Neural Network is trained to predict residual angular rate corrections from multi-sensor inputs, including gyroscope and star tracker measurements. The bias corrections are sent to a flight-representative Gyro-Stellar Estimator. The network produces both mean corrections and input-dependent (heteroscedastic) aleatoric uncertainty, while epistemic uncertainty is estimated via an ensemble of independently trained models.
  The proposed approach is trained under nominal conditions and evaluated in both nominal and structured perturbations that include additive and temporally correlated noise. Gradient-based attribution methods are applied to both the correction and uncertainty outputs, enabling a decomposition of the evidence that drives state updates and uncertainty estimates. By aggregating attribution patterns across rotational axes and regimes, we reveal axis-specific behaviors and characterize how structured perturbations influence the collaboration between aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty.
  Uncertainty analysis shows that aleatoric uncertainty increases with perturbation intensity, but the distributions overlap and the calibration is not consistent across regimes. On the other hand, epistemic uncertainty gives a clear signal that gets clearer as the distributional shift happens, showing that the models disagree more. These results show that aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty work well together and that epistemic uncertainty is better at distinguishing between nominal and perturbed operating conditions. The results provide insight into the behavior of hybrid learning-based state estimation components and motivate the use of uncertainty for downstream monitoring and fault detection.

</details>


### [168] [Sparse Autoencoders Encode Both Concepts and Functions: The Downstream Geometry of Feature Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24645)
*Phu Gia Hoang,Anwoy Chatterjee,Tanmoy Chakraborty,Iryna Gurevych,Subhabrata Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征效应几何分析（FEGA）框架，研究SAE特征干预对模型logits变化的影响几何。发现很少有特征表现为可复用的单一方向，并将特征区分为与静态信息相关的"值类特征"和与上下文相关操作关联的"指针类特征"，前者更易呈现结构化低维效应，后者效应分散。结论：特征可解释且因果相关，但未必提供稳定的操控方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为可解释性工具的大规模应用受到限制，原因在于SAE特征与模型行为之间的关联不一致：特征激活描述清晰但因果效应弱或与预期相悖；基于激活的特征选择可能遗漏能产生所需输出变化的特征。现有研究关注特征在模型内部的计算几何，本文转而研究特征干预所导致的logits变化几何。

Method: 提出特征效应几何分析（FEGA）框架，在不同上下文中移除同一激活的SAE特征，分析由此产生的logits变化云团的几何结构。通过无监督方式区分"值类特征"（与事实属性等静态信息绑定）和"指针类特征"（与上下文相关操作关联）。

Result: 不同SAE变体下，一致的一维效应罕见，即很少有特征表现得像可复用的方向。值类特征更常呈现结构化、低维效应（但通常跨越多个方向），而指针类特征主要表现为弥散效应。

Conclusion: 特征的稳定操控方向并非其因果相关性的必要条件；一个特征可以是可解释且因果相关的，但未必为模型操控提供稳定的方向。这揭示了SAE特征因果效应几何的复杂性，对可解释性工具的实用设计有重要启示。

Abstract: The wide-scale use of sparse autoencoders (SAEs) as interpretability tools is limited by inconsistent links between SAE features and model behavior. Features with clear activation descriptions may have weak or unexpected causal effects; steering can vary across prompts or oppose the intended direction; and activation-based feature selection can miss features that produce the desired output change. Prior work has studied feature geometry inside the model, where features are computed. We instead study the geometry of changes in model logits caused by feature interventions. We introduce Feature-Effect Geometry Analysis (FEGA), an unsupervised framework that removes the same active SAE feature across contexts and analyzes the resulting cloud of logit changes. Across SAE variants, consistent one-dimensional effects are rare: few features behave like reusable directions. To interpret this variation, we distinguish value-like features, tied to static information such as factual attributes, from pointer-like features, associated with context-dependent operations. Value-like features more often exhibit structured, low-dimensional effects, although these effects typically span several directions. Pointer-like features, by contrast, predominantly exhibit diffuse effects. Our results show that a feature can be interpretable and causally relevant without providing a stable direction for steering.

</details>


### [169] [When Can You Correct Distribution Drift in Temporal Graph Generation? A Sharpening--Drift Tension and an Impossibility for Observation-Based Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24662)
*Tianpeng Li,Xuan Guo,Wenjun Wang,Wang Zhang,Pengfei Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了时序图生成模型在训练集与部署集之间存在分布漂移时的性能退化问题。理论上证明了退化源于不可约熵与训练-部署概率比散度，经验上表现为幂律关系（指数-0.605）。漂移导致采样器错误地板上升2.2-34.3倍，且无法通过观测数据修正——任何基于历史观测的修正器至少保留被跟踪统计量的条件方差，趋势外推仅在μ²>v(1-2ρ)时有效，而实际漂移无趋势且均值回归，最佳观测修正仅能恢复5.7%的可消除误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图生成模型在从训练时段部署到下一个时段时性能严重退化，作者试图从理论根源解释这一退化现象，并探索是否可以通过观测数据来修正。

Method: 采用掩码流匹配损失进行精确分解（无需独立性假设），推导出损失由不可约熵项和散度项组成，其中散度沿训练路径的导数对于训练期间稀疏但部署时常见的结构为正且发散。通过理论分析和经验验证（七种不同条件下）揭示退化规律。

Result: 退化为幂律关系，指数-0.605（R²=0.9977）；漂移使采样器错误地板上升2.2-34.3倍，但采样预算在50倍范围内变化时漂移周期边际误差变化不超过6%；理论上任何基于历史观测的修正器至少保留条件方差；趋势外推仅在μ²>v(1-2ρ)时有效；最佳观测修正仅恢复5.7%的可消除误差。

Conclusion: 时序图生成模型的部署退化在理论和经验上都是无法通过观测数据有效修正的，因为漂移无趋势且均值回归，简单的观测修正或趋势外推效果有限甚至不如不做任何处理。

Abstract: Generative models of temporal graphs are trained on one stretch of an evolving network and deployed on the next, and they degrade badly in the gap. We show this degradation is derivable, general, and not fixable from observations. The masked flow-matching loss decomposes exactly, with no independence assumption, into an irreducible entropy plus a divergence whose derivative along the training path is positive precisely for structures rare during training and common at deployment, diverging as their training probability goes to zero. Empirically the trade-off is a power law with exponent $-0.605$ ($R^2=0.9977$), and drift raises the sampler's error floor without changing how many steps reach it: across seven well-powered conditions the drift-period marginal error varies by at most $6\%$ over a $50\times$ range of sampling budgets, while the floor sits $2.2\times$ to $34.3\times$ above the in-period floor. Because the deployment period is observed, correction looks like a matter of measurement. It is not. We prove that any corrector measurable with respect to past observations leaves at least the conditional variance of the statistic it tracks, and that trend extrapolation beats trusting the last observation only when $μ^2>v(1-2ρ)$. Both premises are measurable and both go the wrong way: the drift is trendless and mean-reverting, with a one-step innovation as large as the drift itself. An oracle removes $60\%$ of the error, the best observation-based corrector recovers $5.7\%$ of that, and extrapolation is strictly worse than doing nothing clever.

</details>


### [170] [Explainable Reinforcement Learning via Physics-Aware Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24672)
*Shaker Al-Tamari,Waled Kadour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略蒸馏框架，将不透明的TD3连续控制策略蒸馏为可解释的决策树学生模型，在倒立摆任务中实现了与教师模型相当的性能，并验证了系统的BIBO稳定性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRL在安全关键领域（如机器人、汽车工程）的部署受限于深度神经网络的不可解释性，这阻碍了监管合规和人机信任，因此需要使高性能连续控制DRL系统变得可解释。

Method: 采用策略蒸馏框架，使用TD3作为连续教师模型，将其策略蒸馏为浅层决策树学生模型；通过自定义物理感知特征和"带噪Oracle Rollouts"生成数据集，并进行控制理论对比分析。

Result: 蒸馏后的决策树学生模型达到了与专家教师模型相当的性能；从连续控制转向离散规则控制会导致高频Bang-Bang驱动和稳定的双模态极限环；系统保持了BIBO稳定性，同时提供了全局和局部可解释性。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略蒸馏方法能够在不显著牺牲性能的前提下，将DRL连续控制策略转化为可解释的规则模型，适用于安全关键自主系统；但连续到离散的转换存在固有的控制权衡。

Abstract: In safety-critical sectors such as robotics and automotive engineering, the deployment of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) is often hindered by the black-box nature of deep neural networks. This lack of transparency poses significant challenges for regulatory compliance and human-agent trust. This paper presents an experimental study aimed at making high-performance continuous control DRL systems interpretable. A policy distillation framework is implemented using the classic Inverted Pendulum benchmark. A high-performance Twin Delayed DDPG (TD3) agent serves as an opaque, continuous teacher model, whose policy is distilled into an interpretable student surrogate based on a shallow Decision Tree. By leveraging a custom physics-aware feature and "Noisy Oracle Rollouts" for dataset generation, the distillation process achieves performance equivalent to the expert teacher. Furthermore, comparative control theory analysis reveals a fundamental trade-off: transitioning from continuous to discrete rule-based control induces high-frequency Bang-Bang actuation and a stable bimodal limit cycle. Simulation results indicate that Bounded-Input Bounded-Output (BIBO) stability is maintained while providing both global and local interpretability for safe autonomous systems.

</details>


### [171] [Causal-TS: A Python Library for Causal Discovery in High-Dimensional and Nonstationary Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24673)
*Mohammad Fesanghary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Causal-TS是一个开源Python库，专注于高维非平稳多元时间序列的因果发现，提供四种专用算法、GPU加速的CI测试层、变点检测驱动的机制发现流程，以及从原始数据到因果效应估计的端到端流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现工具在处理高维、非平稳时间序列时存在局限性，且缺乏统一的端到端框架来整合变点检测、因果发现和因果效应估计。

Method: 提供CDNOTS、CDNOTS+、CEDAR、GRACE四种专用算法，以及GES、Granger、LASSO-VAR、LGES等包装器；统一的条件独立性测试层通过PyTorch实现GPU加速；可插拔变点检测器实现机制发现流程；支持命令行界面、合成数据生成器和DoWhy集成。

Result: 实现了从原始时间序列到因果效应估计的端到端流水线，支持Python 3.10-3.12，通过pip安装，代码开源。

Conclusion: Causal-TS为高维非平稳时间序列因果发现提供了一个统一、高效的开源解决方案，具备GPU加速和工业级可部署性。

Abstract: We describe Causal-TS, an open-source Python library for causal discovery in high-dimensional and nonstationary multivariate time series. Causal-TS provides four specialized algorithms-CDNOTS, CDNOTS+, CEDAR, and GRACE-along with wrappers for GES, Granger, LASSO-VAR, and LGES, all sharing a unified conditional independence (CI) test layer with GPU acceleration via PyTorch. A regime discovery pipeline detects structural breaks via pluggable changepoint detectors and runs discovery per regime with regime-specific parameters. A command-line interface, synthetic data generators, and optional DoWhy integration provide an end-to-end pipeline from raw time series to causal effect estimates. The library is pip-installable, tested on Python 3.10--3.12, and available at https://github.com/bloomberg/causal-ts.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Optimal Investment with Switching Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22536)
*Yu-Jui Huang,Liviu Ignat,Traian A. Pirvu,Reihaneh Vafadar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究重大人生事件引发风险厌恶变化对最优投资决策的影响，发现HJB方程因包含事后最优值函数而无法通过Fenchel-Legendre变换线性化，通过不动点、紧性和验证论证建立了完全非线性抛物方程正凸经典解的存在性，并推导出最优交易策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究表明重大人生事件会显著且持续地提高个体的风险厌恶水平，但已有文献缺乏对这种事件引发的风险偏好变化如何影响最优投资决策的理论分析。

Method: 在有限时间水平下，假设重大人生事件独立于金融市场发生，投资者预见到风险厌恶可能变化，通过求解含事后最优值函数的HJB方程，结合不动点定理、紧性论证和验证定理，建立完全非线性抛物方程的解的存在性。

Result: 证明了完全非线性抛物方程存在正凸经典解且具有适当的增长性，该解的凸共轭满足HJB方程并与事前最优值函数一致；最优策略由事前策略（由HJB方程解表示）和事后策略（传统Merton比例）拼接而成。

Conclusion: 成功将重大人生事件引发的时变风险厌恶纳入最优投资框架，在技术上克服了Fenchel-Legendre变换失效的困难，为现实中投资者在经历重大事件后的行为调整提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Major life events can significantly increase individuals' risk aversion over a sustained period of time, as empirical studies reveal. How such an event-triggered shift of risk preferences impacts optimal investment is the focus of this paper. On a finite time horizon where a major life event may occur independently of the financial market, an investor aims to maximize her expected utility from terminal wealth while foreseeing a potential change in her risk aversion. We find that the associated Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation involves the post-event optimal value function (under elevated but fixed risk aversion after the event's occurrence), and the Fenchel--Legendre transform fails to linearize this HJB equation: it yields a parabolic equation with a fully nonlinear term, induced precisely by the post-event optimal value function. Through a combination of fixed-point, compactness, and verification arguments, we establish the existence of a positive convex classical solution with suitable growth to the fully-nonlinear parabolic equation. The convex conjugate of this solution is shown to satisfy the HJB equation and coincides with the pre-event optimal value function. The optimal trading strategy is obtained by concatenating the optimal pre-event and post-event strategies -- the former is expressed in terms of the solution to the HJB equation and the latter is traditional Merton's ratio.

</details>


### [173] [A uniqueness result for finite-state mean field games with non-separable Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22537)
*Alekos Cecchin,Luca Di Persio,Nicola Fraccarolo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限状态空间上连续时间平均场博弈，其中转移率依赖于种群分布导致哈密顿量不可分，在成本强单调性和动态交互项定量边界条件下建立了任意时间区间上的新唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lasry-Lions单调性论证在此类非可分哈密顿量的平均场博弈中无法直接应用，需要建立新的唯一性理论框架。

Method: 结合成本函数的强单调性假设与动力学中交互项的定量界，通过分析系统结构推导唯一性。

Result: 在任意时间区间上，当成本满足强单调性且动态交互项受定量边界约束时，该平均场博弈的解是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了非可分哈密顿量平均场博弈唯一性理论的空白，为更广泛的应用场景提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study a class of continuous-time mean field games on a finite state space with transition rates depending on the population distribution, leading to a non-separable Hamiltonian. In this setting, classical Lasry--Lions monotonicity arguments do not apply directly. We establish a new uniqueness result on arbitrary time horizons under a combination of strong monotonicity assumptions on the costs and quantitative bounds on the interaction term in the dynamics.

</details>


### [174] [Low-Rank KKT Updates and a Parallel Flipping Mechanism for Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22538)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小Frobenius范数更新模型的导数无优化方法，利用KKT矩阵的固定内积结构，通过精确的秩-2更新公式实现O(n²)的KKT逆更新，避免了每次迭代的昂贵分解。集成到并行信赖域算法中，在530个基准问题上展示了更低的模型维护开销和更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的导数无优化依赖于二次插值模型，但维护这些模型通常需要O(m³)线性系统求解，计算成本高昂。本文旨在降低模型维护的计算复杂度。

Method: 利用最小Frobenius范数更新模型中KKT矩阵的固定内积结构，发现单点替换和坐标轴翻转操作仅造成精确的秩-2扰动，基于此推导出O(n²)的KKT逆更新公式，取代传统的重构分解。将此更新集成到并行信赖域算法中，工作节点独立翻转插值轴、刷新局部模型并同步最优配置。

Result: 在530个基准问题上的测试表明，该方法降低了模型维护开销，并在严格函数评估预算下相比标准求解器获得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 利用KKT矩阵的固定内积结构和秩-2扰动特性，可实现高效的逆更新，显著提升基于模型的导数无优化方法的计算效率，尤其适用于函数评估预算受限的场景。

Abstract: Model-based derivative-free optimization relies on quadratic interpolation, but maintaining these models typically requires $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ linear system solves. We show that for the least Frobenius norm updating model, the associated KKT matrix possesses a fixed inner-product structure. Both single-point replacements and a proposed coordinate-axis flipping operation induce exact Rank-2 perturbations to this matrix. Using this structure, we derive an $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ update formula for the KKT inverse, eliminating costly refactorizations at each iteration. We integrate the update into a parallel trust-region algorithm where workers independently flip interpolation axes, refresh local models, and synchronize the best configuration. Tests on 530 benchmark problems show the method reduces model-maintenance overhead and achieves higher success rates under tight function-evaluation budgets compared to standard solvers.

</details>


### [175] [Comparing Optimization Models for Radiotherapy Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22539)
*C. C. Rambaldi Migliore,D. Stanicel,N. Musliu,G. Iacca,M. Roveri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文针对放射治疗调度问题（RTSP），提出了两种新的贪婪启发式算法（RTSP First Fit和RTSP Best Fit）及其与模拟退火（SA）的组合方法，相比整数线性规划方法大幅降低了计算时间和内存消耗，同时保持了接近最优的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于整数线性规划的每日批处理调度方法虽然有效，但需要大量的计算资源和内存，限制了其在实际临床中的应用。

Method: 开发了两种贪婪启发式算法（RTSP First Fit和RTSP Best Fit），并将其作为模拟退火（SA）的构造启发式方法，形成组合优化策略。

Result: 在公开数据集上的评估表明，所提出的启发式算法在解质量上接近精确方法，同时显著降低了运行时间和内存消耗；与SA结合后进一步提升了解质量，且仍保持较低的计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的启发式及混合方法为RTSP提供了一种高效且实用的替代方案，在保证调度质量的前提下大幅降低了计算开销，具有临床部署潜力。

Abstract: The Radiotherapy Scheduling Problem (RTSP) involves determining an optimal schedule for patients undergoing radiation treatments, a task that has a massive impact on clinical outcomes given the central role of radiotherapy in cancer care. The daily batch approach--which consists of scheduling all the newly arrived patients together at the end of each day--modelled with Integer Linear Programming, is currently one of the most effective methods for the RTSP. However, this kind of formulation requires substantial computational resources in terms of time and memory. Here, we address these limitations by developing two novel greedy heuristics (named RTSP First Fit and RTSP Best Fit) and use them as constructive heuristics for a Simulated Annealing (SA) approach to optimize the scheduling. The proposed methods--the heuristics alone and their combination with SA--are evaluated on a publicly available dataset against an integer linear program formulation solved with two different state-of-the-art exact solvers. Evaluation metrics include six scheduling objectives capturing patient waiting times, preference satisfaction, and changes in linear accelerator assignment (aggregated in four different weight configurations), solving time, and memory consumption. The results show that the novel heuristics achieve solutions close to those of exact methods, while dramatically reducing runtime and memory usage; furthermore, when combined with SA, they further improve the solution quality while maintaining low runtime and memory usage.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Path Routing in Decentralized Exchange Networks: Convex Allocation and an Improving-Path Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22540)
*Ilia Zhavoronkov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图论和凸优化的去中心化交易所多路径路由框架，包含候选路径生成和连续流分配两个层，通过KKT条件实现边际输出均衡，并设计了改进路径证书来验证解的最优性。在以太坊主网上评估显示，中位短差低于5个基点，前三报价排名超过57%的时期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化交易所（DEX）聚合器的多路径路由问题需要高效地在大量AMM流动性池中找到最优交易路径，现有方法在计算效率和最优性保证之间存在权衡。

Method: 1) 将市场建模为带AMM兑换函数的有向代币多重图；2) 两层路由分解：候选路径生成采用gas感知的边缘k-最短路径枚举（嵌入预期执行成本，并跟踪池-简单约束），流分配建模为带每池价格影响上限的单纯形上凹函数最大化；3) k=20时通过改进路径证书（单次最短路径查询）验证全局最优性。

Result: 在以太坊主网上针对6种交易规模重复进行WETH-USDT报价观测，与4个生产级DEX聚合器相比：中位短差低于5个基点，前三报价排名超过57%的时期。

Conclusion: 框架在计算效率和路由质量之间取得了良好平衡，改进路径证书在大多数时期确认了当前解的最优性，验证了所提方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: We present a graph-theoretic and convex optimization framework for multi-path routing in decentralized exchange networks, together with its implementation and empirical evaluation on Ethereum mainnet. The framework models the market as a directed token multigraph whose arcs carry AMM exchange functions. Routing is decomposed into two implemented layers: candidate path generation via gas-aware marginal k-shortest-path enumeration, where edge scores embed expected execution cost directly into graph traversal with an explicit pool-simple constraint tracked during path construction, and continuous flow allocation over the selected candidates solved as a concave maximization over a simplex with a per-pool price-impact cap. Under standard concavity and monotonicity assumptions, the KKT conditions imply marginal-output equalization across active paths. The central technical contribution is an improving-path certificate: after solving the allocation on k=20 candidate paths, the KKT multiplier is used as a threshold to determine via a single shortest-path query whether any omitted pool-simple path could improve the current solution; in our implementation the certificate confirms sufficiency in the majority of epochs. Execution is protected by an on-chain slippage tolerance enforced at the smart-contract level. We evaluate the implemented engine against four production DEX aggregators on repeated WETH-USDT quote observations across six trade sizes on Ethereum mainnet: median shortfall is below 5 bps across all sizes and top-3 quote rank exceeds 57% of epochs.

</details>


### [177] [SDE Guided Monte Carlo Reinforcement Learning: A Stochastic Maximum Principle Approach for Robust Decision Making in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22541)
*Juncai Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出SDE-MC-AC框架，将随机极大值原理（SMP）的最优性条件启发式地映射到表格型强化学习中，构建了势场梯度→势能奖励塑形、扩散系数→softmax温度、值函数梯度→TD误差的对应关系，并推导了SMP启发的自适应温度调度策略。在含三种噪声的固定迷宫中验证了五个可证伪假设，实验表明该方法能实现“宏观确定、微观随机”的导航模式，并在鲁棒性、收敛加速和泛化方面取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究随机极大值原理（SMP）的定性最优性条件能否作为设计鲁棒表格型强化学习算法的指导性启发方法，旨在弥合随机最优控制理论与实用强化学习之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出SDE-MC-AC框架，建立SMP与RL的对应关系：势场梯度→势能奖励塑形，扩散系数→softmax温度，值函数梯度→TD误差；推导SMP启发的自适应温度调度；采用蒙特卡洛Actor-Critic进行情节更新，在含感知噪声、动态目标噪声、动作噪声三种干扰的固定迷宫中测试五个可证伪假设。

Result: 验证了五个假设：(i)倒U型最优随机性曲线，(ii)自适应调度在非平稳噪声下的鲁棒性，(iii)势场引导加速收敛，(iv)跨噪声泛化能力，(v)通过消融实验证明组合SDE组件的不可或缺性。轨迹可视化呈现“宏观确定、微观随机”的导航模式。

Conclusion: 即使将SMP启发式地移植到离散MDP中也能带来显著的实证收益，为连接随机最优控制与鲁棒强化学习开辟了桥梁。

Abstract: This work investigates whether the qualitative optimality conditions of the SMP can serve as guiding heuristics for designing robust tabular RL algorithms. We propose the \textbf{SDE-MC-AC} framework, which establishes a set of SMP-inspired correspondences: the potential field gradient is mapped to potential-based reward shaping, the diffusion coefficient to a softmax temperature, and the value function gradient magnitude to the episode-averaged absolute temporal-difference (TD) error. Crucially, we derive an SMP-motivated adaptive temperature schedule that scales exploration stochastically in response to local value uncertainty. The resulting Monte Carlo Actor-Critic agent integrates potential-shaped rewards, entropy regularization, and adaptive temperature control within episodic updates. We conduct a set of five principled experiments in a minimal, fixed maze testbed under three canonical noise regimes (perceptual, dynamic goal, action) to test five falsifiable hypotheses derived from the SMP. Our results provide evidence for (i) an inverted-U optimal stochasticity curve, (ii) the robust failure prevention of the adaptive schedule under non-stationary noise, (iii) convergence acceleration by potential field guidance, (iv) cross-noise generalization, and (v) the indispensability of the combined SDE components through systematic ablation. Trajectory visualizations reveal a characteristic ``macroscopically deterministic, microscopically stochastic'' navigation pattern consistent with the SDE model. The study demonstrates that even a heuristic transposition of the SMP into a discrete MDP can yield significant empirical gains, thereby opening a bridge between rigorous stochastic optimal control and practical robust reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [178] [A Hybrid Matheuristic Framework for the Chinese Postman Problem with Load-Dependent Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22542)
*Thieu Khang Nguyen,Thu Huong Dang,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有负载依赖成本的邮递员问题（CPP-LC），提出了一种将元启发式搜索与数学规划相结合的混合优化框架，包括局部搜索、简化混合整数线性规划模型和蚁群优化算法。实验表明，该方法在解质量和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际物流和运输系统中，旅行成本依赖于车辆负载和能耗，传统的邮递员问题模型无法准确刻画此类场景，因此需要针对CPP-LC设计高效求解方法。

Method: 提出混合优化框架，整合元启发式搜索与数学规划：结合局部搜索过程与简化的混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型以平衡探索与强化；同时开发蚁群优化（ACO）算法以提升大规模实例的可扩展性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提框架能够一致地获得高质量解，在解质量上优于现有方法，同时保持具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 混合优化策略对于解决实际应用中复杂的负载依赖路由问题具有有效性，且代码已开源。

Abstract: The Chinese Postman Problem with load-dependent costs (CPP-LC) arises in real-world logistics and transportation systems where travel costs depend on vehicle load and energy consumption. In this work, we propose a hybrid optimization framework that integrates metaheuristic search with mathematical programming to efficiently solve CPP-LC. The proposed method combines local search procedures with reduced mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) models to balance exploration and intensification. In addition, we develop an Ant Colony Optimization (ACO) algorithm to enhance scalability on large instances. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves high-quality solutions and outperforms existing approaches in solution quality, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of hybrid optimization strategies for complex, load-dependent routing problems in practical applications. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/HySonLab/MatCPP

</details>


### [179] [Route Based Map Matching via a Structured Codebook and Token Sequence Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22543)
*Takara Sakai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于路线编码本和模糊字符串匹配的高效轻量级地图匹配方法，利用命名道路和交叉口的符号结构，通过DAFSA×Levenshtein自动机实现快速解码，适用于城市快速路网的GPS轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图匹配方法（如HMM）在计算上较重，且未充分利用城市快速路网中命名道路和命名交叉口（立交桥）的层级符号结构信息。本文旨在利用这一结构设计一种计算更轻量、解码更高效的地图匹配方法。

Method: 1) 将每条候选路线表示为线（道路名）和交叉口（立交桥名）的序列，构建路线编码本；2) 通过网格量化器将GPS探针轨迹转换为令牌序列（将每个坐标映射到最近的线或交叉口令牌）；3) 将地图匹配建模为探针令牌序列与编码本成员之间的评分对齐问题；4) 利用DAFSA×Levenshtein自动机（近似字符串匹配/语音识别技术）对编码本进行模糊索引，实现亚线性解码。

Result: 在东京首都高速道路网的变形副本上评估：中等GPS噪声下能精确恢复路线，重噪声下仍能识别线/交叉口序列；给出了网格量化器的分辨率操作范围；解码成本相比暴力扫描降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出的方法在计算效率上显著优于传统方法，利用符号结构进行地图匹配具有可行性；但真实探针数据评估、信道模型校准以及与HMM的对比实验留待后续版本完成。

Abstract: This study proposes an efficient and computationally light route based map matching method for GPS track data on urban expressway networks. The key idea is to exploit a symbolic structure of named lines and named junctions that link level map matching leaves unused. We represent each candidate route as a sequence of line and junction names, take the set of such sequences as a route codebook, and formulate map matching as scored alignment of a probe trajectory against members of the codebook. Probes become token sequences via a mesh quantizer, a precomputed grid mapping each coordinate to a line or junction token, and the decoder returns a member of the codebook by construction.
