In particular, the graph formed by the pair supports producing $2$ values contains no triangle. These arguments also gives anohter proof that the maximum Pareto capacity in order $3$ is equal to $9$.
  For each of the $3$ profiles, we give an explicit full-capacity matrix with $9$ regular Pareto eigenvalues. We also prove that each profile occurs on a nonempty Euclidean-open set where every Pareto eigenvalue has a unique producing support.

</details>


### [219] [New Globalized Newton-Type Methods for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23433)
*Vo Thanh Phat,Tuyen Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用线搜索牛顿框架用于无约束优化，避免每次迭代都进行Hessian正则化，仅在牛顿方向定义良好且适当时使用。该框架涵盖多种混合梯度-牛顿方法，并自然导出一种新的外梯度牛顿方法。在PLK条件等温和假设下建立全局收敛性，在适当正则性假设下证明局部超线性和二次收敛，并首次将牛顿型算法应用于强拟凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局收敛牛顿型方法通常要求目标函数凸性或强凸性，非凸优化方法则依赖于每次迭代的Hessian正则化，缺乏一个通用框架来避免重复正则化，同时处理凸与非凸（特别是强拟凸）优化问题。

Method: 提出一个通用的线搜索牛顿框架，仅在牛顿方向定义良好且适当时使用该方向，否则切换至梯度方向。该框架包含混合梯度-牛顿方法和一种新的外梯度牛顿方法作为特例。

Result: 在PLK条件下建立了全局收敛性（允许孤立和非孤立聚点），在正则性假设下证明了局部超线性和二次收敛，并将该框架成功应用于强拟凸优化，给出了首个牛顿型算法及其完整收敛性分析。

Conclusion: 该通用牛顿框架有效避免了重复Hessian正则化，在非凸优化尤其是强拟凸问题上展现出良好的理论性质和实际性能，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Newton's method is one of the most effective second-order algorithms for smooth optimization because of its fast local convergence. However, existing globally convergent Newton-type methods typically require convexity or strong convexity of the objective function, while approaches for nonconvex optimization often rely on Hessian regularization at every iteration. In this paper, we propose a general line-search Newton framework for unconstrained optimization that avoids repeated Hessian regularization by exploiting the Newton direction only when it is well-defined and suitable. The proposed framework encompasses several existing hybrid gradient--Newton methods as special cases and naturally yields a new extragradient Newton method. We establish global convergence under mild assumptions, including the Polyak--Lojasiewicz--Kurdyka (PLK) condition, allowing both isolated and nonisolated accumulation points. We further prove local superlinear and quadratic convergence under appropriate regularity assumptions. Finally, we apply the proposed framework to strongly quasiconvex optimization and provide, to the best of our knowledge, the first Newton-type algorithm together with a comprehensive convergence analysis for this important class of nonconvex optimization problems. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [220] [On the Bellman equation in recursive stochastic dynamic programming with the CES aggregator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23475)
*Anna Jaśkiewicz,Andrzej S. Nowak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有CES加总器和熵风险度量确定性等价的递归效用马尔可夫决策过程，证明了Bellman方程解的存在唯一性，以及最优平稳策略的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态规划模型中引入递归效用和熵风险度量，解决非标准风险偏好下Bellman方程的理论基础问题。

Method: 利用CES加总器和负熵风险度量定义递归效用，在原始数据的温和条件下，通过不动点理论和可测选择定理证明Bellman方程解的存在唯一性以及Koopmans方程的唯一解。

Result: 证明了Bellman方程解的存在唯一性；任意平稳策略下Koopmans方程存在唯一解；Bellman方程的最大值可测选择构成最优平稳策略。

Conclusion: 所提方法可推广至其他确定性等价（包括风险中性情形），为递归效用动态规划提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate discrete-time infinite horizon Markov decision processes (dynamic programming models) with recursive utilities defined by the classical \emph{CES} aggregator and the certainty equivalent operator being the negative of the entropic risk measure. Under mild conditions on the primitive data we prove existence and uniqueness of a solution to the Bellman equation. Moreover, we show that for any given stationary policy, the Koopmans equation has a unique solution. These two facts imply that a measurable selector of maxima in the Bellman equation is an optimal stationary policy. The methods of our proofs are applied to study solutions to the Bellman equation for other certainty equivalents, including risk-neutral case.

</details>


### [221] [A Homogeneous Tensor Framework for High-Order Trust-Region and Spherical Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24046)
*Wenqi Zhu,Haibin Chen,Guanglu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于均匀张量表示和半径控制边界方法的高阶优化算法Ada-HTM，通过将p阶Taylor多项式转化为球面上的齐次多项式优化问题，并针对三次情形设计了近端交替最小化（PAM）方法求解。在特定条件下，算法达到了ARp类型的一阶平稳性评估复杂度O(ε^{-(p+1)/p})，数值实验表明其在病态和尺度不良问题上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶方法可改善最坏情况评估复杂度，但p≥3时的Taylor子问题是非凸多项式优化问题，难以求解。现有方法缺乏高效且可扩展的求解手段，亟需开发新的框架来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出半径控制的边界方法：将p阶Taylor多项式通过增广常数坐标表示为阶-p均匀张量形式，在指定半径上转化为球面多项式优化问题。针对三次情形（p=3），引入二次移位并证明在显式移位界下等价于三块多线性形式的全局最优，进而采用近端交替最小化（PAM）方法进行求解。将边界步机制嵌入自适应均匀张量方法（Ada-HTM）中。

Result: 在显式光滑性、保证下降、弱曲率非退化及局部精炼条件下，Ada-HTM达到ARp类型的一阶平稳性评估复杂度O(ε^{-(p+1)/p})。数值上，PAM在结构化的三次实例上匹配二阶矩-SOS证书，对低秩张量扩展性良好，Ada-HTM在病态和尺度不良问题上优于信赖域和立方正则化方法。

Conclusion: 提出的均匀张量边界方法有效解决了高阶方法中子问题求解的困难，PAM方法在三次情形下高效且可扩展，Ada-HTM算法在理论上保证复杂度、在实践上具有竞争力，尤其适用于病态问题。

Abstract: High-order methods can improve worst-case evaluation complexity, but for orders $p\geq3$ their Taylor subproblems are nonconvex polynomial optimization problems and are generally difficult to solve. We develop a radius-controlled boundary approach based on homogeneous tensor representations. By augmenting the step with a constant coordinate, any $p$th-order Taylor polynomial can be represented exactly as an order-$p$ homogeneous tensor form; at a prescribed radius, the boundary model is a spherical polynomial optimization problem. The representation applies to arbitrary $p$, while the algorithmic development focuses on the cubic case $p=3$. For an inhomogeneous cubic on the sphere, we introduce a quadratic shift and prove, under an explicit shift bound, equivalence with a three-block multilinear formulation at global optimality. This motivates a proximal alternating minimization (PAM) method with closed-form block updates; its objective values decrease and every accumulation point is stationary. We embed the boundary-step mechanism in an Adaptive Homogeneous Tensor Method (Ada--HTM). Under explicit smoothness, safeguarded-decrease, weak-curvature nondegeneracy, and local-refinement conditions, Ada--HTM attains the adaptive-regularization-type (AR$p$-type) evaluation complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-(p+1)/p})$ for first-order stationarity. Numerically, PAM matches order-$2$ moment--sum-of-squares (SOS) certificates on the structured cubic instances for which certification is tractable, scales particularly well for low-rank tensors, and makes Ada--HTM competitive with trust-region and cubic-regularization methods, with its largest gains on ill-conditioned and badly-scaled problems.

</details>


### [222] [Convexity and SOS-Convexity of Sum of Separable and Biquadratic Quartic Polynomials and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23476)
*Shaon Naskar,Sujeet Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类名为SPBQ的四次多项式结构子类，分析了其凸性与SOS凸性之间的关系，证明了在特定条件下（双二次型规模为n×2）凸性蕴含SOS凸性，并给出了3×3的反例，最后展示了SPBQ在无约束/约束优化、凸多项式回归和流体动力学中的计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四次及以上多元多项式的非负性和凸性判定是强NP-hard问题，SOS凸性作为可计算代数松弛虽能提供充分条件，但凸性与SOS凸性之间的差距尚不明确。本文旨在通过引入SPBQ子类，系统分析这一差距，并探索其实际应用价值。

Method: 定义SPBQ（可分离与双二次之和）形式的结构子类，系统分析凸性与SOS凸性的关系：针对n×2双二次型证明凸性蕴涵SOS凸性；构造3×3反例展示凸性但不满足SOS凸性的情况；将SPBQ应用于无约束/约束优化问题，并与通用SOS方法对比计算性能。

Result: 证明了当双二次型规模为n×2时，每个凸的SPBQ多项式必然是SOS凸的；构造了一个3×3双二次型的显式SPBQ反例，该多项式凸但不满足SOS凸性；在凸多项式回归和流体动力学应用中展示了显著的计算优势。

Conclusion: SPBQ子类有效缩小了凸性与SOS凸性之间的差距，在特定结构下两者等价，而在一般情形下存在反例；SPBQ在优化问题中相比通用SOS方法具有计算效率优势，适用于实际工程应用如回归分析和流体动力学。

Abstract: Determining whether multivariate polynomials of degree four or higher are nonnegative and convex is a strongly NP-hard problem. To mitigate these computational difficulties, sum-of- squares (SOS) convexity has been proposed as a tractable algebraic relaxation that yields a checkable sufficient condition for convexity and can be expressed as a semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we introduce a structured subclass of quartic polynomials, called the Sum of Separable and Biquadratic (SPBQ) forms, and conduct a systematic analysis of the connection between convexity and SOS-convexity within this class. Specifically, we show that every convex SPBQ polynomial is necessarily SOS-convex when the associated biquadratic form has size n x 2. We then construct an explicit SPBQ example with a 3 x 3 biquadratic form that is convex but fails to be SOS-convex. Finally, we examine both unconstrained and constrained optimization problems involving SPBQ polynomials, demonstrate notable computational benefits compared to general SOS-based methods, and illustrate their use in convex polynomial regression and fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [223] [Existence of stable Lur'e systems for which the O'Shea-Zames-Falb stability test fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23599)
*Andrey Kharitenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个具体的Lur'e系统实例，证明O'Shea-Zames-Falb（OZF）稳定性测试对于具有斜率限制非线性的Lur'e系统的鲁棒稳定性并非必要条件，从而推翻了J. Carrasco的一个猜想。具体地，我们构造了一个全块乘子来验证所构造的Lur'e互连的稳定性，而该系统不承认OZF乘子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，O'Shea、Zames和Falb提出的稳定性测试被认为是判断具有斜率限制非线性的Lur'e系统鲁棒稳定性的重要工具。然而，关于该测试是否是必要条件（即对于任何稳定的Lur'e系统是否都存在一个OZF乘子）的问题一直悬而未决。J. Carrasco曾提出猜想认为OZF测试是必要的。本文旨在通过构造反例来驳斥这一猜想，澄清该领域的理论基础。

Method: 本文采用构造性反例方法。具体地，研究者设计了一个特定的Lur'e互连系统，该系统具有斜率限制非线性特性。然后构造一个全块乘子（full-block multiplier）来证明该系统的稳定性。同时证明该系统不存在任何OZF乘子，从而表明OZF测试的充分非必要性。

Result: 成功构造了一个具有斜率限制非线性的Lur'e系统，该系统是鲁棒稳定的，但不存在任何OZF乘子来证明其稳定性。这一结果直接反驳了OZF测试是必要条件的断言。

Conclusion: O'Shea-Zames-Falb稳定性测试对于具有斜率限制非线性的Lur'e系统的鲁棒稳定性仅是充分条件而非必要条件。该结论推翻了J. Carrasco的猜想，表明在判断此类系统稳定性时，需要更广泛的乘子类（如全块乘子）来获得更紧的稳定性条件。

Abstract: In this note we show that the well-known stability test proposed by O'Shea, Zames and Falb is not necessary for robust stability of Lur'e systems with slope-restricted nonlinearities and thereby disprove a conjecture by J. Carrasco. Specifically we construct a full-block multiplier which certifies stability of a constructed Lur'e interconnection that does not admit an OZF multiplier.

</details>


### [224] [Exact Worst-case Convergence Rates of Distributed Gradient Tracking Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23601)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过特征分解方法，精确推导了DIGing和AugDGM两种梯度跟踪分布式优化算法的最坏情况收敛率，发现AugDGM的最优收敛率优于DIGing，并揭示了图连通性σ=1/3的临界点：当连通性优于该阈值且目标函数条件数足够差时，AugDGM可达集中式梯度下降的最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化算法的收敛性分析多给出保守的充分条件，缺乏精确的最坏情况收敛率。本文旨在填补这一空白，为DIGing和AugDGM两种典型梯度跟踪算法提供精确的收敛率公式。

Method: 通过特征分解将多输入多输出(MIMO)系统稳定性分析简化为一组参数变化的单输入单输出(SISO)系统，进而推导出精确的最坏情况收敛率显式公式。

Result: 1) DIGing和AugDGM具有相同的平均状态动力学，但梯度跟踪子系统不同；2) DIGing的最优最坏情况收敛率大于AugDGM；3) 图连通性存在拐点σ=1/3，连通性优于该阈值时AugDGM可达集中式最优率，否则不可达。

Conclusion: AugDGM在收敛性能上优于DIGing，且当图连通性足够好且目标函数条件数足够差时，AugDGM能够达到集中式梯度下降的最优收敛率。

Abstract: Different from most existing literature in the analysis of distributed optimization algorithms that reports sufficient convergence conditions leading to a conservative convergence rate, this work provides the exact worst-case convergence rates for two typical gradient tracking algorithms, DIGing and AugDGM. By eigen-decomposition, we show that two algorithms share the same average-state dynamics, while they differ from each other in the gradient tracking subsystems, which entirely govern algorithm convergence. Exploiting the diagonal structure of this decomposition, we reduce the stability analysis of MIMO systems to that of a set of parameter-varying SISO systems, from which explicit formulas for the exact worst-case convergence rate can be derived. These formulas clearly show that the optimal worst-case convergence rate of DIGing is larger than that of AugDGM under the same assumptions on objective functions and communication networks. Furthermore, we find that there is an inflection point in the graph connectivity, which is $σ=1/3$. For graphs with connectivity better than this inflection point, the optimal convergence rate of centralized gradient descent can be achieved by AugDGM provided the condition number of objective functions is worse enough. On the other hand, for graphs with connectivity worse than this inflection point, the centralized optimal rate can never be achieved. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [225] [When Rates Are Geometric: Rate-Certificate Transfer for Contact Splittings in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23642)
*George A Kevrekidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出接触哈密顿系统作为联系连续时间ODE与离散优化算法收敛性分析的统一框架，通过接触分裂方法将连续时间收敛证书精确传递到离散算法，并在二次重球法等案例中验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散优化算法常通过连续时间ODE分析，但ODE的收敛性并不能自动保证离散算法的收敛性。现有理论缺乏一个能够精确传递收敛证书的通用框架。

Method: 利用接触哈密顿系统$H$的内禀衰减恒等式$\dot H = -H\,\partial_s H$构建增广能量$\mathcal{E}$作为连续时间收敛证书；提出阶数为$r$的接触分裂方法（contact splitting），通过后向误差分析证明在有限时间范围内离散衰减包络由修正共形因子主导，且修正哈密顿量本身仍是接触哈密顿量，从而精确继承衰减机制。

Result: 给出了三个独立可检验的假设下离散衰减包络的$O(h^r)$精度保证；二次重球法作为完全可解的例子，其投影耗散跳蛙谱与共形辛优化理论一致；对强凸带状态依赖阻尼的目标，通过辅助阴影推论实现了Bregman型Lyapunov证书的传递；数值实验验证了共形因子跟踪阶数，并在病态基准和深度学习任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: 接触哈密顿系统为离散优化算法的收敛性分析提供了一个精确的理论传递框架，$H=K+V+D$的分解可作为设计模板，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Discrete optimization algorithms are often analyzed through continuous-time limiting ODEs, but a convergence certificate for the ODE is not automatically one for the discrete algorithm. We develop contact Hamiltonian systems as a setting where the transfer can be made precise. A contact Hamiltonian $H$ on $J^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ obeys the intrinsic decay identity $\dot H = -H\,\partial_s H$, so an augmented energy $\mathcal{E}$ built from $H$, together with the conformal rate $\partial_s H$, is a continuous-time rate certificate whenever $\mathcal{E}$ controls the objective gap. Our main theorem states, under three named and independently checkable hypotheses, that an order-$r$ contact splitting with step $h$ transfers this certificate over the finite horizon set by backward error analysis. The discrete decay envelope is governed by the modified conformal factor up to $O(h^r)$ perturbations plus a backward-error shadowing defect, and the mechanism is inherited exactly because the modified Hamiltonian is itself a contact Hamiltonian. Quadratic heavy ball is a fully solvable example: its projected dissipative-leapfrog spectrum agrees with established conformal-symplectic optimization theory, while the augmented contact Hamiltonian yields a sharp objective-to-certificate comparison that verifies the transfer hypotheses. For strongly convex objectives with state-dependent damping, an explicit Bregman-type Lyapunov certificate instead transfers by an auxiliary-shadowing corollary. The decomposition $H=K+V+D$ into kinetic, objective-encoding potential, and dissipation terms serves as a design template, with a catalogue of closed-form sub-flows including contact-specific damping families. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted conformal-factor tracking orders and show competitive performance on ill-conditioned benchmarks and deep-learning tasks.

</details>


### [226] [Projected-Gradient Analysis for Open-Domain Convex Optimization under Boundary Blow-Up:Application to Controllability Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23674)
*Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个处理定义域边界有爆破条件的凸优化框架，分析了Armijo投影梯度法的收敛性，并将其应用于可控性评分与输入分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标函数仅在开集上光滑凸，定义域边界存在爆破条件，现有方法无法直接处理此类边界奇异优化问题。

Method: 提出域感知的Armijo投影梯度法，通过安全邻域分析保证目标函数求值可行、回溯有限、充分下降，并建立了步长正下界；利用正曲率条件证明唯一性和线性收敛。

Result: 获得了次线性的目标函数和驻点保证，以及全迭代序列收敛性；在正曲率条件下进一步得到唯一解和线性收敛率。

Conclusion: 该框架可应用于带紧凑凸分配约束的可控性评分问题，精确刻画了可行性与可控性的关系，并通过有向网络实例展示了候选排除对可行性和最优分配的影响。

Abstract: We study convex optimization over a compact convex set when the objective is smooth and convex only on an open domain. Under a boundary-blow-up condition, every feasible initialization yields a compact invariant sublevel set separated from the complement of the objective domain, and an optimal solution exists. For a domain-aware Armijo projected-gradient method, a safe-neighborhood analysis establishes well-defined objective evaluations, finite backtracking, sufficient decrease, and a run-specific but iteration-independent positive lower bound on the accepted step sizes. These properties yield explicit sublinear objective and stationarity guarantees, together with convergence of the full iterate sequence. Positive curvature restricted to feasible displacement directions further guarantees uniqueness and linear convergence. We apply the framework to controllability scoring with prescribed input directions and compact convex allocation constraints. Feasibility is characterized exactly by controllability of the input directions eligible for positive allocation, while restricted injectivity of the Gramian map guarantees uniqueness of the optimal allocation and provides explicit strong-convexity bounds. A directed-network example illustrates how candidate exclusion can preserve or destroy feasibility and alter the optimal allocation in a criterion- and horizon-dependent manner.

</details>


### [227] [LiFT-MPC: Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning of Cost Previews for MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23832)
*Xinyi Yi,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，通过语言循环反馈调优（LiFT）校正方案，在模型预测控制（MPC）中在线更新预测机制，利用控制性能损失函数优化时变目标（如能源价格预测），并给出闭环系统的性能保证。在储能管理场景中使用真实价格和新闻上下文进行数值实验，证明了经济性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有时变目标的MPC中，预测信号（如能源系统中的价格）很难仅从历史轨迹准确预测，因为它们依赖于其他上下文事件。现有方法缺乏利用外部上下文信息在线优化预测的机制，导致控制经济性能受限。

Method: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，将语言循环反馈调优（LiFT）校正方案集成到MPC中。通过控制性能损失函数在线更新预测机制，利用语言模型处理上下文信息（如新闻）来改进预测，并对闭环系统建立了性能保证。

Result: 在基于真实电价和新闻上下文的储能管理数值实验中，LiFT-MPC相比传统方法实现了更优的经济性能。

Conclusion: LiFT-MPC框架通过在线整合上下文语言信息来改进MPC中的预测，能够提升时变目标场景下的控制经济性能，且具有理论性能保证。

Abstract: In model predictive control (MPC) with time-varying objectives, predicted signals need to be often incorporated in the cost function, such as prices in energy system operation. These are, however, often difficult to predict from the historical trajectory of these signals alone, as they may depend on other contextual events. We propose LiFT-MPC, an MPC framework that integrates a LiFT (Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning) correction scheme to refine such predictions within the MPC loop. The prediction mechanism is updated online via a control-performance loss function, and we establish a performance guarantee for the resulting closed loop system. Numerical experiments using a realistic example of energy-storage management with real prices and news context to improve predictions, demonstrate an improved economic performance

</details>


### [228] [Closed-loop solvability of infinite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic problem for Markov regime-switching jump-diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24004)
*Kai Ding,Fan Wu,Jie Xiong,Xinyue Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类无限时域下马尔可夫链切换跳跃扩散系统的随机线性二次型（SLQ）控制问题，其中状态过程的跳跃与马尔可夫链的模态切换同步发生，通过耦合代数Riccati方程（CAREs）的镇定解给出了最优控制的反馈表示，并将其应用于终身财富追踪问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的马尔可夫调制扩散模型仅考虑连续路径，而实际系统中状态过程在马尔可夫链模态切换时会发生同步跳跃（如资产配置中的转换成本或损失），现有研究对此类跳跃的SLQ问题缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 将状态跳跃建模为马尔可夫链转移诱导的完全可观测跳跃过程，区别于经典泊松跳跃扩散模型；通过引入耦合代数Riccati方程组（CAREs）的镇定解概念，建立闭环可解性的充分必要条件，并推导最优控制的反馈形式。

Result: 给出了SLQ控制问题闭环可解的充要条件，证明了最优控制可由CAREs的镇定解进行状态反馈表示；在终身财富追踪应用中导出了最优投资策略的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为马尔可夫链切换诱导跳跃的SLQ控制提供了完整的理论框架，拓展了经典随机LQ控制理论，并在金融投资策略优化中展现了应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates a class of stochastic linear-quadratic (SLQ) control problems over an infinite horizon for Markov regime-switching jump-diffusion systems. Unlike classical diffusion models modulated by a Markov chain, we assume that the state process undergoes abrupt jumps that are synchronous with the regime switches of the Markov chain. In contrast to conventional Poisson jump-diffusion models, the jumps in the state process are entirely induced by the state transitions of the Markov chain, which can be interpreted as losses or gains of state process incurred during regime changes. Under this formulation, we thoroughly discuss the closed-loop solvability of the SLQ control problem and provide a feedback representation of the optimal control via the stabilizing solution of a system of coupled algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs). Finally, we further apply our results to a lifetime wealth tracking problem and derive the corresponding optimal investment strategy.

</details>


### [229] [Smooth Learning with Hard Constraints via Legendre-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24007)
*Zikun Lin,Rui Chen,Yijie Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Legendre正则化策略，将决策参数化为原始可行域上正则化优化问题的解，从而同时满足表达力强、严格可行且光滑可微的要求，在报童问题和资源分配问题上优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文优化方法在设计策略类时，难以同时满足三个关键需求：足够的表达能力以学习丰富的上下文-决策关系、强制满足硬可行性约束而非软惩罚项、以及足够光滑以支持基于梯度的下游决策损失训练。已有方法通常只侧重其中部分要求。

Method: 提出Legendre正则化策略，将决策参数化为在原始可行域上求解正则化优化问题的解。该构造使策略天然满足可行性约束，且对学习的潜在参数可微。利用Legendre正则化性质，证明了优化器映射是单值的、映射到可行集相对内部、具有显式Jacobi矩阵、Lipschitz连续且可任意光滑。同时建立了通用逼近定理。

Result: 在上下文报童问题和资源分配问题上的实验表明，所提方法在决策效果上优于现有基准方法。理论分析证明了映射的良定义性、光滑性及逼近能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架统一了显式正则化优化器和基于隐式扰动的光滑优化器，为上下文优化提供了兼具理论保证和实用性能的策略类设计范式。

Abstract: We revisit contextual optimization from the perspective of policy class design. A desirable policy class should be expressive enough to learn rich context-decision relationships, should enforce hard feasibility constraints rather than soft penalty terms, and should remain smooth enough for gradient-based training on downstream decision losses. Existing approaches usually emphasize only part of these requirements. We propose Legendre-regularized policies, which parameterize decisions as solutions of regularized optimization problems over the original feasible region. This construction yields policies that are feasible by construction and differentiable with respect to learned latent parameters. We prove that the associated optimizer map is single-valued, maps onto the relative interior of the feasible set, admits an explicit Jacobian, is Lipschitz continuous, and can be made arbitrarily smooth. We also establish a universal approximation result showing that the proposed class can approximate any continuous feasible policy on compact context sets. The framework unifies explicitly regularized optimizers and implicit perturbation-based smooth optimizers. Experiments on contextual newsvendor and resource allocation problems show that our approach improves prescriptive performance relative to the benchmark methods.

</details>


### [230] [Stochastic Control of Addiction with State-Dependent Jump Relapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24099)
*Dounia Aissi,Ioana Ciotir,Dan Goreac,Juan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间理性成瘾模型，其中成瘾资本遵循带状态依赖跳跃（复发与恢复）的分段确定马尔可夫过程。通过幂函数形式的瞬时效用，推导出含非局部跳项的HJB方程。在有界控制情形下，得到唯一有界粘性解并证明最优策略为bang-bang形式；在无界情形下，给出显式线性反馈控制与闭式价值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将理性成瘾模型拓展至连续时间框架，并引入成瘾资本的随机跳跃过程以刻画复发与恢复现象，填补现有文献中缺乏动态随机跳跃建模的空白。

Method: 采用分段确定马尔可夫过程建模成瘾资本，建立带非局部跳项的HJB方程；对有界控制情形使用粘性解理论证明唯一性及bang-bang最优策略；对无界情形通过参数化假设推导显式解。

Result: 有界控制下得到唯一粘性解，最优策略为最小与最大消费间的切换；无界控制下获得多个参数区间的线性反馈控制与闭式价值函数。

Conclusion: 该模型为理性成瘾行为提供了新的连续时间随机分析框架，揭示了跳跃动态对最优消费策略的关键影响。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time rational addiction model where addiction capital follows a piecewise deterministic Markov process with state-dependent jumps capturing relapse and recovery. The instantaneous utility combines consumption and addiction capital via a power specification, leading to Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations with nonlocal jump terms. In the capped, bounded-control case we obtain a unique bounded viscosity solution and prove that optimal policies are bang-bang, switching between minimal and maximal consumption, while in the uncapped case we derive explicit linear feedback controls and closed-form value functions in several parameter regimes.

</details>


### [231] [Optimal Control with Expectation Constraint in a Smooth Boundary Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24114)
*Bruno Bouchard,Lucas Gnecco Heredia,Ludovic Moreau,Kim-Anh Pham*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带期望约束的效用最大化问题。首先在一致椭圆情形下证明内生的状态边界光滑性，推导出合适的Dirichlet边界条件。通过截断论证构造近似PDE序列，证明收敛到原最优控制问题。退化情形通过加小噪声恢复椭圆性。首次对该类控制问题进行完整分析，为数值方法开辟道路。使用神经网络进行数值求解和误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带期望约束的效用最大化问题中缺乏完整理论分析和数值方法的问题，特别是边界条件处理和非一致椭圆情形下的逼近。

Method: 1) 一致椭圆情形：证明状态边界光滑性，导出Dirichlet条件；2) 利用截断论证构造逼近PDE系统，证明比较原理和收敛性；3) 退化情形：添加小噪声恢复椭圆性；4) 神经网络数值求解并估计误差。

Result: 成功建立了带期望约束的最优控制问题的完整分析框架，证明了光滑边界的存在性及逼近序列的收敛性，在玩具示例上通过神经网络实现了数值求解和误差估计。

Conclusion: 首次对该类控制问题进行完整分析，为数值方案的采用奠定了基础。

Abstract: As in Bouchard et al. (2010) and Bouchard and Nutz (2014), we study a utility maximization problem with expectation constraint. We first consider a uniformly elliptic case in which the endogenous state boundary associated with the constraint in expectation is proved to be smooth. This allows one to derive a proper Dirichlet condition for the value function of the optimal control problem on this boundary. We then propose a new truncation argument in the martingale representation of the expectation constraint. This leads to an approximating sequence of auxiliary systems of PDEs for which comparison holds. Convergence to the initial optimal control problem is proved. In the degenerate case, we propose another approximation which consists in adding a small noise term to recover uniformly ellipticity. Convergence is also proved. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first time that a full analysis is performed for such control problems, so as to open the doors to the use of numerical schemes. Numerical resolution in a toy example is performed using neural networks. It is complemented by an estimation of the numerical error, also performed by using a neural network approach.

</details>


### [232] [On the hardness of deterministic second-order optimization of functions with Lipschitz gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24120)
*Jiewen Guan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了确定性零尊重算法（以及一般确定性算法）在有限次（或不超过n-3次，n为输入维度）二阶预言机调用内，无法计算具有Lipschitz连续梯度的Goldstein近似二阶稳定点，从而表明确定性二阶弱凸优化是难解的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确定性算法在计算Goldstein近似二阶稳定点时的理论极限，特别是针对具有Lipschitz连续梯度的弱凸函数，揭示确定性二阶优化的固有困难。

Method: 通过构造反例和复杂度下界证明，分别针对零尊重算法和一般确定性算法，证明其所需二阶预言机调用次数的下界。

Result: 确定性零尊重算法无法在有限次二阶调用内找到Goldstein近似二阶稳定点；一般确定性算法至少需要n-2次二阶调用。

Conclusion: 确定性二阶弱凸优化本质上是难解的，需要随机性或更高阶信息来克服这一障碍。

Abstract: We show that no deterministic zero-respecting algorithm (resp., (general) deterministic algorithm) can compute Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of functions with Lipschitz continuous gradients within a finite number of (resp., no more than $n-3$ with $n$ being the input dimension) second-order oracle calls. This, among other consequences, shows that deterministic second-order weakly convex optimization is intractable.

</details>


### [233] [On computing Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of structured nonsmooth nonconvex programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24122)
*Jiewen Guan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机一阶算法，利用随机平滑工具计算L-光滑函数的Goldstein近似二阶驻点，其Oracle复杂度为Õ(n²/ε⁹ + n³/ε⁷)，并扩展到了弱凸函数及双层优化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维优化问题中，寻找二阶驻点（即同时满足一阶和二阶最优性条件）通常需要计算Hessian矩阵，计算成本高昂。本文旨在利用随机一阶方法（无需显式计算Hessian）高效地逼近二阶驻点，降低计算复杂度。

Method: 采用随机平滑（randomized smoothing）技术，将原始非光滑或非凸函数平滑化，结合随机一阶梯度信息来近似Hessian信息，从而构造Goldstein近似二阶驻点的判定和搜索条件。算法通过精心设计的采样策略平衡了维度n和精度ε对复杂度的影响。

Result: 对于L-光滑函数，算法达到Goldstein近似二阶驻点的Oracle复杂度为Õ(n²/ε⁹ + n³/ε⁷)。该复杂度在维度n和精度ε的依赖关系上提供了理论保证。同时，方法可扩展至弱凸函数，并成功应用于双层优化问题。

Conclusion: 本文证明了仅使用随机一阶信息即可有效逼近二阶驻点，为无需Hessian计算的高维非凸优化提供了新思路。其扩展至弱凸和双层优化的结果表明该方法具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we exhibit a randomized first-order algorithm to compute Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of $L$-smooth functions, using tools from randomized smoothing. The algorithm has oracle complexity $\widetilde{O}({ n^2}/{\varepsilon^9}+{ n^3}/{\varepsilon^7})$, where $n=1,2,\ldots$ is the input dimension and $\varepsilon>0$ is the (common) tolerance. We also present extensions to weakly convex functions and applications to bilevel optimization.

</details>


### [234] [Optimal Rank Selection for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization via $\ell_{2,0}$-Norm Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24193)
*Yulin He,Ran Gu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于列ℓ₂,₀范数正则化的非负矩阵分解秩选择方法，通过惯性邻近交替线性最小化算法和邻近有效集方法，在过估计的分解维度上逐步消除冗余成分，实现数据驱动的秩估计，并提供了收敛性分析和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非负矩阵分解中确定最优分解秩是一个基础但极具挑战性的问题，传统方法依赖启发式阈值或计算昂贵的交叉验证，缺乏系统且高效的数据驱动解决方案。

Method: 在两个因子矩阵上引入列ℓ₂,₀范数正则化以促进秩降低；从过估计的分解维度开始，采用热启动的λ路径逐步消除冗余成分；提出了惯性邻近交替线性最小化方法及其尺度平衡变体，以及基于P-稳定性的邻近有效集方法；尺度平衡策略通过保持重构矩阵的同时控制因子列之间的数值不平衡。

Result: 在合成数据集和基准数据集上的数值实验表明，与奇异值硬阈值和交叉验证相比，所提框架在秩估计准确性和计算效率上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 建立了模型恢复潜在非负秩的条件，基于Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质给出了整个序列收敛到临界点的收敛性分析，证明了所提方法在算法条件下的理论保证。

Abstract: Determining the optimal factorization rank is a fundamental yet notoriously challenging problem in Nonnegative Matrix Factorization, conventionally relying on heuristic thresholds or computationally expensive cross-validation. We introduce column $\ell_{2,0}$-norm regularization on both factor matrices to promote rank reduction. Starting from an overestimated factorization dimension, a warm-started $λ$-path is employed to progressively eliminate redundant components and produce a data-driven rank estimate. For the resulting nonconvex and discontinuous optimization problem, we develop an inertial Proximal Alternating Linearized Minimization method, its scale-balanced variant, and a Proximal Active-Set Method based on P-stationarity. The scale-balancing strategy controls numerical imbalance between paired factor columns while preserving the reconstructed matrix. We establish conditions under which the proposed model recovers the underlying nonnegative rank and provide a Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz-based convergence analysis showing that, under the stated algorithmic conditions, the entire sequences generated by the proposed methods converge to critical points. Numerical experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets, together with comparisons against Singular Value Hard Thresholding and cross-validation, show that the proposed framework provides favorable rank-estimation accuracy and computational efficiency in the tested settings.

</details>


### [235] [A Nonlinear CTH-Based Adaptive Cruise Controller With Safety and String Stability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24228)
*Dionysios Theodosis,Amirhossein Samii,Nikolaos Bekiaris-Liberis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对异构车辆队列的纵向控制问题，提出了一种基于恒定时间间隔（CTH）策略的非线性自适应巡航控制器，该控制器通过速度依赖非线性增益和饱和型非线性保证车速与加速度有界，同时保持碰撞避免特性。通过引入合适的间距误差变量，建立了显式非线性误差动力学模型，证明了无碰撞区域的正不变性及间距误差的收敛性，并直接从闭环动力学中推导了L_p弦稳定性（1≤p≤∞）的充分条件，无需线性化或频域分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆队列控制方法在保证安全性（碰撞避免）和弦稳定性方面存在局限，尤其是面对异构车辆和实际非线性约束（如车速和加速度有界）时，传统基于线性化或频域分析的方法难以直接给出严格保证。因此需要一种非线性的控制框架，能够同时处理安全性、有界性约束和弦稳定性。

Method: 提出一种非线性自适应巡航控制器，包含：(1) 基于CTH策略的间距策略；(2) 速度依赖非线性增益以限制车速；(3) 饱和型非线性以限制加速度。通过定义新的间距误差变量，推导显式非线性误差动力学，并利用正不变集理论分析碰撞避免性质，直接基于时域非线性动力学推导L_p弦稳定性条件。

Result: 证明了无碰撞区域是正不变的，控制器保证间距误差收敛；建立了非线性弦稳定性性质，给出了L_p弦稳定性（1≤p≤∞）的充分条件，该条件针对扰动、前车外部输入和初始条件扰动均成立。

Conclusion: 所设计的非线性控制器能够在不依赖线性化或频域方法的前提下，为异构车辆队列同时提供碰撞避免保证和弦稳定性保证，为实际车辆队列安全控制提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of longitudinal control, with safety and string stability guarantees, in vehicle platoons. A nonlinear adaptive cruise controller based on the Constant Time Headway (CTH) policy is designed for platoons of heterogeneous vehicles, that incorporates a speed-dependent nonlinear gain guaranteeing bounded vehicle speeds and a saturation-type nonlinearity to guarantee bounded (or non-aggressive) accelerations, while preserving collision-avoidance properties of time-headway-based spacing policies. By introducing a suitable spacing-error variable, explicit nonlinear error dynamics are obtained, allowing a direct analysis of the closed-loop system. It is shown that the collision-free region is positively invariant and that the proposed controller guarantees convergence of the spacing errors. Furthermore, nonlinear string-stability properties are established directly from the closed-loop dynamics without resorting to linearization or frequency-domain arguments. In particular, sufficient conditions are derived for $L_p$ string stability, $1\le p\le\infty$, with respect to disturbances, external inputs acting on the leading vehicle, and perturbations of the initial conditions.

</details>


### [236] [Optimal Convex Optimization with Inexact Second-Order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24520)
*Lesi Chen,Chengchang Liu,Luo Luo,John C. S. Lui,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为加速非精确牛顿外梯度法（AINE）的二阶凸优化方法，可在使用δ-非精确Hessian矩阵的情况下分别达到O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_2/ε)^{2/7})和O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_3/ε)^{1/5})的oracle复杂度，且每轮迭代运行时间与矩阵乘法相当（对数因子内），并建立了匹配的复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶优化方法在处理非精确Hessian信息时，其oracle复杂度与精度的关系尚未达到最优，且缺少匹配的理论下界。本文旨在设计一种在非精确Hessian条件下具有最优复杂度保证的加速二阶方法。

Method: 提出AINE算法，将加速梯度方法与牛顿外梯度框架相结合，利用δ-非精确Hessian构造搜索方向，并在每次迭代中通过高效矩阵运算实现。方法分别考虑了Hessian的L₂-Lipschitz连续和Hessian导数的L₃-Lipschitz连续两种情形。

Result: 在Hessian L₂-Lipschitz连续下达到O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_2/ε)^{2/7})的ISO复杂度；在三阶导数L₃-Lipschitz连续下达到O((δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_3/ε)^{1/5})的更好复杂度。同时建立了两种设置下的匹配oracle复杂度下界，证明了方法的渐近最优性。

Conclusion: AINE是非精确二阶凸优化中首个达到最优复杂度的加速方法，且每次迭代的计算成本与矩阵乘法同阶，具有理论最优性和实际可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel second-order method called Accelerated Inexact Newton Extragradient (AINE) for convex optimization using $δ$-inexact Hessians. We show that AINE can find an $ε$-solution in the inexact second-order oracle (ISO) complexity of $\mathcal{O}( (δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_2/ε)^{2/7} )$ when the Hessian is $L_2$-Lipschitz continuous, and a better complexity of $\mathcal{O}( (δ/ε)^{1/2} + (L_3/ε)^{1/5} )$ when the third-order derivative is $L_3$-Lipschitz continuous. Notably, each iteration of our method can be conducted in the same running time as matrix multiplication up to logarithmic factors. In addition, we also establish matching oracle complexity lower bounds for both setups, demonstrating the optimality of our methods.

</details>


### [237] [Using Subproblem Objective Gaps in Inexact Augmented Lagrangian and ADMM Algorithms, with Applications to Stochastic Mixed Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24535)
*Jonathan Eckstein*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过“部分强凸性”引理，展示了如何将子问题目标值次优性边界用于不精确增广拉格朗日方法和ADMM算法。ADMM结果对长期存在的子问题近似求解标准进行了重要改进。这些成果为计算随机混合整数规划问题最优值的拉格朗日边界提供了两种新方法，且收敛分析比现有技术更简洁。每种方法中，子问题均由经典Frank-Wolfe算法变体求解，但与同类方法相比，Frank-Wolfe变体的选择更加灵活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为随机混合整数规划问题提供更高效、更灵活的拉格朗日边界计算方法，简化收敛分析，并放松对子问题求解算法的限制。

Method: 提出“部分强凸性”引理，将子问题目标值次优性边界应用于不精确增广拉格朗日方法和ADMM算法；对ADMM子问题近似求解标准进行精细化改进；采用Frank-Wolfe算法变体求解子问题。

Result: 给出了两种计算随机混合整数规划问题拉格朗日边界的新方法；收敛分析比现有技术更简洁；Frank-Wolfe变体的选择比以往方法更自由。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在保证收敛性的同时，显著提升了算法灵活性和分析简洁性，适用于求解随机混合整数规划问题。

Abstract: Through a "partial strong convexity" lemma, this paper shows how bounds on subproblem objective value suboptimality can be used in inexact augmented Lagrangian methods and ADMM algorithms. The ADMM result uses a small but important refinement on a long-standing criterion for approximately solving subproblems. The results enable two new approaches to computing Lagrangian bounds on the optimal values of stochastic mixed-integer programming problems, with simpler convergence analysis than the prior state of the art. In each case, the subproblems are solved by variants of the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm. However, as compared to prior methods of the same type, there is much more freedom in the choice of Frank-Wolfe variant.

</details>


### [238] [Communication Security via Temporal Dependency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24578)
*Mohsen Abedi,Ahmed Badawy,Amr Mohamed*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用时间依赖性作为安全资源的新型通信安全范式，通过状态链式随机线性网络编码，使原始包与虚假包计算不可区分，导致窃听者持续失同步，在无密钥、无信道优势等最坏条件下实现亚秒级失同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信安全依赖共享密钥或通信优势，但在无基础设施、紧急通信、高动态网络等场景中，这两种资源均无法保证，亟需新的安全通信原理。

Method: 提出状态链式随机线性网络编码（state-chained RLNC）框架：将解码每个传输块所需的同步状态嵌入前一个块中，使连续传输之间有意耦合，未来通信的正确解读依赖于对先前传输的正确解读；同步错误会级联传播，导致窃听者持续失同步。

Result: 在最坏对抗模型下（无信道优势、无密钥假设、完全协议知识、任意更强的窃听者），数值结果显示可实现亚秒级窃听者失同步。同时分析了窃听者失同步的概率与持久性，以及重新同步的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 时间依赖可作为新的安全资源，在缺乏传统安全资源的场景中提供有效通信安全保障。

Abstract: Communication security has traditionally been built upon one of two external resources: shared secret keys or a communication advantage over the eavesdropper. However, many practical wireless scenarios, including infrastructure-less, emergency, and highly dynamic networks, cannot guarantee either resource, motivating the need for a new communication security principle. This paper introduces a new communication security paradigm that exploits temporal dependency as a security resource. Unlike conventional secrecy techniques that prevent an eavesdropper from recovering transmitted bits, the proposed paradigm allows packet decoding but prevents correct interpretation by making original and dummy packets computationally indistinguishable. Rather than protecting individual transmissions, successive transmissions are intentionally coupled so that future communication depends on correctly interpreting previous ones. As one realization, we develop a state-chained random linear network coding (RLNC) framework in which the synchronization state required to interpret each transmission block is embedded in the previous block. Therefore, synchronization failures propagate across future transmissions, resulting in persistent eavesdropper asynchronization. We analytically characterize the probability and persistence of eavesdropper asynchronization, together with the computational complexity of resynchronization, and develop transmission strategies based on transmit power and intentional-interference optimization. Numerical results demonstrate sub-second eavesdropper asynchronization under a worst- case adversarial model with no channel advantage, no secret assumptions, complete protocol knowledge, and an arbitrarily stronger eavesdropper.

</details>


### [239] [Fixed-time stabilization of systems with nilpotent matrices in the presence of large unknown delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24595)
*Kang-Kang Zhang,Emilia Fridman,Pengfei Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有幂零矩阵的连续时间受扰线性系统在未知大常数输入/测量时滞（已知上界）下的状态反馈固定时间镇定问题。通过引入额外的人为时滞，设计了反馈控制器，使系统状态在固定时间内归零，并在加性扰动、小系统矩阵摄动和非线性存在时实现输入-状态稳定性及大指数衰减率。对于受扰单积分器，在快变未知测量时滞下实现了固定时间镇定。结果还扩展到了离散时间系统。数值仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定时间控制理论通常假设系统无时滞或时滞已知，而实际系统中时滞往往未知且有界。本文旨在解决具有未知大常数时滞（输入/测量）的幂零矩阵线性系统的固定时间镇定问题，填补该理论空白。

Method: 通过引入额外的人为时滞来补偿未知时滞的影响，设计状态反馈控制器。利用幂零矩阵的性质和固定时间控制理论，结合输入-状态稳定性分析，证明闭环系统在固定时间内收敛，并对扰动、摄动和非线性具有鲁棒性。对于快变测量时滞的单积分器，采用特殊处理策略；同时将方法推广到离散时间系统。

Result: ① 对于精确已知的幂零矩阵线性系统，所设计的反馈使状态在大于未知时滞上界的固定时间内镇定到零。② 在加性扰动、小矩阵摄动和非线性下，系统具有输入-状态稳定性且指数衰减率大。③ 单积分器在快变未知测量时滞下实现固定时间镇定。④ 结果成功推广到离散时间系统。⑤ 数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效解决了具有未知大常数时滞的幂零矩阵线性系统的固定时间镇定问题，通过人为时滞技巧增强了系统对时滞不确定性和扰动的鲁棒性，并拓展了固定时间控制理论的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we study state-feedback fixed-time stabilization of continuous-time perturbed linear systems with nilpotent matrices in the presence of unknown large constant input/measurement delays with known bounds. By using an additional artificial delay, we design a feedback that for a precisely known linear system with a nilpotent matrix drives the state to zero in fixed time (which is larger than the bound of the unknown delay) and achieves input-to-state stability with a large exponential decay rate in the presence of additive disturbances and small system matrix perturbations and nonlinearities. For perturbed single integrators, fixed-time stabilization is achieved in the case of fast-varying unknown measurement delays with known bounds. The results are extended to discrete-time systems. Numerical simulations illustrate the efficiency of the results.

</details>


### [240] [Long-time Stability and Convergence of Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24696)
*Giacomo Borghi,Hui Huang,Dohyeon Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过连接经典PSO稳定性分析与平均场方法，为时间离散的PSO算法提供了新的定量估计。研究了一个无记忆、带噪声底限的正则化PSO模型，通过线性化动力学的Schur稳定性分析、非线性平均场系统的Laplace原理分析，以及建立阶为N^{-1/2}的平均场近似定量误差界，确定了动力学稳定并收敛到全局极小值邻域的定量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSO算法虽然启发式动机明确且广泛应用，但由于其动力学的二阶、随机且高度非线性特性，理论分析仍然有限。本文旨在通过连接经典稳定性分析与平均场方法，弥补PSO理论分析的不足，为该算法提供更严格的定量理解。

Method: 本文提出一个无记忆、带非退化噪声底限的正则化PSO模型作为替代模型。采用三阶段分析方法：首先研究线性化动力学的Schur稳定性；其次通过Laplace原理分析非线性平均场系统的收敛性质；最后建立平均场近似的定量误差界。

Result: 确定了动力学稳定并收敛到全局极小值邻域的定量条件；建立了阶为N^{-1/2}的平均场近似误差界，其中N为粒子数量。

Conclusion: 通过正则化PSO模型的分析，本文为PSO算法的稳定性与收敛性提供了新的定量理论保证，平均场近似误差随粒子数增加以N^{-1/2}速率衰减，为实际应用中的参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) is a global optimization algorithm defined by an interacting set of particles evolving over the search space. Heuristically motivated, its theoretical analysis remains limited due to the second-order, stochastic, and highly nonlinear nature of the dynamics. In this paper, we connect classical PSO stability analysis under the stagnation assumption with more recent mean-field methods, providing new quantitative estimates for the time-discrete algorithm. We study in particular a regularized PSO model without memory, with non-degenerate noise by adding a noise floor to the original model. Studying such a surrogate model allows us to identify quantitative conditions under which the dynamics is stable and converges toward a small neighborhood of a global minimizer. We do so by first studying the Schur stability of the linearized dynamics, then analyzing the convergence properties of a nonlinear mean-field system via a Laplace principle, and finally establishing a quantitative error bound for the mean-field approximation of order $N^{-1/2}$.

</details>

### [241] [Continuous-Time Random Walk Description of Anomalous Spin Transport in Dilute Dipolar Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22626)
*Cooper M. Selco,Christian Bengs,Ashok Ajoy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过连续时间随机游走（CTRW）模型研究天然丰度钻石（1.1% $^{13}$C）中核自旋扩散的异常输运现象，发现无序平均后的动力学呈现亚扩散行为（均方位移指数γ=0.56, δ=0.87），并追溯其微观起源为几何俘获效应：强耦合团簇（如二聚体）内快速交换，弱团簇间连接控制长程探索。动力学逾渗构建揭示了与截断时间（~19 s）一致的团簇穿越时间，并将顺磁杂质映射为硬球陷阱来复现实验弛豫时间尺度，表明稀薄偶极自旋网络需要超越菲克扩散方程的微观网络解析描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核自旋扩散通常被简化为单个扩散系数，但这种粗粒化描述在稀薄固体中可能失效，因为位置无序和长程偶极耦合会产生广泛的跳跃率分布。本文旨在建立微观解析的输运描述以捕捉这些异常行为。

Method: 构建基于偶极介导的翻转-翻转耦合的速率矩阵，采样精确连续时间轨迹，发展CTRW描述；采用动力学逾渗构造将全局输运与团簇间穿越时间联系起来；将顺磁杂质映射为硬球陷阱。

Result: 等待时间分布呈重尾（指数α=0.64，指数截断t_cutoff=19 s）；平均跳跃长度与等待时间相关（τ≳0.1 s）；均方位移在步数和时间上均呈亚线性增长（γ=0.56, δ=0.87）；识别出几何俘获为微观起源；CTRW框架可复现实验弛豫时间尺度，而连续扩散方程不能。

Conclusion: 稀薄偶极自旋网络需要超越菲克扩散方程的微观、网络解析的输运描述。

Abstract: Nuclear spin diffusion is often summarized by a single diffusion coefficient, but this coarse-grained description can fail in dilute solids where positional disorder and long-range dipolar couplings generate a broad distribution of hopping rates. We develop a continuous-time random-walk (CTRW) description of $^{13}$C polarization transport in natural-abundance diamond (1.1%), constructing the rate matrix from dipolar-mediated flip-flop couplings and sampling exact continuous-time trajectories. Although site-to-site hopping is Markovian, the disorder-averaged dynamics give rise to emergent, anomalous transport. The empirical waiting-time distribution exhibits a heavy tail with exponent $α=0.64$ and exponential cutoff $t_{\rm cutoff}=19$ s; the mean jump length becomes correlated with the waiting time $τ$ for $τ\gtrsim0.1$ s; and the mean-squared displacement grows sublinearly in both step number and physical time, with exponents $γ=0.56$ and $δ=0.87$ respectively. We trace the microscopic origin of these signatures to geometric trapping: polarization can rapidly exchange within strongly coupled clusters, including dimers, while weak inter-cluster links control long-range exploration. A kinetic percolation construction links global transport to inter-cluster crossing times, and identifies a corresponding crossing time of $\sim20$ s, consistent with $t_{\rm cutoff}$. Finally, mapping paramagnetic impurities onto hard-sphere traps connects the CTRW framework to classic studies of trapping in reaction-diffusion theory and reproduces the qualitative timescale of experimentally measured relaxation, whereas a continuum diffusion equation description does not. These results show that dilute dipolar spin networks require a microscopic, network-resolved transport description beyond the Fickian diffusion equation.

</details>


### [242] [Statistical Mechanics of Thermal Diffusion in Rough Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22742)
*Soham Chattopadhyay,Blas P. Uberuaga*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从质量输运的变分原理出发，提出了一个描述复杂固体中热扩散的广泛适用的统计框架，揭示了微观热力学和动力学涨落如何支配非阿伦尼乌斯扩散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散理论大多基于阿伦尼乌斯公式，难以描述复杂固体中的非阿伦尼乌斯扩散行为，缺乏一个统一的统计框架来同时解释热力学和动力学因素对扩散的影响。

Method: 从质量输运的变分原理出发，构建了一个统计框架。其中动力学项通过机器学习可学习的相对扩散贡献来描述，将微观热力学和动力学涨落纳入考虑。

Result: 揭示了非阿伦尼乌斯扩散的微观机制：在固溶体中的空位扩散过程中，当有序化效应开始变得重要时会出现偏离阿伦尼乌斯行为；而在较高温度下，由于构型熵和竞争性能量涨落的共同作用，近似阿伦尼乌斯行为可以恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架广泛适用于复杂固体中的热扩散分析，阐明了微观涨落如何决定非阿伦尼乌斯扩散行为，为理解和预测复杂材料中的扩散现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Starting from a variational principle for mass transport, we present a broadly applicable statistical framework describing thermal diffusion in complex solids. We show that microscopic thermodynamic and kinetic fluctuations govern non-Arrhenius diffusion, with kinetic terms described by machine-learnable relative diffusion contributions. For vacancy diffusion in solid solutions, deviation from Arrheniusness may occur when ordering effects start to become important but at higher temperatures, approximately Arrhenius behavior can occur, aided by configurational entropy and competing energy fluctuations.

</details>


### [243] [Response Morphologies of a Canonical Fluctuation Diagnostic Across Ehrenfest Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22978)
*Fangfang Wang,Wei Liu,Ying Tang,Zengru Di*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种基于能量涨落不对称性的正则系综诊断方法，用于探测相变的涨落级行为。通过在八态Potts模型（一级相变）、二维Ising模型（二级相变）和理想三维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（三级相变）中的检验，发现该诊断量在不同相变类型中呈现截然不同的响应形态：Potts和Ising体系在转变区附近呈现稳健的配对极值结构，而BEC在热力学极限下呈现不连续响应。该诊断量能反映相变本质的差异性，但不能直接作为Ehrenfest分类的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前微正则系综的拐点分析（MIPA）可用于识别高阶相变结构，但缺乏直接从可测量的能量涨落中探测相应涨落级行为的正则系综方法。前人工作虽引入了基于能量涨落不对称性的正则诊断量，但该诊断量在不同阶相变中的行为特征尚不清楚。

Method: 采用正则系综的涨落不对称性诊断量，分别对八态Potts模型（一级相变）、二维Ising模型（二级相变）和理想三维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（三级相变）进行数值模拟与分析，观察该诊断量在不同Ehrenfest分类相变中的响应形态。

Result: Potts模型和Ising模型在相变区附近呈现稳健的配对极值结构；理想Bose-Einstein凝聚在热力学极限下的凝聚温度处呈现不连续响应。该诊断量在不同热力学体系中展现出定性不同的响应形态。

Conclusion: 该基于涨落的诊断量能够反映相变本质的差异，对相变类型具有敏感性，但不能作为直接判定Ehrenfest阶数的分类器。

Abstract: Microcanonical inflection-point analysis identifies MIPA-type higher-order transition structures from derivatives of the microcanonical entropy. Until recently, however, there was no canonical formulation for probing the corresponding fluctuation-level behavior directly from measurable energy fluctuations without reconstructing the density of states. Previous work addressed this limitation by introducing a canonical fluctuation diagnostic that quantifies energy-fluctuation asymmetry. Here, we investigate how this diagnostic behaves across representative first-, second-, and third-order phase transitions in the Ehrenfest classification. We consider the eight-state Potts model, the two-dimensional Ising model, and ideal three-dimensional Bose--Einstein condensation. The Potts and Ising systems exhibit a robust paired-extremum structure of the diagnostic near their respective transition regions, whereas ideal Bose--Einstein condensation exhibits a discontinuous response at the condensation temperature in the thermodynamic limit. These results show that a single fluctuation-based observable can display qualitatively distinct response morphologies across thermodynamic regimes, reflecting sensitivity to the nature of the underlying phase transition rather than serving as a direct classifier of Ehrenfest order.

</details>


### [244] [Quantum fluctuation-driven transport crossover between two liquid states in distinguishable helium-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23127)
*Mika Tanabe,Momoko Tsujimoto,Kenichi Kinugawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过路径积分质心分子动力学模拟，展示了可区分氦-4中由量子涨落驱动的两种液态（低量子色散液体LQDL和高量子色散液体HQDL）之间的输运交叉。LQDL符合斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系，而HQDL在更低温度下表现出反常的气体状输运（超扩散、超低粘度），伴随斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系失效。这种冷却时出现气体状动力学的反直觉现象反映了核量子涨落的主导作用。跨LQDL-HQDL边界识别出输运交叉，表现为速度自相关函数定性变化、普朗特数转变以及多种输运系数的最小值。振荡型到单调型VAF的转变定义了第二Frenkel线。LQDL是热输运主导的耗散流体，HQDL是动量主导的惯性流体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索核量子涨落（而非原子交换或超流性）是否能单独驱动液态输运行为的根本性变化，理解量子波动在低温高压条件下对经典液体输运理论（如斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系）的修正作用。

Method: 采用路径积分质心分子动力学（PICMD）模拟，在0.1-3.3 K、1-60 bar范围内对服从玻尔兹曼统计的可区分氦-4进行模拟，分析速度自相关函数（VAF）、扩散系数、粘度、热导率等输运性质。

Result: 发现两种液态：LQDL（高温区）遵循斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系，显示常规液体行为；HQDL（低温区）出现超扩散、超低粘度的气体状输运，斯托克斯-爱因斯坦关系失效。在LQDL-HQDL边界观察到输运交叉：VAF从振荡型变为单调型（定义第二Frenkel线），普朗特数转变，剪切粘度、运动粘度、热导率、热扩散率均出现最小值。这些最小值不同于超临界区已知的通用输运最小值，出现在低温亚临界区域。

Conclusion: 核量子涨落本身足以诱导出气体状液体行为，而不需要超流性或原子交换。LQDL为热输运主导的耗散流体，HQDL为动量主导的惯性流体。这一发现为可区分氦-4中的输运现象提供了统一图像，并建立了区别于超临界Frenkel线的第二Frenkel线。

Abstract: We show the emergence of a quantum fluctuation-driven transport crossover between two liquid states in distinguishable helium-4 obeying Boltzmann statistics, in the absence of atomic exchange. Using path integral centroid molecular dynamics simulations over 0.1-3.3 K and 1-60 bar, we investigate the transport properties of two distinct liquid states: the low quantum-dispersion liquid (LQDL) and the high quantum-dispersion liquid (HQDL). While LQDL exhibits conventional liquid behavior consistent with the Stokes-Einstein (SE) relation, HQDL emerges at lower temperatures and displays anomalous gas-like transport characterized by superdiffusion and ultralow viscosity, accompanied by a breakdown of the SE relation. This counterintuitive emergence of gas-like dynamics upon cooling reflects the dominant role of nuclear quantum fluctuations, in contrast to thermal fluctuations at higher temperatures. Across the LQDL-HQDL boundary, we identify a transport crossover marked by a qualitative change in the velocity autocorrelation function (VAF), a transition in the Prandtl number, and the emergence of transport minima in shear and kinematic viscosities, thermal conductivity, and thermal diffusivity. These minima reflect a crossover from liquid-like to gas-like transport upon cooling in the low-temperature subcritical region, in addition to the universal transport minima observed in the supercritical regime. The transition from oscillatory to monotonic VAF defines a second Frenkel line, distinct from the conventional Frenkel line observed in the supercritical region. LQDL is a heat-transport-dominated dissipative fluid, whereas HQDL is a momentum-dominated inertial fluid. These results demonstrate that nuclear quantum fluctuations alone induce gas-like liquid behavior and provide a unified picture of transport phenomena in distinguishable helium-4 without superfluidity.

</details>


### [245] [Chiral Dynamics of an Intruder across Dilute and Hydrodynamic Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23221)
*Raphaël Maire,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个解析可解的模型，研究任意形状的侵入者在非平衡手性浴中的动力学，揭示了在稀薄和稠密极限下，侵入者几何形状如何通过不同机制（稀薄时来自Boltzmann-Lorentz方程描述的棘轮效应和奇异性响应，稠密时来自包含手性扭矩密度的Stokes方程描述的水动力学模式和边缘流）产生手性输运和奇异性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对手性输运的理解多局限于特定条件，缺乏统一框架来阐明侵入者几何形状如何在不同浓度极限下影响手性响应和奇异输运行为。

Method: 构建解析可解模型，在稀薄极限下基于Boltzmann-Lorentz方程推导Langevin描述，在稠密极限下利用包含手性扭矩密度的Stokes方程分析水动力学模式，并通过数值模拟验证。

Result: 发现侵入者几何形状在稀薄极限中通过棘轮效应控制手性输运，在稠密极限中通过手性扭矩密度驱动的水动力学模式主导边缘流和奇异响应。

Conclusion: 手性输运与侵入者形状密切相关，奇异性响应在稀薄和稠密极限下源自截然不同的物理机制。

Abstract: We introduce and simulate an analytically tractable model for an intruder of arbitrary shape in a nonequilibrium bath, with chirality originating from the bath, the intruder, or their coupling. In the dilute regime, a Langevin description derived from a Boltzmann-Lorentz equation shows how intruder geometry governs ratchet effects and odd response. In the dense regime, the dynamics of the intruder are instead governed by the hydrodynamic modes of the bath and edge currents, which are described by a Stokes equation including a chiral torque density. Our results link shape to chiral transport and show that odd response arises from distinct mechanisms in the dilute and dense limits.

</details>


### [246] [Kinetic and Hydrodynamic Theories of Chiral Intruder Dynamics in Nonequilibrium Baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23223)
*Raphaël Maire,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了浸没在非平衡浴中的入侵者在稀薄动力学区与稠密流体力学区两种极限下的手性动力学。在稀薄极限下，从Boltzmann-Lorentz描述推导出有效Langevin方程，系数由几何依赖的边界积分显式给出，分离了形状手性与相互作用手性的效应。在稠密极限下，入侵者动力学主要由浴流体力学和扭矩密度驱动的边缘电流控制，可产生反对称阻力与曲率诱导扭矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡浴中入侵者手性动力学的跨尺度机制，特别是稀薄与稠密两种极限下手性效应（形状手性vs相互作用手性）的不同表现及其物理起源。

Method: 稀薄极限：基于Boltzmann-Lorentz方程推导有效Langevin方程，系数通过几何边界积分显式表达；稠密极限：基于浴流体力学分析，考虑扭矩密度驱动的边缘电流。

Result: 稀薄极限下：手性相互作用产生奇响应和扭矩，形状手性导致棘轮效应；存在涨落-耗散类关系；某些对称性允许的耦合在稀薄区消失。稠密极限下：边缘电流产生反对称阻力和曲率诱导扭矩，计入惯性后产生反对称响应。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解非平衡浴中入侵者手性动力学的跨尺度机制提供了重要一步，阐明了稀薄与稠密两种极限下不同的主导物理过程。

Abstract: We study the chiral dynamics of an intruder immersed in a nonequilibrium bath in two complementary limits: the dilute kinetic regime and the dense hydrodynamic regime. In the dilute limit, starting from a Boltzmann-Lorentz description, we derive an effective Langevin equation whose coefficients are given explicitly by geometry-dependent boundary integrals. This formulation separates the effects of intruder-shape chirality from those of chiral intruder-bath interactions. We find that the chiral interactions generate an odd response and a torque, whereas the chirality of the intruder leads to a ratchet effect. We also show that fluctuation-dissipation-like relations exist and that certain symmetry-allowed couplings vanish in the dilute regime. In the dense regime, we argue that intruder dynamics are governed primarily by bath hydrodynamics and torque-density-driven edge currents not captured by the previous framework. These currents can generate both an antisymmetric drag and a curvature-induced torque, leading to an antisymmetric response when inertia is accounted for. Taken together, these results provide a step toward understanding the mechanisms governing the chiral dynamics of an intruder in a nonequilibrium bath across different scales.

</details>


### [247] [Reminiscences about Hans Capel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24380)
*Constantino Tsallis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文是作者为纪念Hans Willem Capel而撰写的个人回忆与感想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向Hans Willem Capel表达敬意与怀念。

Method: 以个人回忆和思考的方式进行叙述。

Result: 呈现了作者对Capel的个人回忆与感想。

Conclusion: 通过个人回忆表达对Capel的纪念。

Abstract: As an homage to the memory of Hans Willem Capel, I present some personal reminiscences and thoughts that come to my mind in this special occasion.

</details>


### [248] [The Uhlenbeck-Ford model in two dimensions: Reference system for fluid-phase free-energy calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23824)
*Samuel Cajahuaringa,Rodolfo Paula Leite,Maurice de Koning*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了Uhlenbeck-Ford (UF) 模型作为二维流体自由能计算的参考体系，精确计算了至十阶的二维维里系数，结合分子模拟构建了高精度状态方程和过剩亥姆霍兹自由能表达式，确定了流体相的热力学稳定区域（缩放参数p≲70时流体始终稳定），并通过Lennard-Jones流体的热力学积分验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维流体自由能计算建立一个高精度、适用范围明确的参考系统，从而简化复杂流体体系的热力学性质计算。

Method: 精确计算至十阶的二维维里系数，结合分子模拟数据构建状态方程和过剩亥姆霍兹自由能的数值表示；通过相图分析确定流体相的热力学稳定极限；利用热力学积分计算二维Lennard-Jones流体的自由能进行验证。

Result: 识别出固态、六角相和流体相；发现缩放参数p≲70时，流体在所有密度下均为唯一热力学稳定相；通过Lennard-Jones流体的热力学积分验证了UF模型作为参考系统的实用性。

Conclusion: UF模型可作为二维流体自由能计算的有效参考系统，尤其适用于p≲70的参数范围，为二维流体热力学性质研究提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We investigate the Uhlenbeck-Ford (UF) model as a reference system for free-energy calculations in two-dimensional (2D) fluids. The 2D virial coefficients are computed exactly up to tenth order and combined with molecular simulation data to construct highly accurate numerical representations of the equation of state and the excess Helmholtz free energy.We then determine the phase diagram of the model in order to establish the thermodynamic stability limits of the fluid phase and thereby identify the range of applicability of the UF model as a fluid reference system. In the course of this analysis, we identify the solid, hexatic, and fluid phases, and show that the fluid remains the only thermodynamically stable phase, independent of density, for scaling parameters up to $p\lesssim 70$. Finally, we demonstrate the practical applicability of the 2D UF model as a reference system through thermodynamic integration calculations of the free energy of a two-dimensional Lennard-Jones fluid.

</details>


### [249] [Thermodynamics with thermodynamic variable first-passage time. I. From stochastic trajectories to nonlinear transport equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24078)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文探索了外部非平衡热力学、非平衡统计算子方法和随机首次通过时间热力学的理论基础，证明将亚稳态随机寿命引入广义分布函数可使首次通过时间成为完全的宏观坐标，并建立了基于热力学一致性条件的输运方程封闭程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立外部非平衡热力学、非平衡统计算子方法与随机首次通过时间热力学之间的理论基础联系，解决将首次通过时间作为宏观坐标的理论依据问题。

Method: 将亚稳态随机寿命引入广义分布函数；提供微观统计力学论证；引入广义热力学势；基于热力学一致性条件建立输运方程封闭程序；利用更新理论推导非马尔可夫过程的记忆核精确数学结构。

Result: 广义Maxwell-Catteneo方程中经典弛豫时间被严格替换为平均首次通过时间，赋予系统内在的宏观非线性；推导出非马尔可夫过程输运记忆核的精确数学结构。

Conclusion: 首次通过时间可被视为完备的宏观坐标，广义热力学框架可统一描述非平衡现象，且该框架与现有方法（如更新理论）具有内在一致性。

Abstract: The theoretical foundation of combining external nonequilibrium thermodynamics, the nonequilibrium statistical operator method, and stochastic first passage time thermodynamics are explored. It is shown that including a random lifetime of a metastable state in the generalized distribution function allows the first passage time to be considered as a fully - fledged macroscopic coordinate. A microscopic justification for this approach is provided, and a generalized thermodynamic potential is introduced. A procedure for closing the transport equations based on on thermodynamic consistency conditions is developed. It is shown that in the generalized Maxwell - Catteneo equation, the classical relaxation time is strictly replaced by the mean first passage time, which imparts internal macroscopic nonlinearity to the system. Using renewal theory, the exact mathematical structure of transport memory kernels for non-Markovian processes is derived. A qualitative comparative analysis of various approaches to the thermodynamic description of nonequilibrium phenomena is presented.

</details>


### [250] [Thermodynamics with thermodynamic variable first-passage time. II. Dynamics of explosive boiling of superheated liquid, critical indices, fractional calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24094)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在随机首次通过时间热力学（TFPT）和非高斯幂律分布（Mittag-Leffler和Levy）框架下，论证了分数阶Caputo导数在流体动力学方程中的出现，并将其应用于过热水液体的爆炸沸腾问题，推导了非局域熵产生的积分公式，计算了耗散临界指数，发现了耗散由宏观物质流J(t)的完整积分历史决定，以及临界指数z严格描述了动力学灾变和Prigogine最小熵产生原理在旋节线附近的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将随机首次通过时间热力学框架与分数阶微积分结合，严格推导分数阶Caputo导数在流体动力学中的出现，并应用于解释过热水液体爆炸沸腾过程中的非平衡热力学行为，特别是耗散临界现象和Prigogine最小熵产生原理的违背。

Method: 基于随机首次通过时间热力学（TFPT）和幂律分布（Mittag-Leffler分布和Levy分布）理论框架，推导分数阶Caputo导数在流体动力学方程中的自然出现；将该框架应用于过热水液体爆炸沸腾问题，推导非局域熵产生的积分表达式，计算耗散临界指数z，并分析外部压力引入对上述参数的定性影响。

Result: 推导了非局域熵产生的积分公式，发现当前时刻的耗散严格由宏观物质流J(t)在整个演化区间内的积分历史决定；计算了耗散临界指数z，该指数精确描述了动力学灾变行为；发现了Prigogine最小熵产生原理在旋节线附近的失效。

Conclusion: 分数阶Caputo导数作为非局域记忆效应的自然数学表达，在TFPT框架下可严格导出；爆炸沸腾过程中的耗散具有完整的历史依赖性；临界指数z为动力学灾变提供了定量描述；外部压力会定性改变熵产生和耗散指数的行为；Prigogine最小熵产生原理在接近旋节线时不再成立。

Abstract: Within the framework of stochastic first-passage time thermodynamics (TFPT) and power-law distributions (Mittag-Leffler and Levy), the appearance of fractional Caputo derivatives in hydrodynamic equations is substantiated. The developed framework is applied to the problem of explosive boiling of a superheated liquid: an integral formula for nonlocal entropy production is derived and the critical index of dissipation intensity is calculated, along with qualitative changes in these parameters upon introducing external pressure. It is found that dissipation at a given instant is strictly determined by the integral history of the macroscopic matter flow J(t) over the entire evolutionary interval up to the moment of phase explosion. The calculated critical dissipation index z strictly describes the kinetic catastrophe and the violation of Prigogine's minimum entropy production principle near spinodals.

</details>


### [251] [Dynamic hysteresis in an autocatalytic reaction network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24163)
*Sheela Yadav,Jason R. Green,Moupriya Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了自催化反应网络中动态滞后的产生机制：周期性驱动与系统内在弛豫时间尺度竞争导致滞后，通过Schlögl模型量化了驱动频率、噪声强度和系统尺寸对滞后环面积的影响，并建立了滞后与Shannon熵及熵产生率的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示自催化反应网络中动态滞后的产生机制及其控制因素，为化学逻辑门和化学计算机的设计提供理论基础。

Method: 以周期性驱动的Schlögl模型为代表性双稳态化学体系，通过滞后环面积量化滞后响应强度，系统考察驱动频率、驱动振幅、涨落强度和系统尺寸对滞后环面积的影响规律。

Result: 驱动频率和涨落强度导致滞后环面积出现非单调变化（翻转），而驱动振幅和系统尺寸则引起单调变化。滞后的浓度响应与Shannon熵及总熵产生率存在对应关系。

Conclusion: 驱动协议和涨落强度是动态滞后大小的主要控制因素；动态滞后相比随机共振适用于更广泛的外部控制条件；滞后与信息论和随机热力学不可逆性度量相关联，对化学逻辑门和化学计算机具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Here we show that an autocatalytic reaction network can exhibit dynamic hysteresis as a result of the competition between its intrinsic relaxation time scale and that of the periodic drive. The autocatalytic reaction steps generate bistability in the concentration of the autocatalytic species, and periodic pumping of the product species provides the external drive. Hysteresis arises from the lag between the concentration response of the autocatalytic species and the external periodic drive. The resulting hysteresis-loop area quantifies the extent of the system's hysteretic response. Using the periodically driven Schlögl model as a representative bistable chemical system, we use this loop area to determine how the magnitude of the hysteretic response is controlled by the driving protocol, intrinsic fluctuations, and the size of the system. Varying the driving frequency and the strength of the fluctuations causes a turnover of the hysteresis loop area, whereas the area changes monotonically with driving amplitude and system size. These trends identify the driving protocol and fluctuation strength as primary controls on the magnitude of dynamic hysteresis. In contrast to stochastic resonance, which is typically associated with weak periodic forcing and noise-assisted amplification, dynamic hysteresis can characterize the reaction-system response over a wider range of external control conditions. We further show that the delayed concentration response is mirrored in Shannon entropy and in the total entropy production rate, connecting dynamic hysteresis to information-theoretic and stochastic-thermodynamic measures of irreversibility. Overall, we identify and interpret the role of the controlling factors in chemical dynamic hysteresis and suggest implications for efficient chemical logic gates and eventually, chemical computers.

</details>


### [252] [Non-Reciprocal yet Equilibrium Critical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24252)
*Emir Sezik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过场论重整化群分析，研究了两个具有U(n)对称性的非互易耦合n矢量序参量的动力学。在ε=4-d的最低阶展开下，发现非互易耦合是RG无关的，临界行为由具有2n个矢量分量的Model A平衡普适类控制，表明非互易性本身可能不足以诱导新的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用在非平衡和生命系统中广泛应用，但其是否能诱导新的普适类以及在何种条件下成立仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 对两个非互易耦合的n矢量序参量（具有U(n)对称性）进行场论重整化群分析，在ε=4-d的最低阶展开下研究其临界行为。

Result: 在ε=4-d的最低阶下，非互易耦合是RG无关的（irrelevant），临界行为由Model A平衡普适类（2n个矢量分量）的平衡固定点控制。

Conclusion: 尽管体系经历的是非平衡相变，但非互易性本身可能不足以诱导新的普适类。

Abstract: Non-reciprocal interactions find broad applicability in non-equilibrium and living systems. Their canonical implementation involves asymmetric couplings between two entities, which generally induce spatio-temporal patterns and time-dependent steady states that break time-translational invariance, representing a clear deviation from equilibrium physics. Although their phenomenology is well understood, whether non-reciprocal interactions induce new universality classes, and if so, under what conditions, remains an open question. In the present work, we perform a field-theoretic renormalization group (RG) analysis of the dynamics of two non-reciprocally coupled $n$-vector order parameters possessing a $U(n)$ symmetry, generalizing previous results to order parameters with multiple components, a feature that has been shown to generate novel non-equilibrium critical behavior in certain non-reciprocal systems. To lowest order in $ε= 4-d$, we find that the non-reciprocal coupling is RG-irrelevant, and the critical behavior is governed by the equilibrium fixed point of the Model A universality class of Hohenberg and Halperin with $2n$ vector components, even though the transition is into a non-equilibrium state. Our results demonstrate that non-reciprocity alone may not be sufficient to induce novel universality classes.

</details>


### [253] [Amortized Posteriors for Estimation of Material Constitutive Parameters from Multimodal Measurements on Small Punch Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24534)
*Mohammad Ali Seyed Mahmoud,Aditya Venkatraman,Raj Mahat,Samantha Mitra,Surya R. Kalidindi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无似然框架，结合高斯过程代理与条件流匹配，从多模态力学测试数据中推断材料本构参数，避免了传统MCMC采样中手工构建似然函数的困难，并通过小冲杆试验与DIC数据的融合验证了该方法在后验收缩和参数辨识精度上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯标定材料本构参数时，多模态力学测试数据在维度、噪声结构和物理单位上存在差异，导致联合似然难以构建，且后验分布宽泛或强相关，标准MCMC采样器混合性能差。

Method: 提出一个分阶段的无似然框架：首先构建高斯过程代理模型替代物理仿真，然后使用条件流匹配学习从多模态观测到本构参数的条件后验分布，无需显式似然函数，训练后即可快速生成后验样本。

Result: 仅用力-位移曲线数据时后验较宽；加入DIC位移场数据后后验显著收缩并向独立测量的拉伸参考值偏移，证明了多模态数据融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为多模态力学数据的材料本构参数推断提供了一种鲁棒的无似然方案，小冲杆试验与DIC的集成验证了其实用性。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of material constitutive parameters from multimodal mechanical test data is often limited by the need to specify a joint likelihood across measurement modalities that differ in dimensionality, noise structure, and physical units. The resulting posteriors are often broad or strongly correlated, causing standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) samplers to mix poorly. Here, we present an amortized, likelihood-free framework that combines Gaussian process (GP) surrogates with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) to learn conditional posteriors over constitutive parameters directly from synthetic multimodal parameter--observation pairs, avoiding hand-crafted likelihoods and repeated MCMC sampling. Once trained, the GP--CFM model generates posterior samples for each new specimen at negligible cost. The utility of this novel approach is demonstrated in this paper by estimating the values of Young's modulus and yield strength from the early portion of the force--displacement ($F$--$D$) curve and a Digital Image Correlation (DIC)-based displacement field measured in a Small Punch Test (SPT). It is observed that the $F$--$D$ data alone produce broad posteriors, consistent with limited parameter discrimination in the global response. Adding the DIC-measured displacement field was seen to contract the posteriors and shift them towards the independently measured tensile reference values. This work establishes a robust likelihood-free framework for the inference of material constitutive parameters from multimodal data, demonstrated through SPT--DIC integration.

</details>


### [254] [Control of morphology and topology in a lattice model of branching morphogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24619)
*Christian Hanauer,Frank Jülicher,Efe Ilker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于形态素控制的分支形态发生的晶格模型，将非平衡物理学与发育生物学的概念相结合。该模型将细胞的随机占据动力学与细胞产生的信号分子（形态素）耦合，研究了受形态素浓度梯度调控的生长模式，涵盖了从扩散受限聚集极限到随机表面生长（Eden模型）极限的多种机制。通过引入局部算子控制拓扑结构，研究了分支模式的生长、衰退和稳态动力学。拓扑保持的稳态团簇呈现回转半径与团簇尺寸的幂律标度关系，在方晶格中指数为0.68±0.01，在六角晶格中为0.67±0.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非平衡物理学与发育生物学相结合，建立形态素控制的分支形态发生的理论模型，以理解生物发育中分支模式形成的物理机制。

Method: 构建了一个晶格模型，将细胞的随机占据动力学与形态素信号分子耦合，通过形态素浓度梯度控制生长模式，并引入局部拓扑算子来控制分支结构的拓扑特性。

Result: 模型涵盖了从扩散受限聚集（DLA）极限到Eden模型极限的生长行为；拓扑保持的稳态团簇在方晶格和六角晶格中分别呈现0.68±0.01和0.67±0.01的幂律标度指数。

Conclusion: 该模型能够统一描述多种生长机制，并通过局部拓扑控制实现生长、衰退和稳态动力学，揭示了分支形态发生中的普适标度规律。

Abstract: We present a lattice model for morphogen-controlled branching morphogenesis which combines ideas and concepts from non-equilibrium physics and developmental biology. In this model, the stochastic occupation dynamics of cells is coupled with signaling molecules (morphogens) produced by the cells. We investigate growth patterns governed by morphogen concentration gradients, spanning regimes ranging from the diffusion-limited aggregation limit to the stochastic surface growth (Eden model) limit. Moreover, we introduce control over topology by a local operator and study growth, degrowth, and steady-state dynamics of branched patterns. The topology-preserving steady-state clusters exhibit a power-law scaling of radius of gyration with cluster size, yielding the exponents $0.68\pm0.01$ in square lattice and $0.67\pm 0.01$ in hexagonal lattice.

</details>


### [255] [Fluctuation theorems for autonomous work in the quantum regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24690)
*Xiu-Hua Zhao,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将经典自治功的涨落定理推广到量子领域。通过对功源和系统进行连续投影测量，推导了从初始混合热态出发的Jarzynski型和Crooks型自治包含功涨落定理。量子非对易性导致即使在大功源极限下也无法一致约化为非自治情况，而排他功定义在特定可对易条件下可恢复非自治极限。用Dicke模型进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统中自治功的涨落定理已被推广，但量子领域中由于不确定原理的存在，自治功涨落定理的推广受到约束。本文旨在将经典自治功的涨落定理拓展到量子 regime。

Method: 通过对功源和系统进行连续投影测量，从初始混合热态出发推导Jarzynski型和Crooks型自治包含功涨落定理。分析量子非对易性对非自治极限约化的影响，并在排他功定义下考察可恢复非自治极限的条件。以Dicke模型（单模辐射场与二能级原子系综分别作为系统和功源）进行示例说明。

Result: 成功推导了量子自治包含功的涨落定理，其形式与经典自治功涨落定理类似。量子非对易性阻止了其在大功源极限下一致约化为非自治涨落定理。在排他功定义下，当功源被测可观测量与其裸哈密顿量对易且系统对功源的反作用可忽略时，可恢复非自治极限。

Conclusion: 量子自治功涨落定理在形式上类比经典结果，但量子非对易性从根本上阻碍了其向非自治情况的约化，揭示了量子涨落定理中自治功与非自治功之间的本质区别。

Abstract: Fluctuation theorems for work provide universal constraints on nonequilibrium fluctuations, yet their quantum generalizations often rely on externally prescribed classical driving protocols. While for classical systems, fluctuation theorems have been extended to autonomous work, where the dynamics of the work source is subject to the backaction of the system, their generalization to the quantum regime is constrained by the uncertainty principle. Here, we extend fluctuation theorems for autonomous work from the classical regime to the quantum regime. By performing successive projective measurements over the work source and the system, we derive Jarzynski-type and Crooks-type fluctuation theorems for autonomous inclusive work from initial mixed thermal states. These relations are analogous to fluctuation theorems for autonomous work in the classical regime and explicitly incorporate the fluctuations of the work source. However, quantum noncommutativity prevents a consistent reduction to the nonautonomous counterparts, even in the limit of a large work source and correspondingly negligible backaction. By contrast, under the exclusive work definition, the nonautonomous limit is recovered when the measured observable of the work source commutes with its bare Hamiltonian and the backaction of the system on the work source is negligible. Our results are illustrated with the Dicke model, where a single-mode radiation field and an ensemble of two-level atoms act as the system of interest and the work source, respectively.

</details>
