### [256] [TLRNet: Estimating Individual Treatment Effect based on Local Information and Single Learner Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22762)
*Ali Haghpanah Jahromi,Mohammad Taheri,Zohreh Azimifar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度神经网络和伪单学习器的创新方法，用于估计治疗效应的异质性。该方法使用单一估计器来估计两个治疗组的潜在结果，在IHDP基准测试上取得了可接受的结果，为服务个性化工具（如为患者选择最佳治疗方案）提供了支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断已成为多个领域的关键问题，而观察数据中因果效应的估计因成本低、数据量大而受到关注。现有方法在估计治疗效应异质性方面仍有改进空间，特别是在使用单一模型同时估计多个治疗组潜在结果方面。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度神经网络和伪单学习器（pseudo-single learner）的模型结构，使用一个估计器来估计两个治疗组的潜在结果，从而实现治疗效应异质性的估计。

Result: 在IHDP基准数据集上与其他最先进方法进行了比较，取得了可接受的结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为未来进一步发展和改进奠定了基础，有望提升服务个性化工具的性能，例如在医疗中为每位患者选择最有效且成本合理的治疗方案。

Abstract: Causal inference has become a central issue across various fields, including computer science, statistics, economics, education, healthcare, and medicine. The broad applicability of this discipline has garnered increased research funding and attention. In recent years, the estimation of causal effects from observational data has gained traction due to the vast amounts of collected data and the lower costs compared to randomized controlled trials. Advances in causal effect estimation methods have enhanced service personalization tools. For instance, these tools can help identify the most effective type of treatment (considering both cost and success rate) for each patient among different medical service options. This paper proposes an innovative method for estimating the heterogeneity of treatment effects. The structure of the proposed model is based on a deep neural network and a pseudo-single learner. The proposed method has been compared with other state-of-the-art methods on the IHDP benchmark. Acceptable results have been obtained by using one estimator to estimate the potential outcomes of two treatment groups. Accordingly, this paves the way for further development and improvement of the proposed method.

</details>


### [257] [Amortized Bayesian Causal Discovery of Extended Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22934)
*Yichen Gu,Yuxuan Song,Weizhou Qian,Yixin Wang,Joshua Welch*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ABCDEFG方法，一种用于因果图推断的摊销贝叶斯方法，能够扩展到数千节点、处理未知目标的干预、保证精确无环性，并提供后验分布和可识别性保证，在模拟数据和单细胞扰动数据上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从干预数据中学习因果图在分子生物学等领域有广泛应用（如基因调控网络），但现有方法（基于评分的优化或近似贝叶斯推断）难以同时满足大规模扩展、处理未知目标干预、不确定性量化和可识别性保证等要求。

Method: 开发了ABCDEFG（Amortized Bayesian Causal Discovery of Extended Factor Graphs）方法，通过摊销贝叶斯推断实现：保证精确无环性、扩展至数千节点、自然处理未知目标的干预，并估计后验分布，其最大后验估计能以可证明的方式识别真因果图直到某个等价类。

Result: 在模拟数据集上达到最先进精度，产生良好校准的后验分布，优于先前的基于评分和近似贝叶斯方法；应用于大规模单细胞扰动数据时，识别了已知和新的生长因子基因靶点。

Conclusion: ABCDEFG有效解决了大规模因果图推断的多项挑战，兼具可扩展性、处理未知干预的能力、不确定性量化和理论可识别性保证，在生物学实证中展现了实用价值。

Abstract: Learning causal graphs from interventional data is a challenging problem with broad applications. In molecular biology, for example, a central goal is to uncover gene regulatory networks from large-scale perturbation data. An ideal algorithm for this task should scale to thousands of nodes, incorporate interventions even when their targets are unknown, quantify uncertainty, and provide identifiability guarantees. However, existing approaches---e.g. approaches using score-based optimization or approximate Bayesian inference---often fail to meet all of these criteria. To address these limitations, we develop Amortized Bayesian Causal Discovery of Extended Factor Graphs (ABCDEFG). Our method guarantees exact acyclicity, scales to graphs with thousands of nodes, and naturally handles interventions even when their targets are unknown. Additionally, ABCDEFG estimates a posterior distribution whose maximum a posteriori estimate provably identifies the true causal graph up to an equivalence class. On simulated datasets, ABCDEFG achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, producing a well-calibrated posterior distribution while outperforming previous score-based and approximate Bayesian methods. Applied to large-scale single-cell perturbation data, ABCDEFG identifies both established and novel gene targets of growth factors.

</details>


### [258] [Modeling Memory-Dependent Reliability of LLMs: A Hidden Markov Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22951)
*Robab Aghazadeh Chakherlou,Siddartha Khastgir,Peter Popov,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于隐马尔可夫模型的层次贝叶斯框架，用于在存在序列依赖性的场景中评估LLM的可靠性，实验表明忽略序列依赖性会导致过于自信的可靠性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM可靠性评估方法（如基准测试、聚合准确率）未能刻画不确定性，且新兴的统计推断方法假设测试结果独立同分布，这在交互式、上下文依赖的序列场景中不成立，需要更合理的模型。

Method: 扩展层次贝叶斯框架，引入隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）来建模序列依赖性：潜在交互状态遵循一阶马尔可夫过程，生成观测到的任务结果，从而捕捉上下文变化。

Result: 在四个数据集上使用Anthropic Claude和OpenAI模型进行实验，结果表明忽略序列依赖性会导致可靠性估计过于自信（即低估不确定性）。

Conclusion: 序列依赖性对LLM可靠性评估有显著影响，所提出的HMM方法能更准确地反映实际交互场景中的不确定性。

Abstract: Reliability assessment of large language models (LLMs) seeks to estimate the probability that a model produces correct responses under a specified operational profile. Conventional benchmark-based evaluation, often summarized by aggregate accuracy, provides a point estimate of performance but does not characterize the uncertainty associated with reliability claims. Currently, statistical inference methods for LLM reliability assessment are emerging. However, a key assumption underlying these models is that test outcomes can be treated as independent repeated trials. This assumption may be inappropriate in sequential settings, where later responses depend on earlier interactions through retained context, error propagation, or an evolving interaction state. We extend a hierarchical Bayesian framework for LLM reliability assessment by relaxing the assumption of independent task outcomes and introducing a Hidden Markov Model to capture sequential dependence in benchmark-constructed interaction sessions. In this formulation, outcomes are generated from a latent interaction state evolving according to a first-order Markov process, capturing changes in interaction context. Through experiments using Anthropic Claude and OpenAI on four datasets, we demonstrate the potential impact of sequential dependence on reliability assessment. The results suggest that ignoring sequential dependence may lead to overconfident reliability estimates.

</details>


### [259] [Variable Importance Identification Through Lazy Training for Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22979)
*Anand Singh,Luke Pennella,Eshan Kabir,Xiaoxi Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对二分类问题，结合变量重要性框架和惰性训练思想，提出了一种高效的特征重要性识别算法，该算法假设条件少、错误率可控，并通过模拟和真实数据验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习可解释性研究多聚焦于回归框架，而二分类场景下的可解释性研究相对不足，且已有方法往往假设较强或计算效率较低。

Method: 采用变量重要性框架，结合惰性训练（lazy training）思想，设计了一种用于二分类任务的高效特征重要性识别算法。

Result: 所提方法在仅需最小化假设的条件下实现了可控的错误率；通过大量模拟研究和真实数据应用验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了二分类深度模型可解释性研究的空白，提供了一种理论保证充分且计算高效的变量重要性评估方法。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have been widely used in many applications (e.g., computer vision and natural language processing); however, understanding their explainability remains a challenging task. Recently, substantial research has been devoted to improving the explainability of deep neural networks, with most of this work focusing on the regression framework. In this paper, we instead focus on the binary classification framework and adopt a variable-importance framework combined with the idea of lazy training to propose an efficient algorithm for identifying important features. From a theoretical perspective, our method relies on only a minimal set of assumptions and achieves well-controlled error rates. The validity of the proposed method and algorithm is examined through extensive simulation studies and real-data applications.

</details>


### [260] [Robust Conformalized Selection with Noisy Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22985)
*Chengyao Yu,Hongxin Wei,Bingyi Jing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒共形选择（RCS）框架，解决校准数据存在标签污染时共形选择方法无法控制错误发现率（FDR）或功效严重下降的问题。RCS通过对不同类别分别进行条件化处理，将标签噪声转化为局部协变量偏移问题，并利用协变量调整的经验贝叶斯估计实现FDR控制。理论性质（渐近FDR控制、功效最优性、鲁棒性）和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形选择方法假设校准数据是干净的，但在实际应用中（如可靠标注、药物发现、大模型对齐）校准数据常存在标签污染（噪声），导致FDR失控或功效严重下降。

Method: 提出RCS框架：通过对不同类别分别进行条件化处理，将不可处理的标签噪声问题转化为局部协变量偏移问题，进而利用协变量调整的经验贝叶斯型估计来估计错误选择的数量，实现有效的FDR控制。

Result: 建立了RCS的渐近FDR控制、功效最优性和鲁棒性等统计性质；在随机响应模型下实现了RCS，并将其拓展到选择大响应值候选者的任务；在模拟和真实数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: RCS是一个统一的鲁棒共形选择框架，能够在一般标签污染下实现对选择性分类的有效FDR控制，具有扎实的理论保证和良好的实证表现。

Abstract: Conformalized selection has been widely applied to select high-quality candidates from large datasets with rigorous uncertainty quantification, such as reliable labeling, drug discovery, and the alignment of large language models. Nevertheless, existing methods assume clean responses on calibration data, an assumption that rarely holds in practice. In this paper, we formulate the above tasks as selecting candidates with true predicted labels or with responses exceeding certain values. We demonstrate that existing conformal selection methods fail to control the false discovery rate (FDR) or suffer from severe power loss under contaminated calibration data. To that end, we propose Robust Conformalized Selection (RCS), a unified framework for selective classification with valid FDR control under general label contamination. The key insight of RCS lies in a novel statistical reduction: by separately conditioning on different classes, we translate the intractable label noise into a localized covariate shift problem, which then enables a covariate-adjusted empirical-Bayes-type estimate of the number of false selections. Statistical properties such as the asymptotic FDR control, power optimality, and robustness of RCS are established. We further develop an instantiation of RCS under randomized response model, and also apply RCS to the task of selecting candidates with large response values. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of RCS.

</details>


### [261] [Covariance-Boosted Gaussian Processes for Spatiotemporal Irregularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23018)
*Jeremy Ovadia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出协方差增强高斯过程（CBGP）框架，通过迭代增强协方差先验来发现非平稳潜在函数，并引入部分白化观测的GP建模进行相对误差估计，结合过拟合防止和后验不确定性膨胀策略，在电离层建模等安全关键应用中达到三九完整性标准，相比现有SBAS局部拟合方法更准确可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳GP模型虽能捕捉输入依赖的变异性，但在有限采样和高参数化协方差结构下容易过拟合且不确定性估计过于自信，导致安全关键应用中的误导性预测。本文以星基增强系统（SBAS）的电离层建模为驱动力，需解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CBGP框架：1）通过增强协方差先验发现非平稳潜在函数，捕捉信号与观测变异中的输入域不规则性；2）对部分白化的观测进行额外GP建模，指导潜在函数相对误差估计；3）采用类似梯度下降的迭代过程更新弱先验；4）增强后对先验协方差施加限制防止过拟合，并膨胀后验不确定性防止模型过度自信。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界应用的外样本测试中均达到三九完整性标准。对南美洲大范围电离层风暴数据集的建模表明，CBGP相比当前运行的SBAS局部拟合法更准确可靠，能在最具挑战性的空间天气环境中计算SBAS电离层修正。

Conclusion: CBGP框架通过协方差增强和不确定性校正，为非平稳GP模型在安全关键应用中的可靠使用提供了有效方案，并在电离层建模这一实际应用中展现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Nonstationary Gaussian process (GP) models are powerful tools for capturing input-dependent variability by adapting to observed data. However, with limited sampling and highly parameterized covariance structure, they are often prone to overfitting and overconfident uncertainty estimates, potentially leading to misleading predictions in safety-critical applications. Motivated by ionospheric modeling for satellite-based augmentation systems (SBAS), this paper proposes a Covariance-Boosted Gaussian Process (CBGP) framework centered upon boosting covariance priors to discover nonstationary latent functions for signal and observation variation that capture irregularities in the input domain. An additional layer of GP modeling of "partially-whitened" observations guides latent function relative error estimation that is used to iteratively update weak priors in a gradient descent-like procedure. Following boosting, restrictions are imposed upon prior covariances to prevent overfitting while posterior uncertainties are inflated to prevent model overconfidence. CBGP model efficacy and robustness are demonstrated through out-of-sample testing of both simulated and real-world applications that meet a three-nines integrity standard. The modeling of an extensive ionospheric storm dataset over South America suggests accurate and reliable means to compute SBAS ionospheric corrections in the most challenging space weather environment using regional models that are more informed and responsive than local fitting performed by currently-operating SBAS.

</details>


### [262] [Operator Neural Jump ODEs: $L^2$-optimal prediction in function spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23110)
*Florian Krach,Oliver Löthgren,Josef Teichmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将神经跳跃常微分方程（NJ-ODE）框架从有限维扩展到无限维函数空间，使过程取值于$L^2$空间而非$\mathbb{R}^{d_X}$，从而能够直接处理函数值预测问题（如收益率曲线、波动率曲面），避免离散化导致的信息损失，并通过新的近似策略在更弱的假设下证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NJ-ODE系列工作仅限于有限维随机过程，处理函数值问题（如收益率曲线、波动率曲面预测）时必须离散化，导致信息损失。本文旨在将该框架推广到无限维函数空间，以直接处理这类函数值预测问题。

Method: 借鉴神经算子方法的思想，将NJ-ODE扩展为算子NJ-ODE（Operator NJ-ODE），使底层过程取值于$L^2(Ξ, \mathbb{R}^{d_X})$无限维函数空间；提出新的近似策略以证明收敛到最优预测过程，该策略在更弱假设下也推广了有限维的先前工作。

Result: 成功建立了无限维NJ-ODE框架，能够直接处理函数值随机过程的最优在线预测问题，无需离散化；在新的近似策略下证明了收敛性，且假设条件比以往工作更弱。

Conclusion: 本文通过将NJ-ODE扩展到无限维函数空间，克服了此前框架只能处理有限维过程的局限性，为收益率曲线、波动率曲面等函数值预测任务提供了理论框架，并给出了更一般化的收敛性证明。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the extension of Neural Jump ODEs to infinite-dimensional function spaces. In particular, the underlying process $X$ now takes values in $L^2(Ξ, \mathbb{R}^{d_X})$ instead of $\mathbb{R}^{d_X}$ and the Operator NJ-ODE approximates the optimal predictor of this process by producing a representative of the conditional expectation. The NJ-ODE model is a framework for online learning the optimal prediction of continuous-time stochastic processes, given discrete, possibly irregular and incomplete past observations. In a series of works, this model has been extended to deal with generic path-dependent processes, with observation noise and dependent observations, with long-term predictions, and with input-output systems. However, throughout all of these works, the underlying processes were restricted to be finite-dimensional. In particular, function-valued problems, like yield curve or volatility surface predictions, could only be handled through discretization, which inherently leads to a loss of information. In this work, we build on ideas from Neural Operator methods that allow us to extend the NJ-ODE framework to an infinite-dimensional output process. To prove convergence of the NJ-ODE to the optimal prediction process, we develop a new approximation strategy that also generalizes previous works in the finite-dimensional setting by considerably weakening the assumptions.

</details>


### [263] [Adaptive Multi-Scale Forecasting and Gate-Localized Conformal Prediction for Multivariate Nonstationary Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23165)
*Ziling Ma,Junshu Jiang,Ángel López-Oriona,Ying Sun,Hernando Ombao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ABF-T-GLCP框架，通过自适应预测状态表示和门控局部化共形预测，在非平稳多元时间序列中实现点预测与不确定性量化，在商品预测基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳多元时间序列中，点预测和不确定性量化面临时序动态演化挑战，现有方法难以同时实现两者的自适应校准。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) 点预测模块通过门控融合多个时域专家，并利用稀疏预测传递跨相关序列；(2) 不确定性量化模块GLCP利用门控状态和时序近邻性选择局部校准残差，实现与预测模型一致的自适应共形校准。

Result: 在大规模高频商品预测基准上，点预测精度持续提升，预测区间显著收窄且经验覆盖率接近名义水平。额外实验表明框架可推广至金融以外领域。

Conclusion: 共享预测状态表示使点预测和不确定性量化在非平稳动态中一致自适应，同时保持共形预测的模型无关性，在温和稳定条件下可实现近似局部覆盖。

Abstract: We propose ABF-T-GLCP, a model-agnostic framework for forecasting and uncertainty quantification in nonstationary multivariate time series. The central idea is to learn an adaptive predictive state representation for point forecasting and reuse it for conformal calibration. The forecasting module combines horizon-specific temporal experts through a learned gate and refines predictions using sparse predictive transfer across related series. The uncertainty module, Gate-Localized Conformal Prediction (GLCP), uses the learned gate state, together with temporal recency, to select locally relevant calibration residuals, thereby coupling uncertainty calibration to the predictive regimes used by the forecasting model. This shared representation allows point forecasts and prediction intervals to adapt consistently under evolving temporal dynamics while retaining the model-agnostic nature of conformal prediction and yielding approximate local coverage under mild stability conditions. Experiments on a large-scale high-frequency commodity forecasting benchmark show consistent gains in point forecasting accuracy and substantially narrower prediction intervals with empirical coverage close to the nominal level. Additional results indicate that the framework extends beyond the motivating financial application.

</details>


### [264] [Beyond ICA: Identifiability by Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23182)
*Pengzhou Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有分段仿射解码器和高斯混合模型先验的深度生成模型在纯无监督设置下的可识别性，通过三种代数对比原则（域对比、机制对比、交互对比）打破对称性，建立了从律可识别性到映射可识别性再到后验可识别性的层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度生成模型在无监督学习中的不可识别性问题，传统方法依赖连续性和单射性假设，而本文旨在放宽这些限制，处理非连续、非单射的解码器场景。

Method: 引入三种代数对比原则：域对比（平凡化混合对称群）、机制对比（确保每个解码器分支有唯一边界）、交互对比（禁止潜变量分量与解码器分支之间的参数共谋）。利用分段仿射映射的离散组合结构与潜在高斯混合模型的连续对称结构之间的相互作用，以代数对称条件替代连续性假设。

Result: 建立了从律可识别性（LID，潜分布至全局仿射变换）到映射可识别性（MID，解码器至同一仿射变换）再到后验可识别性和逐点可识别性的层次结构。在分量协方差对角化条件下出现了ICA形式的模糊性。

Conclusion: 本文是首个将代数对称性破缺作为非线性可识别性驱动机制的工作，首个允许非连续解码器的工作，也是首个处理完全非单射解码器（每个观测对应多个潜编码）的工作。

Abstract: We prove the identifiability of deep generative models (DGMs) with piecewise-affine (PWA) decoders and Gaussian mixture model (GMM) priors, in a purely unsupervised setting. We introduce three algebraic contrast principles for symmetry breaking: domain contrast, which trivializes the mixture symmetry group; mechanism contrast, which ensures every decoder branch is witnessed by a unique boundary; and interaction contrast, which forbids parameter conspiracies between latent components and decoder branches. Together they exploit the interplay between the discrete combinatorics of the PWA map and the continuous symmetry structure of the latent GMM. Continuity is replaced by algebraic symmetry conditions; injectivity is decoupled from structural identification and required only for pointwise inversion. Our results form a hierarchy: from law identifiability (LID; latent distribution up to a global affine map) through map identifiability (MID; decoder up to the same map) to posterior and pointwise identifiability. The ICA-form ambiguity emerges under conditions on diagonal component covariances. Assumptions are only on the data-generating process, not on learning methods, except for the interaction contrast. To our knowledge this is the first to make algebraic symmetry-breaking the engine of nonlinear identifiability, the first to admit discontinuous decoders, and the first to handle fully non-injective decoders, where every observation admits multiple latent codes.

</details>


### [265] [FedSLIM: Privacy-Preserving Federated MDL-Based Descriptive Pattern Mining Across Data Silos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23236)
*Samar Samir Khalil,Noha S. Tawfik,Marco Spruit*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FedSLIM 是首个基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的联邦 descriptive pattern mining 框架，通过两种变体在分布式数据库间协作优化紧凑模式模型，实验表明其在保持高质量压缩结构的同时能以更低搜索成本恢复全局信息模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在预测建模上已有显著成功，但联邦描述性分析（descriptive analytics）仍未被充分探索。现有联邦模式挖掘方法主要基于支持度（support-based），缺乏像 MDL 这样有原则的全局目标函数，因此需要引入联邦 MDL 挖掘框架。

Method: 基于 SLIM 原理构建 FedSLIM 框架，提出两种互补变体以平衡隐私、通信和优化保真度；引入保真度（fidelity）和发现导向（discovery-oriented）指标，量化与集中式基线的一致性和全局信息模式恢复能力。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上（IID 和非 IID 划分）的实验表明，两种变体均能保持高质量压缩结构，搜索量比集中式基线低数个数量级；且能恢复所有局部模型均无法发现的全局信息模式，揭示了分布式 MDL 挖掘中存在的局部-全局发现差距（local-global discovery gap）。

Conclusion: 联邦 MDL 挖掘可作为跨分布式数据孤岛进行隐私保护描述性分析的实用基础，联邦优化优于独立的局部挖掘。

Abstract: Federated learning has achieved considerable success for predictive modelling, yet federated descriptive analytics remains largely unexplored. Existing federated pattern mining approaches are predominantly support-based and do not optimise a principled global objective such as Minimum Description Length (MDL). We introduce FedSLIM, the first federated MDL-based framework for descriptive pattern mining. Building on the SLIM principle, FedSLIM enables collaborative optimisation of compact pattern models across distributed databases without sharing raw transactions. We propose two complementary variants that balance privacy, communication, and optimisation fidelity under different deployment assumptions. To evaluate federated MDL mining, we introduce fidelity and discovery-oriented metrics that quantify agreement with a centralised baseline and assess recovery of globally informative patterns. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets under IID and non-IID partitioning show that both variants preserve high-quality compression structure while requiring orders of magnitude less search than the centralised baseline. We further reveal a local-global discovery gap in distributed MDL mining, where globally compressive patterns may be undiscoverable through isolated local optimisation. Both variants recover globally informative patterns absent from all standalone local models, demonstrating the benefits of federated optimisation beyond independent local mining. These results establish federated MDL mining as a practical foundation for privacy-preserving descriptive analytics across distributed data silos.

</details>


### [266] [Learning Asymptotics with Convergence-Rate Guarantees using Linear Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23287)
*Christos N. Efrem*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了渐近学习理论（ALT），将优化理论与渐近分析相结合，用于从渐近展开式中计算未知常数/参数。重点研究了滑动线性最小二乘（sLLSQ）和滑动Tikhonov线性最小二乘（sT-LLSQ）两种数值方法，证明了其收敛性和收敛率条件，并应用于解析组合学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的渐近分析方法在处理未知常数/参数时缺乏统一的优化框架，传统方法（如比率法及其变体）存在局限性。ALT旨在提供一种系统化的优化理论方法来求解渐近展开中的未知参数。

Method: 提出了ALT框架，研究两种数值方法：sLLSQ和sT-LLSQ。严格证明了这两种方法的渐近估计、收敛的充分条件以及收敛率保证。方法基于优化理论与渐近分析的结合。

Result: 证明了sLLSQ和sT-LLSQ的收敛性条件与收敛率保证；发现了这两种方法的局限性（如慢收敛甚至在某些情况下发散）；在解析组合学中的应用验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: ALT为渐近分析中的参数计算提供了统一的优化框架，sLLSQ和sT-LLSQ是有效的数值工具，但仍存在局限性。未来可进一步探索ALT中的有趣研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce a new research area that is called Asymptotics Learning Theory (ALT) and combines optimization with asymptotic analysis. In particular, ALT provides a unified approach for computing unknown constants/parameters in proven asymptotic expansions using optimization theory. In this paper, we focus on a general asymptotic form which includes a broad class of asymptotics. Furthermore, we study two powerful numerical methods, namely, sliding Linear Least Squares (sLLSQ) and sliding Tikhonov Linear Least Squares (sT-LLSQ). For these techniques we rigorously prove asymptotic estimates that lead to sufficient conditions for convergence (to the correct values of unknown parameters) and convergence-rate guarantees. Despite their strengths, both methods have also limitations, e.g., slow convergence---or even, counterintuitively, divergence---in some cases. Moreover, we present fundamental applications in analytic combinatorics, a beautiful field of mathematics that deals with asymptotic enumeration of discrete structures using complex analysis. The proposed techniques complement existing approaches, such as the ratio method and its variants. Numerical examples also verify the theoretical results. Finally, we discuss interesting research directions in ALT.

</details>


### [267] [Context-Adaptive Inference: A Unified Statistical and Foundation-Model View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23304)
*Yue Yao,Caleb N. Ellington,Jingyun Jia,Baiheng Chen,Dong Liu,Rikhil Rao,Jiaqi Wang,Samuel Wales-McGrath,Yixin Yang,Zhiyuan Li,Eric P. Xing,Ben Lengerich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了"上下文自适应推理"的统一视角，涵盖显式统计自适应、元学习快速适应和大模型隐式适应三类方法，证明它们在数学上等价于对输入与上下文的联合特征进行核岭回归，并据此提出设计原则与评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预测系统需要在不同情境下自适应调整行为（如临床模型对患者差异化处理、检索增强模型根据证据改变回答、MoE模型对不同输入路由到不同专家），但现有方法分散在不同领域缺乏统一理论框架，亟需建立统一的数学理解和设计原则。

Method: 将自适应统一形式化为：用上下文c映射到自适应参数θ(c)，然后进行预测f(x; θ(c))。在平方损失、线性预测头和固定特征假设下，证明显式参数自适应和隐式路由在数学上等价于对输入和上下文联合特征做核岭回归。

Result: 建立了三类方法（统计显式自适应、元学习快速适应、大模型隐式适应）的统一数学框架，提出了适应效率（adaptation-efficiency）、路由稳定性（routing stability）、上下文特定鲁棒性（context-specific robustness）等评估指标。

Conclusion: 指出了可识别性、分布偏移下的鲁棒性、大规模高效自适应等开放问题，为构建可扩展、可靠且透明的上下文自适应系统提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Modern predictive systems are expected to adapt their behavior to the specific situation they are facing. A clinical model should not treat every patient the same; a retrieval-augmented model should change its answer when given different evidence; a mixture-of-experts model should route different inputs to different experts. We call this capability context-adaptive inference: before predicting, the system uses information about the current context to specialize its parameters or computation for that instance.
  This article provides a unified view of context-adaptive inference across three traditions that are usually treated separately: (i) explicit adaptation in statistics (e.g. varying-coefficient models, local regression, hierarchical sharing), (ii) rapid task-specific adaptation in meta-learning and transfer, and (iii) implicit adaptation in large foundation models via prompting, retrieval, and expert routing. We formalize these approaches under a common objective: to map context $c$ to adapted parameters $θ(c)$, then to predict via $f(x; θ(c))$. Under squared loss, linear prediction heads, and fixed features, we prove that explicit parameter adaptation and implicit routing are mathematically equivalent to kernel ridge regression on joint features of inputs and context. Building on this bridge, we propose practical design principles and evaluation metrics including adaptation-efficiency, routing stability, and context-specific robustness to guide when to specialize, how to constrain that specialization, and how to audit context-adaptive models in deployment. Finally, we identify open problems in identifiability, robustness under distribution shift, and efficient large-scale adaptation, outlining design principles for methods that are scalable, reliable, and transparent in real-world settings.

</details>


### [268] [Logit-Coordinate Generative Models for Mixed Continuous-Categorical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23348)
*Yuefei Shen,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于logit坐标的框架统一处理混合连续-分类数据生成，并证明了其在理论稳定性和非参数速率上的优势，实验表明在类别不平衡和真实数据场景下优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合连续-分类数据对连续生成模型（如Flow Matching和高斯扩散）构成表示挑战：连续变量在欧几里得空间，分类变量在概率单纯形上且常高度不平衡，需要统一且稳定的建模框架。

Method: 提出logit坐标框架：将分类变量编码为平滑后的自然参数（logit坐标），与变换后的数值变量结合；引入混合分布差异度量（分离分类边际误差和条件连续Wasserstein误差）；推导稳定性界限和不平衡感知的非参数速率。

Result: 模拟实验显示缩放logit坐标在严重稀有细胞不平衡下优于或匹配独热坐标；四个真实数据集上，Logit FM在三个数据上改进主要分布指标，Block-Conditional Logit FM一致改进平面模型，Logit Diffusion普遍优于或匹配One-Hot Diffusion。

Conclusion: Logit坐标框架为混合连续-分类生成建模提供了理论上有保障、实践上更优的解决方案，特别适用于类别不平衡场景。

Abstract: Mixed continuous--categorical data pose a representation problem for continuous generative models. Flow Matching and Gaussian diffusion operate in Euclidean spaces, whereas categorical laws lie on probability simplices and may be highly imbalanced. We study a logit-coordinate framework that encodes categorical variables as smoothed natural parameters and combines them with transformed numerical variables. This yields common formulations of Logit Flow Matching and Logit Diffusion. We introduce a mixed-distribution discrepancy separating categorical marginal error from conditional continuous Wasserstein error, and derive stability bounds and imbalance-aware nonparametric rates linking vector-field or drift error to decoded mixed-distribution error. Controlled simulations show that scaled-logit coordinates improve or match one-hot coordinates, especially under severe rare-cell imbalance. Across four real-data benchmarks and ten splits per dataset, Logit FM improves the primary distributional metrics on three datasets and is comparable on Churn2; Block-Conditional Logit FM consistently improves the flat model; and Logit Diffusion generally improves over or matches One-Hot Diffusion.

</details>


### [269] [Two-Timescale Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Resilient Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23434)
*Young Hyun Cho,Franz Stoll,Will Wei Sun,Guang Lin,Stephan Biller*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双时间尺度分层强化学习框架，用于联合调整长期和短期策略以增强运营韧性，并首次证明了耦合双时间尺度学习的收敛性保证，在二手车案例研究中平均利润提升9.2%-11.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球运营中频繁出现意外冲击，需要能够随市场条件变化而自适应的决策规则。许多运营系统具有层级结构，长期与短期决策追求共同目标，但现有方法缺乏对这些相互依赖规则的联合自适应机制。

Method: 开发了一个双时间尺度分层强化学习框架，在各自的时间尺度上调整长期和短期策略。通过同步策略更新解决策略间的相互依赖问题，并证明了耦合双时间尺度学习的收敛性保证。

Result: 策略与最优策略对的平均差距为O(T^{-1/2})，在决策失误导致明显利润损失时改进为O(log T/T)。在二手车案例中，相较于最强部分自适应基准，联合需求-供应冲击下平均利润提升9.2%，长期冲击场景下提升11.8%，且利润轨迹更稳定。

Conclusion: 短期自适应能应对常规季节性和单边中断，但面对联合需求-供应冲击时不足；需要长期自适应为短期决策创造有利条件。联合自适应能在冲击和恢复期间实现更高且更稳定的利润。该框架无需改变现有决策结构即可增强运营韧性。

Abstract: Unexpected shocks recur in global operations, requiring decision rules that adapt as market and operating conditions change. Many operational systems also have hierarchical structures in which long-term and short-term decisions pursue a shared objective. We study how hierarchical reinforcement learning can strengthen resilience by adapting these interdependent rules jointly. We develop a two-timescale hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that adapts long-term and short-term policies at their respective time scales. Because the policies are interdependent, we synchronize their updates and prove, to our knowledge, the first convergence guarantees for coupled two-timescale learning. Over $T$ periods, our policies' average gap from an optimal policy pair is $O(T^{-1/2})$, improving to $O(\log T/T)$ when poor decisions produce clearer profit losses. In a used-car case study, inventory replenishment is the long-term decision and customer-arrival pricing the short-term decision. Relative to the strongest partially adaptive benchmark, the framework increases mean profit by $9.2\%$ under joint demand-supply shocks and by $11.8\%$ under a prolonged shock scenario, while maintaining a more stable profit trajectory over time. Short-term adaptation addresses routine seasonality and one-sided disruptions by responding immediately to changing conditions. Under joint demand-supply shocks, however, it is insufficient alone; long-term adaptation is also needed to create favorable conditions for short-term decisions. Joint adaptation thus yields higher and more stable profits through disruption and recovery. Because many organizations already use hierarchical planning, the framework strengthens operational resilience without altering existing decision structures.

</details>


### [270] [Learning switched non-linear dynamical systems from a single trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23502)
*Sunny G. W. Wang,Hemant Tyagi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了切换非线性动力系统的经验风险最小化问题，在稳定性假设和独立同分布切换的K种模式条件下，推导了预测风险的非渐近界，该界依赖于函数类的度量熵。针对Hölder和线性函数类给出了显式收敛速率，数值实验验证了理论结果。这是首个从单条轨迹学习切换非线性动力系统的非渐近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习动力系统理论主要针对非切换系统，对于切换动力系统的学习理论分析（特别是非渐近保证）尚属空白。作者希望从理论上理解从单条轨迹中学习切换非线性动力系统的可行性和样本复杂度。

Method: 在稳定性假设和K种模式独立同分布切换条件下，利用经验风险最小化框架，基于函数类的度量熵推导预测风险的非渐近上界。针对Hölder函数类和线性函数类分别给出具体的收敛速率，其中有效样本量为Tp_i（T为轨迹长度，p_i为模式i的观测概率）。

Result: 推导出了切换非线性动力系统预测风险的非渐近界，该界由函数类的度量熵决定。对于Hölder和线性函数类，得到了依赖于有效样本量Tp_i的显式收敛速率。数值模拟结果与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 本文首次为从单条轨迹学习切换非线性动力系统提供了非渐近理论保证，揭示了切换概率和轨迹长度对学习性能的影响。

Abstract: We study empirical risk minimization for learning non-linear dynamical systems whose transition dynamics may switch over time. Under stability assumptions, and i.i.d switching over a set of $K$ modes, we derive non-asymptotic bounds on the prediction risk expressed in terms of the metric entropy of the underlying function class. We instantiate our general result for Hölder and linear function classes, obtaining explicit convergence rates that depend on the effective sample size $Tp_i$, where $T$ is the trajectory length and $p_i$ is the probability of observing mode $i$. Numerical simulations support our theoretical findings. To the best of our knowledge, these results are the first non-asymptotic guarantees for learning switched nonlinear dynamical systems from a single trajectory.

</details>


### [271] [Distributional Split Criteria for Random Forests: Extensions, Shrinkage, and the Robustness of Mean Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23721)
*Silas Koemen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了分布随机森林中多种分裂准则（各向同性/各向异性MMD、自适应频率选择、切片Wasserstein以及后验核均值收缩），发现在标量回归中基于均值的CART分裂仍是稳健默认选项，分布分裂仅在多元响应且存在非位置结构时才有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机森林使用基于均值的CART分裂准则，无法捕捉条件分布的完整信息（如方差、相关性等）。作者希望系统研究用全条件分布比较替代均值比较的分裂准则，明确各扩展方法的适用场景和收益边界。

Method: 在同一诚实森林框架内实现了五类分布分裂准则：各向同性随机傅里叶特征MMD、各向异性对角带宽MMD、自适应逐分裂频率选择MMD、非核切片Wasserstein距离，以及后验核均值收缩。使用配对种子比较方法，在合成分位数机制、真实单变量基准、加州房价子样本曲线、多元合成/真实响应等场景中进行评估。

Result: 1) 各向同性MMD在分布准则中已接近最优，各向异性、自适应频率、切片Wasserstein和后验收缩未带来系统性改进；2) 在标量表格回归中，基于均值的CART分裂仍是稳健默认选项，赢下多数对比单元；3) 多元响应是分布分裂的明确优势场景，尤其在纯依赖copula上能量分数能区分各准则而边际CRPS不能。

Conclusion: 分布分裂仅在非位置结构既存在又可估计时才有帮助，否则会稀释分裂选择中来自均值的信息。所有准则和诚实森林实现已开源在drforest库中，其Rust后端的分裂搜索使大规模准则扫描成本低廉。

Abstract: Distributional random forests replace mean-based CART splitting with criteria that compare the full conditional response distribution in candidate children. We implement and systematically study a family of such criteria inside a single honest-forest implementation: isotropic random-Fourier-feature maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), an anisotropic diagonal-bandwidth variant, an adaptive per-split frequency-selection variant, and a non-kernel sliced-Wasserstein criterion, together with post-hoc kernel-mean shrinkage of the forest weights. Using paired-seed comparisons across synthetic quantile mechanisms, real univariate benchmarks, a California-housing subsample curve, and multivariate synthetic and real responses, we characterize where each extension pays. Three findings recur. First, among distributional criteria ordinary isotropic MMD is already close to best in class: the anisotropic, adaptive-frequency, and sliced-Wasserstein extensions, and post-hoc shrinkage, do not systematically improve on it. Second, on scalar tabular regression mean-based CART splitting remains the robust default and wins many cells. Third, multivariate responses are the regime where distributional splitting clearly earns its keep, most sharply on a pure-dependence copula where the energy score separates the criteria even though marginal CRPS does not. The evidence supports a simple allocation story: distributional splitting helps only when non-location structure is both present and estimable; otherwise it dilutes split-selection power away from the mean. All criteria, the honest forest, and the paired-comparison harness are implemented in the open-source \texttt{drforest} library, whose Rust-backed split search makes broad criterion sweeps inexpensive.

</details>


### [272] [On Non-Stationary Dynamic Pricing: Adaptivity and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24115)
*Feiyu Jiang,Zifeng Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平稳环境下的情境动态定价问题，提出了一种基于多尺度变点检测的自适应算法，实现了最优遗憾界（分段平稳段数量平方根与设计调整变差预算的折中），并首次达到了“两全其美”的速率，同时给出了匹配的极小化下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态定价研究多假设需求模型固定不变，但实际市场中消费者行为常随时间变化；同时，现有的自适应非平稳Bandit方法无法直接用于情境动态定价，因此需要设计能自适应变化类型并达到最优遗憾的算法。

Method: 提出基于多尺度变点检测的算法，将需求模型假设为广义线性模型（GLM），算法不依赖于分段段数s_T或变差预算V_T的先验知识，自适应检测变化并调整学习策略。

Result: 算法达到遗憾界 Õ(√(s_T d T) ∧ {V_T^{1/3} d^{1/3} T^{2/3} + √(dT)})，并构造了匹配的极小化下界，证明算法在log因子意义下的最优性；数值实验验证了算法的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个在情境动态定价中自适应变化类型并达到最优遗憾率的算法，填补了文献中长期存在的空白；所提设计调整变差预算（V_T）为刻画非平稳性提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the contextual dynamic pricing problem under non-stationarity, where a firm sells products to $T$ sequentially arriving consumers that behave according to an unknown demand model that can change over time. The demand model is assumed to be a generalized linear model (GLM), allowing for a feature vector in $\mathbb{R}^d$ that encodes products and consumer information. To achieve optimal revenue (i.e., least regret), the firm needs to learn and exploit the unknown GLMs while monitoring for potential changes. We propose a multiscale change-point detection based algorithm that achieves a regret of order $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{s_TdT}\wedge\{V_T^{1/3}d^{1/3}T^{2/3}+\sqrt{dT}\})$, where $s_T$ is the number of piecewise stationary segments and $V_T$ is a newly defined notion of design-adjusted variation budget of model parameters. Our algorithm is adaptive and does not require knowing $s_T$ or $V_T$. Moreover, to our knowledge, this is the first dynamic pricing algorithm that is adaptive to the nature of changes and achieves the best-of-both-worlds rate, thus closing a long-standing gap in the literature. We remark that, due to the varying contexts, existing works in the adaptive non-stationary bandit literature cannot be applied to achieve optimality for contextual dynamic pricing. The regret is further accompanied with a newly constructed minimax lower bound, confirming the optimality of our algorithm (up to logarithmic factors). Extensive numerical experiments are conducted to illustrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed algorithm in non-stationary dynamic pricing.

</details>


### [273] [Minimax Lower Bounds of Kernel Discrepancy Estimation: MMD, HSIC, KSD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24235)
*Jose Cribeiro-Ramallo,Florian Kalinke,Zoltán Szabó*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明在一般拓扑空间和温和核假设下，MMD、HSIC和KSD等核差异的估计具有n^{-1/2}的极小值最优下界，解决了这些核差异最优估计的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅在有限维欧几里得空间和有界核的严格假设下知道n^{-1/2}是极小值最优的，但对于更一般的拓扑空间和无界核的情形，其最优性尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过理论分析，在一般拓扑空间上，在核的温和假设下，证明了最流行的核差异（MMD、HSIC、KSD）估计的极小值下界为n^{-1/2}，该结果也适用于均值嵌入和中心化交叉协方差算子的估计。

Result: 证明了在一般拓扑空间和温和核假设下，MMD、HSIC和KSD的估计极小值下界为n^{-1/2}，该速率与已知的参数量级相匹配，从而确立了最优性。

Conclusion: 这些结果解决了这些核差异最优估计的开放问题，将其最优性从有限维欧几里得空间推广到了更一般的拓扑空间设置。

Abstract: Over the past 20 years, kernel discrepancies have been leveraged as a highly powerful tool for quantifying the disagreement of distributions, with numerous successful applications in two-sample, goodness-of-fit, and independence testing, among others. Their fastest estimators are known to converge at a parametric rate---$n^{-1/2}$---under mild conditions. While this rate is known to be minimax optimal on $\mathbb R^d$ under strict assumptions with bounded kernels, little is known about its optimality beyond the finite-dimensional Euclidean setting with unbounded kernels. In this work, we prove that the minimax lower bound of estimation of the most popular kernel discrepancies (maximum mean discrepancy, Hilbert-Schmidt independence criterion and kernel Stein discrepancy; MMD, HSIC, KSD) is $n^{-1/2}$ on general topological spaces, and under mild assumptions on the kernel; the same rates are shown (as corollaries) to hold for the estimation of the mean embedding and the centered cross-covariance operator. Our results settle the question of optimal estimation of these kernel discrepancies.

</details>


### [274] [proxymate: Diagnosis and Adjustment of Proxy Estimates for Reliable Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24401)
*Alexandra N. M. Darmon,Deeksha Sinha,Steve Wilkins-Reeves,Caner Gocmen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了proxymate框架和开源Python包，用于代理变量的验证和调整，将代理问题分为四个层级（代表性、单位、估计、领域），并提供诊断检查和针对性调整策略。在Meta内部已广泛应用于实验、流行率估计和监控等多个场景，评估和修正了数百万个代理-原始单元比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理结果（如短期行为信号、模型预测或替代终点）常被用于替代成熟慢、罕见或难以直接测量的主要结局。但基于代理的推断可能存在系统性偏差且难以预测，导致置信区间校准不当，因此需要系统化的代理验证与调整框架。

Method: proxymate框架将代理验证与调整组织为四个层级：代表性层级（总体效度）、单位层级（测量质量）、估计层级（决策效度）、领域层级（跨领域可迁移性）。每个层级提供诊断检查和针对性调整策略，将特定失效映射到相应修正方法。

Result: 在Meta内部被多个用例采用，涵盖实验、流行率估计和监控场景，面临不同代理挑战（有限的人工审查时间、长结果成熟窗口、低可检测性）。在所有产品中评估和修正了数百万个代理-原始单元比较，促进多个工作流的发布，包括支持数千个实验的快速决策。

Conclusion: proxymate提供了一种模块化、系统化的代理验证与调整方法，能够有效解决代理偏差问题，在实际生产环境中具有可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Proxy outcomes (such as short-term behavioral signals, model predictions, or surrogate endpoints) are frequently used in place of primary outcomes that are too slow to mature, rare, or challenging to measure directly. But valid inference on a proxy does not guarantee valid inference on the primary estimate as proxy-based estimates can be systematically biased in ways that are difficult to predict, leading to improperly calibrated confidence intervals.
  We present proxymate, a framework and open-source Python package for proxy validation and adjustment. proxymate organizes into four levels: The Representativity Level (population validity), the Unit Level (measurement quality), the Estimate Level (decision validity), and the Domain Level (cross-domain transportability). Within each level, proxymate provides diagnostic checks, and targeted adjustment strategies that map specific failures to appropriate corrections.
  At Meta, proxymate has been adopted by many different use cases, spanning experimentation, prevalence estimation, and monitoring use cases, all facing different proxy challenges (limited human review time, long maturation window of outcomes, low detectability) and showcasing the modularity of the framework. Across all products, proxymate assessed and corrected millions of proxy, primary unit comparisons. It has facilitated launches across multiple work streams including enabling quick decision making on thousands of experiments.

</details>


### [275] [Frequency-Based Reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24420)
*Arthur S Powanwe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受大脑振荡动力学和层级时间尺度启发的频率基储层计算框架，将储层解译为独立振荡单元的集成，每个单元处理输入的部分频率内容。该频率基储层在性能上与随机储层相当或更优，且可优化以改善短期预测，并能预测复杂时空动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有储层计算依赖随机连接网络和大量超参数优化，缺乏精确解释其工作机制和优化方法的理论框架。

Method: 提出频率基储层，将储层建模为受外部输入驱动的非线性振荡单元集成，借鉴受复杂周期输入驱动的非线性振荡器理论，分析单元如何选择性放大和存储特定输入频率用于预测。

Result: 频率基储层性能与随机储层相当或更优；可优化以改善短期预测（随机储层缺乏此特性）；能预测复杂时空动力学。

Conclusion: 储层计算可借鉴大脑特性和受迫非线性振荡器物理理论进行设计，提供了可解释、可优化的新范式。

Abstract: Reservoir computing has emerged as an efficient machine learning framework for predicting time series generated by dynamical systems. In contrast to other machine and deep learning approaches, a reservoir computing trains only the output layer via linear regression, leaving the reservoir (recurrent layer) untrained. This simplification makes reservoir computers easier to train and more amenable to experimentation. However, because current reservoirs consist of networks of randomly connected nodes and require the optimization of numerous hyperparameters, a framework that precisely explains how reservoir computing operates and how it can be optimized remains missing. Here, we propose a frequency-based reservoir inspired by the brain's oscillatory dynamics and its hierarchy of timescales. The frequency-based reservoir can be interpreted as an ensemble of independent oscillatory units, each processing a portion of the input's frequency content.
  This allows us to understand the reservoir's internal behavior by modeling it as a single unit driven by an external input. Borrowing from the theory of a nonlinear oscillator forced by complex periodic inputs, we found that units of the frequency-based reservoir selectively amplify and store specific input frequencies, which are then used for prediction. The frequency-based reservoir performs as well as or better than equivalent random reservoirs. Furthermore, the frequency-based approach can be optimized to improve short-term prediction, a property that random reservoirs lack. Finally, we show that the frequency-based reservoir can also predict complex spatiotemporal dynamics. Our results show that reservoir computing can be designed using brain properties and theoretical insights borrowed from the physics of forced nonlinear oscillators.

</details>


### [276] [A Model for Imbalanced Label Aggregation: A Focus on Minority-Class Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24622)
*Gabriel Singer,Samuel Gruffaz,Olivier Vo Van,Nicolas Vayatis,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对不平衡众包场景，提出了一种结合项目难度和类别相关标注者能力的生成式聚合模型，解决了现有模型无法同时捕捉类别相关误差和项目难度的问题。在33个真实数据集上，该模型在保持竞争性平衡精度的同时，始终实现最高的少数类召回率，特别适用于稀有标签恢复为主要目标的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的检测系统中，最具操作重要性的标签往往也是最稀有的，而现有的众包模型要么只捕捉类别相关误差但忽略项目难度，要么建模项目难度却无法捕捉类别相关误差，无法同时处理两者。

Method: 提出一种生成式聚合模型，同时建模项目难度和类别相关的标注者能力，允许标注者能力和项目难度在不同类别间变化。此外，重新审视了类别不平衡设置下的Condorcet陪审团定理，并证明了多数投票渐近地保持底层类别比例。

Result: 在33个真实众包数据集（覆盖图像和文本的多分类任务，以及大规模标注数据集和大规模项目数据集）上，该模型始终实现最高的少数类召回率，同时在平衡精度上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该模型在不平衡众包场景下有效填补了现有方法的空白，当稀有标签恢复是主要目标时尤为适用。

Abstract: We study imbalanced crowdsourcing with a focus on class-dependent annotator accuracy, a setting that, to the best of our knowledge, remains relatively underexplored despite its importance in real-world inspection systems where the labels of greatest operational importance are also the rarest ones. In this setting, annotators may be reliable on both classes, unreliable on both classes, majority-class specialists, or minority-class specialists. Existing models only partially address this problem: they either capture class-dependent errors but ignore item difficulty, or they model item difficulty without capturing class-dependent errors. To fill this gap for imbalanced datasets in crowdsourcing, we introduce a generative aggregation model combining item difficulty with class-dependent annotator competence. The model allows both annotator abilities and item difficulties to vary across classes. We then revisit Condorcet's Jury Theorem in the class-imbalanced setting. We also show that majority voting asymptotically preserves the underlying class proportion. We evaluate our model on $33$ real-world crowdsourcing datasets, covering multiclass tasks such as images and text, as well as two large-scale regimes: large-scale annotation datasets, with many annotations per item, and large-scale item datasets, with a large number of annotated instances. Across these diverse settings, our model consistently achieves the highest minority recall while remaining competitive in balanced accuracy, making it particularly relevant when rare-label recovery is the primary objective.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Curl-based Electric-Field Boundary Condition for the Accurate and Stable Electromagnetic Scattering Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23260)
*Eduard Ubeda,Alex Heldring,Juan M. Rius,Vladimir Okhmatovski*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于旋度的电场积分方程（Curl-EFIE），用于理想导体的电磁散射分析。该方程通过在边界流形上强制EFIE的旋度为零推导得到，并采用正交切向螺线管盘进行测试。MoM离散化表明，当测试盘尺寸趋于零时，Curl-EFIE收敛到Galerkin离散化的MFIE，且阻抗矩阵在低频和密集网格下稳定、无奇异性。相比MFIE的强奇异核，Curl-EFIE使用弱奇异核，简化了源积分计算。作为第一类积分方程，它避免了MFIE的Gram矩阵需求，便于处理非匹配三角剖分。此外，Curl-EFIE与传统EFIE的线性组合可提供与CFIE类似的无内谐振公式。最后，Curl-EFIE在捕捉尖边和角点几何的散射行为方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EFIE在低频和密集网格下存在低频崩溃和矩阵病态问题，而MFIE虽无低频崩溃但核函数强奇异且需Gram矩阵，计算复杂。本文旨在开发一种兼具两者优点的新积分方程形式。

Method: 基于切向流形上EFIE旋度为零的约束，利用正交切向螺线管盘测试内部电场，推导Curl-EFIE。采用MoM进行离散化，证明测试盘尺寸趋于零时收敛到Galerkin-MFIE。通过弱奇异核简化计算，并引入与EFIE的线性组合实现内谐振消除。

Result: Curl-EFIE在低频和密集网格下阻抗矩阵稳定无奇异；弱奇异核简化了源积分评估；无需Gram矩阵，支持非匹配三角剖分；与EFIE线性组合可实现CFIE等效的无内谐振特性；在尖边和角点几何中散射计算表现优异。

Conclusion: Curl-EFIE是一种新型、稳定、计算高效的积分方程方法，克服了EFIE和MFIE的各自局限，特别适用于复杂几何的低频电磁散射问题。

Abstract: We introduce a curl-based Electric-Field Integral Equation (Curl-EFIE) for the electromagnetic scattering analysis from perfect electric conductors. The formulation is derived by enforcing a vanishing curl on the EFIE over the boundary manifold, achieved by testing the internal electric field with orthogonal tangent solenoidal disks. We demonstrate that a Method-of-Moments (MoM) discretization of the Curl-EFIE converges to a Galerkin-discretized MFIE as the testing disk dimensions vanish, yielding stable, breakdown-free impedance matrices for low frequencies and dense grids. Unlike the strongly singular kernels of the MFIE, the Curl-EFIE utilizes weakly singular kernels, significantly simplifying source integral evaluations. As a first-kind integral equation, it bypasses the MFIE's Gram matrix requirement, facilitating the analysis of non-matching triangulations. Furthermore, a linear combination of the Curl-EFIE and the conventional EFIE provides an interior-resonance-free formulation analogous to the Combined Field Integral Equation (CFIE). Finally, the Curl-EFIE performs particularly well at capturing the scattering behavior of sharp- edged and cornered geometries.

</details>


### [278] [PI-GINOT: Data-free geometry-informed neural operator learning for finite-strain hyperelasticity on parametric DogBone specimens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23299)
*Aamir Dean,Betim Bahtiri*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: PI-GINOT是一个物理-informed神经算子，能够在无有限元训练数据的情况下预测不同几何形状的DogBone试件在有限应变下的超弹性响应，通过点云编码几何特征、自动微分计算应力，并利用物理约束（平衡、无牵引、对称、稳定性和内力一致性）指导训练，在8种测试几何上实现了2.1%-7.1%的位移误差和0.9%-13.3%的峰值von Mises应力误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化非线性固体力学模拟广泛用于虚拟测试、优化和不确定性分析，但几何变化时重复的有限元模拟计算成本高昂，因此需要一种无需标注仿真数据即可预测几何相关力学响应的高效方法。

Method: PI-GINOT采用物理-informed神经算子架构：将边界点云编码为几何特征，通过交叉注意力解码器预测任意点的位移；精确施加位移边界条件；利用自动微分和可压缩Neo-Hookean平面应力模型计算应力；训练由平衡条件、无牵引边界、对称条件、变形稳定性和内力一致性等物理损失指导。

Result: 在8种测试几何上，PI-GINOT的位移误差为2.1%-7.1%，峰值von Mises应力误差为0.9%-13.3%，截面力误差低于10.3%。窄试件在过渡区附近应力梯度陡峭处，个别应力分量误差较大。

Conclusion: PI-GINOT能够在无标注仿真数据的情况下为非线性固体力学提供有用的几何相关预测，同时揭示了局部应力分辨率仍有待改进的方向。

Abstract: Parametric nonlinear solid-mechanics simulations are widely used in virtual testing, optimisation, and uncertainty analysis, but repeated finite-element simulations become costly when geometry changes. This paper presents PI-GINOT, a physics-informed neural operator that predicts finite-strain hyperelastic responses across a four-parameter family of DogBone specimens without using finite-element training data. Each specimen is described by a boundary point cloud, which is encoded into geometry features. A cross-attention decoder then predicts displacement at arbitrary points. Displacement boundary conditions are enforced exactly, while stresses are computed using automatic differentiation and a compressible Neo-Hookean plane-stress model. Training is guided by equilibrium, traction-free and symmetry conditions, deformation stability, and internal force consistency. Abaqus simulations are used only after training for validation. Across eight test geometries, PI-GINOT achieves displacement errors of 2.1%-7.1%, peak von Mises stress errors of 0.9%-13.3%, and section-force errors below 10.3%. Larger errors occur in individual stress components, especially for narrow specimens, mainly because of steep stress gradients near the gauge-to-fillet transition. These results show that PI-GINOT can provide useful geometry-dependent predictions for nonlinear solid mechanics without labelled simulation data, while also revealing where better local stress resolution is still needed.

</details>


### [279] [Self-consistent orbital-free nuclear density functional theory with a physics-constrained learned nonlocal kinetic energy functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23328)
*Fumihiro Imoto*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种密度依赖核的非局域动能密度泛函（KEDF），通过从Kohn-Sham参考数据学习校正项，实现了轨道自由密度泛函理论（OFDFT）中核壳结构的自洽描述。该方法利用线性核形状获得解析Euler-Lagrange响应，采用精确能量匹配等式和软二次不等式惩罚进行拟合，并通过重整径向Euler-Lagrange方程作为单轨道本征问题实现稳定收敛。在$^{16}$O基准测试中，该方法无需密度混合即可收敛，并与虚时演化（ITE）结果吻合（亚keV能量误差）。针对$A=16$至$140$的球形$N=Z$系统，核特异性拟合成功再现了壳层模式和半径；跨核迁移训练表明壳层模式和半径可迁移，但绝对能量不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非局域动能密度泛函（KEDF）虽能编码核壳结构，但在轨道自由密度泛函理论（OFDFT）中实现自洽计算时，面临泛函导数不精确和Euler方程求解不稳定的挑战。本文旨在通过学习Kohn-Sham参考数据构建具有精确解析导数的密度依赖核KEDF，实现稳定自洽的壳结构描述。

Method: 提出密度依赖核的KEDF校正项，利用线性核形状获得解析Euler-Lagrange响应；采用精确能量匹配等式和针对尾响应及选定路径能量上升边界的软二次不等式惩罚进行拟合，通过主动集重复惩罚最小二乘迭代求解；将径向Euler-Lagrange方程重整为单轨道本征问题以实现稳定求解。

Result: 在恒定$k_F$的$^{16}$O基准中无需密度混合即收敛，与虚时演化（ITE）的偏差在亚keV量级；自适应步长ITE的EL残差不超过0.07 MeV；重整对角化达到相同驻点密度。对于不含自旋-轨道和Coulomb项的球形$N=Z$系统，$A=16$至$140$的核特异性拟合再现了壳层模式和半径；三核训练后的校正项可迁移壳层模式和半径至未见的$A=140$系统，但绝对能量不可迁移。

Conclusion: 所提出的密度依赖核KEDF方法能够通过数据驱动学习实现OFDFT中核壳结构的自洽描述，具有良好的收敛性和可迁移性（壳层模式与半径可迁移，绝对能量不可迁移），为大规模电子结构计算提供了新路径。

Abstract: Nonlocal kinetic-energy density functionals (KEDFs) can encode nuclear shell structure in orbital-free density functional theory (OFDFT), but self-consistency requires accurate functional derivatives and a stable solution of the Euler equation. We construct a density-dependent-kernel KEDF whose correction is learned from Kohn-Sham (KS) reference data. Linearity in the kernel shape yields analytic Euler--Lagrange (EL) responses. The fit uses exact energy-matching equalities and soft quadratic inequality penalties for violations of prescribed tail-response and selected-path energy-rise margins, evaluated by an active-set repeated penalized least-squares iteration. We formulate the radial EL equation as the rearranged one-orbital eigenproblem. In a constant-$k_F$ $^{16}$O benchmark it converges without density mixing and agrees with imaginary-time evolution (ITE) to sub-keV energy. Adaptive-step ITE gives final species EL residuals of at most 0.07 MeV in the reported calculations, and rearranged diagonalization reaches the same stationary densities. For spherical $N=Z$ systems without spin--orbit or Coulomb terms, nucleus-specific fits for $A=16$ to $140$ reproduce shell patterns and radii. A correction trained on three nuclei transfers the shell pattern and radius, but not the absolute energy, to a previously unseen $A=140$ system.

</details>


### [280] [Tunable mesoscopic numerical model for bacterial biofilms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23677)
*J. Martin-Roca,B. Wu-Zhang,J. Oller,J. Ramirez,C. Valeriani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可调谐的介观模型，结合耗散粒子动力学与Gillespie算法，模拟细菌生物膜中胞外聚合物基质的可逆交联动态，揭示了聚合物-聚合物与聚合物-细菌交联竞争对 biofilm 结构的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为细菌生物膜的流变学计算提供基础，显式纳入胞外聚合物基质中的可逆交联，捕捉网络固有的动态演化特性。

Method: 采用耗散粒子动力学框架结合Gillespie启发的算法，实现聚合物与细菌之间键的动态形成与断裂，构建可调谐的介观模型。

Result: 生物膜结构由聚合物-聚合物与聚合物-细菌交联之间的竞争主导，受结合能、连接子可用性和键刚度控制。

Conclusion: 提供了一个最小模型来帮助理解两种交联物种之间的竞争机制。

Abstract: We present a tunable mesoscale model to provide a basis for future rheological calculations of of bacterial biofilms, explicitly incorporating reversible crosslinking within the extracellular polymeric substance (EPS) matrix. Using a Dissipative Particle Dynamics framework combined with a Gillespie-inspired algorithm, bonds between polymers and bacteria dynamically form and break, capturing the intrinsically evolving nature of the network. We show that biofilm structure is governed by a competition between polymer-polymer and polymer-bacteria crosslinks, controlled by binding energy, linker availability, and bond stiffness and provide a minimal model that helps to understand the competition between both species.

</details>


### [281] [Stochastic Counterdiabatic Driving via Biorthogonal Liouvillian Eigenmodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24393)
*Sandeep Suresh Cranganore,Sebastian Lehner,Johannes Brandstetter,Max Welling*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种基于规范变换（而非坐标变换）的数值框架，通过对含时Fokker-Planck生成元进行精确谱分解，得到反绝热校正项，从而在任意驱动速度下完美消除非绝热滞后，实现零方差自由能估计。数值验证表明，该方法在双阱势和谐波陷阱中可将非绝热滞后抑制约12个数量级（总变差距离）和16个数量级（KL散度），并实现耗散功在任意协议速度下近似为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间驱动随机系统会产生额外的耗散，导致概率分布滞后于瞬时平衡态，从而降低基于Jarzynski等式的非平衡自由能估计器的收敛性。现有"护送"方法（如流场法、靶向自由能微扰、学习微分同胚）虽能消除滞后，但构造封闭形式的护送场仍是一个挑战。

Method: 基于规范变换的理论框架，对含时Fokker-Planck生成元进行精确谱分解（Liouville算子的双正交分解），直接得到反绝热校正项（counterdiabatic correction），该校正作用于瞬时平衡分布，在任意驱动速度下精确抵消非绝热滞后，形式类比于量子系统中的Berry过渡驱动（shortcuts-to-adiabaticity）。

Result: 在过阻尼粒子穿越含时双阱势和谐波陷阱的数值模拟中，反绝热条件被满足至机器精度：非绝热滞后在总变差距离上被抑制约12个数量级，在KL散度上被抑制约16个数量级。确定性传播的Fokker-Planck密度在所有协议速度下的耗散功近似为零。

Conclusion: 该规范变换数值框架为构造完美护送自由能估计提供了一种互补方案，不依赖于广义坐标变换，且在数值精度上表现出色，可视为经典随机系统中Shortcuts-to-Adiabaticity的一种实现。

Abstract: Finite-time driving of stochastic systems generates excess dissipation, causing the evolving probability distribution to lag behind the instantaneous equilibrium, and consequently degrading the convergence of nonequilibrium free energy estimators based on the Jarzynski equality. Escorted free energy simulations address the non-adiabatic lag by engineering control fields $\mathbf{u}$ that eliminate the lag, enforcing the trajectory-wise equality $\mathcal{W}_\mathbf{u} = Δ\mathcal{F}$, and yielding zero-variance estimators. However, constructing the escorting field in closed form remains a challenge, approached variously through flow-field methods, targeted free energy perturbation, or learned diffeomorphisms. In this work, we construct a complementary numerical framework based on gauge-type transforms instead of generalized coordinate transforms for perfect escorting based on the exact spectral decomposition of the time-dependent Fokker-Planck generator. The biorthogonal decomposition of the Liouville operator directly yields a counterdiabatic correction whose action on the instantaneous equilibrium distribution exactly cancels the non-adiabatic lag at arbitrary driving speed in formal analogy with shortcuts-to-adiabaticity techniques such as Berry's transitionless driving for quantum systems. Numerical verification for simulations of an overdamped particle in a time-varying double-well potential and harmonic traps confirms that the counterdiabatic condition is satisfied to machine precision, with the non-adiabatic lag suppressed by roughly twelve orders of magnitude in total variation distance and sixteen orders in KL divergence relative to the unescorted dynamics. As a diagnostic, we demonstrate vanishing dissipated work $\mathcal{W}_{\text{diss}}(t) \approx 0$ for the deterministically propagated Fokker-Planck density across all protocol speeds.

</details>


### [282] [Reframing the coupling force of adaptive resolution simulation in terms of the Liouville-type hierarchy for open systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24574)
*Julian F. Hille,Rupert Klein*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应分辨率模拟(AdResS)中热力学力的新视角，将其与开放系统的Liouville型层级理论模型建立联系，推导了简单原子流体的积分表达式，并通过超临界Lennard-Jones流体的模拟进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AdResS模拟中用于补偿界面伪影的耦合力（热力学力）缺乏系统的理论框架，其依赖性不明确，计算复杂且验证标准不足。

Method: 将热力学力置于开放系统Liouville型层级理论中，推导简单原子流体的一维积分表达式，分析其关键参数依赖性，并基于界面特性提出简化的数值计算方法和新的验证准则。

Result: 成功建立了热力学力与Liouville型层级理论的对应关系，获得了积分表达式，明确了热力学力对重要仿真参数的依赖关系，并在不同热力学状态下的超临界Lennard-Jones流体模拟中验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 热力学力可以从开放系统的理论模型角度理解，所提简化计算方法和新的界面验证准则有效，为AdResS模拟提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel perspective on the coupling force employed to compensate the interface artifacts prevalent in adaptive resolution simulations (AdResS) of open many-particle systems. We show that a substantial part of this "thermodynamic force" can be framed in terms of the theoretical model of the Liouville-type hierarchy for open systems. The correspondence is made explicit for the case of a simple atomistic fluid, for which a one-dimensional integral expression is derived. This enables the analysis for dependencies of the thermodynamic force on important simulation parameters, which is taken to inspire both simplifications for the numerical calculation of the thermodynamic force and new criteria for its validation that are adequate to the interfacial nature of the problem. The theoretical claims are then verified in a simulation study of the atomistic supercritical Lennard-Jones fluid at different thermodynamic states.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [283] [Coupling of Local and Nonlocal Problems Using Local Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22672)
*Burak Aksoylu,Fatih Celiker,Patrick Diehl*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种全新的局部-非局部扩散问题耦合方法，仅使用局部边界条件，实现了O(h)收敛的量化保证，并通过有限元与Galerkin投影验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的局部与非局部扩散问题耦合方法通常需要处理复杂的非局部边界条件，作者希望利用其构造的非局部算子天然强制执行局部边界条件这一特性，设计更加自然、简便的耦合方案。

Method: 提出一种仅使用局部边界条件的局部-非局部耦合方法，受区域分解法启发，局部问题采用有限元离散，非局部问题采用Galerkin投影离散。

Result: 该耦合方法对任意解均可实现量化的O(h)收敛率，并通过大量数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 所提耦合方法简洁自然，利用局部边界条件简化了耦合过程，具有良好的收敛性能，为局部问题中的数值方法向非局部问题迁移提供了可能。

Abstract: We present a novel coupling method for local and nonlocal diffusion problems in 1D. Unlike other methods, our coupling method exclusively uses local boundary conditions. This is possible because our nonlocal operators enforce them by construction. Leveraging this advantageous property, we construct a seamless coupling that is remarkably natural. The utilization of local boundary conditions allows for the transfer of well-established numerical methods from local problems to nonlocal ones. Our local-to-nonlocal coupling method is inspired by the domain decomposition method, which we would like to transfer to nonlocal problems. The main result of our study is the construction of a local-to-nonlocal coupling method with a quantifiable $O(h)$ convergence that holds for an arbitrary solution. For discretization of the local and nonlocal problems, the finite element method and the Galerkin projection are employed, respectively. We verify our convergence rate with extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [284] [Can the perfect Swiss alphorn be designed? A combination of reduced basis method and machine learning for shape optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22823)
*Fabio Marcinnò,Clément Froidevaux,Leonardo Bocchieri,Riccardo Tenderini,Gerald Pot,Simone Deparis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了瑞士阿尔卑斯号的形状优化问题，通过实验测量与成像技术构建精确几何模型，采用全结构化网格划分，结合降阶基方法和机器学习模型，实现了从几何参数到共振频率的正向预测和指定音高的逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统乐器形状设计与音高调整依赖经验和试错，缺乏高效的计算优化方法。本文旨在通过计算建模与机器学习相结合的方式，实现对瑞士阿尔卑斯号形状的系统优化，使其共振频率精确匹配目标音高。

Method: 1) 结合实验测量与成像技术构建精确的3D几何模型；2) 采用全结构化网格进行网格划分；3) 建立几何参数化的Helmholtz方程有限元公式；4) 应用降阶基方法高效生成大规模仿真数据集；5) 训练机器学习模型实现正向预测（几何→共振频率）和逆向设计（目标音高→几何参数）。

Result: 成功构建了精确的阿尔卑斯号几何模型；通过RBM高效生成了大量仿真数据；训练的ML模型能够准确预测共振频率并实现逆向几何设计。

Conclusion: 所提出的计算框架能够有效优化阿尔卑斯号的形状以达到指定的音高目标，实现了从传统经验设计到数据驱动设计方法的转变。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the shape optimization of a Swiss alphorn to achieve resonance frequencies as close as possible to prescribed target notes. We construct an accurate geometric model of the alphorn based on both experimental measurements and imaging techniques. The latter are used to reconstruct geometric information where direct measures are not available. The resulting 3D model is meshed using a fully structured approach. Then, we introduce a geometry-parametrized finite element (FE) formulation of the Helmholtz equation enabling the application of the Reduced Basis Method (RBM) to efficiently generate a large dataset of simulations, in which each set of geometric parameters corresponds to a specific set of resonance frequencies (notes). This dataset enables the training of machine learning (ML) models for forward prediction of resonance frequencies from geometric parameters, as well as inverse design, where geometries are estimated to achieve specified target notes.

</details>


### [285] [Parameterized Methods for Game Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22899)
*Yijie Jin,Haomin Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个参数化计算框架，用于演化连续博弈中的策略行为，通过参数化前推映射表示概率密度，将非梯度流博弈动力学纳入该框架，并在Wasserstein度量下提供了可证明的误差界，数值实验验证了其在经济学非梯度系统中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化Wasserstein梯度流（PWGF）框架适用于可被表述为Wasserstein梯度流的概率密度演化方程，但并非所有博弈动力学都能表示为梯度流形式，现有框架存在局限性。

Method: 将概率密度表示为参考分布的参数化前推映射（pushforward），考虑参数化方程的演化，将参数化前推映射框架推广到非梯度流情形，并应用于博弈动力学。

Result: 在Wasserstein度量下提供了可证明的误差界，数值实验展示了该框架在多个非梯度经济学系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数化框架成功将PWGF方法推广到非梯度流博弈动力学，在理论上保证了误差可控，在实践上适用于更广泛的博弈场景。

Abstract: We introduce a parameterized computational framework for the evolution of strategic behavior in continuous games. We consider the collective dynamics of players through a time-dependent probability density over the strategy space, representing the likelihood of each strategy being chosen at any given time. Instead of directly solving the high-dimensional Fokker-Planck equation that governs this evolution, we represent the probability density as the pushforward of a reference distribution with parameterized pushforward maps and consider the evolution of the parameterized equations. The motivation for this work comes from a limitation of the parameterized Wasserstein gradient flow (PWGF) framework when it is applied to game dynamics. PWGF provides a parameterized approach for evolution equations of probability density that can be formulated as Wasserstein gradient flows. However, not all game dynamics admit such a gradient flow formulation. We generalize the parameterized pushforward map framework to the non-gradient flows and apply it to the game dynamics with provable error bound in Wasserstein metric. Numerical experiments with several non-gradient systems in economics are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of this new framework.

</details>


### [286] [AlgMortar: a fully algebraic multiscale mortar preconditioner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22905)
*Luan F. Santos,Fabricio S. Sousa,Roberto F. Ausas,Rafael T. Guiraldello,Felipe Pereira*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgMortar方法，一种完全代数的多尺度Mortar混合有限元方法实现，仅利用细网格系统矩阵信息，通过图划分定义子域，构建局部Dirichlet问题并通过代数界面条件耦合，可加速Krylov子空间方法。理论证明了当细网格矩阵对称正定且非对角元非正时方法的适定性，数值实验表明作为CG预条件子在多孔介质流动问题中具有良好的可扩展性，与代数多重网格方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模对称正定线性系统求解困难，尤其是高度异质系数（如多孔介质流动）导致的严重病态问题。多尺度方法已被用于加速Krylov子空间方法，但现有实现缺乏完全代数化的形式，限制了其在现有求解器中的嵌入。

Method: AlgMortar方法：利用图划分从矩阵图直接定义区域分解；在每个子域构建模拟Dirichlet问题的局部线性系统；通过代数界面条件耦合局部解，恢复弱通量连续性机制。该方法完全代数化，仅需细网格系统矩阵信息。

Result: 数值实验基于有限体积离散的Darcy流动问题，测试了高度异质、高对比度及高度不规则角点网格等挑战性案例。作为CG预条件子时，该方法表现出良好的可扩展性，与最先进的代数多重网格方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: AlgMortar是MMMFEM的完全代数实现，仅依赖矩阵信息，易于嵌入现有求解器。理论上证明了当矩阵对称正定且非对角元非正时方法的适定性。数值上在困难的多孔介质流动问题中展现了良好的可扩展性和竞争力。

Abstract: The solution of large-scale symmetric positive definite linear systems arising from discretizations of second-order elliptic equations is challenging, especially in applications with highly heterogeneous coefficients, such as flow in porous media, which can lead to severely ill-conditioned systems. In this context, multiscale methods have recently been used to accelerate Krylov subspace methods, owing to their favorable parallel scalability. In this work, we present AlgMortar, a fully algebraic realization of the Multiscale Mortar Mixed Finite Element Method (MMMFEM). The method uses only information extracted from the fine-grid system matrix, which facilitates its implementation in existing solvers. AlgMortar uses graph partitioning to define a domain decomposition directly from the matrix graph. On each subdomain, it builds local linear systems that mimic Dirichlet problems, and couples the resulting local solutions through an algebraic interface condition that recovers the weak flux-continuity mechanism of MMMFEM. We prove that the method is well posed when the fine-grid matrix is symmetric positive definite and has nonpositive off-diagonal entries, a structure commonly arising from discretizations of elliptic problems. Numerical experiments on fine-grid linear systems arising from finite-volume discretizations of Darcy flow problems show that, when used as a preconditioner for the conjugate gradient method, the proposed approach exhibits good scalability and is competitive with state-of-the-art algebraic multigrid methods for challenging heterogeneous, high-contrast test cases, including highly irregular corner-point grids.

</details>


### [287] [Optimal block preconditioners for a mass-conserving mixed stress formulation of Stokes flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22932)
*Kaibo Hu,Jongho Park,Jindong Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Stokes流的质量守恒混合应力公式，提出了最优的块对角和块三角预条件子。该公式导致一个双重鞍点系统，未知量包括离散应力、速度、涡量和压力。分析了结合增广拉格朗日公式的块对角预条件子下的MINRES方法，证明了其收敛速度与网格尺寸和运动粘度等关键参数无关（最优性）。采用值域分析方法分析了块三角预条件子下的GMRES方法。二维和三维数值实验验证了预条件子的参数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Stokes流的质量守恒混合应力公式产生的双重鞍点系统，设计高效的预条件子，使得迭代求解器的收敛速度对网格尺寸和物理参数（如运动粘度）具有鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 将问题转化为双重鞍点系统（应力、速度、涡量、压力）；2) 对增广拉格朗日公式设计块对角预条件子，用MINRES求解；3) 设计块三角预条件子，用GMRES求解并通过值域分析证明最优性。

Result: 块对角预条件子下的MINRES收敛速度与网格尺寸和运动粘度无关（最优性）；块三角预条件子下的GMRES同样具有参数鲁棒性；二维和三维数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的块对角和块三角预条件子对Stokes流双重鞍点系统是最优的，能有效处理质量守恒混合应力公式带来的数值挑战，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present optimal block diagonal and triangular preconditioners for a mass-conserving mixed stress formulation of Stokes flow. The algebraic formulation leads to a double saddle point system with unknowns corresponding to discrete stress, velocity, vorticity, and pressure. MINRES equipped with a block diagonal preconditioner for an augmented Lagrangian formulation of this system is analyzed and shown to be optimal, in the sense that the convergence rate is independent of key parameters such as mesh size and kinematic viscosity. GMRES equipped with a block triangular preconditioner is also analyzed using a field-of-values approach. Finally, we present numerical results for both two- and three-dimensional model problems to validate the parameter robustness of the proposed preconditioners.

</details>


### [288] [Isoperimetric-Combinatorial Bounds for Range-Controlled Matchings and Quasi-Interpolation from Scattered Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22968)
*Alexander Panchenko,Ben Hellwig,Oleh Rudenko*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个介观框架，用于分析有限点集的扰动。给定一个具有已知数值积分和逼近性质的参考点集Y，以及一个仅通过r尺度立方体内点数观测到的无序点集Q，通过施加Hall型组合约束，证明了Y和Q之间存在O(r)范围的完美匹配，从而将粗尺度h>r上的积分逼近估计从Y转移到Q，并给出显式误差控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为处理无序点集（如无结构网格点）的数值积分与逼近问题提供理论框架，特别是当点集仅能通过粗粒度统计信息（立方体计数）观测时，建立其与规则参考点集之间的可匹配性，进而利用规则点集的良好性质。

Method: 1. 介观框架：在尺度r>0上通过立方体种群信息描述无序点集Q；2. Hall型组合约束保证Y与Q间存在O(r)范围的完美匹配；3. 利用匹配将粗尺度h>r上的积分逼近从Y转移到Q；4. 采用平移不变拟插值方法获得h^s阶高精度估计。

Result: 建立了无序点集与规则参考点集之间的完美匹配，实现了积分逼近估计的转移，并获得了与准均匀设置类似的h^s阶高精度估计，适用于不同类别的几何构型。

Conclusion: 核心限制条件为Hall型条件和r≤Ch（C<1为尺度无关常数），在此条件下无序点集可被锚定到周期网格上，并继承规则点集的高阶逼近性质。

Abstract: We develop a mesoscopic framework for analyzing perturbations of finite point sets. Given a reference node set $Y$ with known cubature and approximation properties, we consider a disordered node set $Q$ that is observed only through its populations in cubes at scale $r>0$. By imposing Hall-type (HT) combinatorial constraints on these populations, we prove the existence of a perfect matching between $Y$ and $Q$ with $O(r)$ range. This allows integral approximation estimates on coarser cubes at scale $h>r$ to be transferred from $Y$ to $Q$ with explicit error control and anchors $Q$ to a periodic grid. We then use translation-invariant quasi-interpolation methods to obtain high-order estimates of order $h^s$ as in the quasi-uniform setting, but for a different class of geometries. The key restrictions are the HT conditions and the bound $r\le Ch$, where $C<1$ is scale independent.

</details>


### [289] [Geometry-aware LegONet for PDE Learning on Arbitrary Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23069)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yueqi Wang,Guang Lin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出几何感知的LegONet（gLegONet），通过将物理机制预训练为模块化变分流形块，仅在边界流形上改变代数坐标接口，实现了任意几何域上的PDE求解与物理定律发现，无需针对新几何重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习的PDE求解器将控制算子与训练时的几何、边界条件和离散化方式纠缠在一起，导致同一物理定律在新几何域上无法复用，且基于数据的物理定律发现依赖于特定几何形状。

Method: 1) 在环境频谱域上将物理机制预训练为模块化变分流形块；2) 对目标几何，采样边界约束定义仿射可容许流形；3) 利用质量标准正交切坐标演化动力学并直接评估候选定律发现特征；4) 改变几何域仅改变代数坐标接口，不改变已学习的算子块。

Result: 在未见过的几何域上的正向模拟和稀疏辨识测试中，边界残差保持在代数容差附近，并能从短时观测中预测出控制物理定律。

Conclusion: gLegONet将任意域上的PDE学习从几何特定的重训练或软惩罚施加转变为边界保证的可重用机制组装，实现了几何无关的物理建模与定律发现。

Abstract: Learned PDE solvers often entangle governing operators with the geometry, boundary conditions, and discretization used for training. This limits reuse when the same physics is posed on new domains, and it also makes physical-law discovery geometry-dependent. We introduce Geometry-aware LegONet (gLegONet), a boundary-manifold extension of Lego-like operator learning. Physical mechanisms are pretrained once as modular variational blocks on an ambient spectral domain. For a target geometry, sampled boundary constraints define an affine admissible manifold. Its mass-orthonormal tangent coordinates are used to evolve the dynamics and evaluate candidate law-discovery features directly. Changing the domain therefore changes only an algebraic coordinate interface, not the learned operator blocks. This converts arbitrary-domain PDE learning from geometry-specific retraining or soft penalty enforcement into boundary-guaranteed assembly of reusable mechanisms. In forward simulations and sparse identification tests on unseen domains, the method maintains boundary residuals near the algebraic tolerance and yields predictive governing laws from short-time observations.

</details>


### [290] [A mixed residual method for biharmonic equations in spectral Barron spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23080)
*Mengjia Bai,Kuo Gai,Shuai Lu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合残差方法（MIM）用于数值求解带非齐次固定边界条件的双调和方程。通过建立双调和方程在谱Barron空间中的适定性，推导了MIM的误差界，该误差界能够克服维度灾难，由神经网络逼近误差和随机采样泛化误差两部分组成。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维双调和方程数值求解中存在的维度灾难问题，为非齐次固定边界条件提供一种可证明收敛且误差可控的神经网络求解方法。

Method: 提出混合残差方法（MIM），将双调和方程在谱Barron空间中建立适定性理论，利用浅层神经网络进行逼近，推导出由逼近误差和泛化误差两部分组成的误差界。

Result: 推导出MIM的误差界，证明该方法能够克服维度灾难；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MIM方法能够有效求解带非齐次固定边界条件的双调和方程，且具有克服维度灾难的理论保证。

Abstract: We propose a mixed residual method (MIM) for numerically solving the biharmonic equation with nonhomogeneous clamped boundary conditions. By establishing the well-posedness of the biharmonic equation in spectral Barron spaces, we derive an error bound for MIM that relates shallow neural network approximations to the exact solution and overcomes the curse of dimensionality. This error bound consists of two components: the first corresponds to the approximation error of the neural network, while the second represents the generalization error arising from randomly sampled training data. Several numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [291] [Physics-informed token transformer methodology for nonlinear balance laws. I. Schwarzschild--Burgers fluid flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23143)
*Philippe G. LeFloch,Shuyang Xiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息标记变压器（PITT）方法，用于一维非线性双曲平衡律，通过组合符号方程标记化、傅里叶神经算子编码器、显式Rankine-Hugoniot激波运动律和学习校正项，以分段稳态剖面表示近似弱解。在Schwarzschild-Burgers方程上验证了该方法在移动激波、静止激波和稀疏波测试中的表现，并展示了Rankine-Hugoniot先验的主导作用和长期扰动动力学中的渐近传播律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法在处理非线性双曲平衡律的激波动力学和几何效应时存在精度不足的问题，尤其是在捕捉波前位置和保持稳态方面。作者希望开发一种结合物理先验（如Rankine-Hugoniot条件）与深度学习的方法，以提高对激波位置和复杂波动力学的建模精度。

Method: PITT方法结合了四个关键组件：(1) 符号方程标记化，将偏微分方程的结构信息编码为标记序列；(2) 傅里叶神经算子编码器，用于学习解的空间模式；(3) 显式Rankine-Hugoniot条件，用于精确推进主导间断（激波）的运动；(4) 学习校正项，用于重建光滑区域、稀疏波扇、几何依赖性和有限分辨率效应。方法以分段稳态剖面作为近似弱解的表示。

Result: 在移动激波、静止激波和稀疏波测试中，PITT方法表现出色。Rankine-Hugoniot先验在这些测试中起主导作用，而方程标记化提供了额外的系统性改进。与标准高阶有限体积近似相比，该方法能更准确地定位波前并保持相关稳态。通过分析Schwarzschild质量趋近于零的Burgers极限，验证了方法的鲁棒性。此外，利用该方法研究了扰动稳态解的长时动力学，发现了激波位置传播的渐近律。

Conclusion: PITT方法通过嵌入物理先验（Rankine-Hugoniot条件）有效地提升了神经算子对激波问题的建模能力，特别适用于涉及几何效应和复杂激波动力学的问题。分段稳态表示和显式间断推进策略为处理双曲守恒律提供了一种新颖且有效的范式。

Abstract: We introduce a Physics-Informed Token Transformer (PITT) methodology for nonlinear hyperbolic balance laws in one space dimension, using piecewise steady-state profiles for the representation of approximate weak solutions. The method combines symbolic equation tokenization, a Fourier neural operator encoder, an explicit Rankine--Hugoniot law for shock motion, and a learned correction term. For clarity, we present it here for the relativistic Schwarzschild--Burgers equation, a scalar model for spherically symmetric fluid flows on a Schwarzschild background. For this model the steady-state invariant and the generalized Riemann solutions are explicit, and they can therefore be built into the neural evolution. In particular, the leading discontinuities are advanced by the analytical jump condition, while the learned part reconstructs smooth regions, rarefaction fans, geometric dependence, and finite-resolution effects. The method is designed to locate wave fronts accurately and to preserve the relevant steady states. We test our PITT method on moving shocks, stationary shocks, rarefaction waves, and compare it with a standard high-order finite-volume approximation. We also analyze the standard Burgers limit (when the Schwarzschild mass tends to zero). The Rankine--Hugoniot prior plays the dominant role in these tests, while equation tokenization gives a systematic additional gain. The method is relevant for problems involving geometric effects and/or complex shock-wave dynamics, and is used here to study the long-time dynamics of perturbations of steady-state solutions. In particular, we exhibit an asymptotic law of propagation for the shock location of perturbed steady-state flows.

</details>


### [292] [A Comparison of Active Flux Methods for the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23133)
*Lukas Hensel,Yanick Kiechle,Rainer Grauer,Gudrun Grünwald,Christiane Helzel,Katharina Kormann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种近期提出的用于一维一速度Vlasov-Poisson系统的Active Flux方法：分裂步法和非分裂法。Active Flux是一种新颖的三阶精度有限体积法，通过演化单元界面上的节点自由度来计算数值通量，其半拉格朗日特性使其特别适合求解Vlasov方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Active Flux方法作为一种新兴的高精度格式，在双曲守恒律和Vlasov方程求解中展现出潜力，但不同实现方案（分裂步与非分裂）的性能差异尚待系统比较。

Method: 对比分析了两种Active Flux方案：分裂步法（将Vlasov方程按空间和速度方向分步处理）和非分裂法（直接整体求解），两者均基于Active Flux框架中的节点自由度演化和半拉格朗日思想。

Result: 对两种方法在一维一速度Vlasov-Poisson系统上的表现进行了比较，具体结果需查阅全文。

Conclusion: Active Flux方法因其半拉格朗日特性和三阶精度，是求解Vlasov-Poisson系统的有前景的网格方法，分裂步与非分裂方案各有优劣，需根据具体问题选择。

Abstract: Active Flux is a third-order accurate, fairly novel finite volume method for hyperbolic conservation laws that is becoming increasingly popular.
  It evolves additional nodal degrees of freedom (DOF) located on cell interfaces and shared by neighboring cells. The numerical fluxes are then computed from these DOFs.
  A crucial component of Active Flux methods is the evolution operator of the point values, which enables the natural use of semi-Lagrangian ideas and makes Active Flux an attractive candidate for a grid-based approach to the Vlasov equation.
  Here, we compare two recently proposed Active Flux methods for the 1D1V Vlasov-Poisson system: a split-step method and an unsplit method.

</details>


### [293] [Accelerating preconditioned Jacobi methods via perturbation-inspired pivoting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23187)
*Nian Shao,Yuji Nakatsukasa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合谱间隙信息的对称矩阵特征值问题Jacobi方法新主元策略，利用谱间隙小的特征值对非对角扰动更敏感的特性，结合混合精度预处理器，在矩阵存在特征值聚类时显著优于经典贪心策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称矩阵微扰理论表明，谱间隙较小的特征值对非对角扰动更敏感，即不同矩阵元素对特征值的影响不均匀。传统Jacobi方法的主元选择仅依据非对角元的大小，忽略了这一重要信息，因此有必要设计一种能利用谱间隙信息的新型主元策略。

Method: 基于谱间隙信息设计新的Jacobi旋转主元选择策略，该策略与仅由非对角元大小决定的经典策略完全不同。同时结合混合精度预处理器（先以低精度对角化矩阵），形成完整的求解方案。

Result: 数值实验表明，当原始矩阵具有聚类特征值时，所提策略（结合混合精度预处理器）在性能上显著优于经典的贪心主元策略。

Conclusion: 将谱间隙信息纳入Jacobi方法的主元选择是有效的，尤其对于特征值聚类的矩阵，可以大幅提升计算效率，为对称特征值问题的求解提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Perturbation theory for symmetric matrices shows that eigenvalues with small spectral gaps are more sensitive to off-diagonal perturbation, implying that different entries affect the eigenvalues unevenly. Building on this insight, we incorporate spectral gap information into the Jacobi method for symmetric eigenvalue problems and propose a new pivoting strategy, which is completely different from classical ones governed solely by the magnitude of the off-diagonal entries. When combined with a mixed-precision preconditioner that diagonalizes the matrix to low precision, numerical experiments demonstrate that the resulting strategy can significantly outperform the classical greedy approach when the original matrix has clustered eigenvalues.

</details>


### [294] [Central-Hermite Sensing and Collision for Frame-Robust Order-Resolved Relaxation on D3Q125](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23629)
*Bjørn Wu*

Main category: math.NA
