TL;DR: 本文开发了D3Q125动力学模型的中心Hermite公式，比较了三种变体。完全中心Hermite碰撞（模型C）能大幅降低碰撞引起的交叉阶框架差异（相对于模型A降低65.3-98.1%，中位81.1%），保持高阶模态纯度至机器精度，但宏观伽利略输运误差并未同步减少，且输运过程会持续重新注入框架误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Raw-Hermite感知和碰撞在固定离散速度集上会将均匀平移转化为不同非平衡阶次间的人为耦合，需要发展中心Hermite公式来消除这种由平移引起的交叉阶串扰。

Method: 为D3Q125动力学模型开发了中心Hermite公式，采用阶次分辨的对数高斯松弛，比较了三种变体：原始感知/原始碰撞(A)、中心感知/原始碰撞(B)、中心感知/中心碰撞(C)，在均匀平移二阶扰动和网格CFL-速度矩阵下进行测试。

Result: 模型C在平移二阶扰动中保持三阶和四阶模态纯度至机器精度；在网格CFL-速度矩阵上，模型C相对于A的总相对L∞度量降低了65.34-98.10%(中位81.13%)；长期计算保持正值和守恒性；但输运过程会持续重新注入框架误差，且中心Hermite界面重构在抑制三阶差异的同时放大了四阶差异。

Conclusion: 完全中心Hermite碰撞能大幅降低碰撞诱导的交叉阶框架差异，但离散输运和有限速度空间表示仍残留依赖；矩空间改进并不自动带来宏观伽利略输运误差的可比降低。

Abstract: Raw-Hermite sensing and collision on a fixed discrete-velocity set can convert a uniform translation into artificial coupling between nominally distinct nonequilibrium orders. We develop a central-Hermite formulation for a D3Q125 kinetic model with order-resolved log-Gaussian relaxation and compare three variants: raw sensing/raw collision (A), central sensing/raw collision (B), and central sensing/central collision (C). In homogeneous translated second-order perturbations, model C preserves third- and fourth-order modal purity to machine precision, whereas A and B develop boost-dependent cross-order content. Across a grid-CFL-boost matrix, model C reduces the post-transport collision frame discrepancy relative to A by 65.342-98.102% (median 81.131%) in the total relative L-infinity measure. Long-time calculations remain positive and conservative to numerical precision, although the accumulated benefit is configuration dependent because transport continually re-injects frame error. A transport study further reveals a clear trade-off: central-Hermite interface reconstruction strongly suppresses the third-order discrepancy but amplifies the fourth-order discrepancy. The fully central-Hermite collision therefore substantially reduces collision-induced cross-order frame discrepancy, while residual dependence remains due to discrete transport and finite velocity-space representation. This moment-space improvement does not by itself establish a comparable reduction in macroscopic Galilean transport error.

</details>


### [295] [Data-Driven Diffusion Processes on Differential Forms via the Projected Ambient Connection Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23192)
*Alvaro Almeida Gomez,Jorge Duque Franco*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于点云数据的微分形式投影环境连接拉普拉斯算子的数据驱动近似方法，通过交替微分阵列表示和扩散映射框架，实现了从点云数据直接逼近任意阶微分形式的算子，无需网格或单纯复形，并验证了其在热方程求解中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法（如扩散映射、向量扩散映射）仅能处理标量函数和切向量场，缺乏对任意阶微分形式的数据驱动近似方法。同时，已有的Hodge拉普拉斯数据驱动近似收敛性保证较弱，需要更优的框架。

Method: 1) 通过扩展经典的音乐同构，将微分形式表示为交替微分阵列（alternating differential arrays）；2) 构建矩阵值扩散算子，直接从点云数据逼近投影环境连接拉普拉斯算子；3) 采用扩散映射中渐近最优的核带宽缩放策略，获得更优的收敛保证；4) 基于该算子设计显式Euler格式求解微分形式的热方程。

Result: 在单位球面上的数值实验验证了解析解的衰减预测，证明了所提离散化方法的有效性，并实现了比先前Hodge拉普拉斯数据驱动近似更好的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 该框架将向量扩散映射自然推广到任意阶微分形式，为直接从点云数据近似几何偏微分方程建立了实用基础，是经典扩散映射理论的重要扩展。

Abstract: We develop a data-driven approximation of the projected ambient connection Laplacian acting on differential forms over smooth Riemannian manifolds sampled by point clouds. The proposed construction extends the classical framework of diffusion maps and Vector Diffusion Maps from scalar functions and tangent vector fields to differential forms of arbitrary degree.
  Our approach is based on a novel representation of differential forms as alternating differential arrays obtained through an extension of the classical musical isomorphism. This representation enables the construction of a matrix-valued diffusion operator that approximates the projected ambient connection Laplacian directly from point cloud data without requiring a mesh or simplicial complex.
  The proposed discretization admits the asymptotically optimal kernel bandwidth scaling inherited from diffusion maps, leading to sharper convergence guarantees than previous data-driven approximations of the Hodge Laplacian. Building upon this operator, we derive a fully data-driven explicit Euler scheme for the heat equation on differential forms and validate the proposed methodology through numerical experiments on the unit sphere. The experiments confirm the predicted decay of the analytical solution and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed discretization.
  The proposed framework provides a natural generalization of Vector Diffusion Maps to differential forms of arbitrary degree and establishes a practical foundation for the numerical approximation of geometric partial differential equations directly from point cloud data.

</details>


### [296] [No Free Lunch in Flow Surrogates under Time-Varying Boundary Conditions: A Two-Regime Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23667)
*Georg Winkler,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统性评估了八种流场代理模型在两种瞬态流动（CMP slurry film和Kármán涡街）中的表现，发现没有单一架构能同时适用于两种流态。决定代理模型成败的关键在于时间处理方式：自持振荡的KVS需要自回归反馈保持相位记忆，而边界驱动的CMP膜更适合直接映射。还发现点状RMSE会错误选择模型，因此提出了包含五个物理问题的评估指标。代理模型推理速度比有限元求解器快10³-10⁴倍，但训练模拟的离线成本使得CMP需要在第一次查询后、KVS需要在第三次查询后才能实现收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"在简单流态上验证有效的代理模型也能在更复杂流态上有效"这一常见假设是否成立，因为当前缺乏跨不同动力学特征流态的系统性比较研究。

Method: 选取两种具有不同动力学特征的瞬态流动（三维CMP slurry film和二维Kármán涡街），构建八个代理模型进行比较，涵盖全场学习与隐空间学习、一步预测与逐步预测等维度。提出了基于物理问题的五维度评估指标（场、结构、发明运动、振幅、时序）替代点状RMSE。

Result: 无单一架构同时胜出两个流态：CMP膜上全场景一步模型将累积壁面剪切应力相对误差降至3.2%；KVS上隐空间自回归DeepONet保留了96%的脱落功率。点状RMSE在两个流态中都选错了模型。

Conclusion: 代理模型的选择应遵循目标流动的动力学特征，验证方法应使用故障模式解析的指标，因为既不存在跨流态通用的获胜架构，也不存在可迁移的验证方式。

Abstract: A flow surrogate validated on a simple regime is often taken as evidence that the approach will carry to a richer one. We test this assumption on two transient flows under time-varying boundary conditions emulating the process startup: the three-dimensional slurry film in chemical-mechanical planarisation (CMP), a core semiconductor-manufacturing process, and the two-dimensional Karman vortex street (KVS) behind a cylinder. Eight surrogate models are compared on one shared evaluation pipeline, differing in whether they learn the full field or a latent representation, and whether they predict trajectories in one shot or step by step. No single architecture wins both regimes. On the film, a one-shot full-field model reconstructs the process-relevant cumulative wall shear stress to 3.2% relative error. On the wake, a latent autoregressive DeepONet retains 96% of the shedding power that direct and one-shot models damp to almost zero. The deciding axis is the treatment of time. The self-sustained wake requires the phase memory that autoregressive feedback provides, while the boundary-driven film rewards a direct map. Pointwise RMSE picks the wrong model in both regimes, so the evaluation scores five physical questions instead, the field, its structure, invented motion, amplitude, and timing. The trained surrogates answer queries $10^3$ to $10^4$ times faster than the finite-element solver, but the offline cost of the training simulations means they pay off from the first query beyond the training set for CMP and the third for the KVS. The choice of surrogate should follow the dynamical character of the target flow, and its validation should use failure-mode-resolved metrics, since neither the winning architecture nor its validation transfers.

</details>


### [297] [Parametric Sensitivity of POD Reduced-Order Models for Semilinear Evolution Equations with Applications to G-Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23212)
*Shengbo Ma,Luhao Xue,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化半线性发展方程的POD降阶模型，开发了灵敏度分析方法，证明了在参考参数下构建的POD基应用于邻近查询参数时，误差变化受相应参数模量的控制，且常数与POD维数、时间步数和扰动幅度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POD方法在参数化问题中面临困境：一个参数值下计算的基在另一参数值下可能不准确，而为每个参数重新计算基代价高昂。因此需要理解POD基对参数变化的灵敏度，以评估基复用的可靠性。

Method: 基于跨空间Lipschitz条件处理非线性算子（适用于不可微、梯度依赖的非线性），并结合双线性形式和非线性的参数依赖性连续模量，建立了参数化半线性发展方程（包括粘性G方程和粘性应变G方程）的POD灵敏度分析框架。

Result: 从理论上证明了：当参考参数下构建的POD基应用于邻近查询参数时，误差变化由参数模量控制，且关键常数与POD维数、时间步数、扰动幅度无关。数值实验验证了模量依赖的灵敏度行为，展示了基复用的实际鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该灵敏度分析为POD基在参数空间中的可移植性提供了理论保证，表明对于邻近参数，无需重新计算POD基即可获得可控的误差，显著降低了多查询场景的计算成本。

Abstract: Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) provides low-dimensional surrogate models of evolution equations from solution snapshots. In parameterized problems, however, a basis computed at one parameter value need not remain accurate at another, while recomputing the basis for every parameter in a many-query study is costly. We develop a sensitivity analysis for POD reduced-order models of a class of parameterized semilinear evolution equations that includes the viscous G-equation and a viscous strain G-equation in their mean-free formulations. The analysis is based on a cross-space Lipschitz condition for the nonlinear operator, which accommodates the nonsmooth, gradient-dependent nonlinearities of these flame-propagation models, and on continuity moduli quantifying the parameter dependence of the bilinear form and of the nonlinearity. We prove that when a POD basis constructed at a reference parameter is applied to nearby query parameters, the resulting error variation is controlled by the corresponding parameter modulus, with constants independent of the POD dimension, the number of time steps, and the perturbation magnitude. Numerical experiments are consistent with the predicted modulus-dependent sensitivity behavior, and illustrate the practical robustness of basis reuse for nearby parameters.

</details>


### [298] [A vectorial lattice Boltzmann scheme for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24059)
*Denise Aregba-Driollet,Thomas Bellotti,Roberto Natalini,Tommaso Tenna*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于不可压缩Navier-Stokes系统的二阶精度矢量格子Boltzmann格式，该格式受Carfora和Natalini提出的离散速度动力学近似启发。通过与松弛方案的对比，在线性化情形下进行谱分析，并在真实非线性问题上进行数值验证，展示了该方案的优缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Carfora和Natalini离散速度动力学近似的启发，旨在为不可压缩Navier-Stokes系统引入一种二阶精度的矢量格子Boltzmann格式，并系统评估其相对松弛方案的性能差异。

Method: 基于Carfora和Natalini的离散速度动力学近似构建矢量格子Boltzmann格式，通过线性化情形下的谱分析进行理论比较，并在真实非线性问题上进行数值验证。

Result: 通过谱分析和非线性数值验证，揭示了该格式相对于松弛方案的优势与不足。

Conclusion: 该矢量格子Boltzmann格式在二阶精度下有效求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes系统，但与松弛方案相比各有优劣，需根据具体问题权衡选择。

Abstract: We introduce a second-order accurate vectorial lattice Boltzmann scheme for the incompressible Navier-Stokes system, inspired by a discrete-velocity kinetic approximation proposed by Carfora and Natalini [ESAIM: M2AN, 42(1), 93-112, 2008]. Advantages and drawbacks compared to relaxation schemes are investigated by providing spectral analyses in the linearized case, and numerical validations on the genuinely non-linear problem.

</details>


### [299] [An a Posteriori Error Estimator for $C^0$ IPG Approximation of Multiple and Clustered Eigenvalues of the Biharmonic Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23215)
*Jianing Guo,Qigang Liang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对双调和算子的多重/聚类特征值问题，基于C^0内部惩罚Galerkin离散化，提出并分析了一种可有效捕捉局部奇异性、在自适应过程中发挥关键作用的后验误差估计器。通过发展鲁棒的聚类投影分析并引入辅助理论误差估计器，实现了不变子空间误差与可计算残差估计器之间的关联，所得到的可靠性和效率界对网格尺寸、网格层次及目标簇内部谱间隙均具有鲁棒性。数值实验验证了理论结果及估计器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和算子的特征值问题中，当特征值出现多重或聚类情况时，传统的误差估计方法难以有效处理。现有方法在网格尺寸、网格层次及内部谱间隙变化时鲁棒性不足，且难以准确捕捉目标特征函数的局部奇异性。因此需要一种适用于聚类特征值场景、对多种参数具有鲁棒性的后验误差估计器，以指导自适应网格细化过程。

Method: 基于C^0内部惩罚Galerkin（C^0IPG）离散化框架，提出了一种后验误差估计器。发展了基于聚类投影的严格分析框架，引入辅助理论误差估计器作为桥梁，建立了不变子空间误差（即聚类特征空间与近似空间之间的误差）与可计算残差估计器之间的定量关系。通过理论分析保证了可靠性和效率界对网格尺寸h、网格层级以及目标簇内部特征值间隙的鲁棒性。

Result: 所提出的后验误差估计器能够有效捕捉目标特征函数的局部奇异性，在自适应网格细化中发挥重要作用。理论上证明了该估计器的可靠性和效率界对网格尺寸、网格层次及目标簇内部谱间隙均具有鲁棒性。数值实验验证了理论分析的正确性，并展示了所提估计器在实际计算中的鲁棒表现。

Conclusion: 本文为双调和算子的多重及聚类特征值问题提供了一种鲁棒的后验误差估计方法，填补了该领域在聚类特征值场景下自适应误差估计的理论空白。该估计器在指导自适应网格细化方面具有实用价值，其鲁棒性保证了在复杂谱结构下仍能可靠地驱动网格自适应过程。

Abstract: In this paper, based on the $C^0$ interior penalty Galerkin ($C^0$IPG) discretization, we propose and analyze an a posteriori error estimator for eigenfunctions associated with multiple and clustered eigenvalues of the biharmonic operator. The proposed estimator may capture the local singularities of the target eigenfunctions efficiently and play an important role in adaptive procedures. We develop a rigorous cluster-projection-based analysis and introduce an auxiliary theoretical error estimator to connect the invariant subspace error with a computable residual estimator. Most importantly, the resulting reliability and efficiency bounds are robust with respect to the mesh size, the mesh level and the internal spectral gaps within the target cluster. Numerical experiments support the theoretical results and demonstrate the robustness of the proposed estimator.

</details>


### [300] [Mechanics-trained neural coordinate mapping for B-spline analysis of crack-tip and corner singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23229)
*Hyunju Kim*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络训练的径向坐标映射方法，用于改善裂纹尖端或重入角附近高阶样条的收敛性。该方法通过训练正神经网络密度来学习径向坐标映射，无需预先指定奇异指数或分级参数，在标量和平面应变B样条公式中测试，相比恒等映射将相对能量范数误差降低了33.42倍，并有效恢复了混合模式应力强度因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂纹尖端或重入角附近的分数阶径向幂会导致导数无界，降低高阶样条的收敛速度。传统奇异映射需要预先指定奇异指数和分级参数，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 将径向坐标训练为正向神经网络密度的归一化积分，固定径向边界并确保远离塌缩尖端时 r'(s)>0。从离散平衡状态下的Galerkin能量推断径向分级指数和密度校正权重，无需指数标签或精确内部场。

Result: 在156个向量自由度下，力学训练映射相比恒等映射将相对能量范数误差降低33.42倍。三个轮廓上恢复的混合模式应力强度因子最大误差为1.923×10^(-5)。对于直线裂纹，密度校正消失，预设的r=s^2映射给出相同改进。对于训练区间外的非线性Robin族，密度校正保持非零，固定q增量能量增益至少为4.839。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经校正方法在所需坐标无法由预设幂映射表示时发挥重要作用，为裂纹尖端等奇异性问题的自适应网格分级提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Near a crack tip or re-entrant corner, fractional radial powers can have unbounded derivatives and slow the convergence of high-order splines. A singular mapping grades the computational radius so that the pulled-back field is smoother without changing the physical domain. Classical maps require the singular exponent and grading in advance. Here the radial coordinate is trained from the mechanics problem. It is the normalized integral of a positive neural density, which fixes both radial boundaries and ensures r'(s)>0 away from the collapsed tip. The radial grading exponent and density-correction weights are inferred from Galerkin energies evaluated at discrete equilibrium, without exponent labels or exact interior fields. We test the mapping in scalar and plane-strain B-spline formulations and compare it with the identity map, radially graded knot vectors, prescribed power maps, and an adaptive enriched B-spline method. At 156 vector degrees of freedom, the mechanics-trained map reduces the relative energy-norm error by a factor of 33.42 compared with the identity map. The maximum error in the mixed-mode stress intensity factors recovered on 3 contours is 1.923 x 10^(-5). For the straight crack, the learned density correction vanishes, and the prescribed r=s^2 map gives the same improvement. For a nonlinear Robin family with test parameters outside the training interval, the density correction remains nonzero and gives a fixed-q incremental energy gain of at least 4.839. Thus, for the problems considered here, the neural correction is useful when the required coordinate is not represented by a prescribed power map.

</details>


### [301] [A Least Squares Weak Galerkin Framework for Linear Elasticity on Polytopal Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23279)
*Chunmei Wang,Shangyou Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线弹性问题，提出并分析了一种最小二乘弱伽辽金（LS-WG）有限元方法。该方法通过弱微分算子定义在弱有限元空间上，避免了离散inf-sup条件，适用于复杂边界和内部界面。算法对称正定，在近不可压缩条件下表现稳健，支持一般多边形/多面体网格。理论证明了数值解的唯一性并给出了最优阶误差估计，数值实验验证了收敛率、稳定性和无闭锁特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线弹性问题的有限元求解面临两个主要挑战：一是离散inf-sup条件的限制（尤其在近不可压缩情形下易出现体积闭锁），二是对复杂网格（多边形/多面体）的几何适应性不足。本文旨在通过最小二乘弱伽辽金方法克服这些困难，提供一种兼顾理论保障与工程适用性的数值方案。

Method: 采用最小二乘弱伽辽金（LS-WG）框架，在弱有限元空间上定义弱梯度、弱应变张量、弱散度等弱微分算子。构建对称正定的最小二乘变分形式，避免了对离散inf-sup条件的要求。通过能量范数进行理论分析，并在一般多面体网格上实现数值离散。

Result: 理论方面：证明了数值解的唯一性，并给出了关于离散能量范数的最优阶误差估计。数值方面：大量实验验证了所提方法的理论收敛阶，在近不可压缩条件下表现出良好的稳定性、计算效率和无闭锁性能。

Conclusion: 所提LS-WG方法在线弹性问题中兼具数学严谨性与工程实用性：对称正定保证了求解效率，回避inf-sup条件使得近不可压缩情形无闭锁，而多面体网格支持极大地增强了几何灵活性。该方法为复杂线弹性问题提供了一种可靠、稳健且灵活的数值工具。

Abstract: This paper develops and analyzes a least-squares weak Galerkin (LS-WG) finite element method for linear elasticity. By employing weak differential operators, specifically the weak gradient, weak strain tensor, and weak divergence, defined on weak finite element spaces, the proposed framework facilitates the treatment of complex boundary conditions and internal interfaces while avoiding the restrictive discrete inf-sup condition. The resulting formulation is symmetric and positive definite and exhibits robust numerical performance in the nearly incompressible regime. In addition, the proposed method offers exceptional geometric flexibility, allowing implementation on general polytopal (polygonal and polyhedral) meshes. We establish the uniqueness of the numerical solution and derive optimal-order error estimates with respect to a tailored discrete energy norm. Extensive numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate the method's stability, efficiency, and locking-free performance for nearly incompressible materials.

</details>


### [302] [Scalably computing metric magnitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23354)
*Steve Huntsman,Jewell Thomas,Cynthia Ukawu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量幅度的数值计算方法，重点解决稠密线性代数在大规模计算中的可扩展性问题，提出了基于分层低秩求解器的方法，并开发了支持CUDA和MPI的容器化管道，可在单台工作站上处理10^5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量幅度的应用通常需要精确数值结果以利用其与信息论的联系，但现有方法在处理大规模数据时面临稠密线性代数的计算瓶颈，亟需可扩展的数值方法。

Method: 对比多种稠密线性代数缩放方法，确定分层低秩求解器为首选方案；开发容器化CUDA-enabled C++/MPI管道实现并行化。

Result: 在单台高性能工作站上可处理10^5规模的点，通过CUDA/MPI管道可进一步扩展至更大规模。

Conclusion: 分层低秩求解器是解决度量幅度计算中稠密线性代数可扩展性的有效途径，所提管道为实现大规模计算提供了实际可行的方案。

Abstract: Applications of metric magnitude often rely on numerically exact results in order to exploit a connection with information theory. We examine various approaches for scaling the dense linear algebra involved and identify hierarchical low-rank solvers as a preferred approach, with a clear path to scales of $10^5$ points on a single powerful workstation, and larger scales using our containerized CUDA-enabled C++/MPI pipeline.

</details>


### [303] [What is Jackson's constant?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23375)
*Rikhav Shah,John Urschel,Nicholas West*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对Jackson定理中Lipschitz函数三角多项式逼近的领先误差项进行了精确刻画，并分析了核多项式方法中一种优于Jackson构造的相关构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jackson定理是逼近论中的经典结果，但此前其领先误差项的具体形式尚未被精确刻画。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，同时分析核多项式方法中常用的一种改进构造。

Method: 通过精细化的数学分析，对Jackson定理中的构造以及核多项式方法中常用的相关构造进行误差项的精确定量化研究。

Result: 精确刻画了Jackson定理中三角多项式逼近Lipschitz函数的领先误差项，并对核多项式方法中使用的改进构造给出了同样的精确刻画。

Conclusion: 本文完善了Jackson定理的理论分析，为核多项式方法在谱密度估计中的应用提供了更精确的误差理论支持。

Abstract: We prove a refinement of Jackson's theorem on the approximation of Lipschitz functions by trigonometric polynomials. Our result precisely characterizes the leading error term associated with Jackson's construction. We do the same for a related construction commonly used in the kernel polynomial method for spectral density estimation, which is slightly better than Jackson's construction in this respect.

</details>


### [304] [The symmetric V-cycle can diverge under the multigrid axioms for cell-centred discretisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23391)
*Ming Hei Wong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在满足Galerkin条件(G3)和Bramble型弱逼近性质(A2)_α的假设下，标准对称V-cycle多重网格方法可能不收敛，而W-cycle和可变V-cycle则一致收敛。通过两种构造证实：一是对任意平滑次数m，存在任意深度层级使V-cycle谱半径大于1；二是一维扩散方程的单元中心有限体积离散中，系数跳跃κ≥3时对称V(1,1)-cycle发散。数值实验验证了所有结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多重网格方法中一个长期悬而未决的理论问题：在满足(G3)和(A2)_α假设下，标准对称V-cycle的一致收敛性是否成立。此前已知W-cycle和可变V-cycle在此条件下一致收敛，但V-cycle的收敛性未知。

Method: 通过两种反例构造：1) 对任意平滑次数m，构造满足所有假设的任意深度层级，其对称V(m,m)-cycle误差算子的谱半径>1且随深度几何增长；2) 一维扩散方程单元中心有限体积离散，含网格对齐系数跳跃1:κ和调和(Samarskii)界面平均，精确满足(G3)和(A2)_{1/2}，但κ≥3时对称V(1,1)-cycle发散。两种构造中W-cycle仍一致压缩。

Result: 标准对称V-cycle在给定假设下不保证一致收敛；恢复一致收敛所需的平滑次数阈值m_0至少与C_{A2}^2成正比增长；理论上分离了V-cycle与W-cycle的收敛性表现；数值结果验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 标准对称V-cycle与W-cycle在相同假设下表现出本质不同的收敛行为，揭示了二者理论性质的根本差异；实际应用中需谨慎使用对称V-cycle，尤其当系数具有大跳跃或平滑次数较小时。

Abstract: The axiomatic convergence theory for multigrid methods applied to cell-centred finite-difference and finite-volume discretisations rests on two hypotheses: an imbalanced Galerkin condition (G3), which states that $R_{\ell-1}A_\ell P_{\ell-1}=2A_{\ell-1}$ with $R_{\ell-1}=\frac{1}{2}P_{\ell-1}^T$, and a weak approximation property $(A2)_α$ of Bramble type. Under these hypotheses, together with Richardson smoothing, the symmetric W-cycle and the variable V-cycle are known to be uniformly convergent, while the uniform convergence of the standard symmetric V-cycle has remained open. We answer this in the negative by two constructions. First, for every smoothing count $m$ we exhibit hierarchies of every depth satisfying (G3), Richardson admissibility with $C_R=1$, and $(A2)_α$ for every $α\in(0,1]$ with the sharp level-independent constant $C_{A2}^2=4m$, whose symmetric $V(m,m)$-cycle error operator has spectral radius $θ_m(1+2θ_m)>1$ already on three levels, where $θ_m=(1-\frac{1}{4m})^{2m}$, and growing geometrically with the depth; the family shows that any smoothing-count threshold $m_0$ that could restore uniform V-cycle convergence must grow at least quadratically in $C_{A2}$. Second, we prove that the same failure occurs in a completely standard discretisation: the cell-centred finite-volume hierarchy for a one-dimensional diffusion equation with a mesh-aligned coefficient jump $1:κ$ and harmonic (Samarskii) interface averaging satisfies (G3) exactly and $(A2)_{1/2}$ with a level-independent constant $C_{A2}=O(κ)$, yet for every $κ\ge3$ its symmetric $V(1,1)$-cycle with any admissible Richardson parameter, including the optimal one, diverges geometrically in the number of levels. In both constructions the W-cycle remains uniformly contractive, so the hypotheses separate the two cycles. All claims are verified numerically.

</details>


### [305] [Adaptive Consistency for Mimetic Finite Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23568)
*Yoo Jin Cha,Omar Duran,Nicola Castelletto,Victor A. P. Magri,Hamdi A. Tchelepi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于残差一致性指示符的自适应MFD框架，通过用户预设容差τ在TPFA和MFD之间动态选择，在保持结构稳定性的前提下显著降低计算成本，且精度与全MFD相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFD方法计算成本高（局部算子稠密、全局稀疏性降低），而TPFA虽廉价但仅适用于K-正交网格。为平衡精度与效率，需要一种自适应框架。

Method: 基于离散本构方程推导残差一致性指示符，通过用户预设容差τ实现TPFA/MFD的自适应模板选择，且在仿射框架内保证任意分区的稳定性和结构保持性。

Result: 理论分析证明了均匀强制性和容差控制的相对通量误差收敛性。数值实验表明，在复杂多面体油藏基准上，精度与全MFD相当，同时显著降低矩阵密度和计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应MFD框架有效平衡了MFD的高精度与TPFA的高效率，在保持结构保持特性的前提下，通过一致性指示符实现了计算代价的大幅降低。

Abstract: The mimetic finite difference (MFD) method provides a robust discretization for flow simulation on general polyhedral meshes, but its computational cost can become significant due to dense local operators and reduced global sparsity. While the two-point flux approximation (TPFA) offers a substantially cheaper alternative, its accuracy is generally restricted to $K$-orthogonal grids. To balance these competing considerations, we present an adaptive MFD framework based on a residual-based consistency indicator derived from the discrete constitutive equations. The indicator measures local inconsistency and enables adaptive TPFA/MFD stencil selection through a user-prescribed tolerance $τ$. Because the adaptation is performed within a mimetic framework, arbitrary TPFA/MFD partitions remain stable and structure preserving. Theoretical analysis establishes uniform coercivity and proves explicit tolerance-controlled convergence of the relative flux error. Numerical experiments on challenging polyhedral reservoir benchmarks demonstrate accuracy comparable to full MFD discretizations while substantially reducing matrix density and computational cost.

</details>


### [306] [Optimal Polynomial Tractability Exponents for the Inverse Star Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23571)
*Josef Dick*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了星偏差逆$n^\ast(d,\varepsilon)$的上界$d\varepsilon^{-2}$中指数$2$是最优的：沿多项式带$d \asymp \varepsilon^{-\alpha}$，下界达到$c d\varepsilon^{-(2-\alpha)}$量级，即$\varepsilon^{-2}$阶。这表明Heinrich等人上界中的指数$p=2$和$q=1$各自不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星偏差逆$n^\ast(d,\varepsilon)$的已知上下界为$d\varepsilon^{-1} \lesssim n^{\ast}(d,\varepsilon) \lesssim d\varepsilon^{-2}$，但$\varepsilon$的精确指数尚未确定。本文旨在证明上界中指数$2$的不可改进性。

Method: 对任意$0<\alpha<1$和固定$0<A\le B$，在满足$A\varepsilon^{-\alpha}\le d\le B\varepsilon^{-\alpha}$的整数$d$上，构造下界$n^\ast(d,\varepsilon)\ge c_{\alpha,B}\,d\,\varepsilon^{-(2-\alpha)}$，该量级为$\varepsilon^{-2}$。

Result: 证明了沿多项式带$d \asymp \varepsilon^{-\alpha}$，下界达到$\varepsilon^{-2}$阶，从而上界$d\varepsilon^{-2}$中$\varepsilon$的指数$2$是最优的。

Conclusion: 结合Hinrichs (2004)的下界（迫使$q\ge 1$），表明Heinrich–Novak–Wasilkowski–Woźniakowski上界中的指数$p=2$和$q=1$各自都是最优的。

Abstract: The inverse of the star discrepancy $n^\ast(d, \varepsilon)$ satisfies \[ d \varepsilon^{-1} \lesssim n^{\ast}(d,\varepsilon)\lesssim d\varepsilon^{-2} \] for all $d \in \mathbb{N}$ and $0 < \varepsilon < \varepsilon_0$. The upper bound was shown by Heinrich, Novak, Wasilkowski and Woźniakowski (2001) and the lower bound was shown by Hinrichs (2004). A proof of this lower bound using elementary arguments was shown by Steinerberger (2023). These bounds show that the inverse of the star discrepancy depends linearly on the dimension, but the exact exponent of $\varepsilon^{-1}$ has remained open.
  In this paper we prove a lower bound which shows that the exponent $2$ of $\varepsilon^{-1}$ in the upper bound cannot be improved. More precisely, we show that for every $0<α<1$ and fixed $0<A\le B$, there are constants $c_{α,B}>0$ and $\varepsilon_{α,A}>0$ such that for every integer $d$ satisfying \[
  A\varepsilon^{-α}\le d\le B\varepsilon^{-α} \] we have \[ n^{\ast}(d,\varepsilon)
  \ge c_{α,B}\,d\,\varepsilon^{-(2-α)}, \] for all $0 < \varepsilon < \varepsilon_{α, A}$. Along these polynomial strips the right-hand side is of order $\varepsilon^{-2}$.
  It follows that every uniform polynomial upper estimate $n^{\ast}(d,\varepsilon)\le C d^q\varepsilon^{-p}$ must satisfy $p \ge 2$. Together with the lower bound of Hinrichs (2004), which forces $q \ge 1$, this shows that the exponents $p=2$ and $q=1$ in the Heinrich--Novak--Wasilkowski--Woźniakowski upper bound are individually optimal.

</details>


### [307] [Random Indexing for Image Change Detection: A Distance-Threshold Vocabulary Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23609)
*Cristiano Tamborrino*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文探索将文本分析中的随机索引（RI）机制移植到多时相遥感影像变化检测中。针对k-means构建视觉词汇表不稳定的问题，提出基于距离阈值（领导聚类）的词汇构建方法，并从覆盖/填充性质给出稳定性半径的形式化论证。结合空间上下文累积，形成免训练的变化检测流程，在四个双时相遥感数据集上接近但未超越变化向量分析基线。多种子验证暴露了概率稀疏RI的退化向量故障并修复，但发现了领导聚类访问顺序敏感性的未解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将文本分析中成熟的随机索引（RI）机制移植到多时相遥感影像变化检测领域，探索一种无需训练的变化检测新方法。

Method: 采用距离阈值（领导聚类）替代k-means构建视觉词汇表，给出基于词汇覆盖/填充性质的稳定性半径形式化论证；结合空间上下文累积构建免训练检测流程；采用多种子验证暴露并修复退化向量故障。

Result: 在农业、河流、城市极化SAR、野火四个双时相遥感数据集上，所提方法一致接近但未超越变化向量分析（CVA）基线；多种子验证暴露并修复了概率稀疏RI的退化向量故障；发现了领导聚类访问顺序敏感性的未解问题，三种修正尝试均未能消除。

Conclusion: 随机索引机制可通过领导聚类词汇构建移植到遥感变化检测，性能接近但未超越CVA基线；访问顺序敏感性是主要待解决问题，RI的增量累积特性对未来长时间序列监测有潜在价值。

Abstract: Random Indexing (RI), used almost exclusively in text analysis (notably Temporal Random Indexing, TRI, for tracking word meaning over time), represents a discrete vocabulary with fixed random vectors and accumulates context by vector summation. We explore transplanting this mechanism to change detection in multitemporal images. A naive transplant using k-means to build the visual vocabulary is unstable: pixels are frequently reassigned between acquisition dates due to small radiometric shifts, destroying the pixel-to-vector correspondence RI depends on. We propose a distance-threshold (leader) clustering vocabulary instead, and give a short formal argument -- a stability radius from the vocabulary covering/packing properties -- for why this is provably more robust to acquisition noise than k-means. Combined with spatial context accumulation, this yields a training-free change detection pipeline, evaluated on four bi-temporal remote-sensing datasets (agriculture, river, urban PolSAR, wildfire) against a Change Vector Analysis baseline, which it consistently approaches but does not surpass. Multi-seed validation exposed and let us fix a degenerate-vector failure mode in a probabilistic-sparsity RI variant, and revealed a second, unresolved source of variability -- sensitivity to the leader-clustering visitation order -- which we characterize but, despite three attempted corrections, could not eliminate, reporting it as the main open problem. Results are cross-checked against an independent re-implementation. We report an ablation, a hyperparameter sensitivity analysis, an Otsu-vs-Gaussian-mixture thresholding comparison, and discuss RI incremental accumulation for future long time-series monitoring.

</details>


### [308] [Stability and error analysis of IMEX-BDFk finite element schemes for the incompressible Navier-Stokes system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23635)
*Qianqian Ding,Yifan Luo,Shipeng Mao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对无滑移边界条件的不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，提出并分析了一类高阶全离散有限元数值格式。时间离散采用k阶(k=1~6)隐式-显式向后差分公式(IMEX-BDFk)，空间离散采用Taylor-Hood有限元。建立了数值解的稳定性和一致有界性，并在无CFL条件限制下证明了时空最优误差估计。三维情形下给出了速度的L2和H1范数误差估计以及压力的L2范数误差估计，时间收敛阶可达六阶。数值实验验证了格式的有效性和理论收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的高精度数值求解需求，现有方法在时间高阶精度与稳定性之间难以兼顾，且通常受限于CFL条件（时间步长依赖空间网格尺寸）。本文旨在设计一种时间精度可达六阶、空间采用有限元离散、且无CFL条件限制的稳定数值格式。

Method: 时间离散采用k阶隐式-显式向后差分公式(IMEX-BDFk)，其中非线性对流项显式处理，线性Stokes部分隐式处理；空间离散采用Taylor-Hood有限元对。通过能量分析和先验估计建立数值解的稳定性和有界性，并采用对偶论证等技术推导无CFL条件下的最优误差估计。

Result: 建立了数值解的稳定性和一致有界性；在无CFL条件（时间步长独立于空间网格尺寸）下，证明了三维情形速度的L2和H1范数最优误差估计以及压力的L2范数最优误差估计，时间收敛阶可达一阶至六阶；数值实验验证了理论收敛阶和格式有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于IMEX-BDFk与Taylor-Hood有限元的高阶全离散格式能有效求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，兼具高阶时间精度和无CFL条件约束的优势，为高精度流体数值模拟提供了理论保证和实用方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and analyze a class of high-order numerical schemes within a fully discrete finite element framework for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations with no-slip boundary conditions. The temporal discretization employs a kth-order (k=1,...,6) implicit-explicit backward difference formula (IMEX-BDFk), in which the nonlinear convection term is treated explicitly and the linear Stokes part implicitly, whereas the spatial discretization utilizes Taylor-Hood finite elements. We establish the stability and uniform boundedness of the numerical solution. We further establish optimal order error estimates in both space and time without any CFL-type condition, in the sense that the time step is independent of the spatial mesh size. In three dimensions, these include L2- and H1-norm error estimates for the velocity and L2-norm error estimates for the pressure, with temporal convergence rates up to sixth order for all variables. Numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the scheme and to confirm the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [309] [Explicit Runge approximation for Helmholtz equation with cylindrical harmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23699)
*Yu Chen,Jin Cheng,Tingyue Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Helmholtz方程，利用柱谐函数研究了空间依赖的定量Runge逼近，显式导出了三圆构型的相关指数，并得到了一般几何设置下的渐近指数，为实施正则化方法和构建谱精度数值逼近提供了关键的范数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Runge逼近在反问题和基于学习的数值方法中具有重要应用，但该问题是病态的，需要显式的定量估计来刻画逼近解的增长与外推距离之间的关系，现有研究尚缺乏对Helmholtz方程空间依赖情形的系统分析。

Method: 利用柱谐函数展开，对Helmholtz方程的内边值问题和外边值问题分别建立空间依赖的定量Runge逼近；通过三圆构型显式推导相关指数，并推广至一般几何设置获得渐近指数。

Result: 显式导出了三圆构型下的相关指数，获得了一般几何设置下的渐近指数，并给出了展开系数的范数估计。

Conclusion: 所建立的界为实施正则化方法提供了理论依据，同时能够用于构造Helmholtz方程解的高谱精度数值逼近。

Abstract: Originating from the complex approximation of holomorphic functions, the Runge approximation for elliptic equations has evolved into a fundamental tool for inverse problems and even learning-based numerical methods since its proposition by Lax, Malgrange, with quantitative characterizations further established by Rüland and Salo. It should be remarked here that Runge approximation is ill-posed. In numerical analysis, explicit quantitative estimates are required to characterize the dependence of the approximant's growth on the outward continuation distance of the original solution. This paper investigates the spatial dependent quantitative Runge approximation for the Helmholtz equation using cylindrical harmonics, considering both interior and exterior boundary value problems. We explicitly derive the relevant indices for the three-circle configuration and obtain asymptotic indices for general geometric settings. The derived results provide norm estimates for the expansion coefficients, which are crucial for the implementation of regularization methods. Furthermore, the established bounds enable the construction of spectrally accurate numerical approximations for solutions to the Helmholtz equation.

</details>


### [310] [A spectral-vanishing-viscosity stabilization of a higher-order consistent splitting scheme for the Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23720)
*M Nader Alhomsi,Akram Moustafa,Mohammad Al-Saqqa,Jiahong Wu,Xiaoming Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高雷诺数下BDF-IMEX分裂格式的数值不稳定性，提出了一种基于方向性Maday-Kaber-Tadmor核的谱消失粘性稳定化方法，在不增加渐近计算成本的前提下，建立了粘性无关的稳定性与误差估计，并通过二维算例验证了k=2,3,4阶格式的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Huang和Shen提出的高阶BDF-IMEX分裂格式在任意粘性下的误差上界系数包含粘性的负幂次，导致高雷诺数下格式可能失效。本文旨在通过稳定化手段挽救该格式在高雷诺数下的数值稳定性，同时保持误差分析框架的完整性。

Method: 在速度更新步中引入基于方向性Maday-Kaber-Tadmor核构建的对称半正定谱消失粘性算子，选择性耗散高波数欠分辨模态，且不增加渐近计算成本。在经典误差分析框架下建立稳定化格式的稳定性和误差估计，证明谱消失粘性提供了粘性无关的高模态强制控制。

Result: 三个二维数值实验表明：(1) 对于制造解，稳定化格式在k=2,3,4阶保持设计精度，而未稳定化格式发散；(2) 对于扰动Kovasznay流（Re=10^4），稳定化格式准确分辨边界层并驱动扰动回归稳态，未稳定化格式爆炸；(3) 对于Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性问题，稳定化格式在整个可靠区间复现参考积分诊断，未稳定化格式产生伪解或爆炸。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱消失粘性稳定化方法有效解决了高阶BDF-IMEX分裂格式在高雷诺数下的崩溃问题，在保持格式原有精度和误差分析结构的同时，实现了粘性无关的数值稳定性，为高雷诺数不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的高阶时间离散提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Huang and Shen developed a novel class of high-order BDF-IMEX consistent-splitting schemes for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations, giving the first rigorous stability and error analysis for a fully decoupled splitting scheme of temporal order higher than two. Extending their analysis from unit viscosity to arbitrary viscosity, this work reveals that the error upper bound coefficient contains inverse powers of the viscosity. Our numerical experiments show that the scheme can break down at high Reynolds number. To save the scheme from this failure, we stabilize it by adding to the velocity update a symmetric positive-semidefinite spectral vanishing viscosity operator, built from the directionally applied Maday-Kaber-Tadmor kernel, which selectively damps the high, under-resolved modes at no additional asymptotic cost and leaves the structure of the error analysis intact. We establish stability and error estimates for the stabilized scheme in which the spectral vanishing viscosity provides viscosity-independent coercive control of the high modes. Three two-dimensional tests demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of the stabilized scheme. For a manufactured solution, the stabilized scheme retains its design order for k=2,3,4, whereas the unstabilized scheme diverges. For a perturbed Kovasznay flow, it accurately resolves the boundary layer at Re=10^4 and drives the perturbation back to the steady state, while the unstabilized scheme blows up. For the Kelvin-Helmholtz instability problem, it reproduces the reference integral diagnostics throughout the reliable regime, whereas the unstabilized scheme produces spurious solutions or blows up.

</details>


### [311] [Implicit Tensor-Train Cross Integration of High-Dimensional Nonlinear PDEs via Fiber-Dependency Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23814)
*Behzad Ghahremani,Hessam Babaee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对张量列（TT）交叉方法在隐式时间积分中面临的“纤维依赖导致方程组不封闭”的障碍，提出了一种基于交叉插值恒等式的纤维依赖消除框架，将相邻纤维表示为交叉选取纤维的线性组合，从而构建出封闭的配置系统。该方法适用于线性和非线性高维PDE，可与牛顿迭代和秩自适应自然结合，数值实验表明其收敛迅速、保持隐式多步格式的时间精度，并能高效处理含高达10^55自由度的全阶离散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TT-cross秩截断方法局限于显式时间积分，无法直接扩展到隐式格式。隐式格式中，交叉选取的纤维对应的配置方程依赖于不属于未知量集合的相邻纤维，导致非线性方程组不封闭，无法直接应用标准隐式求解器。因此，需要一种方法消除这种纤维依赖，使得隐式TT-cross成为可能。

Method: 提出纤维依赖消除框架：通过交叉插值恒等式（cross interpolation identities），将相邻纤维表示为交叉选取纤维的线性组合，从而将原本不封闭的配置系统转变为封闭的非线性系统。该框架与牛顿迭代和秩自适应结合，适用于线性和非线性高维PDE。

Result: 数值实验表明：（1）依赖消除迭代快速收敛；（2）保持隐式多步格式的时间精度；（3）能高效处理全阶离散包含高达10^55自由度的非线性高维问题。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将TT-cross方法从显式推广至隐式格式，克服了纤维依赖导致的不封闭问题，同时保留了TT-cross方法的原有优势（无需切空间投影、避免近奇异低秩因子数值困难、天然适配任意非线性），为高维张量微分方程的隐式求解提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Tensor-train (TT) representations have emerged as an effective framework for mitigating the curse of dimensionality in the numerical solution of high-dimensional tensor differential equations. Among existing approaches, TT-cross methods are particularly attractive because they require only pointwise evaluations of the governing equations, naturally accommodate arbitrary nonlinearities, and avoid tangent-space projections and the numerical difficulties associated with nearly singular low-rank factors. However, existing TT-cross rank-truncation methods have been restricted to explicit time integration. Extending TT-cross methods to implicit schemes presents an obstacle: the collocation equations associated with the cross-selected fibers depend on neighboring fibers that are not part of the unknown set. Consequently, the resulting nonlinear system is not closed, preventing the direct application of standard implicit solvers. In this work, we introduce a principled fiber-dependency elimination framework that resolves this obstacle by expressing neighboring fibers as linear combinations of the cross-selected fibers through cross interpolation identities. The resulting formulation produces a closed collocation system while preserving the principal advantages of TT-cross methods. The proposed framework applies to both linear and nonlinear high-dimensional partial differential equations and is naturally combined with Newton iterations and rank adaptivity. Numerical experiments demonstrate rapid convergence of the dependency-elimination iterations, preservation of the temporal accuracy of implicit multistep schemes, and efficient implicit integration of high-dimensional nonlinear problems with full-order discretizations containing up to $10^{55}$ degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [312] [Goal-Oriented Error Estimation for Least-Squares Finite Element Methods via Physically Meaningful Adjoint PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23850)
*Yueyao Wu,Shun Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对一阶系统最小二乘有限元方法，提出了一种基于物理PDE伴随而非最小二乘诱导伴随的面向目标的误差估计框架。物理伴随拥有自身的一阶通量系统和内置最小二乘估计器，误差恒等式基于原-对偶修正泛函，适用于任意协调逼近且无需Galerkin正交性。针对含加权Dirichlet边界通量的输出，构建了两种修正近似，并证明了原误差与伴随误差的乘积型估计。最小二乘泛函给出可计算的后验界和均衡标记指标，数值实验验证了收敛性和标记有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FOSLS方法的面向目标误差估计通常使用最小二乘诱导的伴随，但该伴随缺乏物理伴随所具有的一阶通量系统与内置最小二乘估计器。本文旨在建立基于物理伴随的误差估计框架，以利用这些天然优势获得更有效的后验误差估计和自适应标记策略。

Method: (1) 基于物理PDE伴随（而非最小二乘诱导伴随）建立原-对偶修正泛函； (2) 从连续原方程和伴随方程导出误差恒等式，无需Galerkin正交性； (3) 针对含加权Dirichlet边界通量的输出，构造两种修正近似（仅含势和含势与通量）； (4) 导出原误差与伴随误差的乘积型估计； (5) 利用最小二乘泛函构建可计算的后验界和均衡标记指标。

Result: 建立了基于物理伴随的误差恒等式和乘积型误差估计，获得了可计算的后验误差界及均衡标记指标（其单元和等于两估计器之积）。数值实验验证了收敛性和标记策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于物理伴随的面向目标误差估计框架在FOSLS方法中是可行且有效的，物理伴随的内置通量系统和最小二乘估计器使其优于最小二乘诱导伴随，为自适应FOSLS方法提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: We develop a goal-oriented error-estimation framework for first-order system least-squares (FOSLS) finite element methods based on the physical PDE adjoint rather than the adjoint induced by the least-squares formulation. Identified explicitly from the differential operator, its mixed boundary conditions, and the output, the physical adjoint admits its own first-order flux system and hence a native, built-in least-squares estimator, which the least-squares-induced adjoint does not. Our error identities rest on a primal-dual corrected functional: they follow from the continuous primal and adjoint equations alone, hold for arbitrary conforming approximations, and require no Galerkin orthogonality. For outputs containing a weighted Dirichlet-boundary flux, whose weight becomes the essential datum of the adjoint, we construct two corrected approximations, one from the potentials and one also using the fluxes, and prove product-type error estimates in the primal and adjoint errors. The least-squares functionals then yield computable a posteriori bounds and a balanced marking indicator whose element sum equals the product of the two estimators exactly. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence and the effectiveness of the marking.

</details>


### [313] [Recursive Governance: A Graph-Theoretic Framework for Risk Propagation and Drift Detection in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23916)
*Sriram Nagaraj,Advaith Nila Narayanan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Inventory-as-Code（IaC）治理循环框架，将模型清单从静态文档转变为动态架构组件，以应对金融机构从传统预测模型向自主智能体系统转型过程中模型风险管理（MRM）面临的结构性过时问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构从传统预测模型转向自主智能体系统，传统模型风险管理（MRM）中静态模型清单要求面临结构性过时，需要一种能够适应动态、自主性系统的治理框架。

Method: (1) 引入校准的自主动度（DoA）实质性评分，采用显式分类化工具复杂度加权方案解决朴素加性风险公式的支配问题；(2) 将有向无环图（DAG）构建智能体清单，定义复合风险传播算法，使上游验证失败对下游所有可达节点产生风险惩罚；(3) 开发基于集成思维链（CoT）嵌入的分布余弦漂移的轨迹监控协议，包括Golden Path基线的构建与认证、匹配自助法校准以防止假阳性伪影，以及区分合理推理变异与有害漂移的两阶段响应机制；(4) 处理LLM基模型版本变化、名义上无环智能体图中的潜在反馈回路、以及将验证前置的两次执行结构等实际问题。

Result: 提出了一套完整的动态模型清单治理框架，包括DoA实质性评分、DAG复合风险传播、CoT轨迹监控协议等核心组件，并解决了基模型版本变化、潜在反馈回路等先前文献中很少涉及的实践难题。

Conclusion: 该IaC治理循环框架能够有效应对自主智能体系统带来的模型风险管理挑战，将模型清单从静态文档转变为动态架构组件，为金融机构的模型风险管理转型提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: As financial institutions transition from traditional predictive models to autonomous agentic systems, the static model inventory requirements of traditional model risk management (MRM) face structural obsolescence. This paper proposes a dynamic Inventory-as-Code (IaC) governance loop that treats the model inventory as a living architectural component rather than a periodic documentation artifact. We make four principal contributions. First, we introduce a calibrated Degree of Autonomy (DoA) materiality score with an explicit, taxonomized tool-complexity weighting scheme that addresses the dominance problem of naive additive risk formulations. Second, we construct the agent inventory as a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) and define a formal Composite Risk Propagation algorithm under which upstream validation failures induce risk penalties on all reachable descendants. Third, we develop a Trajectory Monitoring protocol based on distributional cosine drift across ensembled Chain-of-Thought (CoT) embeddings, with an explicit procedure for constructing and certifying the Golden Path baseline, a matched-bootstrap calibration that we show is necessary to avoid a severe false-positive artifact in the naive alternative, and a two-stage response that separates legitimate reasoning variation from detrimental drift without demanding that a single exceedance event trigger an irreversible action. Fourth, we address practical complications largely absent from the prior literature: LLM base-model version changes, latent feedback loops in nominally acyclic agent graphs, and a two-pass execution structure that stages validation ahead of risk propagation.

</details>


### [314] [A multi-physics structure-preserving integrator with uniform error bounds for relativistic charged-particle dynamics under strong magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24058)
*Mengting Hu,Yifa Tang,Bin Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对强磁场下四维相对论带电粒子动力学系统，提出了一种显式多物理场保结构Strang分裂格式，该格式在最大排序尺度下能保持体积、能量和洛伦兹不变性，并对位置和平行速度分量具有二阶一致误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够同时保持多个物理不变量（体积、能量、洛伦兹不变性）且具有严格误差控制的数值格式，用于模拟强磁场中相对论带电粒子的动力学行为。

Method: 采用最大排序尺度对四维相对论带电粒子动力学系统进行建模，并基于Strang分裂方法设计显式多物理场保结构格式，通过理论分析证明其几何保持性质和误差收敛性。

Result: 所提格式同时保持了体积、能量和洛伦兹不变性，并对位置和平行速度分量达到了二阶一致误差界，理论结果通过数值实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为强磁场下相对论带电粒子的长期精确模拟提供了一种兼具保结构性和高精度的数值方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an explicit multi-physics structure-preserving Strang splitting scheme for a four-dimensional relativistic charged-particle dynamical system in the presence of a strong magnetic field under the maximal ordering scaling. The proposed scheme not only preserves volume, energy, and Lorentz invariance, but also yields second-order uniform error bounds for the position and the parallel velocity component. We present rigorous theoretical proofs for these geometric properties and the error convergence, which are subsequently validated by several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [315] [A note on the discrete Unmapped Tent Pitching for the heterogeneous wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24066)
*Marcella Bonazzoli,Gabriele Ciaramella,Ilario Mazzieri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对UTP（非映射帐篷搭建）算法在异构环境下的时空分解策略，通过离散层面观测发现，连续分析预测的最优分解策略与离散层面实际最优策略存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前在连续层面对UTP算法在异构环境下的时空分解策略进行了计算成本比较，但离散层面的最优策略可能与连续分析结果不同，需要进一步验证。

Method: 在离散层面进行数值观测，对比不同时空分解策略的计算成本，与连续分析结果进行对比。

Result: 离散层面的观测表明，最优时空分解策略与连续分析所预测的策略不同。

Conclusion: 连续分析得出的最优分解策略在离散层面不总是成立，离散层面需要重新评估最优策略。

Abstract: The Unmapped Tent Pitching (UTP) algorithm is a space-time domain decomposition method for the parallel solution of wave-type problems. We have recently extended UTP to heterogeneous settings and compared, at the continuous level, the computational cost of different space-time decompositions. In this note, we report discrete-level observations showing that the optimal decomposition strategy may differ from the one predicted by the continuous analysis.

</details>


### [316] [A convergent Scharfetter-Gummel scheme for a three-species drift-diffusion model for memristors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24086)
*Ansgar Jüngel,Zhiwei Sun,Sara Xhahysa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种用于半导体三物种漂移-扩散模型的全隐式Scharfetter-Gummel有限体积格式，该格式保结构，揭示了隐式Fisher信息分量，建立了离散解存在性、自由能不等式及收敛性，并通过二维数值模拟验证了忆阻器中的细丝形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对忆阻器器件中电子、空穴和氧空位三物种漂移-扩散模型，现有数值方案在保持物理结构（如自由能耗散）方面存在不足，需要一种能够严格保持热力学一致性的全隐式有限体积格式。

Method: 采用全隐式Scharfetter-Gummel有限体积格式，将SG通量重新表述为上风形式，揭示隐藏的Fisher信息分量；针对伯努利函数的退化性，引入额外的边棱度强制估计，建立局部和全局耗散估计。

Result: 证明了离散有限体积解的存在性、离散自由能不等式以及数值格式的收敛性。二维数值模拟成功展示了忆阻器中的细丝形成现象，并验证了格式的保结构性质。

Conclusion: 该全隐式Scharfetter-Gummel格式是一种保结构的可靠数值方法，适用于半导体三物种漂移-扩散模型，能够准确模拟忆阻器中的细丝形成动力学过程。

Abstract: A structure-preserving fully implicit Scharfetter-Gummel finite-volume scheme for a three-species drift-diffusion model for semiconductors is proposed and analyzed. The equations describe the evolution of the electron, hole, and oxygen vacancy densities in a (bounded) memristor device, coupled to the Poisson equation for the electric potential, with mixed-type boundary conditions. Recasting the Scharfetter-Gummel fluxes in an upwind form, a hidden Fisher information component is revealed. Owing to the degeneracy of the Bernoulli function appearing in the fluxes, additional edgewise coercivity estimates are required, leading to refined local and global dissipation estimates. Using these ideas, the existence of a discrete finite-volume solution, a discrete free energy inequality, and the convergence of the numerical scheme are established. Numerical simulations in two space dimensions confirm the structure-preserving properties of the scheme and illustrate the filament formation in a memristor device.

</details>


### [317] [Randomized Krylov-Projected Iterated Tikhonov Regularization for Large-Scale Ill-posed Problems Under A Posteriori Stopping Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24138)
*Ravi Verma,Harshit Bajpai,Ankik Kumar Giri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种新的随机迭代正则化框架 RIGKT 和 RIAT，用于求解大规模线性病态逆问题。它们结合了随机迭代 Tikhonov 正则化与 Krylov 子空间投影技术，采用 Golub-Kahan 双对角化（矩形系统）和 Arnoldi 分解（方系统），并引入了随机方程选择、自适应步长策略以及基于差异的贝叶斯后验早停规则。作者建立了完整的正则化分析，包括 Bregman 距离单调性、有限终止性、精确数据收敛性和噪声路径稳定性，并证明了停迭代几乎必然且均方收敛到真实解。二维 X 射线 CT 和图像去模糊实验表明该方法能在不同噪声水平下可靠重建结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性迭代正则化方法依赖固定迭代次数，缺乏针对随机化场景的理论框架和可实现的早停准则，难以高效处理大规模病态逆问题。

Method: 1) 将随机迭代 Tikhonov 正则化与 Krylov 子空间投影结合（RIGKT 使用 Golub-Kahan 双对角化，RIAT 使用 Arnoldi 分解）；2) 引入随机方程选择机制；3) 设计自适应步长策略；4) 提出基于差异的全局后验早停规则。

Result: 建立了 Bregman 距离单调性、有限终止性、精确数据收敛性和噪声路径稳定性；证明停迭代几乎必然且均方收敛；二维 CT 和图像去模糊实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个同时考虑随机化、Krylov 子空间降维和可实现早停的理论框架，为大规模病态逆问题提供了新的随机正则化途径。

Abstract: We introduce two novel randomized iterative regularization frameworks, termed \texttt{RIGKT} and \texttt{RIAT}, for solving large-scale linear ill-posed inverse problems governed by systems of equations. The proposed methods combine randomized iterated Tikhonov regularization with Krylov subspace projection techniques, utilizing Golub--Kahan bidiagonalization for general rectangular systems (\texttt{RIGKT}) and Arnoldi decomposition for square systems (\texttt{RIAT}). Unlike existing deterministic schemes that rely on fixed iteration counts, our framework incorporates randomized equation selection, an adaptive step-size strategy, and a global, discrepancy-based a posteriori early-stopping rule tailored specifically to the stochastic setting. We present a comprehensive regularization analysis establishing Bregman-distance monotonicity, finite termination, exact-data convergence, and pathwise stability under noise. Furthermore, we prove that the stopped iterates converge almost surely and in the mean-square sense to the true solution, establishing a rigorous regularization property. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theoretical framework to simultaneously account for randomization, Krylov-subspace dimension reduction, and implementable early stopping. Numerical experiments involving two-dimensional X-ray computed tomography (CT) and image deblurring demonstrate that \texttt{RIGKT} and \texttt{RIAT} reliably reconstruct structural features across various noise regimes.

</details>


### [318] [Convergence of a scheme for a one dimensional nonlocal and nonlinear eikonal equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24216)
*Diana Al Zareef,Ahmad El Hajj,Antoine Zurek*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类无符号限制的一维非局部非线性程函方程，在非局部速度场和初始数据弱正则性假设下，推导了周期版本，提出了半显式IMEX格式，证明了其离散梯度熵估计保持性和粘性意义下的收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了模型行为和格式性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类非局部非线性程函方程，该方程对空间梯度无符号限制，且非局部速度场和初始数据的正则性假设较弱，现有理论和方法难以直接适用。

Method: 推导了该模型的周期版本；提出了一种半显式(IMEX)数值格式用于近似求解；通过理论分析证明了格式保持离散梯度熵估计，并在粘性意义下证明收敛性。

Result: 所提出的IMEX格式能够保持离散梯度熵估计；在粘性意义下证明了格式的收敛性；数值结果展示了模型行为及格式性能。

Conclusion: 该IMEX格式能有效处理无符号梯度限制的非局部非线性程函方程，数值表现良好，理论保证充分。

Abstract: In this work, we study a one-dimensional nonlocal and nonlinear eikonal equation without sign restriction on its spatial gradient. The equation is characterized by weak regularity assumptions on both the nonlocal velocity field and the initial data. We derive a periodic version of this model and propose a semi-explicit (IMEX) scheme for its numerical approximation. We prove that the scheme preserves a discrete gradient entropy estimate and establish its convergence in the viscosity sense. Finally, we present numerical results illustrating the behavior of the model and the performance of the proposed scheme.

</details>


### [319] [The $L_1$-Discrepancy with Nonnegative Weights Suffers from the Curse of Dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24290)
*Josef Dick*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 证明了具有非负权重的$L_1$-差异度存在维度诅咒，其逆满足指数下界$N_{1,+}(\varepsilon, d) \ge \frac{(1-\varepsilon)^2}{1 + \varepsilon} \left( \frac{3+2 \sqrt{3}}{6}\right)^d$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$L_1$-差异度在任意非负权重下是否受维度诅咒影响，这是准蒙特卡洛方法中核心问题的理论探索。

Method: 将问题转化为体积偏置概率测度，并结合归一化差异函数的分数矩估计进行下界推导。

Result: 对于任意$\varepsilon \in (0,1)$和$d \in \mathbb{N}$，$L_1$-差异度的逆满足指数增长下界，底数为$(3+2\sqrt{3})/6 \approx 1.07735$。

Conclusion: 非负权重$L_1$-差异度存在维度诅咒，且论证本质上依赖权重的非负性，不适用于任意符号权重情形。

Abstract: We prove that the $L_1$-discrepancy with arbitrary nonnegative weights suffers from the curse of dimensionality. More precisely, for every $\varepsilon \in (0,1)$ and $d \in \mathbb{N}$, the inverse of the $L_1$-discrepancy satisfies \[ N_{1,+}(\varepsilon, d) \ge \frac{(1-\varepsilon)^2}{1 + \varepsilon} \left( \frac{3+2 \sqrt{3}}{6}\right)^d, \] where $(3+2\sqrt{3})/6 = 1.07735\ldots$. The proof combines a change to a volume-biased probability measure with a fractional-moment estimate for the normalized discrepancy function. The lower bound applies, in particular, to equally weighted point sets. The argument uses the nonnegativity of the weights in an essential way and does not cover arbitrary signed weights.

</details>


### [320] [The Convergence and Error Analysis of Coordinate Descent Methods with Compression for Full Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24319)
*Yingzhou Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了坐标下降全组态相互作用（CDFCI）中压缩效应，证明了在适当局部假设下压缩迭代线性收敛到受限问题的解，并表征了收敛点。在目标特征向量指数衰减假设下，特征值误差为τ²阶，数值结果支持分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究坐标下降全组态相互作用（CDFCI）方法中压缩技术的影响，并为其收敛性提供理论保证。

Method: 将全组态相互作用基态问题建模为无约束优化问题，在CDFCI中引入压缩机制；通过局部假设证明压缩迭代的线性收敛性；利用目标特征向量指数衰减假设推导特征值误差阶数。

Result: 压缩迭代线性收敛到受限问题的解；在指数衰减假设下，特征值误差为O(τ²)，其中τ为压缩阈值。

Conclusion: 压缩CDFCI在理论上有保证的收敛性，数值实验验证了理论分析的正确性。

Abstract: We study the effect of compression in Coordinate Descent Full Configuration Interaction (CDFCI) within an unconstrained optimization formulation of the full configuration interaction ground-state problem. Under suitable local assumptions, we prove that the compressed iteration converges linearly to the solution of an associated restricted problem. We also characterize the convergence point of the compressed algorithm. Under an additional exponential decay assumption on the target eigenvector, we show that the resulting eigenvalue error is of order $τ^2$, where $τ$ denotes the compression threshold. Numerical results support the analysis.

</details>


### [321] [Fifth-order finite volume derivative-based Hermite WENO scheme with unified stencils for hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24325)
*Peiwen Chen,Zhuang Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于导数的有限体积Hermite WENO (HWENO)格式，通过排除目标单元导数信息并使用统一模板进行空间重构，在保持高精度的同时增强了格式的鲁棒性，降低了计算成本和算法复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HWENO格式在面临间断时导数可能过大，影响鲁棒性；早期方案采用不同模板分别重构控制方程和导数方程，虽能减少导数影响但显著增加计算成本和算法复杂度。因此需要一种更高效、鲁棒的HWENO格式。

Method: 在空间重构中排除目标单元导数信息，在时间演化中使用相同的重构多项式来限制导数；采用统一模板替代传统HWENO中的不同模板集；支持任意和为1的正线性权重，保持紧凑模板。

Result: 数值结果表明所提出的HWENO格式具有高阶精度、高效率、高分辨率和良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方案克服了传统HWENO格式的局限性，通过统一模板策略在保持精度的同时增强了鲁棒性，且计算效率更高、算法更简洁，是对双曲守恒律数值方法的有意义改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a derivative-based finite volume Hermite WENO (HWENO) scheme for hyperbolic conservation laws, where both the solution and its first-order derivatives are evolved in time and utilized in spatial reconstructions. The key challenge for solving hyperbolic conservation laws is the possible emergence of discontinuities in the numerical solutions. When facing discontinuities, the derivatives can become excessively large, which may compromise the robustness of HWENO schemes. In the first HWENO scheme, different sets of stencils were adopted for reconstructing the governing equation and the derivative equation, respectively, aiming to reduce the influence of the derivatives while preserving high-order accuracy. However, this approach not only substantially increases computational cost but also introduces considerable algorithmic complexity. To overcome these limitations, we exclude the information of the target cell's derivatives from spatial reconstructions, while employing the same reconstructed polynomial during temporal evolution to limit the derivatives. This strategy enhances the robustness of traditional HWENO schemes and allows unified stencils within the derivative-based HWENO framework. Furthermore, the proposed scheme supports arbitrary positive linear weights that sum to one and maintains a compact stencil. Numerical results demonstrate the high-order accuracy, efficiency, high resolution, and robustness of the proposed HWENO scheme.

</details>


### [322] [A Curve-Reference Exponential Integrator for Three-Dimensional Charged-Particle Dynamics under a Strong Nonconstant Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24523)
*Zhirui Shen,Bin Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非相对论动量形式下的三维带电粒子动力学模型，提出了一种曲线参考指数积分器（CREI）。由于线性部分的零特征值非半单，传统方法失效。通过核-值域分解和线性变换，将系统化为具有半单零块和常斜对称振荡块的形式。CREI沿精确振荡曲线展开非线性项，而非固定点。定义了CREI(k+1)方法，证明了均匀局部和全局误差界，并通过数值实验验证了h收敛性和ε均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维带电粒子动力学模型中，系统线性部分的零特征值非半单，导致经典局部线性化扩展指数积分器（LLEEI）所需的谱条件不满足，因此需要构造新的数值方法。

Method: 对主导磁矩阵进行核-值域分解并施加进一步线性变换，将原方程转化为具有半单零块和一个常斜对称振荡块的系统。提出曲线参考指数积分器（CREI），沿精确振荡曲线（而非固定点）展开非线性项，保留至k次单项式得到CREI(k+1)。

Result: 证明了CREI的均匀局部和全局误差界，并通过可重复数值实验验证了在h（步长）上的收敛性以及在ε（参数）上的均匀性。

Conclusion: CREI方法有效解决了非半单零特征值带来的数值困难，在保持精确性的同时实现了对振荡参数的均匀收敛。

Abstract: We study a three-dimensional charged-particle dynamics model in the nonrelativistic momentum formulation and construct a curve-reference exponential integrator (CREI) for this system. Since the zero eigenvalue of linear section is not semisimple, the system after usual change of variables does not meet the spectral condition used in the classical locally linearized extended exponential integrator (LLEEI). A kernel-range decomposition of the dominant magnetic matrix and a further linear transformation reduce the equation to a system with a semisimple zero block and one constant skew-symmetric oscillatory block. The CREI expands the nonlinear term along the exact oscillatory curve rather than at a fixed point. Retaining monomials through degree k defines CREI(k + 1). We prove uniform local and global error bounds and present reproducible numerical experiments for convergence in h and uniformity in ε.

</details>


### [323] [Well-conditioned Electric Field Surface Integral Equations using Reflective Generalized Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24526)
*Yossi Dahan,Suryakumar Sivakumar,Yaniv Brick,Simon B. Adrian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用广义源方法发展了一类无内共振且无需组合公式的良态积分算子。GSIE算子通过在传统EFIE核中添加辅助贡献来增强矩量矩阵块的秩亏缺。研究首次分析了内部散射凸面屏蔽产生的辅助核的GSIE算子谱特性，证明在合适的屏蔽参数下，TM和TE-GSIE算子无内共振和准共振。辅助分量是EFIE的紧扰动，继承了密集离散化问题但可通过Calderón型预条件解决。高频击穿机制受辅助分量影响，在某些情况下更具韧性。这些观察对非圆形模板屏蔽也有效。GSIE-dual和Yukawa核预条件器均展现良好谱特性和可压缩性，适用于快速迭代求解器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统电场积分方程(EFIE)存在的两个核心问题：内共振（internal resonance）和密集离散化击穿（dense-discretization breakdown），同时避免使用组合公式（如CFIE）带来的额外复杂度，为高性能迭代求解器设计提供新途径。

Method: 采用广义源方法(GSIEs)，在传统EFIE核基础上引入由内部散射凸面屏蔽产生的辅助核贡献，增强矩量矩阵块的秩亏缺性。通过同心圆形散射体和屏蔽的闭式表达式分析算子谱特性，并扩展到非圆形模板屏蔽。采用GSIE-dual和Yukawa-kernel预条件器改善谱特性。

Result: (1) 合适的屏蔽参数下，TM和TE-GSIE算子无内共振和准共振；(2) 辅助分量是EFIE的紧扰动，GSIE继承密集离散化击穿但可通过Calderón型预条件处理；(3) 高频击穿机制受辅助分量影响，某些情况下更具韧性；(4) 对非圆形模板屏蔽设计同样有效；(5) 两类预条件器均展现良好谱特性和可压缩性。

Conclusion: 基于屏蔽的GSIE算子在二维问题中展现了优异的谱特性和可压缩性，为扩展到三维问题和更广泛的辅助核几何应用奠定了理论基础，有望推动快速迭代求解器的发展。

Abstract: This work uses the generalized source approach to develop a class of well-conditioned integral operators that are free of internal resonance, without the need for combined formulations. The Generalized source integral equations (GSIEs) kernels are obtained by augmenting the conventional electric field integral equation (EFIE) kernel with auxiliary contributions to enhance the rank deficiency of the corresponding moment matrix blocks. This paper presents the first investigation of the spectra of GSIE operators for auxiliary kernels produced by internal scattering convex shields. Using closed-form expressions for concentric circular scatterers and shields, it is shown that, with shield parameters that are suitable for enhanced compressibility, the transverse magnetic (TM)- and transverse electric (TE)-GSIE operators are free of internal proper and quasi-resonances. The auxiliary components are shown to be compact perturbations of their EFIE counterparts. Hence these GSIESs inherit their dense-discretization breakdown, which remains curable via Calderón-type preconditioning. The mechanisms that govern the high-frequency breakdown are shown to be influenced by the auxiliary component, leading, in some cases, to greater resilience. These observations are shown to remain valid for moment matrices and GSIEs designed with non-circular stencil shields. For both GSIE-dual and Yukawa-kernel preconditioners, the formulations exhibit favorable spectral properties while maintaining their compressibility. This makes the formulations attractive for the design of fast iterative solvers. The results on the two-dimensional shield-based operators provide a foundation for extending the approach to three-dimensional problems and to auxiliary kernels with broader geometric applicability.

</details>


### [324] [Arbitrary-Order Padé-Closed Anchored Two-Derivative Time Discretizations: $s$ Active Stages, Order $2s$, and $L$-Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24592)
*Zhixin Huo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任意阶隐式双导数单步方法，采用锚定活动阶段格式。每个信息节点同时使用向量场及其一阶全时间导数，在不增加未知阶段状态数量的情况下丰富局部Hermite数据。通过$2s$个Hermite矩条件实现全局阶$2s$，系数由第二副对角线Padé逼近$[s-1/s+1]_{e^z}$确定。Padé-Hermite基定理证明该格式是唯一的、保持所有矩条件，且满足$\det(I-zA-z^2\widehat A)=Q_s(z)$和$R_s(z)=P_s(z)/Q_s(z)$，因此耦合阶段系统无隐藏极点，单步映射是$L$-稳定的（从而$A$-稳定）。数值验证至$s=6$，高精度计算确认前四个成员的阶为2,4,6,8。与Gauss-Legendre和Radau IIA方法比较，该方法在宽步长范围内兼具高阶精度和强刚阻尼特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造一种任意阶的隐式双导数单步方法，在保持计算效率的同时提高精度和稳定性，特别是克服传统Runge-Kutta方法在刚性问题上对步长的限制。

Method: 采用锚定活动阶段格式，每个节点同时使用向量场及其一阶导数；通过$2s$个Hermite矩条件达到全局阶$2s$；系数由第二副对角线Padé逼近$[s-1/s+1]_{e^z}$固定；基于Padé-Hermite基定理证明唯一性和所有矩条件的保持。

Result: 符号验证至$s=6$，高精度计算确认前四个成员($s=1,2,3,4$)分别达到2,4,6,8阶。与Gauss-Legendre和Radau IIA相比，在相同活动阶段数下，该方法在宽步长范围内兼具高阶精度和强刚阻尼。

Conclusion: 所构造的隐式双导数单步方法对于任意正整数$s$都是$L$-稳定（从而$A$-稳定）的，且无隐藏极点，在刚性问题上优于传统的高阶隐式Runge-Kutta方法。

Abstract: An arbitrary-order family of implicit two-derivative one-step methods is constructed in an anchored active-stage formulation. At each information node the method uses both the vector field and its first total time derivative, enriching the local Hermite data without increasing the number of unknown stage states. With the known initial value retained as an anchor and $s$ unknown active stages, $2s$ Hermite moment conditions yield global order $2s$. The two remaining coefficients in each stage row are fixed by the second-subdiagonal Padé approximant $[s-1/s+1]_{e^z}$. For every ordered real node set, a Padé--Hermite basis theorem proves that the closure is unique, preserves all moment conditions, and gives $\det(I-zA-z^2\widehat A)=Q_s(z)$ and $R_s(z)=P_s(z)/Q_s(z)$. Hence the coupled stage system has no hidden poles and the accepted one-step map is $L$-stable (and therefore $A$-stable) for every positive integer $s$. Exact symbolic verification is reported through $s=6$, and high-precision computations confirm orders $2,4,6,$ and $8$ for the first four members. At equal active-stage count, comparisons with Gauss--Legendre and Radau IIA methods demonstrate the combined high-order accuracy and strong stiff damping of the construction over broad step-size ranges.

</details>


### [325] [Adaptive Multiphysics Coupling for Hyperbolic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24600)
*Simon Candelaresi,Erik Faulhaber,Michael Schlottke-Lakemper*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在Trixi.jl框架中实现了自适应耦合任意多个不同物理域的功能，通过边界信息交换和用户自定义耦合函数连接不同系统，能够在仿真过程中动态选择耦合模型，相比全区域使用最复杂模型显著降低计算时间，开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多物理场耦合仿真中不同系统变量不兼容、复杂度差异大的问题，实现层次化系统间的自适应耦合，减少计算资源浪费。

Method: 基于Trixi.jl不连续伽辽金代码，通过交换边界信息和用户可定义的耦合函数实现域间耦合，支持自适应选择耦合模型，用户可自定义适配准则。

Result: 与全区域采用最复杂模型相比，高低复杂度耦合系统的计算方法显著降低了计算时间，且引入的开销非常小。

Conclusion: 该方法能够高效处理高度动态的多物理场景（如天体物理学），在保证精度的前提下大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: For the discontinuous Galerkin code Trixi.jl we implement capabilities for adaptively coupling arbitrarily many domains with different physics. The coupling of the systems is realized through the exchange of boundary information and user-definable coupling functions. This gives us the ability to couple systems that do not share a single variable or have a very different number of variables. This is particularly useful when we have a hierarchy of systems. Our implementation is such that we can adaptively select the model of the coupled systems in the course of the simulation. This allows for highly dynamical scenarios as can be found e.g. in astrophysics. The criteria for adaptivity can be user defined and tailored to the physical problem. Compared to computing the most complex model in the entire simulation domain, our method of coupled systems of high and low complexity leads to a significant reduction in computational time with very little overhead.

</details>


### [326] [Anderson acceleration of the proximal point method: the exact adaptive minimax, a spectral phase transition, and optimal safeguarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24643)
*Zheng Jia,Yekini Shehu,Yonghong Yao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文严格分析了近端点方法（PPM）的残差多项式加速，以Anderson加速（AA）为典型自适应方案。精确回答了三个问题：（i）所有自适应方法的极小极大复杂度为每K次预解评估达到d₀/(K+1)，上界由平均反射估计器达到，下界通过显式斜伴随实例证明；（ii）存在尖锐相变：Jackson核多项式在谱基底s满足sK→∞时达到O(d₀/(K²s))，而在临界尺度s≍1/K时屏障恰好为1/(K+1)；（iii）在线性问题中AA-PPM无需保护，非线性问题中认证O(1/k)包络线需要每次迭代恰好两次预言机评估且该因子最优。同时修正完善了结构化问题（仿射、强单调、分段仿射、Hölder增长）的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为近端点方法（PPM）的残差多项式加速（特别是Anderson加速）建立精确的收敛理论，回答此前未解决的最优性、相变和计算复杂度等基础问题。

Method: 采用极小极大优化框架，利用正交多项式理论（Fejér核、Jackson核）、谱分析（斜伴随算子、特征值分布）以及预言机复杂度下界构造（csc²分布质量）进行理论推导，结合数值实验验证。

Result: （i）自适应方法最优复杂度为d₀/(K+1)；（ii）发现精确相变：sK→∞时O(d₀/(K²s))，s≍1/K时精确下界1/(K+1)；（iii）AA-PPM在线性问题上无需保护，非线性问题需要恰好两次预言机评估。

Conclusion: 为PPM的多项式加速建立了完整且精确的理论框架，揭示了最优方法、相变行为和计算成本，同时修正了此前结构化问题的理论结果。

Abstract: \noindent We study residual-polynomial acceleration of the proximal point method (PPM) for maximal monotone inclusions, with Anderson acceleration (AA) as the prototypical adaptive scheme. We answer three questions exactly. (i)~The minimax complexity over all adaptive methods is precisely $d_0/(K+1)$ per $K$ resolvent evaluations. The upper bound is attained by the averaged-reflection estimator; the matching lower bound uses an explicit skew-adjoint instance with resolvent eigenvalues at the roots of $u^{K+1}=-1$ and $\csc^2$-distributed masses, on which every degree-$K$ polynomial method satisfies $\|r(y_K)\|\ge 1/(K+1)$. The optimal polynomial is uniquely the Fejér kernel, and the same instance certifies a per-step floor. (ii)~A sharp phase transition separates regimes: Jackson-kernel polynomials achieve $O(d_0/(K^2 s))$ when the spectral floor $s$ satisfies $sK\to\infty$, while at the critical scale $s\asymp 1/K$ the barrier is exactly $1/(K+1)$. The picture extends to normal operators and the nonlinear family $M=S+N_C$. (iii)~On linear problems AA-PPM needs no safeguarding; on nonlinear problems certification of the $O(1/k)$ envelope requires exactly two oracle evaluations per iteration, and this factor is optimal. We also correct and complete the theory for structured problems---affine, strongly monotone, piecewise-affine, and Hölderian growth---and confirm all predictions numerically.

</details>

### [327] [Characterisation of a multistable turbulent wake: application of an improved regime identification with analytical model training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23206)
*Ariane Barlet,Pierre Bragança,Christophe Cuvier,Joran Rolland*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和建模范式，研究了雷诺数10000下两并排方形柱尾流中的多稳态射流现象。利用PIV测量速度场，以射流加权横向位置$Y_m$和宽度$w$表征随间隙比$G/H$变化的多稳态 regimes：三稳态($G/H\in[1.15,1.25]$)、双稳态($G/H\in[1.5,2.65]$)和单稳态($G/H\in[3.0,3.5]$)。通过数据驱动方法构建了含多项式漂移和扩散项的随机微分方程（SDE）模型，既能恢复分岔理论预期的漂移形式并修正乘性噪声，也能拟合复杂多稳态下的非典型漂移表达式，从而揭示射流行为机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并排方形柱尾流中的多稳态射流现象在工程（如热交换器、建筑风工程）中具有重要应用背景，但其在不同间隙比下的多稳态行为及其随机动力学本质缺乏系统的建模分析，特别是复杂多稳态状态（如三稳态及过渡区间）尚未被充分理解和数学描述。

Method: 采用二维二分量粒子图像测速（PIV）测量尾流速度场；定义射流加权横向位置$Y_m$和宽度$w$作为特征量；针对不同间隙比$G/H$，基于实验数据拟合含多项式漂移和扩散项的随机微分方程（SDE），通过数据驱动方法在模型简洁性与精度间取得平衡。

Result: 识别出三种多稳态 regimes：三稳态（$G/H\in[1.15,1.25]$）、双稳态（$G/H\in[1.5,2.65]$）和单稳态（$G/H\in[3.0,3.5]$），且在前两个区间内存在更复杂的多稳态行为。SDE模型在简单多稳态下恢复了分岔理论预期的漂移形式并修正了乘性噪声，在复杂多稳态下拟合出非典型漂移表达式。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的SDE建模方法能够有效刻画并排柱尾流多稳态射流的随机动力学，兼具理论一致性和复杂状态下的解释能力，为流固耦合系统中的多稳态现象提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: This article presents the experimental study and the modelling of the multistable jet in the wake of two side by side square bars separated by a distance $G$ at Reynolds number $R=U_\infty H/ν=10000$ (with $U_\infty$ the velocity of the incoming flow, $H$ the bar side and $ν$ the kinematic viscosity). The velocity field downstream of the bars is measured by means of two dimensional two components Particle Image Velocimetry (PIV). We use the weighted transverse position of the jet $Y_m$ and the jet width $w$ to characterise the regimes of multistability as the gap ratio $G/H$ is increased. Three main regimes of multistability are possible: tristability, bistability, and monostability. In our wind tunnel, tristability is observed for $G/H\in [1.15,1.25]$, bistability is observed for $G/H\in [1.5,2.65]$ and monostability is observed for $G/H\in [3.0,3.5]$. Within the first two ranges of $G/H$, there exists gap ratios for which multistability is more complex. In order to analyse the simple and complex multistability regimes as well as the transition from bistable to monostable, we construct an analytical stochastic differential equation (SDE) modelling $Y_m$ for each gap ratio. These SDEs are written with polynomial drift and diffusion. For this matter we use a data based method that finds an trade--off between simplicity of the model (smaller number of monomials) and precision. A first key advantage of the use of the data-based model fitting method is that when the flow is tristable, bistable or monostable, we recover the drifts expected from the theory of bifurcations, but we are now able to correct it with the right multiplicative noise expressed by the diffusion. The second key advantage is that we can also fit atypical drift expressions when the multistability regime is complex that help us make sense of the jet behaviour.

</details>


### [328] [Dynamics of fluid-fluid displacements in a model rough fracture beyond the quasistatic limit: A spectral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23759)
*Mykyta Chubynsky,Jordi Ortin,Marco Dentz,Ran Holtzman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种谱计算方法，用于模拟多孔介质中流体-流体界面演化及能量耗散。该方法基于“非完美”Hele-Shaw单元模型，能够高效处理多尺度问题，并验证了在Haines跳跃中粘性力完全解释了介观平衡态间的能量耗散，但粘度仅改变弛豫时间而不改变总耗散量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多孔介质中流体位移过程中的微观Haines跳跃与宏观能量耗散及滞后现象之间的定量联系，以及介质非均质性对界面临界演化与能量耗散的影响，目前仍缺乏定量理解，且直接数值模拟计算成本过高。

Method: 开发了一种谱计算方法（Spectral Computational Approach）模拟变孔径Hele-Shaw单元中的界面演化与能量耗散，通过与CFD模拟和实验对比进行验证，并在含单个缺陷和随机粗糙度的单元中展示计算效率。

Result: 该方法能高效处理多尺度问题（时间尺度跨越多个数量级）；理论证明Haines跳跃中的粘性力完全解释了连续亚稳态平衡态之间的能量耗散，但粘度仅改变弛豫时间而不改变总耗散量。

Conclusion: 该谱计算方法为多孔介质中流体-流体流动的升尺度（upscaling）提供了基础，能够高效模拟非均质介质中多尺度的界面演化与能量耗散问题。

Abstract: In fluid-fluid displacements in porous and fractured materials, viscous friction in microscale interfacial (Haines) jumps is intimately linked to macroscale energy dissipation and the associated pressure-saturation (retention) hysteresis. The mode of control (flow rate vs. pressure) and the driving rate (from quasistatic to finite) can substantially affect hysteresis. Despite the significance of hysteresis and dissipation in various technological and natural processes, a quantitative understanding of the link between the micro- and macro-scales, and of the impact of the inherent heterogeneity of porous media, remains elusive. An ``imperfect'' Hele-Shaw cell of variable aperture is a simple model system which allows to study all these in details. However, simulating fluid-fluid interface evolution in heterogeneous media, even in such a simple system, is computationally prohibitive, as multiple length and time scales need to be resolved simultaneously. We develop here a spectral computational approach for interface evolution and energy dissipation and validate it via comparison to computational fluid dynamics simulations and experiments. Computational efficiency is demonstrated by following interface evolution in a cell with a single ``defect'', as well as with random roughness; in both, disparate length scales lead to nontrivial dynamics over many orders of magnitude in time. We also show theoretically that while viscous forces during Haines jumps fully account for the energy dissipated between consecutive metastable equilibria, viscosity does not change the total dissipated amount, merely the relaxation time. Our approach provides a stepping stone towards upscaling of fluid-fluid flows in porous media.

</details>


### [329] [Adjoint Sensitivity Maps for Passive Flow Control Around Rotating Circular Cylinders Across a Wide Operating Envelope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23831)
*Niklas Kühl*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对旋转圆柱（如Flettner转子）在宽工况范围（Re=1e1~1e7，转速比0~2π）内，提出了基于拓扑的伴随敏感性分析方法，推导了阻力、升力和扭矩的局部敏感场，并通过多孔介质模型进行了验证。研究发现敏感场的大尺度拓扑主要由转速比主导，而雷诺数影响相对较弱，最终构建了可用于被动流动控制设计的敏感性图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转圆柱在工程中应用广泛（如Flettner转子船舶推进），除优化圆柱自身气动性能外，在其附近布置被动流动控制装置还有额外的力调控潜力。现有方法缺乏系统性的敏感性分析框架来指导此类被动控制设计。

Method: 采用基于拓扑的伴随敏感性分析方法，利用多孔介质公式推导了阻力、升力和扭矩的局部敏感场。通过两种方式验证：(1) 分布式Darcy型源项的正向模拟；(2) 敏感性引导的被动流动控制结构模拟。系统分析了宽雷诺数（1e1~1e7）和转速比（0~2π）范围内的敏感场特性。

Result: 阻力与升力敏感性的联合符号分布可生成直观的设计图谱，直接识别局部动量提取同时改善或恶化两个目标的区域。敏感场的大尺度拓扑主要由转速比控制，雷诺数的影响在大部分工况范围内相对较弱。由此构建的敏感性图谱可在中等工况范围内提供鲁棒的被动流动控制设计指导。

Conclusion: 该敏感性图集为被动流动控制概念提供了实用的设计指导，并证明了在中等工况范围内可能存在鲁棒的设计方案。

Abstract: Rotating circular cylinders are employed in a variety of engineering applications, one prominent example being Flettner rotors for wind-assisted ship propulsion. Besides optimizing the aerodynamic performance of the cylinder itself, passive flow-control devices placed in its vicinity offer additional potential for manipulating the resulting aerodynamic forces. The present work introduces a topology-based adjoint sensitivity analysis for rotating circular cylinders over a wide operating envelope covering Reynolds numbers from 1E+01 to 1E+07 and spinning ratios between 0 and 2 pi. Local sensitivity fields associated with drag, lift, and torque are derived using a porous-medium formulation and validated by dedicated forward simulations employing both distributed Darcy-type source terms and a sensitivity-informed passive flow-control structure. Particular emphasis is placed on the combined sign distribution of the drag and lift sensitivities, yielding intuitive design maps that directly identify regions where local momentum extraction simultaneously improves or deteriorates both objectives. A systematic investigation of the resulting sensitivity spectra reveals that the large-scale topology of the sensitivity fields is governed primarily by the spinning ratio, whereas the influence of the Reynolds number remains comparatively weak over large parts of the investigated operating envelope. The resulting sensitivity atlas provides practical design guidance for passive flow-control concepts and demonstrates that robust solutions may exist over moderate operating ranges.

</details>


### [330] [Influence of a vertical-wall leading edge on bouncing and escape bubble rising regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24248)
*A. Rubio-González,E. J. Vega,R. Bolaños-Jiménez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了超纯水中气泡在垂直壁面附近的上升动力学，发现壁面前缘位置（从注入点开始连续存在 vs. 下游起始）显著影响气泡的周期性弹跳（PB）到弹跳-翻滚-逃逸（BTE）的转变阈值、弹跳频率和Strouhal数。当下游壁面构型时，BTE转变发生在更小气泡尺寸（更低Bo、Ga、Re数），PB regime下St数更低且振幅更大，表明气泡动力学不仅依赖于传统控制参数和壁面间距，还受壁面几何及气泡历史水动力影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究壁面前缘位置对可变形气泡近壁动力学的影响，特别是气泡在到达壁面区域前的流动历史（是否已达到终端速度）如何改变其弹跳、翻滚和逃逸行为。

Method: 在超纯水中进行实验，比较两种壁面构型：(i) 壁面从注入点下方11-25倍气泡直径处开始延伸；(ii) 壁面前缘位于注入点上方144 mm（约80-180倍气泡直径）。测量不同气泡尺寸下的动力学行为，分析Bo、Ga、Re、St等无量纲参数。

Result: 1) 下游壁面构型中BTE转变发生在更小气泡尺寸（更低Bo、Ga、Re）；2) 低Bo下两种构型St数均呈下降趋势，但Bo≥0.15后两者分叉；3) 连续壁面构型PB regime下St趋于常数~0.014，下游壁面构型St显著更低；4) 下游壁面构型中气泡反弹幅度更大、返回阶段更长导致频率更低。

Conclusion: 气泡近壁动力学不仅取决于Bo、Ga、Re等传统参数和壁面间距，还取决于壁面几何构型（前缘位置）以及气泡与之相互作用前的流体动力学历史。

Abstract: This work investigates deformable gas bubbles rising near a vertical wall in ultrapure water, focusing on how the position of the wall leading edge affects their near-wall dynamics. Two configurations are considered: (i) a wall extending from 11--25 bubble diameters below the bubble injection point, so that the bubble rises under the continuous influence of the boundary; and (ii) a wall whose leading edge is located 144 mm above the injector, corresponding to approximately 80--180 bubble diameters, allowing the bubble to reach its terminal velocity before entering the wall-bounded region. The results show that the wall leading-edge position influences both the transition from periodic bouncing (PB) to bouncing--tumbling--escaping (BTE) dynamics and the rebound frequency within the PB regime. When the wall leading edge is placed downstream, the onset of BTE occurs at smaller bubble sizes, corresponding simultaneously to lower Bond ($Bo$), Galilei ($Ga$), and Reynolds ($Re$) numbers. The Strouhal number ($St$) follows a similar decreasing trend in both configurations at low Bond numbers, but the two behaviours diverge for ($Bo \gtrsim 0.15$). For the PB regime, when the wall extends from the injector, $St$ approaches an approximately constant value of $St\simeq0.014$, whereas substantially lower values are measured for the downstream-wall configuration. The larger rebound amplitudes and longer return stages observed in the latter configuration account for lower frequencies. These findings indicate that the bubble dynamics depend not only on the conventional control parameters and wall separation, but also on the wall geometry and the associated pre-interaction bubble hydrodynamic history.

</details>


### [331] [Bubble growth on arrays of micro-electrodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24286)
*Mengyuan Huang,Gerd Mutschke,Zhicheng Yuan,Kerstin Eckert*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过三维VOF模拟研究了水电解过程中电极上气泡生长对电流密度分布和欧姆电阻的影响，发现电极岛阵列上的气泡会向外漂移延缓电极覆盖，这种漂移受相邻气泡生长的液体平流驱动，与间距的1/d²成正比，并受电流密度不对称性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型假设润湿电极表面电流密度均匀分布，忽略了气泡几何形状和演化对电场分布的反作用。本文旨在解析非均匀电流密度与气泡生长动力学之间的耦合机制。

Method: 采用Basilisk中的三维几何VOF方法进行数值模拟，考虑相变过程，求解一次电场分布（而非预设均匀电流密度），研究不同尺寸电极上的单气泡生长以及电极岛阵列上的多气泡生长。

Result: 非均匀电流密度显著影响气泡生长动力学。对于电极岛阵列，外围气泡在生长过程中向外漂移，延缓了电极完全覆盖并维持了电流。气泡漂移由相邻气泡生长驱动的液体平流主导，漂移速度与间距平方的倒数（1/d²）成比例，并受电流密度不对称性调制。

Conclusion: 电极图案化和气泡间距可用于定制电场分布，从而减少水电解过程中气泡引起的电阻损耗。

Abstract: Gas bubbles evolving on electrodes during water-electrolysis are blocking active reaction area, thus hindering mass transfer and raising Ohmic resistance. Unlike earlier models that prescribe a uniform current density on the wetted part of the electrode, we resolve the primary electric field, which allows the current density and the interfacial gas production to respond to the geometry of the electrode and the temporal evolution of the bubbles. Using three-dimensional geometrical volume-of-fluid (VOF) simulations with phase change in Basilisk, we examine the growth of single-bubbles on electrodes of different size and of multiple bubbles growing on arrays of catalytic electrode islands. The non-uniform current density and the associated Ohmic resistance significantly affect the growth dynamics. Unlike the case of a single bubble, the outer bubbles in case of electrode islands tend to drift outward during growth, thus delaying full electrode coverage and sustaining current. Footprint tracking and a theoretical analysis show that this drift is governed by the liquid advection driven by the growth of neighboring bubbles, scaling with their separation 1/d2, and modulated by the current-density asymmetry. These results show how electrode patterning and bubble spacing can be exploited to tailor the electric field distribution and reduce bubble-induced resistive losses during water electrolysis.

</details>
