### [332] [The balance between compactness and forecast accuracy of data-driven latent-space reduced-order models in controlled wake flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24569)
*Alberto Solera-Rico,Patricia García-Caspueñas,Carlos Sanmiguel Vila,Stefano Discetti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了主动流动控制中不同空间编码方法（POD、CAE、变分自编码器）对潜在空间动力学可预测性的影响，发现在尾流控制场景下，POD产生的潜在轨迹更平滑、更易学习，长期预测更可靠，而CAE虽然压缩效率高但潜在动力学更不规则，长期预测容易发散，揭示了压缩效率与预测精度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的主动流动控制需要准确、稳定且足够快速的预测模型用于实时优化。在受控尾流中，通常通过降阶模型（ROM）先压缩高维速度快照到潜在空间，再学习潜在空间动力学的时间步进预测器。本文旨在研究空间编码器的选择如何影响潜在坐标在控制输入下的可预测性，为设计适用于实时控制的预测性ROM提供指导。

Method: 使用两个受控二维尾流构型（简化卡车尾流和流体弹球），比较了POD、非线性卷积自编码器（CAE）和两种变分自编码器用于压缩，并基于LSTM网络评估了多种时间预测器。

Result: CAE在压缩效率和短期重建精度上更优，但其产生的潜在动力学更不规则、频谱更宽；长期预测退化更快，且发生灾难性发散的概率更高。POD产生的潜在轨迹更平滑，更容易学习和外推，在短期之外提供了更可靠的预测。

Conclusion: 存在压缩效率与预测精度之间的明确权衡，潜在动力学预测的稳定性比最大压缩更重要，尤其适用于基于动力学预测的控制策略（如模型预测控制和强化学习）。

Abstract: Model-based active flow control requires predictive models that are accurate, stable, and fast enough for real-time optimisation. In controlled wake flows, this is often achieved through Reduced-Order Models (ROMs) that first compress high-dimensional velocity snapshots into a latent space and then learn a time- stepping predictor for the dynamics in the latent space. Here, we study how the choice of the spatial encoder affects the predictability of the resulting latent coordinates for wake flows under control inputs. Using two actuated 2D wake configurations, a simplified truck wake and the fluidic pinball, we compare Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) against nonlinear Convolutional Autoencoders (CAEs) and two types of variational autoencoders for compression, and evaluate several temporal predictors based on Long Short-Term Memory networks. CAEs achieve higher compression efficiency and sharper short-term reconstructions, but they produce latent dynamics that are more irregular and with broadband spectral content. As a consequence, long-horizon forecasts degrade faster and show a higher probability of catastrophic divergence than POD-based models. POD yields smoother latent trajectories that are easier to learn and extrapolate, leading to more reliable predictions beyond the short- term regime. These results reveal a clear trade-off between compactness and forecast accuracy, and suggest that the stability of the latent dynamics prediction can outweigh maximal compression. This is particularly relevant for control strategies rooted in forecasts of the dynamics, such as model predictive control and reinforcement learning. The findings provide practical guidance for designing actuation-aware, hardware-feasible predictive ROMs for real-time flow control.

</details>


### [333] [A Modified Moving Reference Frame Method for Propeller Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24630)
*Denis Andreev,Georgios Bletsos,Antonios Kritikos,Niklas Kühl,Thomas Rung*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的移动参考系（mMRF）方法，通过空间平滑函数缩放参考系旋转速率，消除旋转域与固定域之间的界面不连续性，在不增加计算成本的前提下准确预测螺旋桨-船体相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滑动界面（SI）方法计算成本高，而经典MRF方法无法捕捉非定常相互作用效应；部分旋转网格方法虽试图折衷，但旋转域与静止域之间的突变导致速度场不连续性。亟需一种既能保持计算效率又能消除界面伪影的方法。

Method: 提出改进MRF（mMRF）公式，将参考系旋转速率乘以一个从螺旋桨附近（值为1）到域界面（值为0）平滑衰减的空间函数，恢复界面处的速度和压力连续性。在RANS求解器FreSCo⁺中实现，通过泰勒-库埃特解析解验证，并应用于敞水螺旋桨和日本散货船自航仿真。

Result: MRF和mMRF均能准确复现主要推进积分量（n, KT, KQ, 1-t, 1-wT, ηR），但mMRF显著减少了界面不连续性和局部流场的非物理伪影，尤其在MRF分数较大时，而计算成本基本不变。

Conclusion: mMRF方法在保持与标准MRF相同计算效率的同时，有效消除了旋转-静止域界面的不连续性，提高了局部流场精度，是平衡计算成本与精度的有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate resolution of propeller-hull interaction is essential for predicting the self-propulsion point in ship CFD, yet motion-resolving methods such as sliding interfaces (SI) are computationally expensive, while the classical Moving Reference Frame (MRF) approach cannot capture unsteady interaction effects. Partially rotating grid methods bridge this gap by splitting the propeller rotation into a grid-resolved and an MRF component, but the abrupt transition between the rotating and stationary domains introduces discontinuities in the velocity field. This work presents a modified MRF (mMRF) formulation in which the reference-frame rotation rate is scaled by a spatially varying function that decays smoothly from unity near the propeller to zero at the domain interface, restoring velocity and pressure continuity across the boundary. The governing equations are derived and implemented in the RANS solver FreSCo$^+$, verified against the analytical Taylor--Couette solution, and applied to open-water propeller and Japan Bulk Carrier self-propulsion simulations at model scale. Both MRF and mMRF reproduce the principal integral propulsion quantities ($n$, $K_{\mathrm{T}}$, $K_{\mathrm{Q}}$, $1-t$, $1-w_{\mathrm{T}}$, $η_{\mathrm{R}}$) accurately, but the mMRF markedly reduces interface discontinuities and non-physical artifacts in the local flow field, particularly at large MRF fractions, at essentially the same computational cost.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [334] [Dispersive estimates of Dirac equations with a domain wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22879)
*M. Burak Erdogan,Joseph Kraisler,Amir Sagiv*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维狄拉克方程在有限和无限畴壁条件下的色散衰减估计，并分析了对应的一维问题。这些是拓扑绝缘体中体-边对应的典范模型，但此前其色散动力学尚未被研究。研究表明拓扑等价的模型可能表现出性质不同的动力学。新方法能够分析超出常系数狄拉克算子局部扰动常规框架的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体中体-边对应模型的色散动力学此前未被研究，且现有方法无法处理超出常系数狄拉克算子局部扰动框架的模型。

Method: 建立二维狄拉克方程在有限和无限畴壁条件下的色散衰减估计，并分析对应的一维问题。提出新方法以处理更广泛的模型类别。

Result: 拓扑等价的模型可能表现出性质不同的动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了拓扑绝缘体色散动力学研究的空白，新方法拓展了可分析模型的范畴。

Abstract: We establish dispersive decay estimates for two-dimensional Dirac equations with bounded and unbounded domain walls, together with estimates for the analogous one-dimensional problem. These are paradigmatic models of bulk-edge correspondence in topological insulators, yet their dispersive dynamics have not been previously studied. We show that topologically equivalent models may exhibit qualitatively different dynamics. Our new approach allows us to analyze models which are beyond the usual framework of localized perturbations of constant-coefficient Dirac operators.

</details>


### [335] [Shape-Transition in Non-Euclidean Ribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22593)
*Noam Shalev*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过Γ-收敛从三维模型推导了具有粗糙预应变的细长弹性带的一维极限理论，证明了窄带和宽带之间存在形状转变，推广了Maor & Mora的结果到更实际的粗糙预应力场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究（Maor & Mora）针对光滑预应变建立了弹性带的一维极限理论，但实际物理系统中预应变往往是粗糙的（非光滑），因此需要将理论推广到粗糙预应变情形以更准确地建模相关物理系统。

Method: 从三维弹性模型出发，利用Γ-收敛方法推导一维极限理论；结合Freddi等人关于欧几里得带的研究、Schmidt关于预应变板降维的工作，以及Maor & Mora的尺度化极限应变结构定理，分析窄带和宽带的不同能量极小化行为。

Result: 对于窄带，能量极小化子与参考（有效）第二基本形式沿中线一致；对于宽带，这一般不成立。这证明了弹性带中存在形状转变，且粗糙预应变情形下该结论依然成立。

Conclusion: 本文成功将预应变弹性带的形状转变理论从光滑预应变推广到粗糙预应变，为解释实验中观察到的窄带与宽带之间的形状转变提供了更贴近实际的理论依据。

Abstract: Ribbons are thin elastic bodies whose thickness $t$ is much smaller than their width $w$, which is in turn much smaller than their length. Starting from a three-dimensional model, we derive a one-dimensional limit theory for ribbons with rough prestrain, by means of $Γ$-convergence. Our model shows that for narrow ribbons, the energy minimizer coincides with the reference (effective) second fundamental form along the midline, while for wide ribbons this is generically not the case. This proves the existence of shape-transitions in such ribbons, as observed in many experiments, generalizing recent results by Maor & Mora to rough prestrains, which more accurately model many of the relevant physical systems. Our analysis combines techniques from the study of Euclidean ribbons due to Freddi et al., the work of Schmidt on dimension reduction of prestrained plates, and a new structure theorem on the scaled limiting strain due to Maor & Mora.

</details>


### [336] [Continuous Data Assimilation for the 2D Navier-Stokes Equations from Partial Tangential Boundary Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23707)
*Gianmarco Del Sarto,Buddhika Priyasad*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维Navier-Stokes方程在光滑有界连通域上带Navier-slip边界条件的连续数据同化问题，仅利用边界子集Γ上的切向速度有限维测量数据。证明足够强的边界反馈和足够细的观测能产生同化误差的 coercive 谱隙，该极限谱隙与在Γ上加齐次Dirichlet条件的混合边界问题的谱隙相同，量级为ν。结合非线性误差估计，给出指数同步的充分判据，并在多种情形下验证该判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仅利用边界观测（无内部观测）进行连续数据同化的可行性，解决边界观测下二维Navier-Stokes方程数据同化的数学理论基础问题。

Method: 理论分析：通过构造边界反馈产生 coercive 谱隙，将误差系统线性部分的谱隙与混合边界问题的谱隙等同，并结合非线性误差项的长时平均对称梯度能量估计，推导指数同步的充分条件。

Result: 建立了边界反馈强度与观测精度对同化误差谱隙的影响关系，证明了谱隙量级为ν；在无强迫、小强迫、大黏性（无切向刚体运动或存在正边界摩擦）以及完美滑移（带切向刚体运动）等情形下验证了指数同步判据。

Conclusion: 仅基于边界子集上的切向速度测量，通过足够强的边界反馈即可实现二维Navier-Stokes方程的指数同步，为实际应用中仅依赖边界数据的同化方案提供了严格的理论保障。

Abstract: We study continuous data assimilation for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations on a smooth, bounded, connected domain with Navier-slip boundary conditions, using no interior observations. The available data consist only of finite-dimensional measurements of the tangential velocity on a non-empty relatively open subset $Γ\subset\partialΩ$. We prove that sufficiently strong boundary feedback, constructed from sufficiently fine observations, generates a coercive spectral gap for the assimilation error. The limiting gap is identified with that of a mixed-boundary problem obtained by imposing a homogeneous Dirichlet condition on $Γ$, and is shown to be of order $ν$. Combining this feedback-induced coercivity with an estimate of the non-linear error production in terms of the long-time averaged symmetric-gradient energy of the reference solution, we obtain a sufficient criterion for exponential synchronisation. We verify this criterion in the unforced case, for sufficiently small forcing when the unnudged Navier-slip form has an $\mathrm{L}^2$ spectral gap, for sufficiently large viscosity on domains without tangential rigid motions, and for sufficiently large viscosity in the presence of positive boundary friction. We also treat perfect slip on domains admitting tangential rigid motions, where synchronisation follows under a smallness condition on the non-rigid solenoidal component of the forcing.

</details>


### [337] [Geometry of the Atomic Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22782)
*Jacek Jakimiuk,Sławomir Kolasiński,Maciej Leśniak*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了De Philippis等人提出的AC（原子条件）可积函数类，将其与凸几何相联系，并提出了新的定量版本EC（暴露条件）和QEC（二次暴露条件）。QEC在类2扰动下稳定且支持Caccioppoli不等式，适用于临界点正则性证明。此外，对于所有k≥2且n-k≥3，构造了严格多凸但违反AC的可积函数，表明严格多凸性是AC的必要但远非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC类可积函数在几何测度论中具有重要地位，但长期以来难以验证任何具体可积函数是否满足该条件（除面积可积函数及其类2邻域外），也无法系统构造AC类成员。本文旨在从凸几何角度揭示AC的结构，并发展更易处理且保持良好性质的定量版本。

Method: 1) 从凸几何角度重新解释AC的原子条件；2) 提出暴露条件(EC)和二次暴露条件(QEC)作为AC的定量版本；3) 分析QEC在类2扰动下的稳定性及其对Caccioppoli不等式的支持；4) 给出可积函数与范数关联时EC的充分条件；5) 构造严格多凸但违反AC的反例。

Result: 1) 揭示了AC的凸几何结构本质；2) 建立了QEC的稳定性及其与Caccioppoli不等式的关系；3) 对于所有k≥2且n-k≥3，成功构造了与内积范数关联的严格多凸可积函数，这些函数不满足AC。

Conclusion: 严格多凸性是AC的必要条件但远非充分条件；QEC作为AC的定量版本，继承了USAC的良好性质（类2扰动稳定性、Caccioppoli不等式），更适合用于临界点正则性分析。

Abstract: The class AC of integrands satisfying the atomic condition was introduced by De Philippis, De Rosa, and Ghiraldin in 2018. These integrands give rise to Almgren elliptic geometric functionals as proven by the second author and De Rosa in 2020. So far, it is not known how to verify the atomic condition for any particular integrand (apart from the area integrand and its class 2 neighbourhood) or how to construct, event artificial, members of AC. We reinterpret the atomic condition in terms of convex geometry shedding light on the structure of this class. We also propose quantitative versions of AC, different from the SAC and USAC defined by De Rosa and Tione, which we call the exposed condition EC and the quadratic exposed condition QEC. As is the case with USAC, the QEC is stable under class 2 perturbations and supports a Caccioppoli-type inequality; hence, is suitable for proving regularity of critical points. Moreover, we provide some conditions sufficient for EC in case the integrand is associated to a norm on the exterior power of $\mathbf{R}^{n}$. Finally, for all $k \ge 2$ and $n-k \ge 3$ we construct strictly polyconvex integrands -- associated to inner-product norms on $\bigwedge_{k} \mathbf{R}^{n}$ -- which fail the atomic condition, showing that strict polyconvexity is necessary but far from sufficient for AC.

</details>


### [338] [On a free boundary problem of the Boltzmann equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24053)
*Shengchuang Chang,Renjun Duan,Shuangqian Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究玻尔兹曼方程与运动活塞耦合的自由边界问题，通过共形变换和耦合能量结构，建立了全局Maxwellian附近解的整体存在性、唯一性、非线性稳定性及指数收敛性，首次给出了活塞型玻尔兹曼自由边界问题的严格整体适定性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决稀薄气体与运动壁面相互作用的自由边界问题（活塞问题）中，动理学方程与自由运动边界之间的强耦合这一核心挑战，经典拉格朗日方法由于解的低正则性而不可用。

Method: 引入共形变换将运动区域化为固定区域，并揭示连接动理学分布与自由边界变量的耦合能量结构，从而发现界面处的内在耗散与抵消机制。

Result: 建立了全局Maxwellian附近解的整体存在性、唯一性、非线性稳定性以及指数收敛到平衡态。

Conclusion: 首次提供了活塞型完全耦合玻尔兹曼自由边界问题的严格整体适定性理论。

Abstract: This paper studies a free boundary problem for the Boltzmann equation that models the interaction of rarefied gas with a moving wall---a classical piston problem in kinetic theory. The piston motion is governed by Newton's law under the drag force exerted by the gas, with or without an additional Hookean restoring force. A central challenge for such a problem is the strong coupling between the kinetic equation and the free moving boundary, for which the classical Lagrangian formulation is unavailable due to low-regularity of solutions. Our approach relies on two new ingredients: a conformal transformation is introduced to reduce the moving domain to a fixed domain, and a coupled energy structure linking the kinetic distribution and free boundary variables is uncovered to reveal intrinsic dissipation and cancellation mechanisms at the interface. These structural observations lead to a global nonlinear theory for the fully coupled system. As a result, we establish the global existence, uniqueness, nonlinear stability, and exponential convergence to equilibrium of solutions near a global Maxwellian. This provides the first rigorous global well-posedness theory for a fully coupled Boltzmann free boundary problem of piston type.

</details>


### [339] [Supersolution Construction and Optimal Hardy Inequality for Fractional Laplacians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24169)
*Philipp Hake,Matthias Keller,Felix Pogorzelski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过超解构造来判断图上的Hardy权重最优性的新准则。对于无杀伤项的拉普拉斯算子，通过格林函数总能得到最优Hardy权重，且无需局部有限图限制。该方法适用于分数阶拉普拉斯算子，并在欧几里得格点上得到了与连续情形一致的最优Hardy权重常数和渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于图上Hardy权重最优性的结果通常受限于局部有限图，需要一种更通用的准则来突破这一限制，并推广到更广泛的算子（如分数阶拉普拉斯算子）上。

Method: 基于超解构造（supersolution construction）建立新的最优性判据；对于无杀伤项的拉普拉斯图，利用格林函数直接构造最优Hardy权重；将该方法应用于一般图上的分数阶拉普拉斯算子。

Result: 1）建立了不依赖局部有限图假设的Hardy权重最优性新准则；2）对无杀伤项的图拉普拉斯，格林函数总能给出最优Hardy权重；3）在欧几里得格点上的分数阶拉普拉斯算子情形，得到了与连续情形相匹配的最优Hardy权重常数和渐近行为。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了局部有限图的限制，为图上更广泛算子的Hardy不等式最优性分析提供了统一框架，并成功应用于分数阶拉普拉斯算子，得到了与连续情形一致的结果。

Abstract: We give a new criterion to show optimality of Hardy weights for general operators on graphs via the supersolution construction. For Laplacians on graphs without killing terms this always gives rise to an optimal Hardy weight via the Green's function without any further assumptions. Furthermore, in contrast to earlier results, our result is not restricted to locally finite graphs. This allows us in particular to obtain optimal Hardy weights for the fractional Laplacian on general graphs. For the fractional Laplacian on the Euclidean lattice, we then obtain an optimal Hardy weight with the constant and asymptotics as it is expected from the continuous setting.

</details>


### [340] [On General Linear Degenerate Elliptic PDE Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22888)
*Nikos Katzourakis,Frederick Temple*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究严格凸有界域上带低阶项的线性退化椭圆方程组，在无严格秩1凸性假设下，通过引入自然结构条件，证明了唯一广义解的存在性及其部分正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性退化椭圆系统研究通常要求严格秩1凸性假设，而实际应用中许多问题不满足该条件。本文旨在移除这一严格假设，并进一步将前期工作扩展到包含低阶项（一阶项和零阶项）的更具一般性的情形。

Method: 引入适当的自然结构假设（关于系数A, B, C的条件），定义广义解概念，运用泛函分析和偏微分方程理论建立存在性，并证明解的部分正则性。

Result: 证明了在无严格秩1凸性条件下，所考虑的一般线性退化椭圆系统存在唯一的广义解u∈L²(Ω, ℝ^N)，且该解满足额外的部分正则性性质。

Conclusion: 本文成功将线性退化椭圆系统的存在性理论从纯二阶情形推广到含低阶项的更一般情形，且无需严格秩1凸性假设，推广了Katzourakis(2016)的结果。

Abstract: Let $Ω\Subset \mathbb{R}^{n}$ be a strictly convex bounded domain. Suppose $\mathbf{A} : \mathbb{R}^{Nn} \longrightarrow \mathbb{R}^{Nn}$, $\mathbf{B}: \mathbb{R}^{Nn} \longrightarrow \mathbb{R}^{N}$, $\mathbf{C}: \mathbb{R}^{N} \longrightarrow \mathbb{R}^{N}$ are linear maps, where $\mathbf{A}$ is symmetric and non-negative definite. Given $f \in L^2(Ω, \mathbb{R}^N)$, we consider the problem of existence of solutions $u: Ω\longrightarrow \mathbb{R}^N$ to the PDE system \[
  \left\{ \begin{array}{rl} \displaystyle\sum_{β= 1}^{N}\sum_{i, j = 1}^{n} \mathbf{A}_{αi βj}\mathrm{D}_{ij}^{2}u_β + \sum_{β= 1}^{N} \sum_{i=1}^{n} \mathbf{B}_{αβi}\mathrm{D}_{i}u_β + \sum_{β= 1}^{N} \mathbf{C}_{αβ}u_β = f_α, &\text{ in $Ω$}, \\ u = 0,\ \,& \text{ on $\partial Ω$}. \end{array} \right. \] This is a linear \textit{degenerate elliptic} system, and it has not been considered before without the assumption of strict rank-one convexity. In general, it may not possess not even distributional solutions. By introducing some natural structural assumptions, we prove the existence of an appropriately defined unique generalised solution $u\in L^2(Ω, \mathbb{R}^N)$, satisfying additional partial regularity properties. This paper extends earlier work of the first appearing author [\textit{N. Katzourakis, On linear degenerate elliptic PDE systems with constant coefficients}, Adv.\ in Calc.Var.\ 9:3, 283-291 (2016)] to include lower-order terms.

</details>


### [341] [Non-singular hotspots between closely spaced high-index nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24204)
*Konstantinos Alexopoulos,Bryn Davies,Pierre Millien*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个近乎接触的高折射率介电谐振器之间场的浓度问题，发现梯度在中间距离范围内随间距倒数增长，但在极近距离下会饱和，并通过弱耦合机制解释了这一预渐近效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维空间中两个高折射率介电谐振器在谐振状态下，当它们几乎接触时电场梯度的浓度行为，特别是解释梯度为何不会无限增长而是趋向饱和。

Method: 基于标量亥姆霍兹传输问题的数学模型，采用弱耦合机制分析，将谐振器对的模式视为孤立粒子模式的扰动，并通过从平均控制强化到逐点控制的谱估计方法，结合数值实验验证。

Result: 梯度在中间距离范围内随间距倒数增长，但在极近距离下饱和；当模式在相对边界上保持非零对比度时，跨间隙的平均值论证解释了梯度增长；弱耦合失效后增长停止。

Conclusion: 证实了梯度浓度的预渐近放大与饱和转变，解释了高折射率介电谐振器近场中梯度有界但依然存在显著浓度现象的机制。

Abstract: We study the concentration of the field between two nearly touching high-index dielectric resonators in three dimensions. The model is a scalar Helmholtz transmission problem in the resonant regime, wherein the wavelength inside the resonators of the same order as their typical diameter. The material contrast enters only a lower-order term of the equation and not its principal part. As a consequence, the gradient of the field stays bounded independently of the distance separating the particles, and does not blow up. Nevertheless, simulations and experiments show that the gradient still concentrates in the gap: as the particles approach, it grows like the inverse of their separation over an intermediate range of distances and then saturates once they are very close. We explain this pre-asymptotic effect through a weak-coupling regime, in which the resonant modes of the pair are perturbations of the modes of each isolated particle. When such a mode keeps a nonzero contrast between the two facing boundaries, a mean value argument across the gap accounts for the growth of the gradient; this requires strengthening the standard spectral perturbation estimates from an average to a pointwise control. The growth stops once the interaction between the particles is no longer weak and the perturbation theory fails. Numerical experiments confirm the transition from amplification to saturation.

</details>


### [342] [Parabolic-elliptic reduction suppresses Hopf bifurcation in a forager-exploiter system with cascade taxis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22986)
*Huaizhi Cao,Jiawei Chu,Hai-Yang Jin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类抛物-椭圆捕食-开发模型，证明了在任意维度下大初始种群数据和大趋向敏感性系数时经典解的整体存在性和一致有界性。当资源更新率小时，解指数收敛到唯一常数稳态；当资源更新率大时，系统不会发生Hopf分岔，这与全抛物情况形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究抛物-椭圆捕食-开发模型中两个物种通过共享环境资源（具有恒定更新率）的级联相互作用，特别是解的整体存在性、有界性及长期动力学行为。

Method: 采用偏微分方程理论，分析全局存在性和一致有界性；通过线性化分析研究稳态的稳定性及Hopf分岔条件。

Result: 1) 在任意维度下，对于大初始数据和大趋向敏感性系数，经典解整体存在且一致有界；2) 资源更新率小时，解指数收敛到常数稳态；3) 资源更新率大时，系统不发生Hopf分岔，而全抛物情形则会出现时间振荡。

Conclusion: 抛物-椭圆模型与全抛物模型在动力学行为上存在本质差异：前者不产生Hopf分岔导致的时间振荡，后者则会产生，揭示了模型结构对解长期行为的关键影响。

Abstract: We study a parabolic-elliptic forager-exploiter model describing the cascade interaction between two species through a shared environmental resource with a constant renewal rate. We first establish the global existence and uniform boundedness of classical solutions in arbitrary dimensions {\color{black}for large initial population data and large taxis sensitivity coefficients. We then investigate the long-time behavior of solutions.} When the resource renewal rate is small, all solutions will converge exponentially to the unique constant steady state. Furthermore, for large resource renewal rates, the parabolic-elliptic system does not undergo Hopf bifurcation, which is in sharp contrast to the corresponding fully parabolic case, where temporal oscillations arise via Hopf bifurcation.

</details>


### [343] [Asymptotically sharp Hardy-Rellich inequalities on lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23057)
*Xia Huang,Dong Ye*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文确定了$\mathbb{Z}^d$格点上离散Hardy-Rellich不等式最优常数的渐近行为，证明了当$d\to\infty$时，$m$阶常数$\mathcal{C}_{m,d}\sim 2^m d^m$。方法结合了Fourier约化、加权Hardy-Rellich恒等式以及概率浓度估计（Hoeffding不等式和熵方法）来处理各向异性权重，从而在环面上获得渐近精确的加权估计，再通过迭代得到格点不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定离散Hardy-Rellich不等式最优常数在高维情况下的精确渐近行为，填补该领域对高阶、高维离散不等式常数精确估计的空白。

Method: 采用Fourier约化将格点问题转化为环面上的加权不等式问题；利用一阶和二阶加权Hardy-Rellich恒等式；引入概率浓度估计（Hoeffding不等式和熵方法）以维度一致的方式处理各向异性权重$\omega^\gamma$的估计，从而获得渐近精确的加权估计；最后通过迭代得到格点不等式。

Result: 对于任意固定的整数$m\ge 1$，最优常数满足$\lim_{d\to\infty}\mathcal{C}_{m,d}/d^m = 2^m$，即高维极限下常数呈$2^m d^m$的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 成功建立了离散Hardy-Rellich不等式最优常数的高维渐近公式，方法创新性地将概率论工具（Hoeffding不等式、熵方法）引入加权分析，为离散泛函不等式的高维研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: We determine the sharp asymptotic behavior of the optimal constants in the discrete Hardy-Rellich inequalities on the lattice $\mathbb{Z}^d$. For every fixed integer $m\ge 1$, let ${\mathcal C}_{m,d}$ be the best constant in the $m$-th order Hardy-Rellich inequality. We prove that $$\lim_{d\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal C_{m,d}}{d^m}=2^m.$$ Our approach combines a Fourier reduction to weighted inequalities with the flat torus and general weighted Hardy-Rellich identities of first and second order. A key novelty is the use of probabilistic concentration estimates, specifically Hoeffding's inequality and entropy methods, to handle estimates involving the anisotropic weight $ω^γ$ $(γ\geq 1)$ where $$ω(x)=\sum_{j=1}^{d} \Big(\sin\frac{x_j}{2}\Big)^2$$ in a dimension-uniform manner. These tools yield asymptotically sharp weighted estimates on the torus, from which the lattice inequalities follow by iteration.

</details>


### [344] [The generalized porous medium equation on graphs: well-posedness, extinction, and mass conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23091)
*Davide Bianchi,Bobo Hua,Alberto G. Setti,Radosław K. Wojciechowski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限加权图上广义多孔介质方程的柯西问题，建立了Dirichlet比较原理和弱最大值原理，构造了最小和最大逐点解，推导了定量能量估计，证明了有限时间熄灭和$\ell^1$-$\ell^q$平滑性质，并在随机完备图上建立了精确的广义质量平衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多孔介质方程的理论从欧几里得空间和黎曼流形推广到无限加权图这一离散结构上，研究其解的存在性、比较原理、能量估计、熄灭行为及质量守恒等基本性质。

Method: 利用有限子图上的Dirichlet比较原理和弱最大值原理，通过穷举论证构造逐点解；借助$\nu$-Sobolev不等式（$\nu>2$）推导$\ell^1$-温和解的能量估计；对随机完备图建立广义质量平衡。

Result: 建立了推广的比较原理和最大值原理；在快扩散范围$0<m<2/\nu$得到有限时间熄灭，在$m>2/\nu$得到$\ell^1$-$\ell^q$平滑；恢复了若干模型图上的欧几里得临界指数；建立了含杀伤项和非负解的质量平衡关系。

Conclusion: 将多孔介质方程的核心理论成功推广至无限加权图框架，为离散结构上的非线性扩散方程研究提供了基础工具和关键结果。

Abstract: We study the Cauchy problem for the generalized porous medium equation on infinite weighted graphs. For a general nonlinearity, we establish Dirichlet comparison and weak maximum principles on finite subgraphs and, through an exhaustion argument, construct minimal and maximal pointwise solutions for arbitrary $\ell^\infty$ initial data, controlled by explicit, possibly time-dependent, barriers.
  For the porous nonlinearity $φ(s)=s|s|^{m-1}$, assuming a $ν$-Sobolev inequality with $ν>2$, we derive quantitative energy estimates for $\ell^1$-mild solutions. These yield finite-time extinction in the fast diffusion range $0<m<2/ν$ and $\ell^1$-$\ell^q$ smoothing in the range $m>2/ν$. Interestingly, we recover the Euclidean critical exponent for several model graphs. Finally, we prove an exact generalized mass balance for nonnegative $\ell^1$-mild solutions on graphs that are stochastically complete at infinity, allowing for an arbitrary killing term. The same balance is established for suitable classical and bounded pointwise solutions. In the absence of killing, these reduce to conservation of mass.

</details>


### [345] [On well-posedness theory of very weak solutions to Navier-Stokes equations on irregular domains with nonhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23234)
*Xiaojin Bai,Siran Li,Xiangxiang Su*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了在具有充分小Sobolev乘子范数的有界Lipschitz区域上Navier-Stokes方程非常弱解的适定性理论，推广了经典C^{2,1}光滑区域上的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes方程非常弱解的适定性理论是数学流体动力学的核心课题，此前该理论仅对C^{2,1}光滑有界区域上的不可压缩流体完全建立。本文旨在将该理论推广至更一般的Lipschitz区域上。

Method: 基于Breit和Gaudin（2025）以及Maz'ya和Shaposhnikova（2009）的分析理论，利用Sobolev乘子范数刻画边界正则性，通过小性条件控制Lipschitz边界的局部图函数。

Result: 建立了在边界局部图函数具有充分小Sobolev乘子范数的有界Lipschitz区域上Navier-Stokes方程非常弱解的适定性理论，该类区域包含具有充分小Lipschitz常数的有界Lipschitz区域作为特例。

Conclusion: 将非常弱解适定性理论从C^{2,1}光滑区域推广到更一般的Lipschitz区域，拓展了该理论的应用范围。

Abstract: The well-posedness theory of very weak solutions is a central topic in mathematical hydrodynamics, especially in the regularity theory for Navier-Stokes equations. It has been fully developed for incompressible fluid flows on bounded domains in R^3 of C^{2,1}-regularity. In this paper, based on the analytic theories in [D. Breit and A. Gaudin, ArXiv Preprint: 2511.19091 (2025)] and [V.G. Maz'ya and T.O. Shaposhnikova, Vol.337, Grundlehren der mathematischen Wissenschaften (2009)], we establish the well-posedness theory of very weak solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations on bounded Lipschitz domains whose boundary has local graphing functions with sufficiently small Sobolev multiplier norm, which contain the bounded Lipschitz domains with sufficiently small Lipschitz constants as a special case.

</details>


### [346] [A Dacorogna-Moser construction of transport maps on $\mathbb{R}^d$ with application to geodesics on the space of couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23241)
*Louis-Pierre Chaintron,Matteo Picco*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将Dacorogna-Moser的规则运输映射构造从有界域推广到整个$\mathbb{R}^d$上的严格渐近对数凹测度（涵盖Lipschitz扰动对数凹测度的广泛分布类），并利用此构造研究乘积空间上具有给定边际律的概率测度空间中的测地线（耦合）问题，导出了最优性条件，回答了Conforti、Lacker和Pal未解决的问题。进一步引入测地线问题的熵正则化——可视为耦合空间上的Schrödinger桥问题，并导出最优性条件，最终证明正则化消失时极小元的收敛性及相关拉格朗日乘子的收敛性。技术核心是通过反射耦合的概率工具建立渐近对数凹测度下椭圆和抛物方程在$\mathbb{R}^d$上关于时间一致的整体正则性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Dacorogna-Moser规则运输映射构造从有界域推广到无界欧几里得空间，以处理更广泛的分布类（渐近对数凹测度），并解决关于乘积空间上具有固定边际律的耦合测度空间中测地线最优性条件的开放问题。

Method: 利用反射耦合的概率工具建立渐近对数凹测度下椭圆和抛物方程在$\mathbb{R}^d$上关于时间一致的整体正则性估计，进而构造规则运输映射；在此基础上引入熵正则化处理测地线问题，将其转化为耦合空间上的Schrödinger桥问题。

Result: 成功将规则运输映射扩展到$\mathbb{R}^d$上的严格渐近对数凹测度；导出了乘积空间上耦合测度空间中测地线的最优性条件；建立了熵正则化测地线问题（耦合空间上的Schrödinger桥）的最优性条件；证明了正则化参数趋零时极小元的收敛性及相关拉格朗日乘子的收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架为处理无界域上规则运输映射和耦合空间中的测地线问题提供了统一的理论工具，熵正则化方法确保了数值计算的可行性及理论上的收敛保证。

Abstract: A seminal work by Dacorogna and Moser introduced a way of constructing regular transport maps from a probability distribution on a bounded domain to another one. In this work, we extend this construction to the whole $\mathbb{R}^d$ for strictly asymptotically log-concave measures, a wide class of distributions that encompasses Lipschitz-perturbations of log-concave measures. We then leverage this construction to study geodesics in the space of probability measures on a product set with imposed marginal laws (couplings), for which we derive optimality conditions, answering an open question in a recent work by Conforti, Lacker and Pal. Taking inspiration from Brenier's variational model for incompressible fluids and its regularization, we further introduce an entropic regularization of the geodesic problem, which can be seen as the Schrödinger bridge problem on the space of couplings, for which we also derive optimality conditions. We eventually study convergence of minimizers as the regularization vanishes, and we prove convergence of the related Lagrange multipliers. Our approach involves proving uniform-in-time global regularity estimates on elliptic and parabolic equations on $\mathbb{R}^d$, by exploiting the structure of asymptotically log-concave measures using the probabilistic notion of reflection coupling.

</details>


### [347] [The lifespan of positive solutions of heat equation with power-logarithmic nonlinearity on locally finite graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23251)
*Pengxiu Yu,Yiping Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在局部有限连通图上，利用Kaplan的第一特征值方法和胡-王发展的离散Phragmén-Lindelöf原理，研究了具有幂对数非线性项u^p|log u|^q的半线性热方程正解的生命跨度渐近行为，并将结果推广到非负初始数据情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将胡-王等人关于图上半线性热方程的研究成果扩展到具有幂对数源项的情形，并进一步放宽初始数据的非负条件。

Method: 采用Kaplan的第一特征值方法以及胡-王发展的离散Phragmén-Lindelöf原理进行分析。

Result: 建立了在局部有限连通图上，具有幂对数非线性项的半线性热方程正解的生命跨度渐近行为，并证明了在非负初始数据且某顶点初始值足够大的情况下，类似的生命跨度估计仍然成立。

Conclusion: 成功将图上半线性热方程的生命跨度估计从原有结果推广到更一般的幂对数非线性情形和更宽松的初始条件。

Abstract: On a locally finite connected graph $G=(V,E)$, using the first eigenvalue method introduced by Kaplan \cite{MR160044} and the discrete Phragmén-Lindelöf principle developed by Hu-Wang \cite{cvhuyuanyang}, we first establish the asymptotic behaviour of the lifespan of positive solutions to a semilinear heat equation with the power-logarithmic nonlinearity $u^p|\log u|^q$, provided that the initial datum is bounded below by a positive constant. These results extend those of Hu-Wang \cite{cvhuyuanyang} to equations with a power-logarithmic source term. Moreover, by means of a more direct argument, we show that analogous lifespan estimates remain valid for nonnegative initial datum $u(x,0)$, provided that $u(x_i,0)$ is suitably large at some vertex $x_i\in V$.

</details>


### [348] [A Pseudo-Differential Operator of the $p$-Adic Multiplicative Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23256)
*Alexandra V. Antoniouk,Anatoly N. Kochubei,Mariia V. Serdiuk*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了一个通过p-adic Mellin变换定义的伪微分算子W^α，该算子作用于p-adic数域乘法群上的实值或复值函数。W^α是最大耗散算子，并给出了其热核的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在p-adic分析及其在数学物理中的应用背景下，研究特定伪微分算子的性质，特别是其耗散性和热核估计，以扩展非阿基米德局部域上的算子理论。

Method: 利用p-adic Mellin变换定义伪微分算子W^α，通过分析该算子的谱和半群性质证明其最大耗散性，并估计其热核的衰减行为。

Result: 证明了算子W^α是最大耗散算子，并给出了其热核的估计。

Conclusion: W^α算子具有良好的耗散结构，热核估计为其在p-adic数域上的扩散过程和相关方程的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We introduce and study a pseudo-differential operator $W^α$, defined via the $p$-adic Mellin transformation, acting on real- or complex-valued functions on the multiplicative group of the field of $p$-adic numbers. The operator $W^α$ is maximally dissipative. An estimate of its heat kernel is given.

</details>


### [349] [Variational principles for the interaction of liquid crystals and electric fields in the Oseen--Frank model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23315)
*Giovanni Di Fratta,Valeriy Slastikov,Arghir Zarnescu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为单常数Oseen-Frank近似下的单轴向列液晶在电场中的平衡问题建立了严格的变分框架。通过凸对偶和Hodge分解将鞍点问题转化为纯双极小化问题；在小介电各向异性极限下，严格量化了静电反作用解耦，证明非局域能量与标准局域近似之间的均匀二次能量界；并在严格强制条件下，结合Γ-收敛、一致Sobolev正则性和摄动强制传递，证明物理极小元以最优定量线性速率收敛到极限调和映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液晶电场模型存在非局域、非线性能量的min-max鞍点结构，难以直接进行理论分析和数值模拟；同时，物理学中常用忽略感应退极化场的局域近似，但缺乏严格的数学证明。

Method: 采用凸对偶与Hodge分解，将标量静电势替换为矢量势，获得闭式对偶泛函的唯一极小元，将鞍点问题转化为双极小化问题；利用Γ-收敛、一致Sobolev正则性和摄动强制传递分析收敛行为。

Result: 1) 成功将原min-max问题转化为纯双极小化问题；2) 建立了非局域能量与局域近似之间的均匀二次能量界；3) 证明物理极小元以最优定量线性率收敛到极限调和映射。

Conclusion: 该变分框架为液晶电场问题的理论分析和数值模拟提供了严格的数学基础，并首次从数学上严格证明了忽略感应退极化场这一物理近似的合理性及其误差界。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous variational framework for uniaxial nematic liquid crystals interacting with an external electric field in the one-constant Oseen--Frank approximation. Equilibrium configurations are governed by a nonlocal, nonlinear energy with a challenging min-max saddle-point structure. Our first main result reformulates this problem as a pure double-minimization problem. Using convex duality and the Hodge decomposition, we replace the scalar electrostatic potential with a vector potential, yielding a closed-form dual functional with a unique minimizer. This direct energy-minimization principle is advantageous for both theoretical analysis and numerical simulation. Our second result rigorously quantifies the decoupling of the electrostatic back-reaction in the limit of small dielectric anisotropy. We establish a uniform quadratic energy bound between the exact nonlocal energy and its standard local approximation, formally justifying the widespread physics heuristic of neglecting induced depolarization fields. Finally, under a strict coercivity condition, we combine $Γ$-convergence, uniform Sobolev regularity, and a perturbative coercivity transfer to prove that physical minimizers converge to limiting harmonic maps at an optimal, quantitative linear rate.

</details>


### [350] [Sharp Thresholds for the Porous Medium Equation with a Combustion Reaction in Higher Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23429)
*Maolin Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有燃烧型反应的多孔介质方程的长时间行为，在径向紧支非负初值条件下，对解的渐近行为进行了完整分类。二维空间中所有有界解局部一致收敛于常数0、θ或1；三维及以上空间中，在基态中心值集满足自然全不连通条件下，解收敛于0、θ、1或径向基态，并给出了自由边界传播速度的估计，揭示了退化燃烧动力学中的维度二分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔介质方程与燃烧反应耦合的长时间动力学行为尚未被完全理解，特别是不同空间维度下解的渐近行为是否存在本质差异。本文旨在建立完整的分类理论，并揭示维度在退化燃烧动力学中的关键作用。

Method: 采用比较原理、阈值参数分析、径向对称性约化、基态中心值全不连通条件假设，以及新颖的归一化环形扰动论证方法，结合自由边界传播速度的上下界估计。

Result: (1) N=2时所有有界解收敛于0、θ或1，存在唯一阈值参数区分消失与蔓延，临界解收敛于点火温度θ；(2) N≥3时在基态全不连通条件下解收敛于0、θ、1或径向基态，且θ被排除为过渡极限；(3) N=2时自由边界传播速度b(t)~√t/(log t)^{(m-1)/(2m)}，N≥3时b(t)~t^{m/[N(m-1)+2]}。

Conclusion: 退化燃烧动力学存在清晰的维度二分性：二维空间中动力学相对简单（仅三种常数态），高维空间中则出现更丰富的基态解作为吸引子，且自由边界传播的标律指数随维度变化，这为理解多孔介质燃烧问题提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the porous medium equation with a combustion-type reaction, \[ u_t=Δu^m+f(u),\qquad x\in\mathbb R^N,\ t>0, \] for radial, nonnegative, compactly supported initial data. A complete classification of the long-time behaviour of bounded solutions is established. In dimension $N=2$, every such solution converges locally uniformly to one of the constants $0$, $θ$, or $1$; for ordered families of initial data there exists a unique threshold parameter separating vanishing from spreading, and the critical solution converges to the ignition temperature $θ$. In dimensions $N\ge3$, under a natural total-disconnectedness condition on the set of central values of ground states, every bounded solution converges locally uniformly to either $0$, $θ$, $1$, or a radial ground state $U\in\mathcal S$. Moreover, the ignition state $θ$ is excluded as a transition limit in $N\ge3$ via a novel normalized annular perturbation argument. For transition solutions, we provide estimates for the propagation speed of the free boundary: in dimension two, \[ b(t)\asymp \frac{\sqrt t}{(\log t)^{\frac{m-1}{2m}}}, \] and in dimensions $N\ge3$, whenever the limit is a ground state, \[ b(t)\asymp t^{\frac{m}{N(m-1)+2}}. \] These results reveal a sharp dimensional dichotomy in the degenerate combustion dynamics.

</details>


### [351] [Periodic solution and its stability of a damped BBM equation posed on $\mathbb{T}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23431)
*Chun Ho Lau,Taige Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了环面上一类带阻尼的Benjamin-Bona-Mahony(BBM)方程的时间周期解的存在性与稳定性，该方程内部施加了时间周期为θ的周期力f(x,t)。这些解在H^ℓ (ℓ≥0)空间中建立，且当低正则性ℓ∈[0,1)时采用了I-能量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带阻尼的BBM方程在周期外力驱动下时间周期解的存在性与稳定性问题，并特别关注低正则性情形下的分析。

Method: 采用I-能量方法处理低正则性(ℓ∈[0,1))情形，在环面T上建立H^ℓ空间中的时间周期解。

Result: 在H^ℓ (ℓ≥0)空间中建立了带阻尼BBM方程的时间周期解的存在性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了带阻尼BBM方程在周期外力作用下存在时间周期解，且I-能量方法可有效处理低正则性情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we are concerned with the existence and stability of temporal periodic solutions to a class of the Benjamin-Bona-Mahony (BBM) equation with damping in the torus $\mathbb{T}$, whose interior is applied with periodic force $f(x, t)$ with temporal period $θ$. These types of solutions are established in $H^{\ell}, \ell\ge 0$, and it is noteworthy to see that the I-energy method is invoked when low regularity of $\ell\in[0, 1)$ is pursued.

</details>


### [352] [Decay estimates for a class of dispersive equations with partial inverse-square potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23578)
*Jiabin Qian,Manli Song*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带有奇异反平方势的Schrödinger算子$\mathcal{L}_a=-\Delta_x-\Delta_y+\frac{a}{2}|x|^{-2}$的色散半群$e^{it\phi(\sqrt{\mathcal{L}_a})}$的衰减估计，并推导了分数阶Schrödinger算子$e^{it\mathcal{L}_a^\nu}$的边界Strichartz估计，进而获得多种具体波动方程的Strichartz估计，统一并推广了现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Zhang-Zhang已建立的谱测度显式表示，进一步研究带奇异反平方势的Schrödinger算子相关色散半群的衰减估计，并推广到更一般的色散方程。

Method: 利用谱测度的显式表示，结合频率局部化方法和驻相法处理非齐次相函数$\phi$带来的技术困难，推导衰减估计；在此基础上推导边界Strichartz估计并应用于具体波动方程。

Result: 获得了$e^{it\phi(\sqrt{\mathcal{L}_a})}$的衰减估计，推导了分数阶Schrödinger算子的边界Strichartz估计，并为$\phi(r)=r, r^2, r^2+r^4, \sqrt{1+r^2}, \sqrt{1+r^4}, r^\mu$等具体情形建立了Strichartz估计。

Conclusion: 统一并简化了算子$\mathcal{L}_a$的现有色散估计，并将相关理论推广到更一般的场景。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{L}_a=-Δ_x-Δ_y+\frac{a}{2}|x|^{-2}$ with $a>0$ denote the Schrödinger operator on $L^2(\mathbb{R}^2_x\times \mathbb{R}^n_y)$, which involves a singular partial inverse-square potential. The purpose of this manuscript is twofold. First, relying on the explicit representation for the spectral measure associated with the operator $\mathcal{L}_a$ established by Zhang-Zhang [J. Geom. Anal. \textbf{35}(3), Paper No. 71, 27pp (2025)], we investigate the decay estimate for a class of dispersive semigroups of the form $e^{itφ(\sqrt{\mathcal{L}_a})}$, where $φ: \mathbb{R}^+ \to \mathbb{R}$ is a smooth function. To handle the technical difficulty arising from the inhomogeneity of the phase function $φ$, we adopt the frequency localization and the stationary phase method. In the second part of the paper, we first derive boundary Strichartz estimates for the fractional Schrödinger operator $e^{it\mathcal{L}_a^ν}$, $0<ν\neq\frac{1}{2}$. As applications of the established decay estimates, we further obtain Strichartz estimates for some concrete wave equations associated with the operator $\mathcal{L}_a$, which corresponds to $φ(r)=r, r^2, r^2+r^4, \sqrt{1+r^2}, \sqrt{1+r^4}$, and $r^μ,0<μ\neq 1$. Most notably, our results unify and simplify existing dispersive estimates for the operator $\mathcal{L}_a$, while extending the relevant theory to more general scenarios.

</details>


### [353] [Some counterexamples for the special lagrangian curvature equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23592)
*Guohuan Qiu,Guanyu Tao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对特殊拉格朗日曲率方程构造了三个反例，分别展示了：二维中非C¹的Lipschitz粘性解；二维中一致有界C¹范数但曲率无界，且C^{1,β}估计对β>1/3失效；三维次临界相中梯度在解析曲面上有跳跃间断的Lipschitz粘性解。这些反例表明Qiu-Zhou先验估计中凸性和临界相假设的严格必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Qiu与Zhou关于特殊拉格朗日曲率方程先验估计中凸性和临界相位这两个结构假设的严格必要性，通过构造反例揭示若假设不成立时估计的必然失效。

Method: (1) 二维中利用具有常正Gauss曲率的平行曲面的post-focal分支构造显式Lipschitz粘性解；(2) 二维中在固定矩形上构造一列光滑可容许解，具有一致有界C¹范数但某点曲率无界；(3) 三维次临界相中构造Mooney-Savin型Lipschitz粘性解，梯度在解析曲面上呈跳跃间断。

Result: 成功构造三个反例：第一个反例是二维中非C¹的Lipschitz粘性解；第二个反例表明二维中任何一致C^{1,β}估计对β>1/3均失效；第三个反例是三维次临界相中梯度在解析曲面上跳跃的Lipschitz粘性解。

Conclusion: Qiu-Zhou先验估计中凸性和临界相假设是严格必要的，缺少任一假设均会导致正则性估计失效。

Abstract: We construct three counterexamples for the special Lagrangian curvature equation (SLCE). First, in dimension two, we use a post-focal branch of a parallel surface with constant positive Gauss curvature to construct an explicit Lipschitz viscosity solution which is not $C^1$. Second, still in dimension two, we construct a sequence of smooth admissible solutions on a fixed rectangle with uniformly bounded $C^1$-norm but unbounded curvature at one point; furthermore, we show that any uniform $C^{1,β}$ estimate fails for $β> 1/3$. Third, in dimension three and in the subcritical phase, we construct a Mooney-Savin type Lipschitz viscosity solution whose gradient has a jump discontinuity across an analytic surface.
  These examples demonstrate the sharpness of the recent a priori estimates by Qiu and Zhou, revealing that their structural assumptions-convexity and the critical phase-are strictly necessary.

</details>


### [354] [Hessian degeneracy and non-star-shaped superlevel sets of the torsion function on non-convex domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23655)
*Xiuda Liang,Peng Luo,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族光滑单连通非凸平面域，其扭转函数在原点处达到严格全局最大值，且Hessian矩阵的最大特征值趋近于0⁻，表明Steinerberger的Hessian估计不能推广到非凸域；同时该域具有双对称、单向凸、星形等几何性质，并讨论了超水平集非星形与Gladiali–Grossi问题的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Steinerberger (2018) 关于凸平面域扭转函数Hessian估计的结论是否能推广到非凸光滑单连通域；以及回应Gladiali和Grossi (2022) 关于扭转函数超水平集星形性问题的几何条件思考。

Method: 构造一族带裂缝/狭缝的参数化光滑单连通非凸平面域Ω_{a,ε}，通过分析扭转方程的边值问题，计算原点处Hessian矩阵的最大特征值，并验证域直径与内径比一致有界，同时研究超水平集的星形性。

Result: 严格全局最大值点位于原点且Hessian最大特征值趋于0⁻；域直径/内径比一致有界；该域双对称、单向凸、星形；当裂缝足够长时超水平集失去星形性。

Conclusion: Steinerberger的Hessian估计不能推广到光滑单连通非凸域；Gladiali–Grossi问题中仅靠星形性不足以保证超水平集星形，需更强几何条件。

Abstract: We construct a sequence of bounded $C^\infty$-smooth, simply connected and non-convex planar domains $Ω_{a,ε}$ for which the torsion function \[ -Δu_{a,ε}=1\quad\text{in }Ω_{a,ε},\qquad u_{a,ε}=0\quad\text{on }\partialΩ_{a,ε} \] has a strict global maximum point at origin $0$ satisfying \[ λ_{max}\bigl(D^2u_{a,ε}(0)\bigr)\longrightarrow 0^-. \] Moreover, the ratios $\text{diam}(Ω_{a,ε}) / \text{inrad}(Ω_{a,ε})$ remain uniformly bounded. Hence the Hessian estimate proved by Steinerberger (J. Funct. Anal. 274, 1611--1630, 2018) for convex planar domains cannot be extended to the class of smooth simply connected non-convex domains. \vskip0.2cm Moreover, the domains we construct are doubly symmetric, only one-directionally convex, but star-shaped. We show that some superlevel sets of the torsion function fail to be star-shaped when the slits are sufficiently long, hence we can also give some comments on the question raised by Gladiali and Grossi (Amer. J. Math. 144, 1221--1240, 2022) from the perspective of strengthened geometric conditions.

</details>


### [355] [Uniform $L^\infty$ estimates for complex hessian equations on compact Hermitian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23724)
*Truong Dinh Dat*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了紧Hermitian流形上复Hessian方程的多势理论，通过引入适应Hermitian环境的容量理论和非线性迭代方案，在仅假设密度函数f∈L^p(p>1)的条件下获得了有界ω-m-次调和解的L^∞一致估计，并由此得到弱解的存在性、稳定性和紧致性结果，将复Hessian方程的多势理论从Kähler框架推广到了非Kähler情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将复Hessian方程的多势理论从Kähler流形推广到紧Hermitian流形。Kähler框架下背景度量的闭性在多势理论中起关键作用，而Hermitian背景下背景度量非闭，引入的挠率项阻碍了Kähler理论的直接推广，因此需要建立新的分析工具来处理这一困难。

Method: 1) 含挠率项的弱比较原理；2) 适应Hermitian环境的容量理论；3) 控制次水平集衰减的非线性迭代方案。三者结合得到L^∞一致估计，进而通过标准方法得到存在性、稳定性和紧致性。

Result: 对于方程(ω+dd^c u)^m ∧ ω^{n-m} = cf ω^n，在f∈L^p(p>1)非负条件下，获得了有界ω-m-次调和解的均匀L^∞估计，并由此建立了弱解的存在性、稳定性和紧致性结果。

Conclusion: 成功将复Hessian方程的多势理论从Kähler框架拓展至非Kähler的Hermitian框架，为Hermitian流形上的复分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We develop a pluripotential approach to complex Hessian equations on compact Hermitian manifolds. In this setting, the lack of closedness of the background metric introduces torsion terms that prevent a direct extension of the Kähler theory.
  Our main result is a uniform $L^\infty$ estimate for bounded
  $ω$-$m$-subharmonic solutions of the equation
  \[
  (ω+ dd^c u)^m \wedge ω^{n-m} = cf\,ω^n,
  \]
  under the assumption that $f \in L^p$, $f \ge 0$ for some $p>1$. The proof combines a weak comparison principle with torsion error, a capacity theory adapted to the Hermitian setting, and a nonlinear iteration scheme controlling the decay of sublevel sets.
  As applications, we obtain existence, stability and compactness results for weak solutions with $L^p$ densities. These results extend several aspects of the pluripotential theory. of complex Hessian equations beyond the Kähler framework.

</details>


### [356] [Exponential Decay of Solutions to a Fluid-Plate Model with Small Initial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23756)
*Mario Bukal,Igor Kukavica,Linfeng Li,Boris Muha*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维流体-结构相互作用问题，将时变域上的不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程与移动上边界上的平方根阻尼板方程耦合，证明了在适当Sobolev空间中初始数据足够小时强解的指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究时变域中流体与弹性结构相互作用（特别是带阻尼的板方程）的长期行为，证明强解随时间指数衰减。

Method: 结合高阶能量估计、Stokes型正则性界和非线性bootstrap方案，在小初始数据假设下闭合证明。

Result: 证明了对于足够小的初始数据，耦合系统的强解具有指数衰减性。

Conclusion: 该结果为带阻尼的流固耦合问题提供了长期稳定性理论保证。

Abstract: We consider a three-dimensional fluid-structure interaction problem coupling the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations in a time-dependent domain with a square-root damped plate equation, which is posed on the moving upper boundary of the fluid. We prove, a priori, the exponential decay of strong solutions for initial data that are sufficiently small in a suitable Sobolev space. The proof combines higher-order energy estimates, Stokes-type regularity bounds, and a nonlinear bootstrap scheme that closes under the smallness assumption.

</details>


### [357] [Weak stability and random mean-field limit of the phase-spatially extended kinetic Cucker-Smale model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23906)
*Seung-Yeal Ha,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究动力学Cucker-Smale（KCS）模型在相空间扩展环境下的弱稳定性，针对具有指数速度尾部和有限空间二阶矩的测度值解，建立了有限时间Osgood型弱稳定性，并应用于i.i.d.采样收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注相空间受限情况，而实际应用中速度分布可能无界，导致对齐力缺乏一致Lipschitz界，需要建立更一般的弱稳定性理论。

Method: 基于Osgood型不等式，在指数速度尾部和有限空间二阶矩条件下，推导测度值解的弱稳定性；利用该稳定性证明独立同分布采样经验测度向解析解的收敛性。

Result: 对于有界Lipschitz通信权函数，获得了KCS模型测度值解的有限时间Osgood型弱稳定性；作为应用，证明了经验测度在任意有限时间内依期望收敛到解析解。

Conclusion: 在相空间扩展环境下，KCS模型的解算子虽非Lipschitz连续，但具有Osgood型弱稳定性，足以支撑采样收敛性分析。

Abstract: We study the weak stability of the kinetic Cucker-Smale (in short, KCS) model in a phase-spatially extended setting, which can be formally derived from the infinite Cucker-Smale model in the mean-field limit. For a bounded Lipschitz communication weight function, we derive finite-time Osgood-type weak stability for measure-valued solutions with exponential velocity tails and finite spatial second moments. Unlike the phase-spatially confined setting, the solution operator to the KCS model is not Lipschitz continuous with respect to initial data. This is due to the unbounded velocity tail and the corresponding absence of a uniform Lipschitz bound for the alignment force. As an application of weak stability, we obtain an i.i.d. sampling consequence: empirical measures generated from independent initial samples converge to the measure-value solution for the corresponding kinetic model in any finite time interval, in expectation.

</details>


### [358] [Non-radial solutions for the quasi-linear Hénon type $N$-Laplacian Liouville equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24012)
*Wei Dai,Lixiu Duan,Changfeng Gui,Yuan Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究拟线性加权N-Laplacian Liouville方程的非径向解存在性问题，通过线性化分析、逼近方法和分岔理论，证明在临界参数值处存在非径向解，将二维Laplacian情形推广到N维N-Laplacian情形，并克服了非线性N-Laplacian、缺乏Green积分表示、临界加权Sobolev嵌入不等式缺失、Kelvin变换不适用、总质量在缩放下的不变性以及解变号且发散到负无穷等一系列关键困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将J. Prajapat和G. Tarantello关于二维Laplacian情形的存在性结果推广到更一般的N维N-Laplacian情形，并将F. Gladiali等人和作者先前从1<p<N的结果推广到更复杂的极限情形p=N。

Method: 通过仔细研究线性化问题，应用逼近方法和分岔理论进行证明。

Result: 当参数α等于临界值α(k)时，存在非径向解u，满足u~ln|x|，|∇u|=O(|x|^{-1})在无穷远处，且积分∫|x|^{Nα}e^u dx等于特定常数。

Conclusion: 成功将二维Laplacian情形推广到N维N-Laplacian情形（N≥2），并将1<p<N的结果推广到极限情形p=N。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the following quasi-linear weighted $N$-Laplacian Liouville equation \begin{equation*}\label{0} -Δ_N u=|x|^{Nα}e^{u}, \qquad x\in \R^N, \end{equation*} where $N \geq 2$. For $\al>0$, by carefully studying the linearized problem and applying the approximation method and bifurcation theory, we prove that, when the parameter $α$ equals to the critical values $α(k):=\frac{\sqrt{k(N-1)(k+N-2)}}{N-1}-1$ for $k \geq 2$, there exist non-radial solutions $u$ (bifurcating from $U_{α(k)}$) to the above quasi-linear Hénon type Liouville equation such that $u\sim \ln|x|$, $|\nabla u|= O(|x|^{-1})$ at $\infty$ and $\int_{\R^N}|x|^{Nα}e^{u}\md x=N\left(\frac{N^2}{N-1}\right)^{N-1}(α+1)^{N-1}ω_N$. One should note that, $α(k)=k-1$ for $k\geq2$ when $N=2$. Our results successfully extend the existence result of J. Prajapat and G. Tarantello in \cite{PT} concerning the $2$-dimension and Laplacian case (i.e., $N=2$) to the more general $N$-dimension and $N$-Laplacian cases ($N\geq 2$), and extend the results of F. Gladiali, M. Grossi, and S. L. N. Neves in \cite{GGN} and the authors in \cite{DDGL} from $1<p<N$ to the much more complicated limiting case $p=N$. We introduced some new ideas and overcame a series of crucial difficulties, including the nonlinearity nature of the $N$-Laplacian $Δ_N$, the lack of Green integral representation formula and critical weighted Sobolev embedding inequality, the absence of Kelvin type transforms for linearized/difference equations, the invariance of the total mass under scalings of $u$, and the signs-changing and divergence (to $-\infty$) at $\infty$ of the solutions, which makes the suitable choices of the approximate problems, the (normalized) approximate function sequences and the working space to be quite difficult.

</details>


### [359] [Edge State Propagation Near Multiple and Singular Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24069)
*Nicolas Frantz,{É}ric Vacelet*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维Dirac算子在空间变化质量下的边缘态半经典传播。对于由两条不相交光滑曲线组成的界面，证明了波包长时间局限在单一界面上，并量化了界面间距对近似精度的影响。对于含有孤立奇点的界面，确定了波包逼近奇点时近似保持有效的参数区间，以及估计失去一致性的标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑相界面附近边缘态的动力学行为，特别是在界面存在不同几何结构（分离界面或奇点）时，半经典波包的传播特性及其局限性的定量刻画。

Method: 采用半经典分析框架，对二维Dirac算子在不同界面几何（光滑分离曲线和含奇点界面）下的边缘态传播进行渐近分析，推导波包演化估计并量化近似误差。

Result: 对于分离界面，波包长时间保持局限；对于奇点界面，确定了近似有效的参数区间及估计失效的临界标度。

Conclusion: 界面几何结构显著影响边缘态传播的半经典近似精度，分离距离和奇点尺度是关键参数。

Abstract: We study the semiclassical propagation of edge-states for a two-dimensional Dirac operator with a spatially varying mass. The zero set of the mass models the interface between topological phases and is allowed either to consist of two disjoint smooth curves or to possess an isolated singular point. For disjoint interfaces, we prove that a wave packet initially localized on one component remains confined near this interface over long semiclassical time scales and we quantify the influence of the distance between the two components on the accuracy of the approximation. For singular interfaces, we determine the regime in which the wave-packet approximation remains valid as the packet approaches the singularity and identify the scaling at which our estimates lose their uniformity.

</details>


### [360] [Hausdorff type Time-Trace Observability for Airy Equations on the Line and Point Observability on the Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24076)
*Ze Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了实直线上Airy方程的Hausdorff厚集可观测性不等式、周期集上的可观测性，以及环面上带势能Airy方程的有限点可观测性充要条件（Kalman秩条件）。由于Airy方程缺乏高频指数衰减和逐点光滑效应，论文引入了若干新思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Airy方程（及线性KdV方程）缺乏热方程所具有的高频指数衰减和逐点光滑效应，因此现有基于Hausdorff测度的观测结果无法直接推广。本文旨在建立适用于Airy方程特性的可观测性理论。

Method: (1) 利用块上确界泛函在Hausdorff厚集上建立时间迹意义下的可观测性不等式；(2) 构造周期集上的观测策略，进而导出空间点序列的可观测性；(3) 在环面上引入Kalman秩条件，给出有限点观测的充要条件；(4) 对于有限正则性势能，证明具有聚点的观测集（特别是正Hausdorff维数集）可观测。

Result: (1) 对任意观测时间T>0，实直线上Hausdorff厚集的可观测性不等式成立；(2) 周期集及一类空间点序列的可观测性不等式；(3) 环面上有限点可观测的充要条件为Kalman秩条件，并得到Airy和线性KdV方程的精确点可观测结果；(4) 具有聚点的观测集（包括正Hausdorff维数集）的可观测性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Airy方程在缺乏热方程典型正则化性质下的可观测性理论，填补了该领域的空白。所提出的新思想（如块上确界泛函、Kalman秩条件）为处理类似色散方程的可观测性问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: The main results of this paper are threefold. First, we prove an observability inequality for the Airy equation on the real line from Hausdorff-thick sets in a time-trace sense for every observation time $T>0$. The observation functional is a block supremum of $L^2(0,T)$ time traces over the Hausdorff-thick set. Second, we prove observability inequalities for the Airy equation on the real line with observations on some periodic sets, which in particular yields observability on a class of spatial point sequences. Third, we give a necessary and sufficient condition for finite point observability for the Airy equation on the torus with a bounded real-valued potential. Indeed, for a finite observation set $F$, a Kalman rank condition on a finite-dimensional invariant subspace is found. As a corollary, we obtain sharp point observability results for the Airy and linear KdV equations on the torus. Moreover, for potentials with finite order regularity assumptions we prove that every observation set with an accumulation point, in particular any set of positive Hausdorff dimension, gives an observability inequality for each observation time $T>0$.
  Since the Airy equation has neither high frequency exponential decay nor pointwise smoothing effects, which are essential in recent works on Hausdorff type observation results on heat equations, we introduce several new ideas adapted to the Airy case.

</details>


### [361] [Time-refined Triebel--Lizorkin Estimates and Applications to Keller--Segel Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24089)
*Wenhai Shan,Xiao-song Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在临界齐次Triebel-Lizorkin空间中发展热流估计，并将其应用于二维Keller-Segel型方程。针对$q>2$时热流从$\dot F^0_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2)$到$L^2(0,T;\dot F^1_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2))$的无界性，引入时间精细Triebel-Lizorkin空间，建立热光滑估计与极大正则性，并证明一系列关键估计。在$\dot F^0_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2)$ ($2\le q<\infty$)空间中，对二维抛物-椭圆Keller-Segel方程证明了任意初值的局部适定性和小初值的全局适定性，并将框架推广至双组分漂移-扩散系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热流估计在临界齐次Triebel-Lizorkin空间中对于$q>2$的情形是无界的，限制了Keller-Segel型方程在临界空间中的适定性分析。因此需要引入新的函数空间框架来克服这一障碍。

Method: 引入时间精细Triebel-Lizorkin空间（时间范数先于二进求和的函数空间），建立热光滑估计与极大正则性；证明Banach值Peetre估计、Banach值Jawerth型估计、齐次Poisson估计以及Keller-Segel漂移项的端点双线性估计。

Result: 在临界空间$\dot F^0_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2)$ ($2\le q<\infty$)中，对二维抛物-椭圆Keller-Segel方程证明了任意初值的局部适定性和小初值的全局适定性；类似结果也适用于双组分漂移-扩散系统。

Conclusion: 所建立的时间精细Triebel-Lizorkin空间框架有效克服了临界空间中热流估计的无界性问题，为Keller-Segel型方程在临界空间中的适定性分析提供了统一且有效的工具。

Abstract: We develop heat-flow estimates in critical homogeneous Triebel--Lizorkin spaces and apply them to two-dimensional Keller--Segel type equations. For $q>2$, we show that the heat-flow from $\dot F^0_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2)$ to $L^2(0,T;\dot F^1_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2))$ is unbounded. This motivates the introduction of new spaces, time-refined Triebel--Lizorkin spaces, in which the time norm is taken before the dyadic summation. In this framework, we establish a family of heat smoothing estimates together with the corresponding maximal regularity. We further prove Banach-valued Peetre estimates, Banach-valued Jawerth-type estimate, homogeneous Poisson estimate, and an endpoint bilinear estimate for the Keller--Segel drift. As an application, we obtain local well-posedness for arbitrary initial data and global well-posedness for sufficiently small initial data in the critical space $\dot F^0_{1,q}(\mathbb R^2)$, $2\le q<\infty$, for the two-dimensional parabolic--elliptic Keller--Segel equation. The same analytic framework also yields analogous well-posedness results for a related two-component drift--diffusion system.

</details>


### [362] [Global Existence and Pathwise Uniqueness for a Stochastic Parabolic-Parabolic Keller-Segel System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24128)
*Jinhuan Wang,Qian Li,Hui Huang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维有界区域上由非局部非线性乘性噪声驱动的随机抛物-抛物Keller-Segel系统，在无需小量假设的条件下建立了强解的全局存在性和路径唯一性，通过引入截断系统、Banach不动点定理和Lyapunov泛函克服了耦合、缺乏强制性和全局Lipschitz连续性等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性二维Keller-Segel系统通常需要对初始质量施加小量假设才能保证全局解的存在性，本文旨在研究随机噪声驱动下该系统的全局可解性，探究随机性是否能消除对小量条件的依赖。

Method: 1) 引入定制截断系统，利用Banach不动点定理建立局部存在性；2) 结合局部存在性和路径唯一性构造极大局部强解；3) 引入专门的Lyapunov泛函推导一致估计，将局部解延拓为全局解。

Result: 在无需任何小量假设的条件下，对任意初始数据建立了随机抛物-抛物Keller-Segel系统强解的全局存在性和路径唯一性。

Conclusion: 随机噪声的引入可以克服确定性Keller-Segel系统对小量条件的依赖，使得任意初始数据下全局强解的存在性成为可能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a stochastic parabolic-parabolic Keller-Segel system driven by nonlocal, nonlinear multiplicative noise in a two-dimensional bounded domain. Under suitable assumptions, we establish global existence and pathwise uniqueness of a strong solution for arbitrary initial data, without imposing any smallness conditions. This sharply contrasts with the deterministic two-dimensional Keller-Segel system, which typically requires smallness assumptions on initial mass.
  The main analytical challenges stem from the fully parabolic coupling, combined with a lack of coercivity and global Lipschitz continuity in both the chemotactic drift and noise terms. To overcome this, we introduce a tailored truncated system to establish local existence via Banach's fixed point theorem. Using this local existence and pathwise uniqueness, we construct a maximal local strong solution. Finally, by introducing a specialized Lyapunov functional, we derive uniform estimates to extend this solution globally.

</details>


### [363] [New definitions of a solution\break of a one-dimensional elliptic equation\break with a singular first order divergence term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24308)
*Daniela Giachetti,Pedro Jesús Martínez-Aparicio,François Murat,Francesco Petitta*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文延续先前工作，针对一维椭圆非线性奇异问题提出了两种新的弱解定义，解分别属于W₀^{1,1}∩L¹和W₀^{1,m}∩L^m空间，并证明了非存在性、存在性及解的非孤立性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为含奇异势φ(s)=1/|s|^γ (γ>0)的一维椭圆问题建立更完善的弱解理论框架，推广先前研究[1]。

Method: 提出两种弱解定义：1) u∈W₀^{1,1}(0,L)且φ(u)∈L¹(0,L); 2) u∈W₀^{1,m}(0,L)且φ(u)∈L^m(0,L) (m>1)。在此基础上进行理论分析。

Result: 在两种弱解框架下分别证明了非存在性、存在性以及解的非孤立性等结论。

Conclusion: 为奇异椭圆问题提供了新的弱解定义框架，丰富了该类问题的解的理论性质。

Abstract: In this paper, which is a continuation of our recent paper [1], we give two new definitions of a weak solution of the one-dimensional, elliptic, nonlinear singular problem which is formally written as $$ \begin{cases} \displaystyle -\frac{d}{dx}\left(a(x) \frac{d u}{dx}\right) = - \frac{d φ(u) }{dx}- \frac{d g(x) }{dx}& \text{in}\;(0,L),\\ \newline u(0)=u(L)=0\,, & \end{cases} $$ where $φ:\mathbb{R}\mapsto\mathbb{R}\cup\{+\infty\}$ is a singular function whose model is given by $\displaystyleφ(s)=φ_γ(s)=\frac{1}{|s|^γ}$ with $γ>0$. In these two definitions the solutions belong respectively to the space $u\in W_0^{1,1}(0,L)$ with $φ(u)\in L^1(0,L)$, and to the space $u\in W_0^{1,m}(0,L)$ with $φ(u)\in L^m(0,L)$ for $m>1$. In these frameworks we state and prove results of non-existence, existence, and non-isolation of the solutions.

</details>


### [364] [Energy relations for the generalised Vladimirov derivative via Bruhat-Tits tree extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24316)
*An Huang,Yaojia Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在Bruhat-Tits树$T_p$上建立了非阿基米德版本的Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓的严格证明，通过将边界$\partial T_p$与$\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q}_p)$等同，利用能量关系再现了重缩放边界函数的广义Vladimirov导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓是研究欧氏空间分数阶Laplacian的重要工具，作者希望将其推广到非阿基米德场景（p-adic数域），从而在Bruhat-Tits树这种离散/树状结构上建立类似的延拓理论，为p-adic分析和数论提供新工具。

Method: 论文采用解析与几何结合的方法：首先将Bruhat-Tits树$T_p$的边界$\partial T_p$识别为$\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q}_p)$（p-adic投影直线），然后在该树结构上定义能量（Dirichlet能量）关系，通过延拓过程将边界函数的分数阶导数（广义Vladimirov导数）与树上的能量积分联系起来，从而构造出非阿基米德的Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓。

Result: 成功证明了在Bruhat-Tits树上存在非阿基米德版本的Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓，并严格推导出该延拓通过能量关系重缩放后恰好给出边界函数的广义Vladimirov导数算子。

Conclusion: 该工作为p-adic分析中分数阶微分算子的几何与解析理解提供了统一框架，拓展了Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓的适用范围，对p-adic调和分析、数论及量子信息等领域具有潜在价值。

Abstract: We formulate a proof on non-Archimedean analogue of the Caffarelli-Silvestre extension for fractional Laplacian on the Bruhat-Tits tree $T_p$ in terms of energy relations after identifying $\partial T_p$ with $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q}_p)$, which reproduces the generalised Vladimirov derivative of the rescaled boundary function.

</details>


### [365] [Stochastic phase separation driven by transport noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24326)
*Andrea Di Primio,Andrea Papini,Luca Scarpa*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由迁移噪声驱动的Cahn-Hilliard模型，用于捕捉湍流中相分离过程的随机性。研究在热力学框架下（奇异Flory-Huggins势与可能退化的迁移率）分析了该模型，考虑Ito和Stratonovich两种噪声形式，在二维和三维空间中建立了系统的适定性和热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂流体中的相分离现象对流体动力学效应敏感，特别是湍流引起的平流作用会在多个空间尺度上影响分离过程。现有模型未能充分捕捉湍流固有的随机性，因此需要构建一个能反映这种随机性的数学模型。

Method: 本文采用由迁移噪声驱动的Cahn-Hilliard模型，使用奇异的Flory-Huggins势和可能退化的迁移率，分别考虑Ito和Stratonovich两种随机积分形式的噪声，在二维和三维空间中进行数学分析。

Result: 在二维和三维空间中，建立了该随机Cahn-Hilliard系统的适定性（well-posedness）；证明了模型的热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 本文作为初步研究，为后续探索湍流驱动的相分离随机模型奠定了数学理论基础，验证了模型在热力学框架下的合理性。

Abstract: Phase separation phenomena occurring in complex fluids are known to be sensitive to hydrodynamic effects: when a binary fluid mixture undergoes spinodal decomposition, the emerging pattern of domains is no longer dictated by interfacial dynamics alone, but is reshaped by advection. In several applications of interest, advection effects may even cause the onset of turbulence, further affecting the segregation process on a wide range of spatial scales. Motivated by this, we propose a Cahn-Hilliard model driven by transport noise able to capture the intrinsic stochasticity of turbulence. The model is analyzed in its thermodynamically-relevant framework, namely employing a singular Flory-Huggins potential and a possibly degenerate mobility, and the noise is considered both in Ito and Stratonovich form. In this work, as a first-step investigation, we establish well-posedness of the system in two and three spatial dimensions and its thermodynamical consistency.

</details>


### [366] [Blow up and Concentration without Quantization: sharp Harnack type inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24346)
*Daniele Bartolucci,Paolo Cosentino,Lina Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对带有点奇性的扰动奇异Liouville方程序列，在“爆破与浓度无量子化”现象的临界阈值处，通过建立新的Harnack型不等式，并结合Pohozaev恒等式、椭圆估计和“Sup+CInf”不等式，分别对“快”和“慢”两种爆破模式进行了精细的爆破分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Onsager统计力学描述带有点奇性的湍流Euler流的启发，需要处理奇异Liouville方程在临界阈值处的“浓度无量子化”这一精细现象，现有方法在此阈值处失效。

Method: 首先建立新的锐利Harnack型不等式来应对奇异极限；然后针对奇性带来的共形因子增长问题，分别对“快”和“慢”爆破采用不同策略：基于Pohozaev恒等式、椭圆估计以及“Sup+CInf”不等式进行论证。

Result: 成功在临界阈值处完成了对扰动奇异Liouville方程序列的爆破分析，解决了传统量子化论证因共形因子增长而失效的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解带有奇性的湍流Euler流统计力学提供了更精细的分析工具，完善了“浓度无量子化”现象的理论框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the Onsager statistical mechanics description of turbulent Euler flows with point singularities, we refine the blow up analysis for sequences of solutions of a class of perturbed singular Liouville equations which share the phenomenon of "blow up and concentration without quantization". The problem is delicate because we are dealing with the exact threshold value above which one meets the well known "concentration without quantization" phenomenon, as recently pushed forward in [C.S. Lin, G. Tarantello, C. R. Math. Acad. Sci. Paris (2016)] and in [Y. Lee, C.S. Lin, G. Tarantello, W. Yang, Comm. PDE. (2017)]. First of all we need a new sharp Harnack type inequality for this particularly rich singular limit. However this is not enough, since the growth of the conformal factor inherited by the singularity prevents the use of classical quantization arguments. We solve also this issue with different strategies for "fast" and "slow" blow up, by a careful adaptation of arguments based on the Pohozaev identity, elliptic estimates and "Sup+CInf" inequalities in the same spirit of [C.C. Chen, C.S. Lin, Comm. An. Geom. (1998)].

</details>


### [367] [A logistic equation with non-local operators of order near zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24357)
*Luigi Appolloni,Serena Dipierro,Enrico Valdinoci*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由"近零阶"非局部算子驱动的方程，带有混合Dirichlet-Neumann边界条件，用于建模封闭环境中经历极重尾扩散过程的自竞争种群。针对非线性反应项的不同假设以及Dirichlet/Neumann条件集合的不同配置，建立了种群可存活状态的存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解封闭环境中经历极重尾扩散过程的自竞争种群的动力学行为，探究在不同边界条件和反应项下种群存活的数学条件。

Method: 采用"近零阶"非局部算子驱动的方程，设置混合Dirichlet-Neumann边界条件，通过分析非线性反应项的不同假设以及边界条件集合的不同配置来推导种群可存活状态的存在性。

Result: 建立了在多种非线性反应项假设和Dirichlet/Neumann边界条件配置下，种群可存活状态的存在性定理。

Conclusion: 成功刻画了在极重尾扩散过程和混合边界条件下自竞争种群存活性的数学条件，为理解此类复杂扩散系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper we study an equation driven by a non-local operator of order "near zero" with mixed Dirichlet-Neumann boundary conditions, modelling a biological system consisting of a population self-competing for resources in a closed environment and subject to an extremely heavy-tailed dispersal process. We establish several results concerning the existence of states in which the population can survive requiring different assumptions on the non-linear reaction term and on the various configurations of the sets where the Dirichlet or Neumann conditions hold.

</details>


### [368] [Sharpness and Stability of the Alexandrov-Bakelman-Pucci Estimate: a Convex Geometric Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24388)
*Antonio Porricelli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文基于Talenti(1981)关于Monge-Ampère方程的比较原理和二维先验估计，证明了其先验估计的定量版本，并通过凸几何建立与Blaschke-Santaló不等式及其Mahler逆形式的联系，进而利用该联系获得椭圆PDE中Alexandrov-Bakelman-Pucci (ABP)最大值原理的尖锐洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Talenti分析框架与Mahler/Blaschke-Santaló不等式之间的深层联系，从而为椭圆PDE中的ABP最大值原理提供更精确的理解。

Method: 通过凸几何作为桥梁，将Talenti关于Monge-Ampère方程的先验估计与Blaschke-Santaló不等式及其Mahler逆形式联系起来，并证明Talenti先验估计的一个定量版本。

Result: 获得了Talenti先验估计的定量版本，并利用该定量结果与凸几何中的等周型不等式之间的关联，对ABP最大值原理给出了更尖锐的刻画。

Conclusion: Talenti的分析方法与Mahler/Blaschke-Santaló凸几何不等式之间存在内在联系，这种交叉可为椭圆型偏微分方程的正则性理论和最大值原理提供新的定量视角。

Abstract: Based on a 1981 work by G. Talenti, which established a comparison principle for smooth solutions of the Monge-Ampère equation alongside related two-dimensional a priori estimates, this paper explores the connection to the Blaschke-Santaló inequality and its reverse form by K. Mahler (1939). More specifically, in this work, a quantitative version of one of Talenti's a priori estimates is proved. We exploit the interplay between Talenti's analytical framework and Mahler/Blaschke-Santaló inequalities via convex geometry, demonstrating how this link can be utilized to gain sharp insights into the Alexandrov-Bakelman-Pucci (ABP) maximum principle for elliptic PDEs.

</details>


### [369] [Local Well-Posedness for Vlasov--Poisson with $L^{d+}$ Initial Density and Fractional Velocity Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24400)
*Quoc-Hung Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Vlasov-Poisson系统在初始分布满足各向异性假设时的局部适定性，关键估计是密度在时空中的Hölder正则性，并通过Schauder不动点和Loeper型稳定性建立了解的存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低正则性框架下建立Vlasov-Poisson系统的适定性，特别是允许初始分布仅在速度方向具有任意小的正Hölder正则性，而非传统所需的整体光滑性。

Method: 利用非线性混合估计导出密度在时间积分意义下的空间Hölder正则性，基于Schauder不动点定理构造解，并通过Loeper型稳定性估计证明唯一性。

Result: 得到了密度ρ(t)在C^α_x范数下的衰减估计，以及电场属于L^1_t C^{1,α}_x的正则性结果。

Conclusion: 在较弱的各向异性初始条件下（速度方向仅需任意小正Hölder正则性），Vlasov-Poisson系统仍是局部适定的。

Abstract: We prove a local well-posedness criterion for the Vlasov--Poisson system on $\R^d_x\times\R^d_v$, $d\geq2$, under an anisotropic assumption on the initial distribution. The datum has finite mass, its weighted velocity supremum belongs to $L^p_x$ for some $p>d$, and it has an arbitrarily small positive Hölder regularity in the velocity variable, uniformly with respect to velocity and with the same spatial $L^p$ control. The main estimate is a nonlinear mixing bound \[
  [ρ(t)]_{C^α_x}\lesssim
  t^{-d/p-ε}C(f_0),
  \qquad ε>0~~\text{small}. \] Thus the density is integrable in time with values in a positive spatial Hölder class, and the corresponding electric field belongs to $L^1_tC^{1,α}_x$. We construct a solution by a Schauder fixed point on the density and prove uniqueness by a Loeper-type stability estimate.

</details>


### [370] [Optimization of the total tumor population under Gompertz growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24468)
*Iulia Martina Bulai,Francesca Gladiali,Benedetta Pellacci*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有Gompertz生长的肿瘤细胞种群空间分布的稳态反应-扩散模型的最优控制问题，控制变量表示受L¹-L∞约束的治疗项。当生长率为常数时，均匀治疗分布是唯一最小化器；最大化问题的最优控制呈bang-bang结构；一维情形下扩散率足够大时，最优控制的正集为粘附于边界的一个区间。数值模拟验证了理论结果并揭示了扩散系数对优化总种群的单调依赖这一新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有Gompertz生长的肿瘤细胞种群空间分布模型的最优控制问题，分析治疗项在不同约束下的最优策略，弥补现有理论在Gompertz生长设定下的空白。

Method: 采用稳态反应-扩散方程描述肿瘤细胞空间分布，控制项m(x)作为密度依赖的移除率，受L¹和L∞范数双重约束。通过变分法和最优控制理论分析最小化与最大化问题，证明最优控制的结构性质；在一维情形下利用扩散率条件判断最优控制正集的几何形态；辅以数值模拟探索理论未完全覆盖的参数区域。

Result: 常数增长率下均匀治疗分布是唯一最小化器；最大化问题最优控制为bang-bang类型；一维扩散率足够大时最优控制正集为端点邻接区间；数值模拟确认bang-bang结构，并揭示扩散系数对优化总种群具有单调依赖关系——这是与Logistic设定不同的新现象。

Conclusion: Gompertz生长模型下的最优治疗策略在最小化问题中趋于均匀分布，在最大化问题中呈现bang-bang结构；空间异质性、局部治疗区域和扩散系数显著影响最优控制形态；扩散系数与优化总种群之间的单调关系是Gompertz模型区别于Logistic模型的关键特征。

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for a stationary reaction--diffusion model describing the spatial distribution of a tumor cell population with Gompertz growth. The control $m(x)$ represents a treatment term acting as a density-dependent removal rate and it is subject to $L^{1}-L^{\infty}$ constraints. When the intrinsic growth rate is constant, the uniform distribution of the treatment is shown to be the unique minimizer. For the maximization problem, we prove that every optimal control is of bang-bang type. In addition, we show that in the one dimensional case and for sufficiently large diffusion rates, the positivity set of optimal controls is an interval sticking to one of the extrema of the domain.
  Finally, numerical simulations complement the theoretical analysis and explore regimes that are not fully covered by the results proved in the paper. The computations confirm the bang-bang structure of maximizers, and illustrate how the shape of optimal controls and the associated states are affected by
  spatial heterogeneity in the growth rate, localized admissible treatment regions, and the diffusion coefficient. Moreover, they reveal a monotone dependence of the optimized total population on the diffusion coefficient: this is a new phenomenon with respect to the logistic setting.

</details>


### [371] [Asymptotic behavior of the first Robin eigenvalue of nonlinear operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24488)
*Rosa Barbato,Francesco Della Pietra,Alba Lia Masiello*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界Lipschitz域上p-Laplace算子第一Robin特征值在β→0和β→+∞时的渐近行为，推导出了特征值的尖锐渐近展开式；其中β→+∞（Dirichlet极限）的展开式需额外假设边界为C^{1,1}光滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示p-Laplace算子Robin特征值在极端参数（β→0和β→+∞）下的精确渐近行为，填补该领域在尖锐渐近展开方面的理论空白。

Method: 通过分析Robin边界条件中参数β的极限情形，利用变分方法和谱理论，分别处理β→0（趋向Neumann极限）和β→+∞（趋向Dirichlet极限）两种情形，其中后者需要边界C^{1,1}光滑性假设以保证展开的精确性。

Result: 得到了p-Laplace算子第一Robin特征值在β→0和β→+∞时的尖锐渐近展开式。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了Robin特征值在两种极端参数下的渐近行为，且指出Dirichlet极限下的展开需要更高的边界正则性条件。

Abstract: Let $Ω$ be a bounded Lipschitz domain of $\mathbb R^N$, $N\geq 2$. In this paper, we study the asymptotic behavior of the first Robin eigenvalue of the $p$-Laplace operator as $β$ goes to $0$ and as $β$ goes to $+\infty$, deriving sharp asymptotic expansions of the eigenvalue; the expansion in the Dirichlet limit $β\to+\infty$ is obtained under the additional assumption that $\partialΩ$ is of class $C^{1,1}$.

</details>


### [372] [Weak Solutions for Inviscid SQG with Lorentz Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24500)
*Peter Constantin,Mihaela Ignatova,Quoc-Hung Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在$\mathbb R^2$和光滑有界区域中，针对临界Lorentz空间$L^{4/3,2}$中的任意初始数据，构造了无粘表面准地转方程的全局弱解，这些解在所有时间内守恒Hamilton量$\|Λ^{-1/2}θ(t)\|_{L^2}^2$。方法采用针对Lorentz空间定制的逼近方案，通过分布函数中保留序关系的速度与初始数据平滑化，并利用解的高振幅截断的Lorentz范数一致有界性消除逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临界Lorentz空间$L^{4/3,2}$中构造无粘表面准地转方程的全局弱解，并探究该空间中解的守恒性质。

Method: 采用针对Lorentz空间定制的逼近方案：平滑化平流速度和初始数据，在分布函数中保留序关系；通过解的高振幅截断的Lorentz范数一致有界性消除逼近。

Result: 在$\mathbb R^2$和光滑有界区域中，对任意初始数据在$L^{4/3,2}$中的无粘表面准地转方程，构造了全局弱解，且解守恒$\|Λ^{-1/2}θ(t)\|_{L^2}^2$。

Conclusion: 证明了临界Lorentz空间$L^{4/3,2}$中无粘SQG方程全局弱解的存在性，其中第二个Lorentz指数由$\Lambda^{-1/2}:L^{4/3,2}\to L^2$的 sharp有界性决定，该估计对$L^{4/3,q}$（$q>2$）不成立。

Abstract: We construct global weak solutions of the inviscid surface quasi-geostrophic equation in $\mathbb R^2$ and in smooth bounded domains, for arbitrary initial data in the critical Lorentz space $L^{4/3,2}$. The solutions conserve the Hamiltonian $\|Λ^{-1/2}θ(t)\|_{L^2}^2$ for all times. The second Lorentz exponent is determined by the sharp boundedness $Λ^{-1/2}:L^{4/3,2}\to L^2$; the corresponding estimate fails for $L^{4/3,q}$ when $q>2$. We use an approximation scheme that is tailored for Lorentz spaces. It smooths the advecting velocity and the initial data, and preserves order in distribution functions. Removing the approximation is made possible by uniform bounds for the Lorentz norms of high amplitude cutoffs of the solutions.

</details>


### [373] [Tikhonov-Regularized Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Terminal-State Distributed Optimal Control of Parabolic Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24572)
*Nguyen Thanh Quang,Ta Thi Thanh Mai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Tikhonov正则化的物理信息神经网络(PINNs)框架，用于求解受抛物线型偏微分方程约束的终端状态跟踪最优控制问题。正则化引导优化器寻找最小能量控制，恢复数值稳定性。理论上建立了一致性结果和新的误差估计，数值实验在热方程和Burgers方程上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端状态跟踪最优控制问题本质上是病态的——存在无穷多个分布控制都能使系统达到同一期望状态，需要正则化来引导优化器寻找最小能量控制并恢复数值稳定性。

Method: 提出PINNs框架结合Tikhonov正则化；理论上建立一致性结果（证明PINNs最小化器在残差和求积近似假设下几乎达到连续正则化目标）以及新的误差估计（将学习到的控制与最小能量解的偏差用PINNs训练残差和正则化参数界住）。

Result: 在线性热方程和非线性Burgers方程上的数值实验表明，正则化PINNs框架准确实现了目标终端状态，与未正则化的基线相比，产生的控制能量显著更低且轮廓更平滑。

Conclusion: 正则化PINNs框架能有效求解病态的终端状态跟踪最优控制问题，在保持准确性的同时获得更平滑、能量更低的控制解，且有理论保证。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) framework that incorporates Tikhonov regularization to solve terminal-state tracking optimal control constrained by parabolic partial differential equations (PDEs). This problem is inherently ill-posed, as infinitely many distributed controls may drive the system to the same desired state, so the regularization guides the optimizer toward the minimum-energy control, restoring numerical stability and yielding a smooth, physically meaningful solution. On the theoretical side, we establish a consistency result showing that PINNs minimizers nearly attain the continuous regularized objective under residual and quadrature approximation assumptions, and a novel error estimate that bounds the deviation of the learned control from the minimum-energy solution in terms of the PINNs training residuals and the regularization parameter. Numerical experiments on the linear heat equation and the nonlinear Burgers'equation demonstrate that the regularized PINNs framework accurately achieves the target terminal state while producing controls with significantly lower energy and smoother profiles compared to unregularized baselines.

</details>


### [374] [Positive-regularity norm inflation for the 3D hypodissipative Navier--Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24635)
*Guirong Tang,Shiyang Xiong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维亚耗散Navier-Stokes方程（耗散项(−Δ)^α，0<α<1）在特定正则性空间（W^{s,p}和B^s_{p,q}）中存在范数膨胀现象：存在任意小的光滑散度自由初值，其唯一局部光滑解在任意短时间内变得任意大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分数阶耗散对Navier-Stokes方程正则性理论的影响，特别是在亚耗散情形（0<α<1）下，验证是否仍存在类似完全耗散情形（α=1）的范数膨胀现象，进而理解耗散强度与解的正则性门槛之间的关系。

Method: 将各向异性涡环混合机制（anisotropic vortex-ring mixing mechanism）推广至分数阶耗散情形。关键在于利用严格的标度超临界间隙（scaling-supercritical gap），使得曲率误差和非局部耗散误差均可视为摄动项处理。

Result: 在参数范围0<α<1, 2≤p<∞, 0<s<1-2α+3/p（及Besov空间对应条件）内，存在任意小的C_c^∞光滑初值，其唯一光滑解在任意短时间内可在相同空间范数下变得任意大，即范数膨胀成立。

Conclusion: 亚耗散Navier-Stokes方程在标度超临界区域同样表现出范数膨胀的不稳定性，进一步揭示了分数阶耗散下流体方程解的适定性极限。

Abstract: We prove same-space norm inflation for the three-dimensional hypodissipative Navier--Stokes equations with dissipation $(-Δ)^α$, $0<α<1$. Let $2\le p<\infty$ and \[
  0<s<1-2α+\frac3p. \] In the Besov case, let also $1\le q\le\infty$. There exist divergence-free $C_c^\infty(\mathbb R^3)$ initial data that are arbitrarily small in $W^{s,p}$, respectively in $B^s_{p,q}$, while the corresponding unique local smooth solution becomes arbitrarily large in the same space in arbitrarily short time. The proof adapts the anisotropic vortex-ring mixing mechanism to fractional dissipation; the strict scaling-supercritical gap makes both the curvature error and the nonlocal dissipative error perturbative.

</details>


### [375] [Global $W^{2,p}$ Regularity in Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24666)
*Shibing Chen,Yuanyuan Li,Xianduo Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了有界凸域间二次最优传输凸势函数的全局W^{2,p}估计，仅要求源和目标密度连续且有界远离零和无穷，不要求区域边界正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输正则性理论依赖区域边界光滑性，但实际应用中（如图像处理、经济调度）边界可能不满足光滑条件。本文旨在移除边界正则性假设，在更弱条件下建立全局W^{2,p}估计。

Method: 采用Pogorelov型内估计、边界上的Monge-Ampère方程退化分析，以及修正的Krylov-Safonov型Harnack不等式等技术，在凸域框架下通过精细的几何测度论和势函数估计建立全局先验界。

Result: 在源和目标密度仅为连续且一致正下有界、区域为有界凸域（无需边界光滑性）的条件下，得到了凸势函数的全局W^{2,p}估计。

Conclusion: 该结果显著降低了最优传输正则性理论对区域边界的正则性要求，为实际应用中更广泛的边界几何提供了理论保证。

Abstract: In this paper we establish global $W^{2,p}$ estimates for the convex potential functions of quadratic optimal transport between bounded convex domains. The source and target densities are assumed only to be continuous and bounded away from zero and infinity, and no regularity is imposed on the domain boundaries.

</details>


### [376] [Sharp kinetic trace theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24708)
*Lukas Niebel,Lisa Valentini*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对不同速度模型建立了尖刻的动力学迹估计及其反例。在半空间位置域上，对Lebesgue和标准高斯速度测度证明了自然迹估计。在有界空间域上，确定了边界正则性阈值：对于权重min{|v·n|,|v·n|^p}，估计在C^{1,α}域上当α≥α_p=1/(p+1)时成立，当α<α_p时在某些严格凸域上失效。特别地，自然迹(p=1)的正则性阈值为C^{1,1/2}。在d≥2的有界C^{1,1/2}域上，密度给出了自然迹算子和格林公式。对无限制高斯模型，在d≥2的每个有界C^{1,1}域上为1≤p<2构造了反例，否定了Albritton等人(2024)的问题1.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不同速度模型下动力学迹估计的精确边界正则性阈值，解决现有理论中关于迹算子存在性和格林公式适用性的开放问题。

Method: 通过分析半空间和有界空间域上不同速度模型（Lebesgue、高斯、有界支撑欧几里得速度、球面速度）的迹估计，利用对偶论证、速度平移技巧和反例构造来确定最优边界正则性条件。

Result: 确定了迹权重的边界正则性阈值为α_p=1/(p+1)，自然迹(p=1)的阈值为C^{1,1/2}；否定了无限制高斯模型中1≤p<2情况下迹估计在C^{1,1}域上的成立性。

Conclusion: 不同速度模型下动力学迹估计的最优边界正则性条件已被完全刻画，为相关偏微分方程问题的适定性分析提供了精确的理论基础。

Abstract: We establish sharp kinetic trace estimates and counterexamples across several velocity models. For half-space position domains, without any common bound on velocity support, we prove the natural trace estimate for both Lebesgue and standard Gaussian velocity measures. Density yields natural trace operators and Green's formula on the corresponding kinetic energy spaces. For bounded spatial domains in $d\ge2$, in the bounded-support Euclidean velocity model and in the spherical velocity model, we identify the sharp boundary regularity threshold for the trace weights $\min\{|v \cdot n|,|v \cdot n|^p\}$, $1\le p<\infty$. Writing $α_p=1/(p+1)$, the estimate holds on every bounded $\mathrm{C}^{1,α}$ domain with $α\geα_p$, and it fails for every $0<α<α_p$ on some strictly convex bounded domain of exact regularity $\mathrm{C}^{1,α}$. In particular, the natural trace ($p=1$) has the regularity threshold $\mathrm{C}^{1,1/2}$. On bounded $\mathrm{C}^{1,1/2}$ domains in $d\ge2$, density yields natural trace operators and Green's formula in the bounded-support Euclidean velocity model with either Lebesgue or standard Gaussian measure, and in the spherical velocity model. Norm-preserving velocity translation rules out the unrestricted Lebesgue trace estimate on every bounded $\mathrm{C}^1$ domain.
