In the unrestricted Gaussian model, for each $1\le p<2$, we construct counterexamples on every bounded $\mathrm{C}^{1,1}$ domain in dimension $d\ge2$, answering Question 1.8 of Albritton, Armstrong, Mourrat, and Novack (2024) negatively. For $2\le p<\infty$, the Gaussian $ω_2$ estimate and density instead yield $ω_p$-trace operators.

</details>

### [377] [Corrigendum to "Nonsymplectic automorphisms of prime order on O'Grady's sixfolds"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22645)
*Annalisa Grossi,Stevell Muller*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文纠正了作者此前论文中关于格论分类的遗漏情况，补充了缺失的情形并说明如何恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正此前发表的格论分类工作中的遗漏，完善理论框架。

Method: 识别并补充此前分类中缺失的案例，说明恢复这些案例的具体方法。

Result: 成功补充了此前分类中的缺失情形，并给出了恢复这些情形的途径。

Conclusion: 原分类存在不完整性，通过本文的修正，格论分类得以完善。

Abstract: In this short note we correct the lattice theoretic classification in [Rev. Mat. Iberoam. 38 (2022), no. 4, 1199--1218] of the first named author, including some missing cases and explaining how to recover them.

</details>


### [378] [K-theory of Weighted Blowups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22918)
*Veronica Arena,Alessio Cela,Alberto Landi,Michele Pernice*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文计算了沿光滑中心加权爆破的光滑栈的K-理论（满足消解性质），并将其应用于确定带2个标记点的稳定属1曲线栈的K-理论。同时，用爆破数据表示了爆破态射切复形的Lambda多项式，并将操作K-理论从环面作用推广到光滑仿射代数群作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权爆破是代数几何中的重要构造，但其K-理论计算尚不完善；同时，模空间栈（如带标记点的稳定曲线栈）的K-理论是理解其几何结构的关键，需要系统的方法来计算。

Method: 利用满足消解性质的光滑栈的加权爆破构造，通过K-理论的正则性及操作K-理论工具，结合切复形的Lambda多项式分析，并将操作K-理论从环面作用推广到一般光滑仿射代数群作用。

Result: 成功计算了沿光滑中心加权爆破的光滑栈的K-理论；确定了带2个标记点的稳定属1曲线栈的K-理论；用爆破数据显式表示了爆破态射切复形的Lambda多项式；将操作K-理论推广到光滑仿射代数群作用。

Conclusion: 本文建立了加权爆破K-理论计算的系统框架，为更一般的模空间栈的K-理论研究提供了有效工具，并将操作K-理论推广到更广泛的群作用情形。

Abstract: We compute the K-theory of weighted blowups of smooth stacks satisfying the resolution property along smooth centers. As an application, we determine the K-theory of the stack of stable genus 1 curves with 2 marked points. Furthermore, we express the Lambda polynomial of the tangent complex of the blowup morphism in terms of the blowup data. Along the way, we extend the operational K-theory from torus actions to actions of smooth affine algebraic groups.

</details>


### [379] [De Rham-Betti Groups of Some Abelian Fourfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23171)
*Zekun Ji*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定了多类阿贝尔簇的de Rham-Betti (dRB)群，证明了简单CM阿贝尔四维簇和四元CM自同态域的阿贝尔四维簇的dRB群等于其Mumford-Tate群。采用不同于Moonen-Zarhin的方法，利用Gross和Chudnovsky的两个结果、伽罗瓦理论分析以及极化带来的正定性约束，排除了Mumford-Tate群的某些约化子群作为dRB群的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于目前对dRB结构的认识有限，需要发展新的方法来研究阿贝尔簇上的dRB群。

Method: 采用与Moonen-Zarhin确定Mumford-Tate群不同的方法：利用Gross和Chudnovsky的两个结果，结合伽罗瓦理论分析和极化带来的正定性约束，排除Mumford-Tate群的某些约化子群作为dRB群的候选。

Result: 证明了简单CM阿贝尔四维簇和四元CM自同态域的阿贝尔四维簇的dRB群等于其Mumford-Tate群。

Conclusion: 对于特定类别的阿贝尔四维簇，dRB群与Mumford-Tate群一致；所采用的方法为有限知识条件下研究dRB群提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: We determine the de Rham--Betti (dRB) groups for several classes of abelian varieties over $\bar{\mathbb{Q}}$. We prove that $\mathrm{G}_{\mathrm{dRB}}(A)=\mathrm{MT}(A)$ for simple CM abelian fourfolds and abelian fourfolds with quartic CM endomorphism field. Due to the current limited knowledge of dRB structures, we adopt an approach different from Moonen--Zarhin's method of determining Mumford-Tate groups. Instead, we use two results by Gross and Chudnovsky, Galois-theoretic analysis and positivity constraints arising from polarizations to exclude certain reductive subgroups of Mumford-Tate groups as candidates for dRB groups. This article is based on the second part of the author's PhD thesis (https://pure.uva.nl/ws/files/311471255/Thesis.pdf); see also https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01072.

</details>


### [380] [Unramified Motivic Multiple Mixed Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23455)
*Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多重混合值（MMVs）在 motivic 层面何时可简化为多重zeta值（MZVs，即 level 1）的问题。通过使用Brown等人的下降理论，完全确定了深度小于4的所有无分歧 motivic MMVs，以及一些无界深度族，并提出了深度大于3的一般猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重混合值（MMVs）是多重zeta值的一种变体，其求和指标受限于固定的奇偶模式。一个关键问题是确定这些值何时可表达为多重zeta值（即 level 1）。完全解决该问题需要超越数论的新思想，目前尚超出知识范围，但 motivic 层面已取得重要进展。

Method: 利用Brown等人发展的下降理论（descent theory），对一般情形下的MMVs进行分析。此前已处理了若干具有正则奇偶模式的特例（Hoffman的多重t值、Kaneko和Tsumura的多重T值、作者的多重S值），本文转向一般情形，完全确定了深度小于4的所有无分歧 motivic MMVs，以及若干无界深度族。

Result: 完全确定了深度小于4的所有无分歧 motivic MMVs，以及若干无界深度族的具体结构。

Conclusion: 在 motivic 层面上，对深度小于4的所有MMVs及若干无界深度族完成了完全分类；针对深度大于3的情形，提出了若干一般性猜想。

Abstract: The multiple mixed values (MMVs) are level two variants of multiple zeta values produced by restricting the summation indices to fixed parity patterns. One particularly interesting problem is to determine exactly when such values are actually in level one, namely, expressible in terms of multiple zeta values. To solve this completely is beyond our current knowledge since it calls for new ideas from transcendental number theory. However, much progress has been made on the motivic level. Previously, using the descent theory developed by Brown et al. we have tackled this problem for a few special classes of MMVs with regular parity patterns among the summation indices, including Hoffman's multiple $t$-values, Kaneko and Tsumura's multiple $T$-values, and our own multiple $S$-values. In this paper, we turn to the general case and determine completely all the unramified motivic MMVs of depth less than four as well as a few families of unbounded depths. At the end of the paper, we will present some general conjectures to describe the unramified MMVs at all depths greater than three.

</details>


### [381] [Sylow Criteria for Liftability of Automorphism Groups of Smooth Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23465)
*Baiting Xie,Zhiwei Zheng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立了射影线性群上有限子群可提升性的Sylow准则，以及关于多项式F的F-可提升性的Sylow准则，证明通过群上同调中的限制与余限制结合约化到Klein方法完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于射影线性群上的有限子群，何时可以提升到一般线性群是一个基本问题；同样，对于保持多项式不变的有限群，何时可以提升为多项式的线性对称群也是重要问题。已有结果缺乏统一的Sylow型判据。

Method: 利用群上同调中的限制（restriction）与余限制（corestriction）技巧，结合约化到Klein方法（reduction-to-Klein method），将整体可提升性问题约化到Sylow p-子群层面进行验证。

Result: 给出了射影线性群上有限子群可提升性的Sylow准则；对于特征零域上N元d次非奇异多项式F，证明了有限子群G⊆Lin(F)是F-可提升的当且仅当对每个整除gcd(|G|,N,d)的素数p，G的某个Sylow p-子群是F-可提升的。

Conclusion: 建立了可提升性问题与Sylow子群可提升性之间的等价关系，将整体判断约化为素数幂阶子群的检验，提供了实用的判定工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we first establish Sylow criteria for liftability of finite subgroups of the projective linear groups over arbitrary field. We also obtain Sylow criteria for $F$-liftability: if $F$ is a nonsingular polynomial of degree $d$ in $N$ variables over a field of characteristic zero, then a finite subgroup $G$ of $\mathrm{Lin}(F)$ is $F$-liftable if and only if for every prime $p$ dividing $\gcd(|G|,N,d)$, there exists a Sylow $p$-subgroup of $G$ that is $F$-liftable. Our proof combines restriction and corestriction in group cohomology with the reduction-to-Klein method.

</details>


### [382] [Actions of $(\mathbb{Z}/4)^4$ on rationally connected threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23587)
*Konstantin Loginov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明群$G=(\mathbb{Z}/4)^4$忠实作用在有理连通三维簇上时，该簇必$G$-双有理等价于Fermat四次三维簇；若为terminal $G\mathbb{Q}$-Fano三维簇则等价为双正则。由此得出$G$不能嵌入三维Cremona群$\operatorname{Cr}_3(\mathbb{C})$，并完整分类了$(\mathbb{Z}/m)^r$何时可嵌入$\operatorname{Cr}_3(\mathbb{C})$或三维有理连通簇的双有理自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类有限群在有理连通三维簇上的忠实作用，特别是确定哪些交换群可嵌入三维Cremona群或三维有理连通簇的双有理自同构群，从而推广二维情形下的经典分类结果。

Method: 通过分析$G=(\mathbb{Z}/4)^4$在有理连通三维簇上的忠实作用，证明该作用必$G$-双有理等价于Fermat四次三维簇的标准作用；对terminal $G\mathbb{Q}$-Fano三维簇进一步证明该等价为双正则；结合已有分类结果完成完整分类。

Result: $G=(\mathbb{Z}/4)^4$不能嵌入$\operatorname{Cr}_3(\mathbb{C})$；完整分类了$(m,r)$使得$(\mathbb{Z}/m)^r$可嵌入$\operatorname{Cr}_3(\mathbb{C})$或三维有理连通簇的双有理自同构群。

Conclusion: 完成了交换群嵌入三维Cremona群及三维有理连通簇双有理自同构群的完整分类，推进了双有理几何中有限群作用的研究。

Abstract: Let $G=(\mathbb{Z}/4)^4$. We prove that if $X$ is a rationally connected threefold with a faithful action of $G$, then $X$ is $G$-birational to the Fermat quartic threefold. If $X$ is a terminal $G\mathbb{Q}$-Fano threefold, this birational equivalence is biregular. Consequently, the group $G$ acts faithfully on a rationally connected threefold but does not embed into $\operatorname{Cr}_3(\mathbb{C})$. Combined with earlier results, this yields a complete classification of the pairs $(m,r)$ for which $(\mathbb{Z}/m)^r$ embeds into $\operatorname{Cr}_3(\mathbb{C})$, and of those for which it embeds into $\operatorname{Bir}(X)$ for a rationally connected threefold $X$.

</details>


### [383] [The Chow Characteristic Image of \(\Spin(10)\) via the Affine Cone over the Spinor Variety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23729)
*Sanghoon Baek*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定了分裂自旋群 G=Spin(10) 的积分Chow限制映射 CH(BG)→CH(BT)^W 的像（即BG的Chow环模挠部分）。主要几何创新是通过特殊Clifford群Γ⁺(10)在旋量簇的半旋嵌入中的仿射锥的等变推前构造了类c₂c₃c₅。模2下，像是由包含c₂²,c₃²,c₄²,c₅和c₂c₃c₅的最小Steenrod稳定子环加上半旋表示顶Chern类的环面限制生成的子环；积分像则是该模2子环在模2约化下的完全原像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Spin(10)的BG的Chow环模挠部分的结构，即积分Chow限制映射的像。

Method: 通过构造特殊Clifford群Γ⁺(10)在旋量簇半旋嵌入中的仿射锥的等变推前，得到关键类c₂c₃c₅；结合Steenrod运算分析模2像，再通过模2约化得到积分像。

Result: 模2下像由包含c₂²,c₃²,c₄²,c₅和c₂c₃c₅的最小Steenrod稳定子环加上半旋表示顶Chern类的环面限制生成；积分像为该模2子环在模2约化下的完全原像。

Conclusion: 完整描述了Spin(10)的积分Chow限制映射的像，即BG的Chow环模挠部分的结构。

Abstract: Let \(G=\Spin(10)\) be the split spin group over a field of characteristic different from \(2\), and let \(T\subset G\) be a split maximal torus. We determine the image of the integral Chow restriction map \(\CH(BG)\to \CH(BT)^W\), equivalently \(\CH(BG)\) modulo torsion. The main new geometric ingredient in the proof is a construction of the class \(c_2c_3c_5\), where the \(c_i\) are the elementary Chern classes after restriction to \(T\). This class is obtained from the proper equivariant push-forward associated with the affine cone over the spinor variety in its half-spin embedding for the special Clifford group \(Γ^+(10)\).
  Modulo two, the image is the subring generated, over the smallest Steenrod-stable subring of \(\F[c_2,c_3,c_4,c_5]\) containing \(c_2^2,c_3^2,c_4^2,c_5\) and \(c_2c_3c_5\), by the torus restriction of the top Chern class of a half-spin representation. The integral characteristic image is the full inverse image of this mod-two subring under reduction modulo \(2\).

</details>


### [384] [Projective moduli of log Calabi--Yau fibrations over curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23878)
*Giovanni Inchiostro,Junyan Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为曲线上的log Calabi-Yau纤维化引入了一种新的稳定性条件，证明了该条件能构造出具有射影粗模空间的proper Deligne-Mumford叠，其边界仍参数化平坦纤维化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为log Calabi-Yau纤维化建立适当的模空间理论，需要引入合适的稳定性条件来保证模空间的紧致性和良好性质。

Method: 引入一种新的稳定性条件，利用对数几何和模叠方法构造Deligne-Mumford叠，并证明其具有射影粗模空间。

Result: 该稳定性条件能构造出proper Deligne-Mumford叠，其粗模空间是射影的，且边界仍由平坦纤维化参数化。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定性条件为log Calabi-Yau纤维化的模空间理论提供了合适的框架，保证了模空间的proper性和射影性。

Abstract: We introduce a new stability condition for log Calabi--Yau fibrations over curves. We prove that it gives rise to a proper Deligne--Mumford stack with a projective coarse moduli space, whose boundary still parametrizes flat fibrations over curves.

</details>


### [385] [Stratified motivic invariants and bivariate deformations of Poincaré polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23890)
*Gergely Bérczi,Young-Hoon Kiem*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入分层变体的echelon塔概念，为模空间M¯_{0,n}和Fulton-MacPherson簇Y[n]建立了分层不变量的显式归纳公式，并证明Berczi-Kiem(2026)中神秘的偶度Poincaré多项式双变量形变实际上是分层虚拟Poincaré多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释模空间M¯_{0,n}和Fulton-MacPherson簇Y[n]中出现的双变量形变Poincaré多项式的几何本质，揭示其作为分层不变量的内在结构。

Method: 引入echelon塔概念描述分层变体的递推结构，利用诱导公式计算分层不变量的显式表达式。

Result: 证明了M¯_{0,n+1}和P^1[n]的偶度Poincaré多项式的双变量形变就是分层虚拟Poincaré多项式。

Conclusion: 通过分层不变量理论统一了模空间和Fulton-MacPherson簇的某些形变不变量的几何解释。

Abstract: For a stratified variety $X$ and a motivic invariant $\mathbb{H}$, we consider the stratified invariant which interpolates the invariant of $X$ and that of its interior $X^{\circ}$. Based on observations in moduli theory, we introduce the notion of an echelon tower of stratified varieties and then prove explicit inductive formulae for the stratified invariants of the moduli spaces $\overline{M}_{0,n}$ of stable curves of genus $0$ and the Fulton-MacPherson varieties $Y[n]$ for any smooth projective variety $Y$. Using these, we show that the mysterious bivariate deformations of the even degree Poincaré polynomials of $\overline{M}_{0,n+1}$ and $\mathbb{P}^1[n]$ in \cite{BercziKiem2026} are nothing but stratified virtual Poincaré polynomials.

</details>


### [386] [Biangular lines with angles arccos(1/5) and arccos(3/5)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23897)
*Paul Tricot*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了特定角度（arccos(1/5)和arccos(1/3)）的双角线系统，通过积分格点理论，对7至10维中的最大双角线系统进行了分类，并在15维中构造了一个与已知最大系统规模相当的新系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有7至20维中最大的双角线系统由Ganzhinov和Szöllősi给出，但其在7至10维中的分类尚不明确，且15维中是否存在其他同等规模的系统有待探索。

Method: 利用双角线与积分格点之间的理论联系，对特定角度参数下的双角线系统进行分类和构造。

Result: 完成了7至10维中最大双角线系统的分类，并在15维中构造了一个与已知最大规模相同的新系统。

Conclusion: 进一步揭示了双角线与积分格点之间的深刻联系，为更高维度的双角线系统研究提供了新的思路和构造方法。

Abstract: The largest known biangular lines systems in dimension 7 through 20 are due to Ganzhinov and Szöllősi, and the lines make angles $\arccos(\frac{1}{5})$ and $\arccos(\frac{1}{5})$. They are constructed by making use of the nice connection with integral lattices. We explore further the connection between biangular lines and integral lattices to classify the largest biangular line systems with these angles, in dimension 7 through 10. We also give a new biangular line system in dimension 15, that matches the size of the largest known in this dimension.

</details>


### [387] [Roots of Bernstein-Sato polynomials for projective hypersurfaces with ordinary double points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24142)
*Seung-Jo Jung,Morihiko Saito*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究次数≥3的超曲面（带普通二重点）的Bernstein-Sato多项式的根结构，给出了根由1、(n-1)/2和j/d组成的上界估计，并证明了在特定组合条件下的上界紧致性，同时提出向A₂奇点推广的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定带普通二重点的超曲面其定义多项式的Bernstein-Sato多项式的根的具体形式与上界，并验证该上界在一般位置下的最优性，为奇点理论中的B-S多项式研究提供精确刻画。

Method: 利用Alexander-Hirschowitz定理等代数几何工具，构造具有给定s个一般位置普通二重点的d次齐次多项式，通过组合不等式条件（如二项式系数与奇点数的关系）证明上界p_f≤q_s的紧致性。

Result: 证明了在⌊d/2⌋+n-2选n-1≥s或d+n-3选n-1≥sn的条件下，上界p_f=q_s是可达的，即存在多项式使得B-S多项式的根结构达到该上界。

Conclusion: 给出了普通二重点超曲面B-S多项式根的上界的精确刻画，并猜想该结果可推广至A₂奇点情形。

Abstract: Let $X\subset{\mathbb P}^{n-1}$ be a hypersurface of degree $d\ge3$ with ordinary double points, where $n\ge3$. The roots of Bernstein-Sato polynomial of its defining polynomial $f$ are given up to sign by 1, $(n-1)/2$, and $j/d$ for $j\in{\mathbb Z}\cap[n,nd-n-p_f]$ with $p_f$ a positive integer. Here $p_f$ is bounded above by the minimal positive integer $q_s$ satisfying $\binom{q_s+n-1}{n-1}>s:=|{\rm Sing}\,X|$, and we can verify that $p_f$ coincides with $q_s$ in the case the singular points of $X$ are in ``general position". We show that this upper bound is sharp in the case $\binom{\lfloor d/2\rfloor+n-2}{n-1}\ge s$ or $\binom{d+n-3}{n-1}\ge sn$ by providing a homogeneous polynomial of degree $d$ such that the associated projective hypersurface has ordinary double points at given $s$ points in sufficiently general position and is nonsingular outside them (using a theorem of Alexander and Hirschowitz for the second case). It is conjectured that the above sharp bound under the first hypothesis can be extended naturally to the case where $X$ has only $A_2$-singularities instead of ordinary double points.

</details>


### [388] [From Regions to Hodge Structures: The Topological Study of semialgebraic Curves Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24437)
*Abolfazl Soltanpour*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为具有通常多重交点的有限连通半代数曲线构型建立了组合理论，通过局部节点贡献ψ决定了全局几何与拓扑性质。证明了精确区域计数公式、刻画了极大构型、将删除-限制递归推广至一般曲线构型。在代数情形下，证明了无三重点是OS型代数通过上同调分解的充分条件，但反之不成立。对线构型计算了简化OS模型与H^2的差异，证明了该差异由k_x≥4的节点主导且等于∑binom(k_x-1,2)。节点贡献出现在混合Hodge结构的Euler示性数中，对线构型等于dim Gr_4^W H^2。并发线缺陷复形表明正合性障碍需要曲线-wise关联数据。最后引入了二项式节点不变量{Ψ_k}，证明了Ψ_2是泛线性局部可加不变量，ψ=Ψ_1-Ψ_0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立连通半代数曲线构型的组合理论，统一处理通常多重交点情形，建立局部节点贡献与全局几何拓扑性质之间的精确联系，并扩展Orlik-Solomon类代数理论到更一般的曲线构型。

Method: 发展组合理论框架，引入节点贡献ψ；证明区域计数公式与极大构型刻画；推广删除-限制递归；分析OS型代数的分解条件；通过上同调计算差异；引入二项式节点不变量Ψ_k并证明其泛性质。

Result: (1) 无三重点是OS代数通过上同调分解的充分但非必要条件；(2) 线构型中简化OS模型与H^2的差异为∑_{k_x≥4}binom(k_x-1,2)；(3) 节点贡献在混合Hodge结构中体现为Euler示性数，线构型中等于dim Gr_4^W H^2；(4) Ψ_2是泛线性局部可加不变量，ψ=Ψ_1-Ψ_0。

Conclusion: 建立了曲线构型中局部节点贡献与全局不变量之间的系统联系，揭示了多重交点代数与拓扑性质的精确关系，并引入了新的不变量体系。

Abstract: We develop a combinatorial theory for finite arrangements of connected semialgebraic curves with ordinary multiple intersections, governed by a local node contribution $ψ$ that determines global geometric and topological properties. We prove exact region-count formulas, characterize maximal arrangements, and extend the deletion--restriction recurrence to general curve arrangements. In the algebraic setting, we prove that the absence of triple points ($k_x=3$) is a sufficient condition for the OS-type algebra to factor through cohomology; the converse, however, fails already for line arrangements, where the classical Orlik--Solomon relations ensure factorization even in the presence of triple points. For line arrangements we compute the discrepancy between the simplified OS-model and $H^2$, showing it is governed by nodes with $k_x\ge4$ and equals $\sum_{k_x\ge4}\binom{k_x-1}{2}$. The node contribution appears in the mixed Hodge structure via the Euler characteristic and, for line arrangements, equals $\dim \operatorname{Gr}_4^W H^2$. The defect complex for concurrent lines reveals that exactness obstructions require curve-wise incidence data. Finally, we introduce binomial node invariants $\{Ψ_k\}$, prove $Ψ_2$ is the universal linearly locally additive invariant, and show $ψ=Ψ_1-Ψ_0$.

</details>


### [389] [Simply branched covers of curves and wild conductor exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24454)
*Harry Spencer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用形变理论证明，p-adic域上光滑射影几何连通曲线的覆叠可通过p-adic摄动得到附近简单分支的覆叠，且当D=ℙ¹且π*O(1)非常丰沛时，可保持底曲线C不变。作为应用，给出了素数p>d时曲线的wild conductor指数关于任意d次覆叠C→ℙ¹的分支数据的公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过形变理论建立p-adic域上曲线覆叠的摄动存在性，并利用这种摄动将wild conductor指数的计算化为覆叠分支数据的组合公式。

Method: 运用形变理论（deformation theory）构造p-adic摄动，将一般覆叠转化为简单分支覆叠；结合代数几何中的非常丰沛性条件（π*O(1) very ample）保证在特定情形下底曲线不变。

Result: 证明了p-adic域上曲线覆叠可p-adic摄动为简单分支覆叠；当D=ℙ¹且π*O(1)非常丰沛时，摄动可保持C不变；给出了p>d时曲线wild conductor指数关于d次覆叠C→ℙ¹分支数据的显式公式。

Conclusion: 建立了p-adic域上曲线覆叠的形变—摄动理论，并由此得到了wild conductor指数的一种纯组合计算方式，简化了该不变量的计算。

Abstract: We use deformation theory to show that a cover of smooth, projective, geometrically connected curves $π\colon C\to D$ over a $p$-adic field can be $p$-adically perturbed to obtain a nearby simply branched cover $π'\colon C'\to D$ and that, if $D=\mathbb{P}^1$ and $π^*\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(1)$ is very ample, then we may take $C'=C$. As an application, we give a formula for the wild conductor exponent of a curve at a prime $p>d$ in terms of the ramification data of any degree $d$ cover $C\to\mathbb{P}^1$.

</details>
